(2) 确定平衡位置, 并说明是否稳定平衡;

(3) 写出系统在平衡位置附近做小振动的拉格朗日函数；

(4) 求简正频率和简正坐标；

(5) 画出振动模式图.

解 (1)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L = T - V & = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {y} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k \left\{\left[ (x + 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} - \sqrt {2} \right\} ^ {2} \\ & - \frac {1}{2} k \left\{\left[ (x - 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} - \sqrt {2} \right\} ^ {2} \\ & - \frac {1}{2} k \left\{\left[ (x + 1) ^ {2} + (y + 1) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} - \sqrt {2} \right\} ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

(2) 平衡位置处有

$$
\frac {\partial V}{\partial x} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial V}{\partial y} = 0
$$

由 $\frac{\partial V}{\partial x} = 0$ 得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & k \left[ \sqrt {(x + 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2}} - \sqrt {2} \right] \frac {x + 1}{\sqrt {(x + 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2}}} \\ & \quad + k \left[ \sqrt {(x - 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2}} - \sqrt {2} \right] \frac {x - 1}{\sqrt {(x - 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2}}} \\ & \quad + k \left[ \sqrt {(x + 1) ^ {2} + (y + 1) ^ {2}} - \sqrt {2} \right] \frac {x + 1}{\sqrt {(x + 1) ^ {2} + (y + 1) ^ {2}}} = 0 \\ & x + 1 + x - 1 + x + 1 - \sqrt {2} \left[ \frac {x + 1}{\sqrt {(x + 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2}}} + \frac {x - 1}{\sqrt {(x - 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2}}} \right. \\ & \quad \left. + \frac {x + 1}{\sqrt {(x + 1) ^ {2} + (y + 1) ^ {2}}} \right] = 0 \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\partial V}{\partial y} = 0$ 得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & y - 1 + y - 1 + y + 1 - \sqrt {2} \left[ \frac {y - 1}{\sqrt {(x + 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2}}} \right. \\ & \left. + \frac {y - 1}{\sqrt {(x - 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2}}} + \frac {y + 1}{\sqrt {(x + 1) ^ {2} + (y + 1) ^ {2}}} \right] = 0 \end{array}
$$

x=0,y=0 是上述两个方程的解,因此,(0,0)点是平衡位置.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \left. \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x ^ {2}} \right| _ {(0, 0)} & = k \left\{3 - \sqrt {2} \left[ \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x + 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2}}} + \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x - 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2}}} \right. \right. \\ & \quad \left. + \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x + 1) ^ {2} + (y + 1) ^ {2}}} \right] - \frac {(x + 1) ^ {2}}{\left[ (x + 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2} \right] ^ {3 / 2}} \\ & \quad \left. - \frac {(x - 1) ^ {2}}{\left[ (x - 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2} \right] ^ {3 / 2}} - \frac {(x + 1) ^ {2}}{\left[ (x + 1) ^ {2} + (y + 1) ^ {2} \right] ^ {3 / 2}} \right\} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} = \frac {3}{2} k \\ & \quad \left. \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial y ^ {2}} \right| _ {(0, 0)} = \frac {3}{2} k \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x \partial y} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} & = k (- \sqrt {2}) \left\{- \frac {(x + 1) (y - 1)}{[ (x + 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2} ] ^ {3 / 2}} \right. \\ & \quad - \frac {(x - 1) (y - 1)}{[ (x - 1) ^ {2} + (y - 1) ^ {2} ] ^ {3 / 2}} \\ & \quad - \frac {(x + 1) (y + 1)}{[ (x + 1) ^ {2} + (y + 1) ^ {2} ] ^ {3 / 2}} \Big \} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} = \frac {1}{2} k \\ & \quad \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x ^ {2}} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} x ^ {2} + 2 \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x \partial y} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} x y + \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial y ^ {2}} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} y ^ {2} \\ & = \frac {3}{2} k x ^ {2} + k x y + \frac {3}{2} k y ^ {2} = \frac {3}{2} k \left(x + \frac {1}{3} y\right) ^ {2} + \frac {4}{3} k y ^ {2} > 0 \end{array}
$$

可见，(0,0)点是稳定平衡位置.

(3) 在平衡位置 $(0,0)$ 附近，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} V (x, y) & \approx V (0, 0) + \frac {1}{2} \left[ \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x ^ {2}} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} x ^ {2} + 2 \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x \partial y} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} x y + \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial y ^ {2}} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} y ^ {2} \right] \\ & = \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {3}{2} k x ^ {2} + k x y + \frac {3}{2} k y ^ {2}\right) \\ L & = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {y} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k \left(\frac {3}{2} x ^ {2} + x y + \frac {3}{2} y ^ {2}\right) \end{array}
$$

(4) 由动能、势能的表达式, 可写惯性矩阵和刚度矩阵为.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {M} & = \left( \begin{array}{l l} m & 0 \\ 0 & m \end{array} \right), \quad \boldsymbol {\Lambda} = \left( \begin{array}{l l} \frac {3}{2} k & \frac {1}{2} k \\ \frac {1}{2} k & \frac {3}{2} k \end{array} \right) \\ & \boldsymbol {H} = \boldsymbol {K} - \omega^ {2} \boldsymbol {M} \\ & \boldsymbol {H} \cdot \boldsymbol {u} = 0 \\ & \left[ \begin{array}{c c} \frac {3}{2} k - m \omega^ {2} & \frac {1}{2} k \\ \frac {1}{2} k & \frac {3}{2} k - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right] \left( \begin{array}{l} u _ {1} \\ u _ {2} \end{array} \right) = 0 \\ & \left| \begin{array}{c c} \frac {3}{2} k - m \omega^ {2} & \frac {1}{2} k \\ \frac {1}{2} k & \frac {3}{2} k - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0 \\ & m ^ {2} \omega^ {4} - 3 m k \omega^ {2} + 2 k ^ {2} = 0 \\ & \omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {2 k}{m}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

解出

将 $\omega_{1}$ 代入(1)式，取 $u_{1}^{(1)} = 1$ ，得 $u_{2}^{(1)} = -1$ ；将 $\omega_{2}$ 代入(1)式，取 $u_{1}^{(2)} = 1$ ，得 $u_{2}^{(2)} = -1.$ 由简正坐标和原用坐标的关系，

$$
\binom {\xi} {\eta} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} u _ {1} ^ {(1)} & u _ {2} ^ {(1)} \\ u _ {1} ^ {(2)} & u _ {2} ^ {(2)} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} m _ {1 1} & m _ {1 2} \\ m _ {2 1} & m _ {2 2} \end{array} \right) \binom {x} {y}
$$

$$
= \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 1 \\ 1 & - 1 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} m & 0 \\ 0 & m \end{array} \right) \binom {x} {y} = m \binom {x + y} {x - y}
$$

简正坐标的常数倍也是简正坐标,可取

$$
\xi = x + y, \quad \eta = x - y
$$

为简正坐标.

(5) 振动模式可图示如图 10.23:

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/59cf35f4eb8c4617d39b03b0d877a08970b47fb2612c97d6c5712e50702ce766.jpg)



图10.23


10.1.28 一质量为 $m$ 的质点在重力作用下在一个方程为

$$
z = x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} - x y
$$

的光滑曲面上运动,其中 z 轴指向竖直向上.

(1) 求此质点的运动微分方程；

(2) 求质点围绕稳定平衡位置做小振动的简正频率；

(3) 若将质点由平衡位置稍微移开一点再放开它, 要保证只有较高频率的简正模式被激发, x 和 y 的位移之比应为多大?

解 (1)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & z = x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} - x y \\ & \dot {z} = 2 x \dot {x} + 2 y \dot {y} - \dot {x} y - x \dot {y} = \dot {x} (2 x - y) + \dot {y} (2 y - x) \\ & L = \frac {1}{2} m [ \dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {x} ^ {2} (2 x - y) ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} (2 y - x) ^ {2} \\ & \quad + 2 \dot {x} \dot {y} (2 x - y) (2 y - x) ] - m g (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} - x y) \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{q}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} [ \dot {x} + \dot {x} (2 x - y) ^ {2} + \dot {y} (2 x - y) (2 y - x) ]} \\ & {= 2 \dot {x} ^ {2} (2 x - y) - \dot {y} ^ {2} (2 y - x) + 2 \dot {x} \dot {y} (2 y - x) - \dot {x} \dot {y} (2 x - y) - 2 g x + g y} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} [ \dot {y} + \dot {y} (2 y - x) ^ {2} + \dot {x} (2 x - y) (2 y - x) ]} \\ & {= - \dot {x} ^ {2} (2 x - y) + 2 \dot {y} (2 y - x) - \dot {x} \dot {y} (2 y - x) + 2 \dot {x} \dot {y} (2 x - y) - 2 g y + g x} \end{array}
$$

(2) 在平衡位置, $\dot{x} = 0$ , $\dot{y} = 0$ , $\ddot{x} = 0$ , $\ddot{y} = 0$ , 由上述运动微分方程或由 $\frac{\partial V}{\partial x} = 0$ , $\frac{\partial V}{\partial y} = 0$ , 均可得平衡位置满足的方程为

$$
\begin{array}{l} - 2 g x + g y = 0 \\ - 2 g y + g x = 0 \end{array}
$$

可见， $x = 0,y = 0$ 为平衡位置.

在平衡位置附近的运动,仅保留二级小量,拉格朗日函数可近似为

$$
L \approx \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) - m g (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} - x y)
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{q}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 或由上述运动微分方程作近似，只保留一级小量，均可得在平衡位置附近运动的运动微分方程为

设

$$
\begin{array}{l} \ddot {x} = - 2 g x + g y \\ \ddot {y} = - 2 g y + g x \\ x = u _ {1} \cos (\omega t + \alpha) \\ y = u _ {2} \cos (\omega t + \alpha) \end{array}
$$

代入运动微分方程,得

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c} 2 g - \omega^ {2} & - g \\ - g & 2 g - \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right) \binom {u _ {1}} {u _ {2}} = 0\tag{1}
$$

特征方程为

解得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left| \begin{array}{c c} {2 g - \omega^ {2}} & {- g} \\ {- g} & {2 g - \omega^ {2}} \end{array} \right| = 0} \\ & {\omega^ {4} - 4 g \omega^ {2} + 3 g = 0} \\ & {\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {g}, \qquad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {3 g}} \end{array}
$$

(3) 将 $\omega_{2}$ 代入(1)式得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (2 g - 3 g) u _ {1} ^ {(2)} - g u _ {2} ^ {(2)} & = 0 \\ \frac {u _ {2} ^ {(2)}}{u _ {1} ^ {(2)}} & = - 1 \end{array}
$$

因此，当 $x, y$ 的初始位移大小相等、正负号相反时，从那里静止释放，就只有较高频率的振动模式被激发， $\frac{u_1^{(2)}}{u_2^{(2)}} = -1$ 就是题目要求的 $x$ 和 $y$ 的位移之比.

10.1.29 图 10.24 中两物体质量均为 m、两弹簧的劲度系数均为 k，绳子不可伸长，绳子与滑轮间无摩擦力。当系统在平衡位置时给左边物体向下的初速度 $v_{0}$ ，求系统的运动。

解 取竖直向上的 $x_{1}$ 坐标表示右边物体的位置, 竖直向下的 $x_{2}$ 坐标表示左边物体的位置, $x_{1}, x_{2}$ 的零点分别为系统平衡时 $m_{1}, m_{2}$ 所在位置, 弹簧力和重力的合力的势能为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {V = \frac {1}{2} k x _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k (x _ {2} - x _ {1}) ^ {2} = k x _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x _ {2} ^ {2} - k x _ {1} x _ {2}} \\ & {\qquad T = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad M = \left( \begin{array}{l l} m & 0 \\ 0 & m \end{array} \right), \quad K = \left( \begin{array}{l l} 2 k & - k \\ - k & k \end{array} \right)} \\ & {\qquad H = K - \omega^ {2} M = \left( \begin{array}{l l} 2 k - m \omega^ {2} & - k \\ - k & k - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right)} \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/38b9e64464bf9a37b4ac711428ac72a477e0ad8e3e35721038e2dabeadede9d0.jpg)



图10.24


由 $|\pmb{H}| = 0$ 得

$$
m ^ {2} \omega^ {4} - 3 m k \omega^ {2} + k ^ {2} = 0
$$

$$
\omega_ {1} ^ {2} = \frac {3 + \sqrt {5}}{2} \frac {k}{m}, \quad \omega_ {2} ^ {2} = \frac {3 - \sqrt {5}}{2} \frac {k}{m}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {H} \left(\omega_ {1}\right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} \frac {1 - \sqrt {5}}{2} k & - k \\ - k & - \frac {1 + \sqrt {5}}{2} k \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\operatorname{adj} \boldsymbol {H} (\omega_ {1}) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} - \frac {1 + \sqrt {5}}{2} k & k \\ k & \frac {1 - \sqrt {5}}{2} k \end{array} \right)
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(1)} = 1$ ，则 $u_{2}^{(1)} = \frac{1 - \sqrt{5}}{2}$ 

$$
\boldsymbol {H} (\omega_ {2}) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} \frac {1 + \sqrt {5}}{2} k & - k \\ - k & \frac {- 1 + \sqrt {5}}{2} k \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\operatorname{adj} \boldsymbol {H} \left(\omega_ {2}\right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} \frac {- 1 + \sqrt {5}}{2} k & k \\ k & \frac {1 + \sqrt {5}}{2} k \end{array} \right)
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(2)} = 1$ ，则 $u_{2}^{(2)} = \frac{1 + \sqrt{5}}{2}$ 

两个特解为

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} x _ {1} ^ {(1)} = \sin (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) \\ x _ {2} ^ {(1)} = \frac {1 - \sqrt {5}}{2} \sin (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) \\ x _ {1} ^ {(2)} = \sin (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) \\ x _ {2} ^ {(2)} = \frac {1 + \sqrt {5}}{2} \sin (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) \end{array} \right.
$$

通解为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & x _ {1} = C _ {1} x _ {1} ^ {(1)} + C _ {2} x _ {1} ^ {(2)} = C _ {1} \sin (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + C _ {2} \sin (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) \\ & x _ {2} = C _ {1} x _ {2} ^ {(1)} + C _ {2} x _ {2} ^ {(2)} \\ & \quad = \frac {1 - \sqrt {5}}{2} C _ {1} \sin (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + \frac {1 + \sqrt {5}}{2} C _ {2} \sin (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) \end{array}
$$

初始条件为: t=0 时, $x_{1}=0$ , $\dot{x}_{1}=0$ , $x_{2}=0$ , $\dot{x}_{2}=v_{0}$ , 定出

$$
C _ {1} = - \frac {v _ {0}}{\sqrt {5} \omega_ {1}} = - 0. 2 7 6 v _ {0} \sqrt {\frac {m}{k}}, \quad C _ {2} = \frac {v _ {0}}{\sqrt {5} \omega_ {2}} = 0. 7 2 4 v _ {0} \sqrt {\frac {m}{k}}
$$

$$
\alpha_ {1} = \alpha_ {2} = 0
$$

$$
x _ {1} = - 0. 2 7 6 v _ {0} \sqrt {\frac {m}{k}} \sin \left(1. 6 1 8 \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} t\right) + 0. 7 2 4 v _ {0} \sqrt {\frac {m}{k}} \sin \left(0. 6 1 8 \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} t\right)
$$

$$
x _ {2} = 0. 1 7 1 v _ {0} \sqrt {\frac {m}{k}} \sin \left(1. 6 1 8 \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} t\right) + 1. 1 7 v _ {0} \sqrt {\frac {m}{k}} \sin \left(0. 6 1 8 \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} t\right)
$$

10.1.30 质量均为 m 的两小球固定在长度为 2l 的轻杆上, 杆的两端支撑在劲度系数均为 k 的弹簧上, 如图 10.25 所示, 求系统只做竖直方向的小振动时的简正频率以及节点(振动模式中保持不动的点)的位置.

解 取竖直向上的 $x_{1}$ 、 $x_{2}$ 、 $x_{3}$ 轴分别表示杆的右端、中心和左端的位置，原点分别取在弹簧为原长时 1、2、3 点的位置.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/44fb5183670e1487b26ddda022510aaeb096b5a95c02efae56c6cf140cdab96b.jpg)


$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2}
$$


图10.25


$$
V = \frac {1}{2} k x _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x _ {3} ^ {2} + m g x _ {1} + m g x _ {2}
$$

$x_{1}, x_{2}, x_{3}$ 不是都独立的，有约束关系

$$
x _ {2} = \frac {1}{2} (x _ {1} + x _ {3}), \quad \text {或} \quad x _ {3} = 2 x _ {2} - x _ {1}
$$

取 $x_{1}, x_{2}$ 为广义坐标，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} V & = \frac {1}{2} k x _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k (2 x _ {2} - x _ {1}) ^ {2} + m g x _ {1} + m g x _ {2} \\ & = k x _ {1} ^ {2} + 2 k x _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 k x _ {1} x _ {2} + m g x _ {1} + m g x _ {2} \end{array}
$$

$V$ 中有广义坐标的线性项是因为 $x_{1}, x_{2}$ 的零点未取在平衡位置. 由 $\frac{\partial V}{\partial x_1} = 0, \frac{\partial V}{\partial x_2} = 0$ 可定出平衡位置:

$$
x _ {1} = - \frac {3 m g}{2 k}, \quad x _ {2} = - \frac {m g}{k}
$$

令 $x_{1}^{\prime} = x_{1} + \frac{3mg}{2k}, x_{2}^{\prime} = x_{2} + \frac{mg}{k}$ ，可将动能、势能写成

$$
\begin{array}{l} T = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x ^ {\prime}} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x ^ {\prime}} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ V = V _ {0} + k x _ {1} ^ {n 2} + 2 k x _ {2} ^ {n 2} - 2 k x _ {1} ^ {\prime} x _ {2} ^ {\prime} \end{array}
$$

其中 $V_{0} = V(x_{1}^{\prime},x_{2}^{\prime})\Big|_{(0,0)} = V(x_{1},x_{2})\Big|\left(-\frac{3mg}{2k}, - \frac{mg}{k}\right)$ ，因不影响结果，不必算出.

惯性矩阵、刚度矩阵和特征矩阵分别为

$$
\boldsymbol {M} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} m & 0 \\ 0 & m \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {K} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} 2 k & - 2 k \\ - 2 k & 4 k \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {H} = \boldsymbol {K} - \omega^ {2} \boldsymbol {M} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} 2 k - m \omega^ {2} & - 2 k \\ - 2 k & 4 k - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} | \boldsymbol {H} | & = \left| \begin{array}{c c} 2 k - m \omega^ {2} & - 2 k \\ - 2 k & 4 k - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0 \\ & m ^ {2} \omega^ {4} - 6 k m \omega^ {2} + 4 k ^ {2} = 0 \end{array}
$$

可得

$$
\omega_ {1} ^ {2} = (3 + \sqrt {5}) \frac {k}{m}, \quad \omega_ {2} ^ {2} = (3 - \sqrt {5}) \frac {k}{m}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {H} \left(\omega_ {1}\right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} (- 1 - \sqrt {5}) k & - 2 k \\ - 2 k & (1 - \sqrt {5}) k \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\operatorname{adj} \boldsymbol {H} \left(\omega_ {1}\right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} (1 - \sqrt {5}) k & 2 k \\ 2 k & - (1 + \sqrt {5}) k \end{array} \right)
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(1)} = 1$ ，则

$$
u _ {2} ^ {(1)} = - \frac {1 + \sqrt {5}}{2} = - 1. 6 1 8
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {H} \left(\omega_ {2}\right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} (- 1 + \sqrt {5}) k & - 2 k \\ - 2 k & (1 + \sqrt {5}) k \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\operatorname{adj} \boldsymbol {H} \left(\omega_ {2}\right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} (1 + \sqrt {5}) k & 2 k \\ 2 k & (- 1 + \sqrt {5}) k \end{array} \right)
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(2)} = 1$ ，则

$$
u _ {2} ^ {(2)} = - \frac {- 1 + \sqrt {5}}{2} = 0. 6 1 8
$$

两种振动模式中节点的位置如下图 10.26(a)、(b) 所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/000152cb84269aa9a9f3766638895aad6f29657baa099c4d6ffabec31743cfaa.jpg)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/5056e389a6055b0835b1c387b3b34a9a5ea77e86d4cc163e25bde36d346ed12e.jpg)



(b)



图10.26


对于简正频率为

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {(3 + \sqrt {5}) \frac {k}{m}} = 2. 2 8 8 \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}}
$$

的模式,节点位于2点的右方s处,

$$
\frac {s}{l - s} = 1. 6 1 8, \quad s = 0. 6 1 8 l
$$

对于简正频率为

$$
\omega_ {2} = \sqrt {(3 - \sqrt {5}) \frac {k}{m}} = 0. 8 7 4 \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}}
$$

的模式,节点位于2点的左方s处,

$$
\frac {s}{s + l} = 0. 6 1 8, \quad s = 1. 6 1 8 l
$$

10.1.31 一个质量为 $4m$ 、半径为 $2b$ 的均质半球形碗静置在一光滑的桌面上，其边缘所在平面是水平的。在它内部躺着一个质量为 $m$ 、半径为 $b$ 的完全粗糙的均质球，系统运动时，球心与碗心保持在一个竖直平面内，即系统平衡时它们所在的竖直平面。证明：小

振动的简正频率为 $\omega_{1},\omega_{2}$ ，其平方 $\omega_1^2,\omega_2^2$ 是下列方程的根：

$$
1 5 6 b ^ {2} x ^ {2} - 2 6 0 b g x + 7 5 g ^ {2} = 0
$$

证明 先求半球形碗的质心位置,由图 10.27,

$$
\begin{array}{c} m x _ {\mathrm{C}} = \int x \mathrm{d} m \\ x = r \sin \theta , \quad m = 2 \pi r ^ {2} \rho \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/8be9adabfa6d4ab1758f0078c3edf43c1914891183a8808d09ebee0aa29b0b73.jpg)


位于 $x \sim x + \mathrm{d}x$ 处的质元质量


图10.27


$$
\mathrm{d} m = 2 \pi r \cos \theta \cdot r \mathrm{d} \theta \cdot \rho
$$

$$
x _ {\mathrm{C}} = \frac {1}{2 \pi r ^ {2} \rho} \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{2}} r \sin \theta \cdot \rho 2 \pi r ^ {2} \cos \theta \mathrm{d} \theta = \frac {1}{2} r = b
$$

这里用了半球形碗的半径 r=2b.

半球形碗对通过碗心的水平轴的转动惯量为

$$
I = \frac {2}{3} (4 m) (2 b) ^ {2} = \frac {3 2}{3} m b ^ {2}
$$

用关于转动惯量的平行轴定理,半球形碗对通过质心的水平轴的转动惯量为

$$
I _ {\mathrm{C}} = I - 4 m \cdot b ^ {2} = \frac {2 0}{3} m b ^ {2}
$$

取静坐标系 $Oxy, x_1, y_1$ 表示碗的质心 $C$ 的坐标， $x_2, y_2$ 表示球心 $D$ 的坐标， $x_{O'}$ 表示碗心的 $x$ 坐标， $\varphi$ 是 $O'C$ 与竖直线的夹角， $\theta$ 是 $O'D$ 与竖直线的夹角，用 $\psi$ 表示球围绕球心顺时针的转角，如图10.28所示。

以 $x_{O^{\prime}}$ 、 $\varphi ,\theta$ 作为广义坐标，

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/fe797db7f26536becc7ce5a5ff5ce3e153956d86c5ce1acec5c3b9947bb4bc3b.jpg)



图10.28


$$
\begin{array}{l l} {x _ {1} = x _ {O ^ {\prime}} - b \sin \varphi ,} & {\dot {x} _ {1} = \dot {x} _ {O ^ {\prime}} - b \dot {\varphi} \cos \varphi} \\ {y _ {1} = 2 b - b \cos \varphi ,} & {\dot {y} _ {1} = b \dot {\varphi} \sin \varphi} \\ {x _ {2} = x _ {O ^ {\prime}} + b \sin \theta ,} & {\dot {x} _ {2} = \dot {x} _ {O ^ {\prime}} + b \dot {\theta} \cos \theta} \\ {y _ {2} = 2 b - b \cos \theta ,} & {\dot {y} _ {2} = b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta} \end{array}
$$

考虑碗为参考系, 球与碗的两接触点间无相对滑动, D 点的速度为 $b(\dot{\theta} + \dot{\varphi})$ , 球上的接触点相对于 D 点“平动参考系”(对碗参考系而言) 的角速度也就是球相对于碗的角速度设为 $\dot{\psi}'$ (规定顺时针方向为正), 则

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} b (\dot {\theta} + \dot {\varphi}) - b \dot {\psi} ^ {\prime} & = 0 \\ \dot {\psi} ^ {\prime} = \dot {\theta} + \dot {\varphi} \end{array}
$$

碗相对于静参考系的角速度为 $\dot{\varphi}$ , 球相对于球心平动参考系的角速度 $\dot{\psi}$ 也就是球相对于静参考系的角速度, 用角速度合成法则,

$$
\dot {\psi} = \dot {\psi} ^ {\prime} + \dot {\varphi} = \dot {\theta} + 2 \dot {\varphi}
$$

将 $\dot{x}_1, \dot{y}_1, \dot{x}_2, \dot{y}_2, \dot{\psi}$ 与 $x_{O'}$ 、 $\varphi$ 、 $\theta$ 、 $\dot{x}_{O'}$ 、 $\dot{\varphi}$ 、 $\dot{\theta}$ 的关系计算系统的动能，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} \times 4 m (\dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} I _ {\mathrm{c}} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {2}{5} m b ^ {2} \dot {\psi} ^ {2} \\ & \quad = 2 m (\dot {x} _ {0} ^ {2} + b ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} - 2 b \dot {x} _ {0}, \dot {\varphi} \cos \varphi) + \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} _ {0} ^ {2} + b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \\ & \quad + 2 b \dot {x} _ {0}, \dot {\theta} \cos \theta) + \frac {1 0}{3} m b ^ {2} \varphi^ {2} + \frac {1}{5} m b ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} + 2 \dot {\varphi}) ^ {2} \\ & V = 4 m g y _ {1} + m g y _ {2} \\ & \quad = 4 m g (2 b - b \cos \varphi) + m g (2 b - b \cos \theta) \\ & L = T - V \end{array}
$$

因为

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial x _ {O ^ {\prime}}} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x} _ {O ^ {\prime}}} = \text {常量} = 0
$$

求简正频率与初始条件无关,因此我们可以选取初始条件,使上述常量为零,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} 5 m \dot {x} _ {O ^ {\prime}} - 4 m b \dot {\varphi} \cos \varphi + m b \dot {\theta} \cos \theta & = 0 \\ \dot {x} _ {O ^ {\prime}} & = \frac {4}{5} b \dot {\varphi} \cos \varphi - \frac {1}{5} b \dot {\theta} \cos \theta \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

(1) 式可以积分，

$$
x _ {O ^ {\prime}} = \frac {4}{5} b \mathrm{sin} \varphi - \frac {1}{5} b \mathrm{sin} \theta + \text {常量}
$$

这个式子的物理意义是系统在 $x$ 方向动量守恒. (1) 式可以积分, 是几何约束, 可以用它减少系统的自由度, 这样就消除了一个非振动自由度, 剩下的是两个振动自由度. 将 (1) 式代入动能的表达式, 并做小振动近似. 只保留二级小量,

$$
T \approx \frac {6 8}{1 5} m b ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {3}{5} m b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {8}{5} m b ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \dot {\theta}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {V \approx 5 m g b + 2 m g b \varphi^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g b \theta^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad M = \left( \begin{array}{l l} \frac {1 3 6}{1 5} m b ^ {2} & \frac {8}{5} m b ^ {2} \\ \frac {8}{5} m b ^ {2} & \frac {6}{5} m b ^ {2} \end{array} \right)} \\ & {\qquad K = \left( \begin{array}{l l} 4 m g b & 0 \\ 0 & m g b \end{array} \right)} \\ & {\qquad | H | = | K - \omega^ {2} M | = \left| \begin{array}{l l} 4 m g b - \frac {1 3 6}{1 5} m b ^ {2} \omega^ {2} & - \frac {8}{5} m b ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \\ - \frac {8}{5} m b ^ {2} \omega^ {2} & m g b - \frac {6}{5} m b ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0} \end{array}
$$

令 $x = \omega^2$ 

$$
\left(4 m g b - \frac {1 3 6}{1 5} m b ^ {2} x\right) \left(m g b - \frac {6}{5} m b ^ {2} x\right) - \left(- \frac {8}{5} m b ^ {2} x\right) ^ {2} = 0
$$

约去公因子 $(mb)^2$ ，可得

$$
\frac {6 2 4}{7 5} b ^ {2} x ^ {2} - \frac {2 0 8}{1 5} b g x + 4 g ^ {2} = 0
$$

两边乘以 $\frac{75}{4}$ ，即得

$$
1 5 6 b ^ {2} x ^ {2} - 2 6 0 b g x + 7 5 g ^ {2} = 0
$$

10.1.32 质量为 $m$ 的质点用长为 $l$ 的不可伸长的轻绳系在另一质量为 $m$ 的质点上, 这个质点又用同样的轻绳系在套在光滑水平杆上的、质量为 $2m$ 的小环上. 试证: 在平衡位置附近的小振动中有一种振动模式, 下半段绳子与铅垂线所成的角恒为上半段绳子与铅垂线所成的角的两倍; 另一种振动模式, 上述两个角度大小相等但符号相反.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/08de37b5b146ac76c02a5fa2c892232ebac58944097eb14dcdc80065cc557367.jpg)



图10.29


证明 取图 10.29 的 $x, \theta, \varphi$ 为广义坐标，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} \times 2 m \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m [ (\dot {x} + l \dot {\theta} \cos \theta) ^ {2} + (l \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) ^ {2} ] \\ & \quad + \frac {1}{2} m [ (\dot {x} + l \dot {\theta} \cos \theta + l \dot {\varphi} \cos \varphi) ^ {2} + (l \dot {\theta} \sin \theta + l \dot {\varphi} \sin \varphi) ^ {2} ] \\ & = 2 m \dot {x} ^ {2} + m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + m l \dot {x} (2 \dot {\theta} \cos \theta + \dot {\varphi} \cos \varphi) + m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \cos (\theta - \varphi) \\ & \approx 2 m \dot {x} ^ {2} + m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + m l \dot {x} (2 \dot {\theta} + \dot {\varphi}) + m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \end{array}
$$

这里用了小振动近似， $\theta,\varphi,\dot{\theta},\dot{\varphi}$ 均为小量.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {V = m g l (1 - \cos \theta) + m g l (1 - \cos \theta) + m g l (1 - \cos \varphi)} \\ & {\qquad \approx m g l \theta^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \varphi^ {2}} \\ & {L = T - V} \end{array}
$$

因为

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial x} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = \text {常量}
$$

与上题所述一样,可取初始条件,使此常量为零,

$$
\dot {x} = - \frac {1}{4} l (2 \dot {\theta} + \dot {\varphi})
$$

它也是可积分的几何约束,用它可消除一个非振动自由度,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = 2 m \left[ - \frac {1}{4} l (2 \dot {\theta} + \dot {\varphi}) \right] ^ {2} + m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \\ & \quad - \frac {1}{4} m l ^ {2} (2 \dot {\theta} + \dot {\varphi}) ^ {2} + m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \\ & = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \left(\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {3}{4} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi}\right) \\ & M = \left( \begin{array}{c c} m l ^ {2} & \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \\ \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} & \frac {3}{4} m l ^ {2} \end{array} \right) \\ & K = \left( \begin{array}{c c} 2 m g l & 0 \\ 0 & m g l \end{array} \right) \\ | H | = & \left| \begin{array}{c c} 2 m g l - m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} & - \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \\ - \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} & m g l - \frac {3}{4} m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0 \\ & \omega^ {4} - \frac {5 g}{l} \omega^ {2} + 4 \frac {g ^ {2}}{l ^ {2}} = 0 \\ & \omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = 2 \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} \end{array}
$$

可得

将 $\omega_{1},\omega_{2}$ 分别代入 $H\cdot u = 0$ ，可得两个独立的方程为

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & m g l u _ {1} ^ {(1)} - \frac {1}{2} m g l u _ {2} ^ {(1)} = 0 \\ & - 2 m g l u _ {1} ^ {(2)} - 2 m g l u _ {2} ^ {(2)} = 0 \\ & \frac {u _ {2} ^ {(1)}}{u _ {1} ^ {(1)}} = 2, \quad \frac {u _ {2} ^ {(2)}}{u _ {1} ^ {(2)}} = - 1 \end{array}
$$

这就证明了简正频率为 $\sqrt{\frac{g}{l}}$ 的模式. 任何时刻均有 $\varphi = 2\theta$ , 简正频率为 $2\sqrt{\frac{g}{l}}$ 的模式, 任何时刻均有 $\varphi = -\theta$ .

10.1.33 一个系统运动时,其动能和势能分别为

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} (\dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {q} _ {3} ^ {2})
$$

$$
V = 2 q _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {5}{2} q _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 q _ {3} ^ {2} - q _ {1} q _ {2} - q _ {2} q _ {3}
$$

如开始时， $q_{1} = q_{10}, q_{2} = q_{20}, q_{3} = q_{30}, \dot{q}_{1} = \dot{q}_{2} = \dot{q}_{3} = 0$ ，求此系统的运动.

解

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} \left(\dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {q} _ {3} ^ {2}\right) - 2 q _ {1} ^ {2} - \frac {5}{2} q _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 q _ {3} ^ {2} + q _ {1} q _ {2} + q _ {2} q _ {3}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{q}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 得运动微分方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \ddot {q} _ {1} + 4 q _ {1} - q _ {2} & = 0 \\ \ddot {q} _ {2} + 5 q _ {2} - q _ {1} - q _ {3} & = 0 \\ \ddot {q} _ {3} + 4 q _ {3} - q _ {2} & = 0 \end{array}
$$

设特解具有下列形式: $q_{j}=u_{j}e^{i\omega t}$ ，代入上述微分方程，得

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} (4 - \omega^ {2}) u _ {1} - u _ {2} = 0 \\ - u _ {1} + (5 - \omega^ {2}) u _ {2} - u _ {3} = 0 \\ - u _ {2} + (4 - \omega^ {2}) u _ {3} = 0 \end{array} \right.\tag{1}
$$

特征方程为

可解出

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \begin{array}{c c c} 4 - a ^ {2} & - 1 & 0 \\ - 1 & 5 - \omega^ {2} & - 1 \\ 0 & - 1 & 4 - \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0 \\ \omega_ {1} = \sqrt {3}, \quad \omega_ {2} = 2, \quad \omega_ {3} = \sqrt {6}. \end{array}
$$

将 $\omega_{1}$ 代入方程组(1)，取 $v_{1}^{(i)} = 1$ ，得

$$
\mathcal {U} _ {2} ^ {(1)} = 1, \quad \mathcal {U} _ {3} ^ {(1)} = 1
$$

将 $\omega_{2}$ 代入方程组(1)，取 $u_{1}^{(2)} = 1$ ，得

$$
u _ {2} ^ {(2)} = 0, \quad u _ {3} ^ {(2)} = - 1
$$

将 $\omega_{3}$ 代入方程组(1)，取 $u_{1}^{(3)} = 1$ ，得

$$
u _ {2} ^ {(3)} = - 2, \quad u _ {3} ^ {(3)} = 1
$$

$\omega_{1}, \omega_{2}, \omega_{3}$ 均为实数，说明 $q_{j}$ 均为振动。特解可写为

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} q _ {1} ^ {(1)} = \cos (\sqrt {3} t + \alpha_ {1}) \\ q _ {2} ^ {(1)} = \cos (\sqrt {3} + \alpha_ {1}) \\ q _ {3} ^ {(1)} = \cos (\sqrt {3} + \alpha_ {1}) \end{array} \right.
$$

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} q _ {1} ^ {(2)} = \cos (2 t + \alpha_ {2}) \\ q _ {2} ^ {(2)} = 0 \\ q _ {3} ^ {(2)} = - \cos (2 t + \alpha_ {2}) \end{array} \right.
$$

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} q _ {1} ^ {(3)} = \cos (\sqrt {6} t + \alpha_ {3}) \\ q _ {2} ^ {(3)} = - 2 \cos (\sqrt {6} t + \alpha_ {3}) \\ q _ {3} ^ {(3)} = \cos (\sqrt {6} t + \alpha_ {3}) \end{array} \right.
$$

通解为

$$
q _ {j} = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {3} C _ {i} u _ {j} ^ {(i)} \cos (\omega_ {i} t + \alpha_ {i}) \quad (j = 1, 2, 3)
$$

即

$$
q _ {1} = C _ {1} \cos (\sqrt {3} t + \alpha_ {1}) + C _ {2} \cos (2 t + \alpha_ {2}) + C _ {3} \cos (\sqrt {6} t + \alpha_ {3})
$$

$$
\begin{array}{c} {q _ {2} = C _ {1} \cos (\sqrt {3} t + \alpha_ {1}) - 2 C _ {3} \cos (\sqrt {6} t + \alpha_ {3})} \\ {q _ {3} = C _ {1} \cos (\sqrt {3} t + \alpha_ {1}) - C _ {2} \cos (2 t + \alpha_ {2}) + C _ {3} \cos (\sqrt {6} t + \alpha_ {3})} \end{array}
$$

由初始条件: t=0 时, $q_{1}=q_{10}$ , $q_{2}=q_{20}$ , $q_{3}=q_{30}$ , $\dot{q}_{1}=\dot{q}_{2}=\dot{q}_{3}=0$ , 可定 $C_{1}$ 、 $C_{2}$ 、 $C_{3}$ 、 $\alpha_{1}$ 、 $\alpha_{2}$ 和 $\alpha_{3}$ , 可得

$$
q _ {1} = \frac {1}{6} \left[ 2 \left(q _ {1 0} + q _ {2 0} + q _ {3 0}\right) \cos \sqrt {3} t + 3 \left(q _ {1 0} - q _ {3 0}\right) \cos 2 t + \left(q _ {1 0} - 2 q _ {2 0} + q _ {3 0}\right) \cos \sqrt {6} t \right]
$$

$$
q _ {2} = \frac {1}{3} \left[ \left(q _ {1 0} + q _ {2 0} + q _ {3 0}\right) \cos \sqrt {3} t - \left(q _ {1 0} - 2 q _ {2 0} + q _ {3 0}\right) \cos \sqrt {6} t \right]
$$

$$
q _ {3} = \frac {1}{6} \left[ 2 \left(q _ {1 0} + q _ {2 0} + q _ {3 0}\right) \cos \sqrt {3} t - 3 \left(q _ {1 0} - q _ {3 0}\right) \cos 2 t + \left(q _ {1 0} - 2 q _ {2 0} + q _ {3 0}\right) \cos \sqrt {6} t \right]
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/462eaf742f583a2800edcafaed000fa0c40fa9d78a9bbd834409201f9b7e2b13.jpg)



图10.30


10.1.34 三个质量均为 $m$ 的物体, 被限制在沿交于一点相互成 $120^{\circ}$ 角、处于同一水平面的三条光滑直线运动, 用三根劲度系数均为 $k$ 的轻弹簧将它们相互连结, 如图 10.30 所示. 图中三质点分别位于 $O_{1}, O_{2}, O_{3}$ 时, 系统处于平衡位置. 考虑系统在平衡位置附近的小振动.

(1) 写出系统的运动微分方程；

(2) 求简正频率和振动模式.

解 （1）取广义坐标 $x_{1}$ 、 $x_{2}$ 、 $x_{3}$ ，零点分别取在系统的平衡位置 $O_{1}$ 、 $O_{2}$ 、 $O_{3}$ ，

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m \left(\dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {x} _ {3} ^ {2}\right)
$$

在图10.31位于 $x_{1}, x_{2}$ 轴上的两质点分别从平衡位置位移 $x_{1}, x_{2}$ 时，连结它们的弹簧的伸长量为

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/68123970a6df6528289be27ec5bc2cab8d7da579838c456740e60f2c922e12b2.jpg)



图10.31


$$
= x _ {1} \left(\cos 3 0 ^ {\circ} \cos \alpha - \sin 3 0 ^ {\circ} \sin \alpha\right) + x _ {2} \left(\cos 3 0 ^ {\circ} \cos \alpha + \sin 3 0 ^ {\circ} \sin \alpha\right)
$$

小振动， $x_{1}$ 、 $x_{2}$ 和 $\alpha$ 均为小量，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\cos \alpha \approx 1, \quad \sin \alpha \approx \alpha} \\ & {x _ {1} \cos (3 0 ^ {\circ} + \alpha) + x _ {2} \cos (3 0 ^ {\circ} - \alpha) \approx \frac {\sqrt {3}}{2} (x _ {1} + x _ {2})} \end{array}
$$

同样计算另两个弹簧的伸长量，分别为 $\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} (x_2 + x_3)$ 和 $\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} (x_3 + x_1)$ ，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {V = \frac {1}{2} k \left\{\left[ \frac {\sqrt {3}}{2} (x _ {1} + x _ {2}) \right] ^ {2} + \left[ \frac {\sqrt {3}}{2} (x _ {2} + x _ {3}) \right] ^ {2} + \left[ \frac {\sqrt {3}}{2} (x _ {3} + x _ {1}) \right] ^ {2} \right\}} \\ & {\quad = \frac {3}{8} k [ (x _ {1} + x _ {2}) ^ {2} + (x _ {2} + x _ {3}) ^ {2} + (x _ {3} + x _ {1}) ^ {2} ]} \\ & {\quad = \frac {3}{8} k (2 x _ {1} ^ {2} + 2 x _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 x _ {3} ^ {2} + 2 x _ {1} x _ {2} + 2 x _ {2} x _ {3} + 2 x _ {3} x _ {1})} \\ & {\quad L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {x} _ {3} ^ {2}) - \frac {3}{4} k (x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2} + x _ {3} ^ {2} + x _ {1} x _ {2} + x _ {2} x _ {3} + x _ {3} x _ {1})} \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{x}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial x_i} = 0$ 得运动微分方程为

$$
\begin{array}{l} m \ddot {x} _ {1} + \frac {3}{2} k x _ {1} + \frac {3}{4} k x _ {2} + \frac {3}{4} k x _ {3} = 0 \\ m \ddot {x} _ {2} + \frac {3}{2} k x _ {2} + \frac {3}{4} k x _ {1} + \frac {3}{4} k x _ {3} = 0 \\ m \ddot {x} _ {3} + \frac {3}{2} k x _ {3} + \frac {3}{4} k x _ {1} + \frac {3}{4} k x _ {2} = 0 \end{array}
$$

(2) 由动能、势能的表达式可写出惯性矩阵、刚度矩阵、特征矩阵分别为

$$
\boldsymbol {M} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} \boldsymbol {m} & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & \boldsymbol {m} & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & \boldsymbol {m} \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {K} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} \frac {3}{2} k & \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {3}{4} k \\ \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {3}{2} k & \frac {3}{4} k \\ \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {3}{2} k \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
| \boldsymbol {H} | = \boldsymbol {K} - \omega^ {2} \boldsymbol {M} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} \frac {3}{2} k - m \omega^ {2} & \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {3}{4} k \\ \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {3}{2} k - m \omega^ {2} & \frac {3}{4} k \\ \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {3}{2} k - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right)
$$

为书写简便起见，令 $\eta = \frac{3}{4} k.$ 特征方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} | \boldsymbol {H} | & = \left| \begin{array}{c c c} 2 \eta - m \omega^ {2} & \eta & \eta \\ \eta & 2 \eta - m \omega^ {2} & \eta \\ \eta & \eta & 2 \eta - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0 \\ (m \omega^ {2}) ^ {3} - 6 \eta (m \omega^ {2}) ^ {2} + 9 \eta^ {2} (m \omega^ {2}) - 4 \eta^ {3} & = 0 \end{array}
$$

从各项的系数有 $1 - 6 + 9 - 4 = 0$ 可知. 必有一个因子 $m\omega^2 - \eta$ , 进而可得

$$
(m \omega^ {2} - \eta) (m \omega^ {2} - \eta) (m \omega^ {2} - 4 \eta) = 0
$$

三个简正频率分别为

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {\eta}{m}} = \sqrt {\frac {3 k}{4 m}}
$$

$$
\omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {\eta}{m}} = \sqrt {\frac {3 k}{4 m}}
$$

$$
\omega_ {3} = \sqrt {\frac {4 \eta}{m}} = \sqrt {\frac {3 k}{m}}
$$

将 $\omega_{1},\omega_{2},\omega_{3}$ 依次代入 $H\cdot u = 0$ 。今 $\omega_{1} = \omega_{2}$ ，是简并的，代入 $H\cdot u = 0$ ，只能得到一个独立的方程。

将 $\omega_{1},\omega_{2}$ 代入得

$$
\eta (u _ {1} + u _ {2} + u _ {3}) = 0
$$

$\omega_{1}$ : 取 $u_{1}^{(1)}=1, u_{2}^{(1)}=0$ , 则 $u_{3}^{(1)}=-1$ ;

$\omega_{2}$ : 取 $u_{1}^{(2)} = 1, u_{2}^{(2)} = -\frac{1}{2}$ , 则 $u_{3}^{(2)} = -\frac{1}{2}$ .

将 $\omega_{3}$ 代入得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & - 2 k u _ {1} ^ {(3)} + k u _ {2} ^ {(3)} + k u _ {3} ^ {(3)} = 0 \\ & k u _ {1} ^ {(3)} - 2 k u _ {2} ^ {(3)} + k u _ {3} ^ {(3)} = 0 \end{array}
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(3)} = 1$ ，则 $u_{2}^{(3)} = 1, u_{3}^{(3)} = 1.$ 

10.1.35 三个质量均为 $m$ 的质点在由势能

$$
V = V _ {0} \left(\mathrm{e} ^ {- \alpha} + \mathrm{e} ^ {- \beta} + \mathrm{e} ^ {- \gamma}\right)
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/8884dee1188e252b6551ce95d0e6375ee5a984434ae0d105a94be179b628574d.jpg)



图10.32


[其中 $V_{0}$ 为常量, $\alpha$ 、 $\beta$ 、 $\gamma$ 是它们的角距离(以弧度为单位)]给出的力作用下在半径为 b 的水平圆圈上无摩擦地运动. 当 $\alpha = \beta = \gamma = \frac{2\pi}{3}$ 时, 系统处于平衡. 求系统偏离平衡位置做小振动的简正频率.

解 三质点处于图中 A、B、C 时为一个平衡位置，偏离平衡位置的角位移为 $\theta_{1}$ 、 $\theta_{2}$ 、 $\theta_{3}$ 时，它们之间的角距离分别为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \alpha = \frac {2 \pi}{3} + \theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1} \\ \beta = \frac {2 \pi}{3} + \theta_ {3} - \theta_ {2} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\gamma = \frac {2 \pi}{3} + \theta_ {1} - \theta_ {3}} \\ & {\qquad V (\theta_ {1}, \theta_ {2}, \theta_ {3}) = V _ {0} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 \pi}{3}} (\mathrm{e} ^ {\theta_ {1} - \theta_ {2}} + \mathrm{e} ^ {\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {3}} + \mathrm{e} ^ {\theta_ {3} - \theta_ {1}})} \\ & {\approx V _ {0} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 \pi}{3}} \Big [ 1 + (\theta_ {1} - \theta_ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} (\theta_ {1} - \theta_ {2}) ^ {2} + 1 + (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {3}) + \frac {1}{2} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {3}) ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad + 1 + (\theta_ {3} - \theta_ {1}) + \frac {1}{2} (\theta_ {3} - \theta_ {1}) ^ {2} \Big ]} \\ & {= V _ {0} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 \pi}{3}} \Big [ 3 + \frac {1}{2} (\theta_ {1} - \theta_ {2}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {3}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (\theta_ {3} - \theta_ {1}) ^ {2} \Big ]} \\ & {= V _ {0} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 \pi}{3}} (3 + \theta_ {1} ^ {2} + \theta_ {2} ^ {2} + \theta_ {3} ^ {2} - \theta_ {1} \theta_ {2} - \theta_ {2} \theta_ {3} - \theta_ {3} \theta_ {1})} \\ & {\qquad T = \frac {1}{2} m b ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {3} ^ {2})} \\ & {\qquad M = \left( \begin{array}{l l l} B & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & B & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & B \end{array} \right)} \\ & {\qquad K = \left( \begin{array}{l l l} 2 A & - A & - A \\ - A & 2 A & - A \\ - A & - A & 2 A \end{array} \right)} \end{array}
$$

其中 $B = mb^{2}, A = V_{0} \mathrm{e}^{-\frac{2\pi}{3}}$ 

$$
| \boldsymbol {H} | = \left| \begin{array}{c c c} 2 A - B \omega^ {2} & - A & - A \\ - A & 2 A - B \omega^ {2} & - A \\ - A & - A & 2 A - B \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

第一列加第三列作第一列，再第一行减第三行作第一行，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left| \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & 0 & - 3 A + B \omega^ {2} \\ - 2 A & 2 A - B \omega^ {2} & - A \\ A - B \omega^ {2} & - A & 2 A - B \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0} \\ & {(- 3 A + B \omega^ {2}) [ 2 A ^ {2} - (A - B \omega^ {2}) (2 A - B \omega^ {2}) ] = 0} \\ & {(- 3 A + B \omega^ {2}) (- B ^ {2} \omega^ {4} + 3 A B \omega^ {2}) = 0} \\ & {\omega_ {1} = 0} \end{array}
$$

可得

$$
\omega_ {2} = \omega_ {3} = \sqrt {\frac {3 A}{B}} = \sqrt {\frac {3 V _ {0} e ^ {- 2 \pi / 3}}{m b ^ {2}}}
$$

$\omega_{1}=0$ 不是振动, 是整个系统沿圆圈做等角速转动, 只有两个简正频率, 它们是简并的.

10.1.36 三个质点, 其中两个质量为 $m$ , 一个质量为 $M$ , 被约束在半径为 $r$ 的光滑的水平圆环上运动, 并用三根相同的轻弹簧将它们互相连接. 如图 10.33 所示, 劲度系数为 $k$ . 让系统只围绕一个平衡位置做小振动.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/0d59c3e34d4fc55e79b7aa5940742d26677480e02171e7d2a3c2c7ef5df3e63e.jpg)



图10.33


(1) 定性描述系统做简谐振动的振动模式；

(2) 找出一组简正坐标, 求出简正频率.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/d2e270af4fc03b28d41e13c88706c2dc8ea699113350fefc5946bb3c7000fdbe.jpg)



图10.34


解 方法一:(1) 由于在水平面内所受外力的作用线都通过环心(弹簧力为内力, 在竖直方向, 每个质点所受合外力均为零), 系统对环心的角动量守恒. 必存在一个系统整体以恒定角速度转动, 系统的角动量不为零, 简正频率 $\omega = 0$ , 实际上不是振动, 实际上只有两个振动自由度, 题目要求只围绕一个平衡位置做小振动, 就是这两种振动模式, 系统的角动量均为零.

用 $\theta_{1}, \theta_{2}, \theta_{3}$ (如图 10.34 所示) 分别表示 m, M, m 偏离一个平衡位置 (图中虚线表示的位置) 的角位移, 根据系统的角动量为零, 可以设想:

一种振动模式, $u_{1}^{(1)}=1,u_{2}^{(1)}=0,u_{3}^{(1)}=-1$ . 即两个 m 的振幅相同,但相位相反,M 不动;

另一种振动模式, $u_{1}^{(2)}=1,u_{2}^{(2)}=-\frac{2m}{M},u_{3}^{(2)}=1$ .即两个m的振幅和相位都相同,M的振幅是两个m的振幅的 $\frac{2m}{M}$ 倍,相位相反.

(2) 

$$
\begin{array}{l} T = \frac {1}{2} m r ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {3} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} M r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ V = \frac {1}{2} k \left(\frac {2 \pi}{3} r + r \theta_ {2} - r \theta_ {1} - a\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k \left(\frac {2 \pi}{3} r + r \theta_ {3} - r \theta_ {2} - a\right) ^ {2} \\ + \frac {1}{2} k \left(\frac {2 \pi}{3} r + r \theta_ {1} - r \theta_ {3} - a\right) ^ {2} - 3 \cdot \frac {1}{2} k \left(\frac {2 \pi}{3} r - a\right) ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

其中 a 为弹簧原长.

$$
L = T - V
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{\theta}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial\theta_i} = 0$ 得

$$
m \ddot {\theta} _ {1} + k (2 \theta_ {1} - \theta_ {2} - \theta_ {3}) = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
M \ddot {\theta} _ {2} + k (2 \theta_ {2} - \theta_ {3} - \theta_ {1}) = 0\tag{2}
$$

$$
m \ddot {\theta} _ {3} + k (2 \theta_ {3} - \theta_ {1} - \theta_ {2}) = 0\tag{3}
$$

(1)式+(2)式+(3)式,得

$$
m \ddot {\theta} _ {1} + M \ddot {\theta} _ {2} + m \ddot {\theta} _ {3} = 0\tag{4}
$$

(1)式一(3)式,得

$$
m \left(\ddot {\theta} _ {1} - \dot {\theta} _ {3}\right) + 3 k \left(\theta_ {1} - \theta_ {3}\right) = 0\tag{5}
$$

用(4)、(5)式,可引入两个简正坐标,

$$
\xi = \theta_ {1} + \frac {M}{m} \theta_ {2} + \theta_ {3}
$$

相应的“简正频率”为 $\omega_{1}=0$ ，它不是振动，而是整体以恒定角速度围绕环心 O 转动，

$$
\eta = \theta_ {1} - \theta_ {3}
$$

相应的简正频率为

$$
\omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {3 k}{m}}
$$

简正坐标 $\eta$ 与上述的 $u_{1}^{(1)} = 1, u_{2}^{(1)} = 0, u_{3}^{(1)} = -1$ 的振动模式相对应，简正坐标 $\zeta$ 应与上述的 $u_{1}^{(2)} = 1, u_{2}^{(2)} = -\frac{2m}{M}, u_{3}^{(2)} = 1$ 的振动模式相对应。由此可见试用(1)式+(3)式-2 $\times (2)$ 式得

$$
m \left(\ddot {\theta} _ {1} + \ddot {\theta} _ {3}\right) - 2 M \ddot {\theta} _ {2} + 3 k \left(\theta_ {1} - 2 \theta_ {2} + \theta_ {3}\right) = 0
$$

不能得到第三个简正坐标，改用(1)式 $\times \frac{1}{m} + (3)$ 式 $\times \frac{1}{m} - (2)$ 式 $\times \frac{2}{M}$ ，得

$$
\ddot {\theta} _ {1} - 2 \ddot {\theta} _ {2} + \ddot {\theta} _ {3} + \frac {2 m + M}{m M} k (\theta_ {1} - 2 \theta_ {2} + \theta_ {3}) = 0
$$

可见

$$
\zeta = \theta_ {1} - 2 \theta_ {2} + \theta_ {3}
$$

为第三个简正坐标,相应的简正频率为

$$
\omega_ {3} = \sqrt {\frac {2 m + M}{m M} k}
$$

方法二:用前面已用过的方法,

$$
V = \frac {1}{2} k r ^ {2} (2 \theta_ {1} ^ {2} + 2 \theta_ {2} ^ {2} + 2 \theta_ {3} ^ {2} - 2 \theta_ {1} \theta_ {2} - 2 \theta_ {2} \theta_ {3} - 2 \theta_ {3} \theta_ {1})
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {M} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} m r ^ {2} & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & M r ^ {2} & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & m r ^ {2} \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {K} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 2 k r ^ {2} & - k r ^ {2} & - k r ^ {2} \\ - k r ^ {2} & 2 k r ^ {2} & - k r ^ {2} \\ - k r ^ {2} & - k r ^ {2} & 2 k r ^ {2} \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
| \boldsymbol {H} | = r ^ {2} \left| \begin{array}{c c c} 2 k - m \omega^ {2} & - k & - k \\ - k & 2 k - M \omega^ {2} & - k \\ - k & - k & 2 k - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

第一行减第三行作第一行，再第一列加第三列作第一列，得

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & 0 & - 3 k + m \omega^ {2} \\ - 2 k & 2 k - M \omega^ {2} & - k \\ k - m \omega^ {2} & - k & 2 k - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

$$
(- 3 k + m \omega^ {2}) [ 2 k ^ {2} - (k - m \omega^ {2}) (2 k - M \omega^ {2}) ] = 0
$$

可得 $\omega_{1} = 0$ ， $\omega_{2} = \sqrt{\frac{3k}{m}}$ ， $\omega_{3} = \sqrt{\frac{2m + M}{mM}} k.$ 

将 $\omega_{2}$ 代入 $H\cdot u = 0$ 

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c c} - k & - k & - k \\ - k & k \left(2 - \frac {3 M}{m}\right) & - k \\ - k & - k & - k \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l} u _ {1} ^ {(2)} \\ u _ {2} ^ {(2)} \\ u _ {3} ^ {(2)} \end{array} \right) = 0
$$

$$
u _ {1} ^ {(2)} + u _ {2} ^ {(2)} + u _ {3} ^ {(2)} = 0
$$

$$
- u _ {1} ^ {(2)} + \left(2 - \frac {3 M}{m}\right) u _ {2} ^ {(2)} - u _ {3} ^ {(2)} = 0
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(2)} = 1$ ，则

$$
u _ {2} ^ {(2)} = 0, \quad u _ {3} ^ {(2)} = - 1
$$

将 $\omega_{3}$ 代入 $H \cdot u = 0$ ,

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c c} \frac {M - 2 m}{M} k & - k & - k \\ - k & - \frac {M}{m} k & - k \\ - k & - k & \frac {M - 2 m}{M} k \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l} u _ {1} ^ {(3)} \\ u _ {2} ^ {(3)} \\ u _ {3} ^ {(3)} \end{array} \right) = 0
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (M - 2 m) u _ {1} ^ {(3)} - M u _ {2} ^ {(3)} - M u _ {3} ^ {(3)} & = 0 \\ m u _ {1} ^ {(3)} + M u _ {2} ^ {(3)} + m u _ {3} ^ {(3)} & = 0 \end{array}
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(3)} = 1$ ，则

$$
u _ {2} ^ {(3)} = - \frac {2 m}{M}, u _ {3} ^ {(3)} = 1
$$

将 $\omega_{1}$ 代入 $H \cdot u = 0$ ,

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c c} 2 k & - k & - k \\ - k & 2 k & - k \\ - k & - k & 2 k \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l} u _ {1} ^ {(1)} \\ u _ {2} ^ {(1)} \\ u _ {3} ^ {(1)} \end{array} \right) = 0
$$

$$
2 u _ {1} ^ {(1)} - u _ {2} ^ {(1)} - u _ {3} ^ {(1)} = 0
$$

$$
u _ {1} ^ {(1)} - 2 u _ {2} ^ {(1)} + u _ {3} ^ {(1)} = 0
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(1)} = 1$ ，则

$$
u _ {2} ^ {(1)} = 1, \quad u _ {3} ^ {(1)} = 1
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left( \begin{array}{l} \xi \\ \eta \\ \zeta \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{l l l} u _ {1} ^ {(1)} & u _ {2} ^ {(1)} & u _ {3} ^ {(1)} \\ u _ {1} ^ {(2)} & u _ {2} ^ {(2)} & u _ {3} ^ {(2)} \\ u _ {1} ^ {(3)} & u _ {2} ^ {(3)} & u _ {3} ^ {(3)} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l l l} m _ {1 1} & m _ {1 2} & m _ {1 3} \\ m _ {2 1} & m _ {2 2} & m _ {2 3} \\ m _ {3 1} & m _ {3 2} & m _ {3 3} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l} \theta_ {1} \\ \theta_ {2} \\ \theta_ {3} \end{array} \right)} \\ & {\quad = \left( \begin{array}{l l l} 1 & 1 & 1 \\ 1 & 0 & - 1 \\ 1 & - \frac {2 m}{M} & 1 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l l l} m & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & M & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & m \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l} \theta_ {1} \\ \theta_ {2} \\ \theta_ {3} \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{l} m \theta_ {1} + M \theta_ {2} + m \theta_ {3} \\ m \theta_ {1} - m \theta_ {3} \\ m \theta_ {1} - 2 m \theta_ {2} + m \theta_ {3} \end{array} \right)} \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} {\xi = m \theta_ {1} + M \theta_ {2} + m \theta_ {3}} \\ {\eta = m \theta_ {1} - m \theta_ {3}} \\ {\zeta = m \theta_ {1} - 2 m \theta_ {2} + m \theta_ {3}} \end{array} \text {或} \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} {\xi = \theta_ {1} + \frac {M}{m} \theta_ {2} + \theta_ {3}} \\ {\eta = \theta_ {1} - \theta_ {3}} \\ {\zeta = \theta_ {1} - 2 \theta_ {2} + \theta_ {3}} \end{array} \right. \right.
$$

10.1.37 两个质量为 m 和一个质量为 M 的点状物体用两根劲度系数为 k、自然长度为零的轻弹簧连结，一起被限制在竖直的半径为 R 的光滑圆管道内运动，如图 10.35 所示，物体可以相互通过，求系统围绕平衡位置小振动的振动模式，并描述每一个模式。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/0ecda16942ce64966b420dc7d777f90029fd493da72eff1444c256339d1441c8.jpg)


解 用 $\theta_{1}, \theta_{2}, \theta_{3}$ 表示 $m, M, m$ 三物体偏离平衡位置的角位移，均以逆时针方向为正，

图10.35

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} m (R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {3} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} M R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ & V = \frac {1}{2} k (R \theta_ {2} - R \theta_ {1}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k (R \theta_ {3} - R \theta_ {2}) ^ {2} + m g R (1 - \cos \theta_ {1}) \\ & \quad + M g R (1 - \cos \theta_ {2}) + m g R (1 - \cos \theta_ {3}) \\ & \approx \frac {1}{2} k R ^ {2} (\theta_ {1} ^ {2} + 2 \theta_ {2} ^ {2} + \theta_ {3} ^ {2} - 2 \theta_ {1} \theta_ {2} - 2 \theta_ {2} \theta_ {3}) + \frac {1}{2} m g R (\theta_ {1} ^ {2} + \theta_ {3} ^ {2}) \\ & \quad + \frac {1}{2} M g R \theta_ {2} ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

为书写简便起见，令 $x_{1} = R\theta_{1}, x_{2} = R\theta_{2}, x_{3} = R\theta_{3}$ .

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L = T - V & = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {x} _ {3} ^ {2}) - \left[ \frac {1}{2} \left(k + \frac {m g}{R}\right) (x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {3} ^ {2}) \right. \\ & \quad \left. + \frac {1}{2} \left(2 k + \frac {M g}{R}\right) x _ {2} ^ {2} - k x _ {1} x _ {2} - k x _ {2} x _ {3} \right] \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{x}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial x_i} = 0$ 得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & m \ddot {x} _ {1} + \left(k + \frac {m g}{R}\right) x _ {1} - k x _ {2} = 0 \\ & M \ddot {x} _ {2} + \left(2 k + \frac {M g}{R}\right) x _ {2} - k (x _ {1} + x _ {3}) = 0 \\ & m \ddot {x} _ {3} + \left(k + \frac {m g}{R}\right) x _ {3} - k x _ {2} = 0 \end{array}
$$

设 $x_{i} = u_{i}\cos (\omega t + \alpha)$ ，则

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c c} k + \frac {m g}{R} - m \omega^ {2} & - k & 0 \\ - k & 2 k + \frac {M g}{R} - M \omega^ {2} & - k \\ 0 & - k & k + \frac {m g}{R} - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l} u _ {1} \\ u _ {2} \\ u _ {3} \end{array} \right) = 0\tag{1}
$$

$u_{1}$ 、 $u_{2}$ 、 $u_{3}$ 得非零解，必有

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c c} k + \frac {m g}{R} - m \omega^ {2} & - k & 0 \\ - k & 2 k + \frac {M g}{R} - M \omega^ {2} & - k \\ 0 & - k & k + \frac {m g}{R} - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0   \omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{k} + \frac {k}{m}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{R} + \frac {k}{m} + \frac {2 k}{M}}, \quad \omega_ {3} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{R}}
$$

解得

将 $\omega_{1},\omega_{2},\omega_{3}$ 分别代入(1)式，取 $u_{1}^{(1)} = u_{1}^{(2)} = u_{1}^{(3)} = 1$ ，可得 $u_{2}^{(1)} = 0,u_{3}^{(1)} = -1;u_{2}^{(2)} = -\frac{2m}{M},$ $u_{3}^{(2)} = 1;u_{2}^{(3)} = 1,u_{3}^{(3)} = 1.$ 

三种振动模式(如图 10.36 所示)分别为：

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/c5019372df515b32f94b3b359ca445e4b7a5cf7e5773e40465312a4beb0c7a1b.jpg)



图10.36


$$
\begin{array}{l l} \omega_ {1}: & (1, 0, - 1) \\ \omega_ {2}: & \left(1, - \frac {2 m}{M}, - 1\right) \\ \omega_ {3}: & (1, 1, 1) \end{array}
$$

第一种振动模式; M 不动, 两个 m 振幅相同、相位相反; 第二种振动模式: 两个 m 振幅和相位均相同, M 的振幅是 m 的振幅的 $\frac{2m}{M}$ 倍, 相位相反; 第三种振动模式; 三个物体振幅相同、相位也相同, 是弹簧保持原

长(为零)不起作用的情况.

10.1.38 一块质量为 $m$ 的均质正方形薄板，在相邻的两个角用劲度系数为 $k$ 的、完全相同的弹簧悬挂起来，如图10.37所示，弹簧和薄板在竖直平面内做小振动，求简正频率.

解 取平衡时质心所在位置为坐标系的原点,取质心的 x、y 坐标和绕质心的转角 $\theta$ 为广义坐标,如图 10.38 所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/291463903054777b9c38de38dbc1374edf7502bfed128a8baa4d927ef6f7e3f3.jpg)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/57958c6ffa2a820521a57a6bc6c03b78ecfd0a4d5b028b1c6a4da960f2f71621.jpg)


用关于转动惯量的垂直轴定理. 设正方形边长为 $a$ , 绕通过质心垂直于薄板的轴的转动惯量为 $I = \frac{1}{6} ma^2$ ,


图10.38


图10.37

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{1 2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2}
$$

考虑在平衡位置附近的小振动. $A, B$ 点在 $x, y$ 方向的改变量分别为

$$
\Delta x _ {A} \approx x + \frac {1}{2} a \theta , \quad \Delta y _ {A} = y - \frac {1}{2} a \theta
$$

$$
\Delta x _ {B} \approx x + \frac {1}{2} a \theta , \quad \Delta y _ {B} = y + \frac {1}{2} a \theta
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {F} _ {A} = - k \Delta x _ {A} \boldsymbol {i} - k \Delta y _ {A} \boldsymbol {j} = - k \left(x + \frac {1}{2} a \theta\right) \boldsymbol {i} - k \left(y - \frac {1}{2} a \theta\right) \boldsymbol {j}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {F} _ {B} = - k \Delta x _ {B} \boldsymbol {i} - k \Delta y _ {B} \boldsymbol {j} = - k \left(x + \frac {1}{2} a \theta\right) \boldsymbol {i} - k \left(y + \frac {1}{2} a \theta\right) \boldsymbol {j}
$$

注意:由于坐标原点取在平衡位置,考虑弹簧由平衡位置的再伸长来写的“弹簧力”,实际上已是弹簧力和重力的合力.

$F_{A}, F_{B}$ 均是保守力，势能分别为

$$
V _ {A} = \frac {1}{2} k (\Delta x _ {A}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k (\Delta y _ {A}) ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} k \left(x + \frac {1}{2} a \theta\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k \left(y - \frac {1}{2} a \theta\right) ^ {2}
$$

$$
V _ {B} = \frac {1}{2} k \left(x + \frac {1}{2} a \theta\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k \left(y + \frac {1}{2} a \theta\right) ^ {2}
$$

$$
V = V _ {A} + V _ {B} = k \left(x + \frac {1}{2} a \theta\right) ^ {2} + k y ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} k a ^ {2} \theta^ {2}
$$

可以验证，由上述的势能求得的广义力 $Q_{x}, Q_{y}, Q_{\theta}$ 与由 $\delta W = F_{A} \cdot \delta r_{A} + F_{B} \cdot \delta r_{B}$ 算出的 $Q_{x}, Q_{y}, Q_{\theta}$ 是完全相同的.

写惯性矩阵和刚度矩阵时, 矩阵元的量纲必须相同, 因此我们改取 $x, y, a\theta$ 为广义坐标,

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{1 2} m (a \dot {\theta}) ^ {2}
$$

$$
V = \frac {1}{2} [ 2 k (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) + k (a \theta) ^ {2} + 2 k x (a \theta) ]
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {M} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} m & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & m & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & \frac {1}{6} m \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {K} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 2 k & 0 & k \\ 0 & 2 k & 0 \\ k & 0 & k \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
| \textbf {H} | = \left| \begin{array}{c c c} 2 k - m \omega^ {2} & 0 & k \\ 0 & 2 k - m \omega^ {2} & 0 \\ k & 0 & k - \frac {1}{6} m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

解出

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {2 k}{m}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {(4 + \sqrt {1 0}) \frac {k}{m}}, \omega_ {3} = \sqrt {(4 - \sqrt {1 0}) \frac {k}{m}}.
$$

10.1.39 三个质量均为 $M$ 的质点被图10.39中所画的六根弹簧连接起来， $A, B, C$ 三点是固定的，组成等边三角形，所有弹簧的劲度系数均为 $k$ . 平衡时，三个质点组成一个等边三角形，如质点受到约束，只能沿 $\Delta ABC$ 的三条中线运动，运动所在的平面是光滑的水平面，求小振动的简正频率和振动模式.

解 取各质点向外偏离平衡位置的位移 $x_{1}, x_{2}, x_{3}$ 为广义坐标，

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} _ {3} ^ {2}
$$

考虑图 10.40 一条弹簧的势能, 设平衡时弹簧的长度为 a,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} V _ {1} & = \frac {1}{2} k \left\{\sqrt {\left[ a + (x _ {1} + x _ {2}) \cos \frac {\pi}{6} \right] ^ {2} + \left[ (x _ {2} - x _ {1}) \sin \frac {\pi}{6} \right] ^ {2}} - a \right\} ^ {2} \\ & \approx \frac {1}{2} k \left\{\sqrt {a ^ {2} + 2 a (x _ {1} + x _ {2}) \cos \frac {\pi}{6}} - a \right\} ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

近似中略去了根号内的二级小量 $x_{1}^{2}, x_{2}^{2}, x_{1}x_{2}$ 这些项，它们对 $V_{1}$ 的贡献是四级小量，我们只保留 $V_{1}$ 的二级小量，

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/06bedf06fe35bf0422b11965d6b2389643da0afc1d6018b689e987eedc7b1516.jpg)



图10.39


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/9f1566d86c9596f5e5095f34b823c954aaaedd205f40301aa48d0824a19c98d3.jpg)



图10.40


所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {V _ {1} = \frac {1}{2} k \left\{a \Big [ 1 + \frac {2}{a} (x _ {1} + x _ {2}) \cos \frac {\pi}{6} \Big ] ^ {1 / 2} - a \right\} ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad \approx \frac {1}{2} k \left\{a \Big [ 1 + \frac {1}{a} (x _ {1} + x _ {2}) \cos \frac {\pi}{6} \Big ] - a \right\} ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{2} k \Big [ (x _ {1} + x _ {2}) \cos \frac {\pi}{6} \Big ] ^ {2} = \frac {3}{8} k (x _ {1} + x _ {2}) ^ {2}} \\ {\text {以}} V = \frac {1}{2} k x _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x _ {3} ^ {2} + \frac {3}{8} k (x _ {1} + x _ {2}) ^ {2} + \frac {3}{8} k (x _ {2} + x _ {3}) ^ {2} + \frac {3}{8} k (x _ {3} + x _ {1}) ^ {2} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{2} k \Big (\frac {5}{2} x _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {5}{2} x _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {5}{2} x _ {3} ^ {2} + \frac {3}{2} x _ {1} x _ {2} + \frac {3}{2} x _ {2} x _ {3} + \frac {3}{2} x _ {3} x _ {1} \Big)} \\ & {\qquad M = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} {{M}} & {{0}} & {{0}} \\ {{0}} & {{M}} & {{0}} \\ {{0}} & {{0}} & {{M}} \end{array} \right)} \\ & {\qquad K = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} {{\frac {5}{2} k}} & {{\frac {3}{4} k}} & {{\frac {3}{4} k}} \\ {{\frac {3}{4} k}} & {{\frac {5}{2} k}} & {{\frac {3}{4} k}} \\ {{\frac {3}{4} k}} & {{\frac {3}{4} k}} & {{\frac {5}{2} k}} \end{array} \right)} \end{array}
$$

$$
| \boldsymbol {H} | = \left| \begin{array}{c c c} \frac {5}{2} k - M \omega^ {2} & \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {3}{4} k \\ \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {5}{2} k - M \omega^ {2} & \frac {3}{4} k \\ \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {5}{2} k - M \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

第一行减第二行作第一行，再第一列加第二列作第二列得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left| \begin{array}{c c c} \frac {7}{4} k - M \omega^ {2} & 0 & 0 \\ \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {1 3}{4} k - M \omega^ {2} & \frac {3}{4} k \\ \frac {3}{4} k & \frac {3}{2} k & \frac {5}{2} k - M \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0} \\ & {\left(\frac {7}{4} k - M \omega^ {2}\right) \left[ \left(\frac {1 3}{4} k - M \omega^ {2}\right) \left(\frac {5}{2} k - M \omega^ {2}\right) - \frac {9}{8} k ^ {2} \right] = 0} \\ & {\left(\frac {7}{4} k - M \omega^ {2}\right) \left(M ^ {2} \omega^ {4} - \frac {2 3}{4} k M \omega^ {2} + 7 k ^ {2}\right) = 0} \\ & {\left(\frac {7}{4} k - M \omega^ {2}\right) \left(M \omega^ {2} - \frac {7}{4} k\right) (M \omega^ {2} - 4 k) = 0} \\ & {\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {7 k}{4 M}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {7 k}{4 M}}, \quad \omega_ {3} = \sqrt {\frac {4 k}{M}}} \end{array}
$$

$\omega_{1}, \omega_{2}$ 是简并的，将它们代入 $H \cdot u = 0$ ，得

$$
\frac {3}{4} k \left(u _ {1} + u _ {2} + u _ {3}\right) = 0
$$

只能得到一个独立方程. 今取两个解:

取 $u_{1}^{(1)} = 1, u_{2}^{(1)} = 0$ ，则 $u_{3}^{(1)} = -1$ ;

取 $u_{1}^{(2)} = 1, u_{2}^{(2)} = -2$ ，则 $u_{3}^{(2)} = 1$ .

将 $\omega_{3}$ 代入 $H\cdot u = 0$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & - 2 u _ {1} ^ {(3)} + u _ {2} ^ {(3)} + u _ {3} ^ {(3)} = 0 \\ & u _ {1} ^ {(3)} - 2 u _ {2} ^ {(3)} + u _ {3} ^ {(3)} = 0 \end{array}
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(3)} = 1$ ，则 $u_{2}^{(3)} = 1, u_{3}^{(3)} = 1.$ 

模式图如图 10.41 所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/93172a503b6a393f808fe14d640286d622644db8d1aa07d1fe3eba2007a72e05.jpg)



图10.41


10.1.40 一个质量为 $M$ 、长为 $a$ 、宽度为 $b$ 的均质矩形板，在其每个角用一根劲度系数为 $k$ 的同样的轻弹簧支撑，这些弹簧只能在铅垂方向运动，求小振动时振动的简正频率。

解 取平衡时质心的位置为竖直向上的静坐标轴 $\zeta$ 的原点, 另取固连于薄板的动坐标系 Cxyz、x、y 轴在板上分别与矩形的边平行和垂直, 如图 10.42 所示.

取质心 C 的 $\zeta$ 坐标, 板对 x 轴的转角 $\varphi$ 、对 y 轴的转角 $\theta$ 为广义坐标.

矩形板对过质心的 $x$ 轴、 $y$ 轴的转动惯量分别为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} I _ {x x} & = \frac {1}{1 2} M a ^ {2}, \quad I _ {y y} = \frac {1}{1 2} M b ^ {2} \\ T & = \frac {1}{2} M \zeta^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {x x} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {y y} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} M \dot {\zeta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \\ V & = \frac {1}{2} k \zeta_ {A} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k \zeta_ {B} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k \zeta_ {D} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k \zeta_ {E} ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/a8172d39316e74577274d3bc78e07c07554a4b4d08707480bfd2758797540f89.jpg)



图10.42


其中 $\zeta_{A}, \zeta_{B}, \zeta_{D}, \zeta_{E}$ 分别是矩形板四个角 A、B、D、E 的 $\zeta$ 坐标，

$$
\zeta_ {A} = \zeta - \frac {1}{2} a \varphi + \frac {1}{2} b \theta , \quad \zeta_ {B} = \zeta - \frac {1}{2} a \varphi - \frac {1}{2} b \theta
$$

$$
\zeta_ {D} = \zeta + \frac {1}{2} a \varphi - \frac {1}{2} b \theta , \quad \zeta_ {E} = \zeta + \frac {1}{2} a \varphi + \frac {1}{2} b \theta
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & V = \frac {1}{2} k (4 \zeta^ {2} + a ^ {2} \varphi^ {2} + b ^ {2} \theta^ {2}) \\ & L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} M \dot {\zeta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \\ & - \frac {1}{2} k (4 \zeta^ {2} + a ^ {2} \varphi^ {2} + b ^ {2} \theta^ {2}) \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{q}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & M \ddot {\zeta} + 4 k \zeta = 0 \\ & \frac {1}{1 2} M a ^ {2} \ddot {\varphi} + k a ^ {2} \varphi = 0 \\ & \frac {1}{1 2} M b ^ {2} \ddot {\theta} + k b ^ {2} \theta = 0 \end{array}
$$

小振动的简正频率为

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {4 k}{M}} = 2 \sqrt {\frac {k}{M}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \omega_ {3} = \sqrt {\frac {1 2 k}{M}} = 2 \sqrt {\frac {3 k}{M}}
$$

10.1.41 一个质量为 $M$ 、半径为 $R$ 的均质薄圆盘用两根劲度系数均为 $k$ 、原长均为 $l_0$ 的轻弹簧，连在一个无摩擦的桌面上的两个固定点，盘可在桌面上做平面平行运动。处于平衡时弹簧长度为 $l (>l_0)$ ，求围绕平衡位置做小振动的简正频率。

解 取平衡位置为 $x, y$ 坐标的原点，两个固定点的连线为 $x$ 轴，取盘心的 $x, y$ 坐标及盘的转角 $\varphi$ 为广义坐标，如图10.44所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/f15a7bea771dfd9f4ac538de2b040570b2536131f8505bbac710b2bbd6a34b15.jpg)



图10.43


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/bd8a7463d99889552dea350509145abaca55c920befabae4a99f734dee74eada.jpg)



图10.44


$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {x _ {A} = x - R \mathrm{cos} \varphi , \quad y _ {A} = y + R \mathrm{sin} \varphi} \\ & {x _ {B} = x + R \mathrm{cos} \varphi , \quad y _ {B} = y - R \mathrm{sin} \varphi} \\ & {T = \frac {1}{2} M (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{2} M R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}} \\ & {V = \frac {1}{2} k \Big [ \sqrt {(l + R + x _ {A}) ^ {2} + y _ {A} ^ {2}} - l _ {0} \Big ] ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k (l - l _ {0}) ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad + \frac {1}{2} k \Big [ \sqrt {(l + R - x _ {B}) ^ {2} + y _ {B} ^ {2}} - l _ {0} \Big ] ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k (l - l _ {0}) ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

代入 $x_{A}, y_{A}, x_{B}, y_{B}$ , 保留二级小量, 经计算可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} V = \frac {1}{2} k \left[ 2 x ^ {2} + 2 \left(1 - \frac {l _ {0}}{l}\right) y ^ {2} + 2 R (l + R) \left(1 - \frac {l _ {0}}{l}\right) \varphi^ {2} \right] \\ L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} M (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{4} M R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \\ - k [ x ^ {2} + \left(1 - \frac {l _ {0}}{l}\right) y ^ {2} + R (l + R) \left(1 - \frac {l _ {0}}{l}\right) \varphi^ {2} ] \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i}\right) - \frac{\partial I}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 可得

$$
\begin{array}{c} M \ddot {x} + 2 k x = 0 \\ M \ddot {y} + 2 \left(1 - \frac {l _ {0}}{l}\right) k y = 0 \\ \frac {1}{2} M R \ddot {\varphi} + 2 (l + R) \left(1 - \frac {l _ {0}}{l}\right) k \varphi = 0 \end{array}
$$

小振动的简正频率分别为

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {2 k}{M}}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \omega_ {2} & = \sqrt {\frac {2 k (l - l _ {0})}{M l}} \\ \omega_ {3} & = \sqrt {\frac {4 (l + R) (l - l _ {0}) k}{M R l}} \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/e4404f62ca82f6a6d10d68a3a313334f7a76707535c9aa777e87909301e6caa1.jpg)



图10.45


10.1.42 两个质量为 $M$ 的小球用三根劲度系数均为 $k$ 、原长均为 $\frac{a}{2}$ 的轻弹簧相连，且连到两个固定点上，如图10.45所示，小球能做左右和上下运动，求

系统围绕图示的平衡位置

做小振动的简正频率(重力可以不计).

解 取平衡位置为广义坐标 $x_{1}, y_{1}, x_{2}, y_{2}$ 的原点. $x_{1}, x_{2}$ 轴沿左右方向, $y_{1}, y_{2}$ 沿上下方向, 如图 10.46 所示,

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/be850bf9e49c98c7541a5e619754187b7b383ae6363ec843cbdb876ffa983383.jpg)


$$
\begin{array}{r l} V & = \frac {1}{2} k \left[ \sqrt {(a + x _ {1}) ^ {2} + y _ {1} ^ {2}} - \frac {1}{2} a \right] ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} \\ & + \frac {1}{2} k \left[ \sqrt {(a + x _ {2} - x _ {1}) ^ {2} + (y _ {2} - y _ {1}) ^ {2}} - \frac {a}{2} \right] ^ {2} \\ & - \frac {1}{2} k \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k \left[ \sqrt {(a - x _ {2}) ^ {2} + y _ {2} ^ {2}} - \frac {a}{2} \right] ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k \left(\frac {a}{2}\right) ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

图10.46

简化计算时注意保留二级小量,例如

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left[ \sqrt {(a + x _ {1}) ^ {2} + y _ {1} ^ {2}} - \frac {a}{2} \right] ^ {2}} \\ & {= a ^ {2} + x _ {1} ^ {2} + 2 a x _ {1} + y _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {a ^ {2}}{4} - a \sqrt {a ^ {2} + 2 a x _ {1} + x _ {1} ^ {2} + y _ {1} ^ {2}}} \\ & {= a ^ {2} + x _ {1} ^ {2} + 2 a x _ {1} + y _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {a ^ {2}}{4} - a ^ {2} \Big (1 + \frac {2 x _ {1}}{a} + \frac {x _ {1} ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} + \frac {y _ {1} ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} \Big) ^ {\frac {1}{2}}} \\ & {\approx a ^ {2} + x _ {1} ^ {2} + 2 a x _ {1} + y _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {a ^ {2}}{4} - a ^ {2} \Big [ 1 + \frac {1}{2} \Big (\frac {2 x _ {1}}{a} + \frac {x _ {1} ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} + \frac {y _ {1} ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} \Big) - \frac {1}{8} \Big (\frac {2 x _ {1}}{a} + \frac {x _ {1} ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} + \frac {y _ {1} ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} \Big) ^ {2} \Big ]} \\ & {\approx x _ {1} ^ {2} + 2 a x _ {1} + y _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {a ^ {2}}{4} - a x _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} x _ {1} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} y _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} x _ {1} ^ {2}} \\ & {= x _ {1} ^ {2} + a x _ {1} + \frac {1}{2} y _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {a ^ {2}}{4}} \end{array}
$$

可得

$$
\begin{array}{c} {V = \frac {1}{2} k (2 x _ {1} ^ {2} + 2 x _ {2} ^ {2} + y _ {1} ^ {2} + y _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 x _ {1} x _ {2} - y _ {1} y _ {2})} \\ {L = \frac {1}{2} M (\dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2}) - \frac {1}{2} k (2 x _ {1} ^ {2} + 2 x _ {2} ^ {2} + y _ {1} ^ {2} + y _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 x _ {1} x _ {2} - y _ {1} y _ {2})} \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{q}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 得

$$
M \ddot {x} _ {1} + 2 k x _ {1} - k x _ {2} = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
M \ddot {x} _ {2} + 2 k x _ {2} - k x _ {1} = 0\tag{2}
$$

$$
M \ddot {y} _ {1} + k y _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} k y _ {2} = 0\tag{3}
$$

$$
M \ddot {y} _ {2} + k y _ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k y _ {1} = 0\tag{4}
$$

(1)、(2)两式的特征方程为

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c} 2 k - M \omega^ {2} & - k \\ - k & 2 k - M \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

解出两小球在左右方向运动的简正频率为

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {k}{M}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {3 k}{M}}
$$

(3)、(4)两式的特征方程为

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c} k - M \omega^ {2} & - \frac {1}{2} k \\ - \frac {1}{2} k & k - M \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

解出上下方向运动的两个简正频率为

$$
\omega_ {3} = \sqrt {\frac {k}{2 M}}, \quad \omega_ {4} = \sqrt {\frac {3 k}{2 M}}
$$

附带指出,如作用于小球的重力不能忽略.势能中需加上重力势能,不影响运动微分方程(1)、(2)式,因而不会对 $\omega_{1}$ 、 $\omega_{2}$ 有所影响,但微分方程(3)、(4)式需作修改,都有一项重力的常数项,只是影响在竖直方向的平衡位置,对 $\omega_{3}$ 、 $\omega_{4}$ 也无影响.

10.1.43 四个质量为 $m$ 的全同质点用四根劲度系数为 $k$ 的全同弹簧连起来, 被限制在一半径为 $b$ 的光滑的水平圆周上运动, 如图 10.47 所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/02c624a9ee7607a8d0eca35589edbad687419b28877247c50c689d78e78dc048.jpg)



图10.47


(1) 有几种小振动振动模式；

(2) 小振动简正频率多大?

解 （1）显然四个质点的连线构成正方形时系统可处于平衡，按逆时针方向依次给每一个质点编号，用逆时针方向偏离平衡位置的角位移 $\theta_{1}$ 、 $\theta_{2}$ 、 $\theta_{3}$ 、 $\theta_{4}$ 作为广义坐标，

$$
\begin{array}{c} T = \frac {1}{2} m b ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {3} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {4} ^ {2}) \\ V = \frac {1}{2} k \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {4} \left[ 2 b \sin \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {\pi}{2} + \theta_ {n + 1} - \theta_ {n}\right) - 2 b \sin \frac {\pi}{4} \right] ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

其中 $\theta_{5}=\theta_{1}$ . 计算其中一项的近似式，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {1}{2} k \left[ 2 b \sin \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {\pi}{2} + \theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}\right) - 2 b \sin \frac {\pi}{4} \right] ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{2} k \cdot 4 b ^ {2} \left[ \frac {\sqrt {2}}{2} \cos \frac {1}{2} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) + \frac {\sqrt {2}}{2} \sin \frac {1}{2} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) - \frac {\sqrt {2}}{2} \right] ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad \approx 2 k b ^ {2} \cdot \left(\frac {\sqrt {2}}{2}\right) ^ {2} \cdot \left[ \frac {1}{2} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) \right] ^ {2} = \frac {1}{4} k b ^ {2} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
V = \frac {1}{4} k b ^ {2} \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {4} (\theta_ {n + 1} - \theta_ {n}) ^ {2}
$$

显然， $\theta_{1} = \theta_{2} = \theta_{3} = \theta_{4}, V = 0$ ，是随遇平衡的情况，是整体转动的情况，它不是振动，因此四个自由度的系统，只有三个振动自由度，故有三种小振动振动模式。

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} m b ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {3} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {4} ^ {2}) \\ & V = \frac {1}{4} k b ^ {2} [ (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) ^ {2} + (\theta_ {3} - \theta_ {2}) ^ {2} + (\theta_ {4} - \theta_ {3}) ^ {2} + (\theta_ {1} - \theta_ {4}) ^ {2} ] \\ & = \frac {1}{2} k b ^ {2} (\theta_ {1} ^ {2} + \theta_ {2} ^ {2} + \theta_ {3} ^ {2} + \theta_ {4} ^ {2} - \theta_ {1} \theta_ {2} - \theta_ {2} \theta_ {3} - \theta_ {3} \theta_ {4} - \theta_ {4} \theta_ {1}) \\ & M = \left( \begin{array}{c c c c} m b ^ {2} & 0 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & m b ^ {2} & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & m b ^ {2} & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 0 & m b ^ {2} \end{array} \right) \\ & K = \left( \begin{array}{c c c c} k b ^ {2} & - \frac {1}{2} k b ^ {2} & 0 & - \frac {1}{2} k b ^ {2} \\ - \frac {1}{2} k b ^ {2} & k b ^ {2} & - \frac {1}{2} k b ^ {2} & 0 \\ 0 & - \frac {1}{2} k b ^ {2} & k b ^ {2} & - \frac {1}{2} k b ^ {2} \\ - \frac {1}{2} k b ^ {2} & 0 & - \frac {1}{2} k b ^ {2} & k b ^ {2} \end{array} \right) \\ | H | = | K - \omega^ {2} M | = b ^ {2} & \left| \begin{array}{c c c c} k - m \omega^ {2} & - \frac {1}{2} k & 0 & - \frac {1}{2} k \\ - \frac {1}{2} k & k - m \omega^ {2} & - \frac {1}{2} k & 0 \\ 0 & - \frac {1}{2} k & k - m \omega^ {2} & - \frac {1}{2} k \\ - \frac {1}{2} k & 0 & - \frac {1}{2} k & k - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0 \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

第二列减第四列作第二列，然后第二行加第四行作第二行，可得

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c c c} k - m \omega^ {2} & 0 & 0 & - \frac {1}{2} k \\ - k & 0 & - k & k - m \omega^ {2} \\ 0 & 0 & k - m \omega^ {2} & - \frac {1}{2} k \\ - \frac {1}{2} k & - (k - m \omega^ {2}) & - \frac {1}{2} k & k - m \omega^ {2} \\ k - m \omega^ {2} = 0, & \omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

可见

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left| \begin{array}{c c c} k - m \omega^ {2} & 0 & - \frac {1}{2} k \\ - k & - k & k - m \omega^ {2} \\ 0 & k - m \omega^ {2} & - \frac {1}{2} k \end{array} \right| = 0} \\ & {(k - m \omega^ {2}) \left[ (- k) \left(- \frac {1}{2} k\right) - (k - m \omega^ {2}) ^ {2} \right] - \frac {1}{2} k (- k) (k - m \omega^ {2}) = 0} \\ & {(k - m \omega^ {2}) [ k ^ {2} - (k - m \omega^ {2}) ^ {2} ] = 0} \\ & {(k - m \omega^ {2}) (2 k - m \omega^ {2}) \cdot m \omega^ {2} = 0} \\ & {\omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}}, \qquad \omega_ {3} = \sqrt {\frac {2 k}{m}}, \qquad \omega_ {4} = 0} \end{array}
$$

所以

$\omega_{4}=0$ 不是简正频率,系统的运动是整体一起以恒定的角速度转动.

10.1.44 一质点在一个三维各向同性谐振子势场中振动的角频率为 $\omega_0$ ，若质点带电荷 $e$ ，还受到均匀的电磁场作用， $B = B_0k, E = E_0i$ . 求质点的振动频率，并讨论在弱场和强场两种极限情况下的振动频率.

解

$$
\pmb {B} = B _ {0} \pmb {k}, \quad \pmb {E} = E _ {0} \pmb {i}
$$

可取标势

$$
\phi = - E _ {0} x
$$

矢势

$$
\boldsymbol {A} = \frac {1}{2} B _ {0} (- y \boldsymbol {i} + x \boldsymbol {j})
$$

可以验证有

$$
\nabla \times \mathbf {A} = B _ {0} \mathbf {k}
$$

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2})
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} V & = \frac {1}{2} m \omega_ {0} ^ {2} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}) + e \phi - e \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} \cdot \boldsymbol {A} \\ & = \frac {1}{2} m \omega_ {0} ^ {2} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}) - e E _ {0} x + \frac {1}{2} e B _ {0} (\dot {x} y - x \dot {y}) \end{array}
$$

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) - \frac {1}{2} m \omega_ {0} ^ {2} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}) + e E _ {0} x - \frac {1}{2} e B _ {0} (\dot {x} y - x \dot {y})
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{q}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} m \ddot {x} - e B _ {0} \dot {y} + m \omega_ {0} ^ {2} x - e E _ {0} & = 0 \\ m \ddot {y} + e B _ {0} \dot {x} + m \omega_ {0} ^ {2} y & = 0 \\ m \ddot {z} + m \omega_ {0} ^ {2} z & = 0 \end{array}
$$

立即可得质点在 z 方向振动的角频率为 $\omega_{0}$ .

若令 $x' = x - \frac{eE_0}{m\omega_0^2}$ 

$$
\ddot {x} ^ {\prime} + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} x ^ {\prime} - \frac {e B _ {0}}{m} \dot {y} = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
\ddot {y} + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} y + \frac {e B _ {0}}{m} \dot {x} ^ {\prime} = 0\tag{2}
$$

(1)式+(2)式×i,再令 $w=x'+iy$ ,

$$
\ddot {x} ^ {\prime} + \mathrm{i} \ddot {y} + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} (x ^ {\prime} + \mathrm{i} y) + \mathrm{i} \frac {e B _ {0}}{m} (\dot {x} ^ {\prime} + \mathrm{i} \dot {y}) = 0
$$

即

$$
\dot {w} + \mathrm{i} \frac {e B _ {0}}{m} \dot {w} + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} w = 0
$$

特征方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & r ^ {2} + \mathrm{i} \frac {e B _ {0}}{m} r + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} = 0 \\ & r = \frac {1}{2} \left[ - \mathrm{i} \frac {e B _ {0}}{m} \pm \sqrt {- \frac {e ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2}}{m ^ {2}} - 4 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \right] = \left(- \frac {e B _ {0}}{2 m} \pm \sqrt {\omega_ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {e ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2}}{4 m ^ {2}}}\right) \mathrm{i} \\ & w = C _ {1} ^ {\prime} \exp \left[ i \left(- \frac {e B _ {0}}{2 m} + \sqrt {\omega_ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {e ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2}}{4 m ^ {2}}}\right) t \right] \\ & \quad + C _ {2} ^ {\prime} \exp \left[ i \left(- \frac {e B _ {0}}{2 m} - \sqrt {\omega_ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {e ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2}}{4 m ^ {2}}}\right) t \right] \\ & x ^ {\prime} = \operatorname{Re} w = C _ {1} \cos \left[ \left(- \frac {e B _ {0}}{2 m} + \sqrt {\omega_ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {e ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2}}{4 m ^ {2}}}\right) t + \alpha_ {1} \right] \\ & \quad + C _ {2} \cos \left[ \left(- \frac {e B _ {0}}{2 m} - \sqrt {\omega_ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {e ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2}}{4 m ^ {2}}}\right) t + \alpha_ {2} \right] \\ & y = \operatorname{Im} w = C _ {1} \sin \left[ \left(- \frac {e B _ {0}}{2 m} + \sqrt {\omega_ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {e ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2}}{4 m ^ {2}}}\right) t + \alpha_ {1} \right] \\ & \quad + C _ {2} \sin \left[ \left(- \frac {e B _ {0}}{2 m} - \sqrt {\omega_ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {e ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2}}{4 m ^ {2}}}\right) t + \alpha_ {2} \right] \\ & x = x ^ {\prime} + \frac {e E _ {0}}{m \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} = \frac {e E _ {0}}{m \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} + C _ {1} \cos \left[ \left(- \frac {e B _ {0}}{2 m} + \sqrt {\omega_ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {e ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2}}{4 m ^ {2}}}\right) t + \alpha_ {1} \right] \\ & \quad + C _ {2} \cos \left[ \left(- \frac {a B _ {0}}{2 m} - \sqrt {\omega_ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {e ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2}}{4 m ^ {2}}}\right) t + \alpha_ {2} \right] \end{array}
$$

可见，在 $x, y$ 方向，振动角频率各有两个，它们是 $\sqrt{\omega_0^2 + \frac{e^2B_0^2}{4m^2}} \pm \frac{eB_0}{2m}$ ，在 $z$ 方向，振动角频率为 $\omega_0$ 。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/143fc8d77a0ea64d63967b1a3297265f87d4c4d07ecfcea01d3f40b243df4090.jpg)



图10.48


弱场极限情况下， $\frac{eB_0}{2m} \ll \omega_0, x, y$ 方向的振动角频率各有两个，它们是 $\omega_0 \pm \frac{eB_0}{2m}$ .

强场极限情况下， $\frac{eB_0}{2m} \gg \omega_0, x, y$ 方向的振动角频率仍各有两个，它们是 $\frac{eB_0}{m} + \frac{m\omega_0^2}{eB_0}$ 和 $\frac{m\omega_0^2}{eB_0}$ .

10.1.45 一个苯环模型是由一个光滑的、无质量的圆环上串上六个质量为 $M$ 的小珠子，珠子之间用劲度系数为 $k$ 的六根轻弹簧相连，如图10.48所示那样组成,并考虑圆环是固定的.

(1) 说明系统有多少个小振动振动模式；

(2) 算出所有的小振动简正频率和振动模式并画出振动模式图, 用箭号表示珠子的运动的振幅和相位, 涂黑表示小珠静止不动;

(3) 哪些振动模式与实际的苯分子的振动模式有联系.

提示: 可利用问题的对称性简化计算.

解（1）给六个珠子按逆时针方向依次编号，从1到6，偏离平衡位置的角位移也相应的标为从 $\theta_{1}$ 到 $\theta_{6}$ ，逆时针方向为正.

设圆环的半径为 $r$ ，弹簧的原长为 $a$ ，则系统的势能可写为

$$
V = \frac {1}{2} k r ^ {2} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {6} \left(\frac {\pi}{3} + \theta_ {i + 1} - \theta_ {i} - \frac {a}{r}\right) ^ {2}
$$

其中 $\theta_{7} = \theta_{1}$ 

显然，当 $\theta_{1}=\theta_{2}=\cdots=\theta_{6}$ 时，V=常量，这是随遇平衡的情况，系统整体做恒定角速度的转动（因为系统所受外力均指向环心，对环心的角动量守恒，因此角速度必须恒定），系统有六个自由度，去掉一个 $\omega=0$ 不是振动，尚有五种小振动振动模式。

(2) 

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} M r ^ {2} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {6} \dot {\theta} _ {i} ^ {2}
$$

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} M r ^ {2} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {6} \dot {\theta} _ {i} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k r ^ {2} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {6} \left(\frac {\pi}{3} + \theta_ {i + 1} - \theta_ {i} - \frac {a}{r}\right) ^ {2}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{\theta}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial\theta_i} = 0$ 得

$$
M \ddot {\theta} _ {i} + k (- \theta_ {i - 1} + 2 \theta_ {i} - \theta_ {i + 1}) = 0 \quad (i = 1, 2, \dots , 6)
$$

其中 $\theta_{7} = \theta_{1},\theta_{0} = \theta_{6}$ 

令 $\theta_{i}=u_{i}e^{i\omega t}$ ，代入微分方程组，要得 $u_{1}, u_{2}, \cdots, u_{6}$ 的非零解， $\omega$ 需满足特征方程

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c c c c c} \varepsilon & 1 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 1 \\ 1 & \varepsilon & 1 & 0 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 & \varepsilon & 1 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 1 & \varepsilon & 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 0 & 1 & \varepsilon & 1 \\ 1 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 1 & \varepsilon \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

其中 $\varepsilon=\frac{M\omega^{2}-2k}{k}$ .

把行列式展开, 求 $\varepsilon$ 的六个解, 进而求出六个 $\omega$ 值. 可求出简正频率, 不是太麻烦, 可进而求振动模式 $u_{i}^{(j)}$ , 就比较麻烦了.

下面我们利用问题的对称性,可以简化计算.如前所述,系统对环心的角动量是守恒的,角动量不等于零是整体的转动,五种振动模式系统对环心的角动量均为零,由此考虑对称性,可画出图10.49所示的五种振动模式(尚待验证).

第一种振动模式,如图 10.49(a) 所示.

$$
\theta_ {1} = 0, \quad \theta_ {3} = - \theta_ {2}, \quad \theta_ {4} = 0, \quad \theta_ {6} = - \theta_ {5}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/eb75dd4edaec3e189dc83a9df1667712d238559bfd8433fc61f5a485031bad75.jpg)



图10.49


运动微分方程可简化为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & k [ - (- \theta_ {5}) - \theta_ {2} ] = 0 \\ & M \ddot {\theta} _ {2} + k [ 2 \theta_ {2} - (- \theta_ {2}) ] = 0 \\ & M (- \ddot {\theta} _ {2}) + k [ - \theta_ {2} - 2 (- \theta_ {2}) ] = 0 \\ & k [ - (- \theta_ {2}) - \theta_ {5} ] = 0 \\ & M \ddot {\theta} _ {5} + k [ 2 \theta_ {5} - (- \theta_ {5}) ] = 0 \\ & M (- \ddot {\theta} _ {5}) + k [ - \theta_ {5} + 2 (- \theta_ {5}) ] = 0 \end{array}
$$

六个方程中,只有三个独立的方程,它们是

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & M \ddot {\theta} _ {2} + 3 k \theta_ {2} = 0 \\ & M \ddot {\theta} _ {5} + 3 k \theta_ {5} = 0 \\ & \theta_ {5} - \theta_ {2} = 0 \end{array}
$$

由此可见， $\omega_{1} = \sqrt{\frac{3k}{M}}.$ 

如图 10.49(a)那样, 取 $u_{2}^{(1)} = -1$ , 则 $u_{5}^{(1)} = -1$ , $u_{3}^{(1)} = -u_{2}^{(1)} = 1$ , $u_{6}^{(1)} = -u_{5}^{(1)} = 1$ , $u_{1}^{(1)} = u_{4}^{(1)} = 0$ . 正如解图 10.49(a)所示.

第二种振动模式,如解图 10.49(b) 所示.

$$
\theta_ {1} = 0, \quad \theta_ {6} = - \theta_ {2}, \quad \theta_ {5} = - \theta_ {3}, \quad \theta_ {4} = 0
$$

运动微分方程可简化为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} k (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {2}) & = 0 \\ M \ddot {\theta} _ {2} + k (2 \theta_ {2} - \theta_ {3}) & = 0 \\ M \ddot {\theta} _ {3} + k (- \theta_ {2} + 2 \theta_ {3}) & = 0 \\ k (- \theta_ {3} + \theta_ {3}) & = 0 \\ - M \ddot {\theta} _ {3} + k (- 2 \theta_ {3} + \theta_ {2}) & = 0 \\ - M \ddot {\theta} _ {2} + k (\theta_ {3} - 2 \theta_ {2}) & = 0 \end{array}
$$

独立的方程只有两个,它们是

$$
M \ddot {\theta} _ {2} + 2 k \theta_ {2} - k \theta_ {3} = 0
$$

$$
M \ddot {\theta} _ {3} + 2 k \theta_ {3} - k \theta_ {2} = 0
$$

特征方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left| \begin{array}{c c} {2 k - M \omega^ {2}} & {- k} \\ {- k} & {2 k - M \omega^ {2}} \end{array} \right| = 0} \\ & {\qquad M ^ {2} \omega^ {4} - 4 k M \omega^ {2} + 3 k ^ {2} = 0} \\ & {(M \omega^ {2} - k) (M \omega^ {2} - 3 k) = 0} \end{array}
$$

其中 $\omega_{1} = \sqrt{\frac{3k}{M}}$ 就是前述的第一种振动模式，

由

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {k}{M}}} \\ & {(2 k - M \omega_ {2} ^ {2}) u _ {2} ^ {(2)} - k u _ {3} ^ {(2)} = 0} \\ & {- k u _ {2} ^ {(2)} + (2 k - M \omega_ {2} ^ {2}) u _ {3} ^ {(2)} = 0} \end{array}
$$

或

并按解图10.49(b)所画，令 $u_2^{(2)} = -1$ ，可得 $u_3^{(2)} = -1$ 

所以 $u_{1}^{(2)} = 0, u_{2}^{(2)} = -1, u_{3}^{(2)} = -1, u_{4}^{(2)} = 0, u_{5}^{(2)} = 1, u_{6}^{(2)} = 1.$ 正如解图10.49(b)所画那样.

第三种振动模式,如解图 10.49(c) 所示,

$$
\theta_ {2} = \theta_ {6} = 0, \quad \theta_ {3} = - \theta_ {1}, \quad \theta_ {5} = - \theta_ {4}
$$

运动微分方程可简化为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} M \ddot {\theta} _ {1} + k (2 \theta_ {1}) & = 0 \\ M \ddot {\theta} _ {1} + 2 k \theta_ {1} + k \theta_ {4} & = 0 \\ M \ddot {\theta} _ {4} + 3 k \theta_ {4} + k \theta_ {1} & = 0 \\ M \ddot {\theta} _ {4} + 3 k \theta_ {4} & = 0 \\ \theta_ {4} - \theta_ {1} & = 0 \end{array}
$$

只能得零解,无意义,可见不存在这种模式.

重新考虑第三种振动模式,如解图 10.49(d) 所示,

$$
\theta_ {2} = - \theta_ {1}, \quad \theta_ {4} = - \theta_ {3}, \quad \theta_ {6} = - \theta_ {5}
$$

运动微分方程可简化为

$$
\begin{array}{l} M \ddot {\theta} _ {1} + 3 k \theta_ {1} + k \theta_ {5} = 0 \\ M \ddot {\theta} _ {1} + 3 k \theta_ {1} + k \theta_ {3} = 0 \\ M \ddot {\theta} _ {3} + 3 k \theta_ {3} + k \theta_ {1} = 0 \\ M \ddot {\theta} _ {3} + 3 k \theta_ {3} + k \theta_ {5} = 0 \\ M \ddot {\theta} _ {5} + 3 k \theta_ {5} + k \theta_ {3} = 0 \\ M \ddot {\theta} _ {5} + 3 k \theta_ {5} + k \theta_ {1} = 0 \end{array}
$$

六个方程中最多只能有三个独立的方程，当 $\theta_{1} = \theta_{3} = \theta_{5}$ 时，六个方程都变成

$$
M \ddot {\theta} _ {i} + 4 k \theta_ {i} = 0 \quad (i = 1, 3, 5)
$$

可见

$$
\omega_ {3} ^ {(3)} = \sqrt {\frac {4 k}{M}}
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(3)} = 1$ ，则 $u_{3}^{(3)} = u_{5}^{(3)} = 1, u_{2}^{(3)} = u_{4}^{(3)} = u_{6}^{(3)} = -1.$ 其模式图正如图10.49(d)所示.

现考虑解图 10.49(e) 所示的振动模式，

$$
\theta_ {3} = - \left(\theta_ {2} + \theta_ {4}\right), \quad \theta_ {6} = - \left(\theta_ {1} + \theta_ {5}\right)
$$

运动微分方程可改写为

$$
M \ddot {\theta} _ {1} + k (3 \theta_ {1} + \theta_ {5} - \theta_ {2}) = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
M \ddot {\theta} _ {2} + k (3 \theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1} + \theta_ {4}) = 0\tag{2}
$$

$$
- M \left(\ddot {\theta} _ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {4}\right) + k \left(- 3 \theta_ {2} - 3 \theta_ {4}\right) = 0\tag{3}
$$

$$
M \ddot {\theta} _ {4} + k (3 \theta_ {4} + \theta_ {2} - \theta_ {5}) = 0\tag{4}
$$

$$
M \ddot {\theta} _ {5} + k (3 \theta_ {5} - \theta_ {4} + \theta_ {1}) = 0\tag{5}
$$

$$
- M \left(\ddot {\theta} _ {1} + \ddot {\theta} _ {5}\right) + k \left(3 \theta_ {1} + 3 \theta_ {5}\right) = 0\tag{6}
$$

由式(3)和式(6)可得此振动模式的简正频率为

$$
\omega_ {4} = \sqrt {\frac {3 k}{M}}
$$

考虑到此简正频率，由式(1)或式(4)，可得 $\theta_{2} = \theta_{5}$ ，由式(2)或式(5)，可得 $\theta_{1} = \theta_{4}$ 

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/2f4413d8b33a70ffe040f6175edfce3995ae950c92a0138fd5bdf70566cb69fa.jpg)



图10.50


因此振动模式为 $u_{1}^{(4)} = 1, u_{2}^{(4)} = 1, u_{4}^{(4)} = 1, u_{5}^{(4)} = 1, u_{3}^{(4)} = - (u_{2}^{(4)} + u_{4}^{(4)}) = -2, u_{6}^{(4)} = -(u_{1}^{(4)} + u_{5}^{(4)}) = -2$ ，和图10.49(e)所画的是一致的。这里， $u_{1}^{(4)} = 1, u_{2}^{(4)} = 1$ 是我们可任意选取的，如取 $u_{1}^{(4)} = 1, u_{2}^{(4)} = -1$ ，则 $u_{4}^{(4)} = 1, u_{5}^{(4)} = -1, u_{3}^{(4)} = 0, u_{6}^{(4)} = 0$ ，实际上就成了第一种振动模式，表面上的差别可以给小珠重新编号加以消除。 $u_{1}, u_{2}$ 只能有两种独立的选取法，如取 $u_{1} = 0, u_{2} = -1$ ，就与第一种模式的写法完全相同了；若不重新编号，也可把 $u_{3} = u_{6} = 0$ 的振动视为第一、第四两种振动模式的线性叠加。

现找第五种振动模式,试用图 10.50 的模式图,

$$
\theta_ {1} = - \theta_ {2}, \quad \theta_ {3} = - \theta_ {6}, \quad \theta_ {4} = - \theta_ {5}
$$

独立的微分方程为

$$
M \ddot {\theta} _ {1} + k (3 \theta_ {1} + \theta_ {3}) = 0\tag{7}
$$

$$
M \ddot {\theta} _ {3} + k (\theta_ {1} + 2 \theta_ {3} - \theta_ {4}) = 0\tag{8}
$$

$$
M \ddot {\theta} _ {4} + k (3 \theta_ {4} - \theta_ {3}) = 0\tag{9}
$$

式(7)×a+式(8)×b+式(9)×c,得

$$
M \left(a \ddot {\theta} _ {1} + b \ddot {\theta} _ {3} + c \ddot {\theta} _ {4}\right) + k [ (3 a + b) \theta_ {1} + (a + 2 b - c) \theta_ {3} + (- b + 3 c) \theta_ {4} ] = 0\tag{10}
$$

$a\theta_{1} + b\theta_{3} + c\theta_{4}$ 成为简正坐标. 要求 $a, b, c$ 满足下列方程组：

由此可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & 3 a + b = a \\ & a + 2 b - c = b \\ & - b + 3 c = c \\ & b = - 2 a, \quad c = \frac {1}{2} b \\ & b = - 2, \quad c = - 1 \end{array}
$$

取 $a = 1$ ，则

将 $a, b, c$ 值代入式(10)，得

$$
M \left(\ddot {\theta} _ {1} - 2 \dot {\theta} _ {3} - \ddot {\theta} _ {4}\right) + k \left(\theta_ {1} - 2 \theta_ {3} - \theta_ {4}\right) = 0
$$

所以

$$
\omega_ {5} = \sqrt {\frac {k}{M}}
$$

将 $\theta_{1} = u_{1}^{(5)}\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}\omega_{5}t},\theta_{3} = u_{3}^{(5)}\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}\omega_{5}t},\theta_{4} = u_{4}^{(5)}\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}\omega_{5}t}$ 代入式(7)、(8)、(9)，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} 2 u _ {1} ^ {(5)} + u _ {3} ^ {(5)} & = 0 \\ u _ {3} ^ {(5)} + u _ {1} ^ {(5)} - u _ {4} ^ {(5)} & = 0 \\ 2 u _ {4} ^ {(5)} - u _ {3} ^ {(5)} & = 0 \end{array}
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(5)} = 1$ ，则解得 $u_{3}^{(5)} = -2, u_{4}^{(5)} = -1$ ，再用 $\theta_{1} = -\theta_{2}, \theta_{3} = -\theta_{6}, \theta_{4} = -\theta_{5}$ ，得 $u_{2}^{(5)} = -1$ ， $u_{6}^{(5)} = 2, u_{5}^{(5)} = 1$ ；正是图10.50所画的模式图。

(3) 实际的苯分子和题目所给的苯模型类似, 但苯分子不存在固定的圆环, 没有受到圆环给予的作用线通过环心的力, 因而质心的加速度为零, 因此凡是振动模式中质心加速度为零的与实际的苯分子的振动模式有联系, 它们是简正频率为 $\omega_{1}, \omega_{3}$ 和 $\omega_{4}$ 的振动模式, 模式图分别是图 10.49(a)、(d)、(e) 图. 如果整体的转动也算一种模式, 它也是与苯分子有联系的.

10.1.46 在小振动理论中,常遇到如下形式的拉格朗日函数:

其中

$$
\begin{array}{c} L = T - V \\ T = \sum_ {i, j = 1} ^ {N} a _ {i j} \dot {q} _ {i} \dot {q} _ {j} \\ V = \sum_ {i, j = 1} ^ {N} b _ {i j} q _ {i} q _ {j} \end{array}
$$

矩阵 $A = (a_{ij})$ 和 $\pmb {B} = (b_{ij})$ 是实对称的.

（1）证明 $\pmb{A}$ 是正定的，即 $x^{T} \cdot A \cdot x \geqslant 0$ ，对任何的列矩阵均成立。证明一般这样一个矩阵的本征值大于或等于零，并说明我们不会涉及零本征值；

(2) 证明矩阵 $A^{\pm1/2}$ 是存在的；
