因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial \psi} = 0, \frac{\partial L}{\partial \dot{\psi}} =$ 常量，其物理意义是转子在其质心平动参考系中对其旋转对称轴的角动量守恒.

如(2)中选取的一组广义坐标是系统质心的坐标 x、y、z，粒子在系统质心平动参考系中的球坐标为 $r_{m}$ 、 $\theta_{m}$ 、 $\varphi_{m}$ ，以及转子在其质心平动参考系中的定点转动所用的三个欧拉角为 $\psi$ 、 $\varphi$ 、 $\theta$ ，再将 $r_{m}$ 改成粒子与转子质心间的距离 r，则系统的动能可写作

$$
\begin{array}{r l} T & = \frac {1}{2} (m + M) (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {m M}{m + M}\right) (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {m} ^ {2} \\ & + r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {m} \cdot \dot {\varphi} _ {m} ^ {2}) + T _ {3} \end{array}
$$

其中 $T_{3}$ 就是前面给出的 $T_{3}$ .

$V$ 的式子仍如前述的积分式子，它与 $r, \varphi, \theta$ 三个坐标有关.

除有上述两个运动积分以外，还因有 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial x} = \frac{\partial L}{\partial y} = \frac{\partial L}{\partial z} = 0, \frac{\partial L}{\partial \varphi_m} = 0$ ，有运动积分

$$
\dot {x} = \text { 常量 }, \qquad \dot {y} = \text { 常量 }, \quad \dot {z} = \text { 常量 }
$$

其物理意义是系统的动量守恒，

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi} _ {m}} = \text {常量}
$$

因为系统质心平动参考系中的三个坐标方向可以随意规定,因此 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{\varphi}_{m}}=$ 常量的物理意义是系在对其质心平动参考系中对质心的角动量守恒.

(5) 当粒子与转子相距较远,说得更确切些,当粒子与转子质心的距离比转子的线度大得多时,粒子和转子质心的轨道十分近似于两个点质量的轨道,它们之间有(小的)差别的原因在于转子的质心和引力中心不重合.反之,与此有关,粒子对转子各质元的引力对转子质心的力矩之和不等于零,这是转子相对于其质心有转动的原因.

11.1.16 地球以恒定的角速度 $\omega = \omega k$ 相对于惯性参考系运动， $k$ 是固连于地球的 $z$ 轴的单位矢量，用固连于地球的笛卡儿坐标 $x, y, z$ 为广义坐标，写出质量为 $m$ 的质点在势场 $V(x, y, z)$ 中运动的哈密顿函数，证明 $H$ 不是总能量，但是一个守恒量。

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {v} & = \dot {x} \boldsymbol {i} + \dot {y} \boldsymbol {j} + \dot {z} \boldsymbol {k} + \omega \boldsymbol {k} \times (x \boldsymbol {i} + y \boldsymbol {j} + z \boldsymbol {k}) = (\dot {x} - \omega y) \boldsymbol {i} + (\dot {y} + \omega x) \boldsymbol {j} + \dot {z} \boldsymbol {k} \\ T & = \frac {1}{2} m \boldsymbol {v} \cdot \boldsymbol {v} = \frac {1}{2} m [ (\dot {x} - \omega y) ^ {2} + (\dot {y} + \omega x) ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2} ] \\ & = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2} - 2 \omega \dot {x} y + 2 \omega x \dot {y} + \omega^ {2} x ^ {2} + \omega^ {2} y ^ {2}) \end{array}
$$

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2} - 2 \omega \dot {x} y + 2 \omega x \dot {y} + \omega^ {2} x ^ {2} + \omega^ {2} y ^ {2}) - V (x, y, z)
$$

$$
p _ {x} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = m \dot {x} - m \omega y
$$

$$
\dot {x} = \frac {1}{m} (p _ {x} + m \omega y)
$$

$$
p _ {y} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {y}} = m \dot {y} + m \omega x
$$

$$
\dot {y} = \frac {1}{m} (p _ {y} - m \omega x)
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & H = \frac {1}{m} p _ {x} (p _ {x} + m \omega y) + \frac {1}{m} p _ {y} (p _ {y} - m \omega x) + \frac {1}{m} p _ {z} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m \left[ \frac {1}{m ^ {2}} (p _ {x} + m \omega y) ^ {2} \right. \\ & \quad \left. + \frac {1}{m ^ {2}} (p _ {y} - m \omega x) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{m ^ {2}} p _ {z} ^ {2} - 2 \omega y \cdot \frac {1}{m} (p _ {x} + m \omega y) + 2 \omega x \cdot \frac {1}{m} (p _ {y} - m \omega x) \right. \\ & \quad \left. + \omega^ {2} x ^ {2} + \omega^ {2} y ^ {2} \right] + V (x, y, z) \\ & = \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {x} ^ {2} + p _ {y} ^ {2} + p _ {z} ^ {2}) + p _ {x} \omega y - p _ {y} \omega x + V (x, y, z) \end{array}
$$

因为 $T \neq T_{2}$ (不是广义速度的二次齐次函数), $H$ 不是总能量. 又因为 $\frac{\partial H}{\partial t} = 0$ , 所以 $H$ 是守恒量.

11.1.17 质量为 $m$ 的质点作一维运动的拉格朗日函数 $L = \frac{1}{2} m e^{\gamma t} (\dot{x}^2 - \omega^2 x^2)$ , 其中 $m, \gamma$ 和 $\omega$ 都是正实数.

(1) 求运动微分方程, 根据作用在质点上的力的类型对运动微分方程作些说明;

(2) 求广义动量, 由此构造哈密顿函数;

(3) 该哈密顿函数是否是运动常数？机械能是否守恒？

(4) 若初始条件为 $x(0) = 0, \dot{x}(0) = v_0$ ，问当 $t \to \infty$ 时， $x(t)$ 渐近地等于什么？

解 (1)

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} (\dot {x} ^ {2} - \omega^ {2} x ^ {2})
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = m \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} \dot {x}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}}\right) = m \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} \ddot {x} + m \gamma \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} \dot {x}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial x} = - m \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} \omega^ {2} x
$$

运动微分方程为

或

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & m \ddot {x} + m \gamma \dot {x} + m \omega^ {2} x = 0 \\ & m \ddot {x} = - m \gamma \dot {x} - m \omega^ {2} x \end{array}
$$

质点受两个力:一个是 $-m\gamma\dot{x}$ 为与速率成正比的阻力;一个是弹性力,是与偏离平衡位置的位移成正比的恢复力,微分方程是有阻尼的振动所遵循的方程.

(2) 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & p = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = m e ^ {\gamma_ {t}} \dot {x}, \quad \dot {x} = \frac {1}{m} e ^ {- \gamma_ {t}} p \\ H = p \dot {x} - L \\ & = p \cdot \frac {1}{m} e ^ {- \gamma_ {t}} p - \frac {1}{2} m e ^ {\gamma_ {t}} \left[ \left(\frac {1}{m} e ^ {- \gamma_ {t}} p\right) ^ {2} - \omega^ {2} x ^ {2} \right] \\ & = \frac {1}{2 m} e ^ {- \gamma_ {t}} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m e ^ {\gamma_ {t}} \omega^ {2} x ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

(3) $\frac{\partial H}{\partial t} \neq 0, H$ 不是运动常数, 机械能也不守恒. 弹性力是保守力, 可引入势能, 但阻力是非保守力, 且总做负功, 机械能是不断减少的.

(4) 

$$
\ddot {x} + \gamma \dot {x} + \omega^ {2} x = 0
$$

特征方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & r ^ {2} + \gamma r + \omega^ {2} = 0 \\ & \gamma = \frac {1}{2} (- \gamma \pm \sqrt {\gamma^ {2} - 4 \omega^ {2}}) \end{array}
$$

如 $\gamma^2 < 4\omega^2$ ，即 $\frac{\gamma}{2} < \omega$ 时，通解为

其中

$$
\begin{array}{r l} x & = A \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {1}{2} \gamma_ {t}} \cos (\omega_ {\gamma} t + \alpha) \\ & \omega_ {r} = \sqrt {\omega^ {2} - \frac {1}{4} \gamma^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

如 $\gamma^2 > 4\omega^2$ ，即 $\frac{\gamma}{2} > \omega$ 时，通解为

$$
x = C _ {1} \mathrm{e} ^ {\frac {1}{2} \left(- \gamma + \sqrt {\gamma^ {2} - 4 \omega^ {2}}\right) t} + C _ {2} \mathrm{e} ^ {\frac {1}{2} \left(- \gamma - \sqrt {\gamma^ {2} - 4 \omega^ {2}}\right) t}
$$

如 $\gamma^2 = 4\omega^2$ ，即 $\frac{\gamma}{2} = \omega$ 时，通解为

$$
x = (B _ {1} + B _ {2} t) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {1}{2} \gamma_ {t}}
$$

不论 $\frac{\gamma}{2}$ 与 $\omega$ 的大小关系是上述哪种情况，也不论初始条件如何， $t \to \infty$ 时，均有 $x \to 0$ . 11.1.18 一个自由度数为 1 的系统的哈密顿函数为 $H = \frac{p^2}{2\alpha} - bqpe^{-\alpha} + \frac{1}{2} bae^{-\alpha} (\alpha + be^{-\alpha}) + \frac{1}{2} kq^2$ ，式中 $a, b, \alpha$ 和 $k$ 都是常量，求此系统的拉格朗日函数.

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} H & = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 \alpha} - b q p e ^ {- \alpha t} + \frac {1}{2} b a e ^ {- \alpha t} (\alpha + b e ^ {- \alpha t}) + \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2} \\ & \dot {q} = \frac {\partial H}{\partial p} = \frac {p}{\alpha} - b q e ^ {- \alpha t} \\ & p = \alpha (\dot {q} + b q e ^ {- \alpha t}) \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & H = p \dot {q} - L \\ & L = p \dot {q} - H = \alpha (\dot {q} + b q e ^ {- \alpha t}) \dot {q} - \frac {1}{2 \alpha} [ \alpha (\dot {q} + b q e ^ {- \alpha t}) ] ^ {2} \\ & \quad + b q \alpha (\dot {q} + b q e ^ {- \alpha t}) e ^ {- \alpha t} - \frac {1}{2} b a e ^ {- \alpha t} (\alpha + b e ^ {- \alpha t}) - \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2} \\ & = \frac {1}{2} \alpha (\dot {q} + b q e ^ {- \alpha t}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} b a e ^ {- \alpha t} (\alpha + b e ^ {- \alpha t}) - \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

11.1.19 两个惰性气体原子,每一个质量都是 m,它们之间的经典相互作用势能为

$$
V (r) = - \frac {2 A}{r ^ {6}} + \frac {B}{r ^ {1 2}}
$$

$A, B > 0$ ，且为常量， $r$ 为两原子间的距离.

(1) 写出这两个原子构成的系统的哈密顿函数(取适当的广义坐标);

(2) 求出这个系统的能量最低的经典态；

(3) 若能量稍高于(2)所求的最低能态, 求这个系统的振动频率.

解（1）取系统的质心坐标 $x, y, z$ 和一个原子相对于另一个原子的球坐标 $r, \theta, \varphi$ 为广义坐标.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {L = \frac {1}{2} \times 2 m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + 2 \times \frac {1}{2} m \Big [ \Big (\frac {\dot {r}}{2} \Big) ^ {2} + \Big (\frac {r}{2} \Big) ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \Big (\frac {r}{2} \Big) ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \Big ] + \frac {2 A}{r ^ {6}} - \frac {B}{r ^ {1 2}}} \\ & {\quad = m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{4} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) + \frac {2 A}{r ^ {6}} - \frac {B}{r ^ {1 2}}} \\ & {\qquad p _ {x} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = 2 m \dot {x}} \\ & {\qquad p _ {y} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {y}} = 2 m \dot {y}} \\ & {\qquad p _ {z} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {z}} = 2 m \dot {z}} \\ & {\qquad p _ {r} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {r}} = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {r}} \\ & {\qquad p _ {\theta} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = \frac {1}{2} m r ^ {2} \dot {\theta}} \\ & {\qquad p _ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = \frac {1}{2} m r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi}} \\ & {\qquad H = p _ {x} \dot {x} + p _ {y} \dot {y} + p _ {z} \dot {z} + p _ {r} \dot {r} + p _ {\theta} \dot {\theta} + p _ {\varphi} \dot {\varphi} - L} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {1}{4 m} (p _ {x} ^ {2} + p _ {y} ^ {2} + p _ {z} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{m} \Big (p _ {r} ^ {2} + \frac {p _ {\theta} ^ {2}}{r ^ {2}} + \frac {p _ {\varphi} ^ {2}}{m r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta} \Big) - \frac {2 A}{r ^ {6}} + \frac {B}{r ^ {1 2}}} \end{array}
$$

(2) 最低的经典态, T=0, V 取最小值,

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} r} \left(- \frac {2 A}{r ^ {6}} + \frac {B}{r ^ {1 2}}\right) = 0
$$

$$
\frac {1 2 A}{r ^ {7}} - \frac {1 2 B}{r ^ {1 3}} = 0
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & r = \left(\frac {B}{A}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{6}} \\ & E _ {\min} = V _ {\min} = \left(- \frac {2 A}{r ^ {6}} + \frac {B}{r ^ {1 2}}\right) \Big | _ {r = \left(\frac {B}{A}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{6}}} = - \frac {A ^ {2}}{B} \end{array}
$$

(3) 将 $V(r)$ 在 $r = r_0 = \left(\frac{B}{A}\right)^{\frac{1}{6}}$ 处作泰勒展开，或令 $r' = r - r_0$ ，在 $r' = 0$ 处作泰勒展开，只保留二级小量，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} V (r ^ {\prime}) & \approx V (r _ {0}) + \frac {1}{2} \times \left. \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} V}{\mathrm{d} r ^ {2}} \right| _ {r = r _ {0}} r ^ {\prime 2} \\ & = - \frac {A ^ {2}}{B} + \frac {1}{2} \left(- 8 4 \frac {A}{r ^ {8}} + 1 5 6 \frac {B}{r ^ {1 4}}\right) \Bigg | _ {r = \left(\frac {B}{A}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{6}}} r ^ {\prime 2} \\ & = - \frac {A ^ {2}}{B} + 3 6 \frac {A ^ {7 / 3}}{B ^ {4 / 3}} r ^ {\prime 2} \end{array}
$$

在最低能态附近，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L = & m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{4} m \left[ \dot {r} ^ {\prime 2} + (r _ {0} + r ^ {\prime}) ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \right. \\ & \left. + (r _ {0} + r ^ {\prime}) ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \right] - 3 6 \frac {A ^ {7 / 3}}{B ^ {4 / 3}} r ^ {\prime 2} + \frac {A ^ {2}}{B} \end{array}
$$

注意： $r^{\prime}$ 、 $\dot{r}^{\prime}$ 、 $\ddot{r}^{\prime}$ 、 $\dot{\theta}$ 、 $\dot{\varphi}$ 、 $\ddot{\theta}$ 、 $\ddot{\varphi}$ 均为小量，由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{q}_{i}}\right)-\frac{\partial L}{\partial q_{i}}=0$ 得运动微分方程，只保留微分方程中的一级小量，可见只有关于 $r^{\prime}$ 的微分方程是振动的方程。可得

$$
\frac {1}{2} m \ddot {r} ^ {\prime} + 7 2 \frac {A ^ {7 / 3}}{B ^ {4 / 3}} r ^ {\prime} = 0
$$

在最低能态附近做小振动的角频率为

$$
\omega = \sqrt {7 2 \left. \frac {A ^ {7 / 3}}{B ^ {4 / 3}} \right| \frac {1}{2} m} = 1 2 m ^ {- 1 / 2} A ^ {7 / 6} B ^ {- 2 / 3}
$$

11.1.20 一个质量为 $m$ 、带电荷量为 $-e$ 的非相对论电子在圆柱形磁控管内运动，磁控管由一个半径为 $a$ 、电势为 $-\phi_0 (\phi_0 > 0$ ，为常量)的导线和电势为零的半径为 $R$ 的同轴圆柱面组成。磁控管内有一平行其轴的匀强磁场 $B$ ，选用柱坐标 $r, \varphi, z$ 为广义坐标，标势 $\phi = -\phi_0 \ln \left( \frac{r}{R} \right) / \ln \left( \frac{a}{R} \right)$ ，矢势 $A = \frac{1}{2} B r e_\varphi$ （其中 $e_\varphi$ 是沿 $\varphi$ 增大方向的单位矢量）。

(1) 写出哈密顿函数；

(2) 证明存在三个运动常数, 并讨论可能出现的运动类型;

(3) 假定某电子以等于零的初始速度离开里面的那根导线, 则存在某个磁场值 $B_{c}$ , 当 $B \leqslant B_{c}$ 时, 该电子能到达外面的圆柱面, 当 $B > B_{c}$ 时, 它不能到达外面的圆柱面, 求出 $B_{c}$ 的值.

解 (1)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\boldsymbol {A} = \frac {1}{2} B r \boldsymbol {e} _ {\varphi}} \\ & {V = (- e) \phi - (- e) \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} \cdot \boldsymbol {A}} \\ & {\quad = e \phi_ {0} \frac {\ln (r / R)}{\ln (a / R)} + e (\dot {r} \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} + r \dot {\varphi} \boldsymbol {e} _ {\varphi} + \dot {z} \boldsymbol {k}) \cdot \frac {1}{2} B r \boldsymbol {e} _ {\varphi}} \end{array}
$$

$$
= e \phi_ {0} \frac {\ln (r / R)}{\ln (a / R)} + \frac {1}{2} e B r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi}
$$

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m \left(\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}\right) - e \phi_ {0} \frac {\ln (r / R)}{\ln (a / R)} - \frac {1}{2} e B r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi}
$$

$$
p _ {r} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {r}} = m \dot {r}
$$

$$
p _ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} - \frac {1}{2} e B r ^ {2}
$$

$$
p _ {z} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {z}} = m \dot {z}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & H = p _ {r} \dot {r} + p _ {\varphi} \dot {\varphi} + p _ {z} \dot {z} - L \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{2 m} \left[ p _ {r} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} \left(p _ {\varphi} + \frac {1}{2} e B r ^ {2}\right) ^ {2} + p _ {z} ^ {2} \right] + e \phi_ {0} \frac {\ln (r / R)}{\ln (a / R)} \end{array}
$$

(2) 

$$
\frac {\partial H}{\partial \varphi} = 0, p _ {\varphi} = m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} - \frac {1}{2} e B r ^ {2} = \text {常量}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial H}{\partial z} = 0, p _ {z} = m \dot {z} = \text {常量}, \dot {z} = \text {常量}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial H}{\partial t} = 0, H = \text {常量}
$$

即

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {1}{2 m} \Big [ p _ {r} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} \Big (p _ {\varphi} + \frac {1}{2} e B r ^ {2} \Big) ^ {2} + p _ {z} ^ {2} \Big ] + e \phi_ {0} \frac {\ln (r / R)}{\ln (a / R)} = \text {常量}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + e \phi_ {0} \frac {\ln (r / R)}{\ln (a / R)} = \text {常量}} \end{array}
$$

可能的运动类型有：

(i) 在 z 方向做匀速直线运动 (r=常量, $\dot{\varphi}=0$ );

(ii) 在 $z$ 保持不变的平面上做以 $z$ 轴为圆心的匀速率圆周运动 ( $z = \text{常量}, r = \text{常量}, \dot{\varphi} = \text{常量}$ );

(iii) 在 z 保持不变的平面上做螺旋运动 (z=常量, r 增大时, $\dot{\varphi}$ 减小);

(iv) (i)、(ii)两种运动的叠加运动；

(·) (i)、(iii)两种运动的叠加运动.

(3) 用初始条件: t=0 时, r=a, $\dot{r}=0$ , $\dot{\varphi}=0$ , $\dot{z}=0$ . 确定三个运动常数:

$$
\dot {z} = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} - \frac {1}{2} e B r ^ {2} = - \frac {1}{2} e B a ^ {2}\tag{2}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} m \left(\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}\right) + e \phi_ {0} \frac {\ln (r / R)}{\ln (a / R)} = e \phi_ {0}\tag{3}
$$

将式(1)、(2)代入式(3). 得

$$
\frac {1}{2} m \left\{\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \left[ \frac {\frac {1}{2} e B (r ^ {2} - a ^ {2})}{m r ^ {2}} \right] ^ {2} \right\} + e \phi_ {0} \frac {\ln (r / R)}{\ln (a / R)} = e \phi_ {0}\tag{4}
$$

将 $B = B_{c}, r = R$ 时， $\dot{r} = 0$ 代入上式，

$$
\frac {1}{2} m R ^ {2} \frac {1}{m ^ {2} R ^ {4}} \frac {1}{4} e ^ {2} B _ {c} ^ {2} (R ^ {2} - a ^ {2}) ^ {2} = e \phi_ {0}
$$

解出

$$
B _ {c} = \frac {2 R}{R ^ {2} - a ^ {2}} \sqrt {\frac {2 m \phi_ {0}}{e}}
$$

由式(4)可见, 当 $B < B_{c}$ 时, 在 $r = R$ 处, $\dot{r}^{2} > 0$ ; 在 $r < R$ 时, $\dot{r} > 0$ . 随着 $r$ 增大, $\dot{r}^{2}$ 减小. 可见, 在 $r = R$ 时, $\dot{r} > 0$ , 说明电子能到达外面的圆柱面.

当 $B > B_{c}$ 时，在 $r = R$ 处，由式(4)得 $\dot{r}^2 < 0$ ，这是不可能的，说明电子不可能到达 $r = R$ 处，在 $r < R$ 的某处，就变成 $\dot{r} = 0$ 了.

作出上述结论时,用了 $\frac{(r^{2}-a^{2})^{2}}{r^{2}}$ 是r的单调递增函数,因为

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} r} \left(\frac {r ^ {2} - a ^ {2}}{r}\right) = \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} r} \left(r - \frac {a ^ {2}}{r}\right) = 1 + \frac {a ^ {2}}{r ^ {2}} > 0
$$

11.1.21 对于处在重力场中的对称陀螺, 使用劳斯方程获得非循环坐标满足的运动微分方程, 已知质心离固定点的距离为 $h$ , 对固定点的三个主转动惯量为 $I_{1}, I_{1}, I_{3}$ .

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} (I _ {1} \omega_ {x} ^ {2} + I _ {1} \omega_ {y} ^ {2} + I _ {3} \omega_ {z} ^ {2}) \\ & \omega_ {x} = \dot {\varphi} \sin \theta \sin \psi + \dot {\theta} \cos \psi \\ & \omega_ {y} = \dot {\varphi} \sin \theta \cos \psi - \dot {\theta} \sin \psi \\ & \omega_ {z} = \dot {\varphi} \cos \theta + \dot {\psi} \\ & T = \frac {1}{2} I _ {1} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {1} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + \frac {1}{2} I _ {3} (\dot {\varphi} \cos \theta + \dot {\psi}) ^ {2} \\ & V = m g h \cos \theta \\ & L = \frac {1}{2} I _ {1} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {1} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + \frac {1}{2} I _ {3} (\dot {\varphi} \cos \theta + \dot {\psi}) ^ {2} - m g h \cos \theta \\ & \frac {\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \psi} = 0 \end{array}
$$

$\varphi,\psi$ 均为循环坐标,选取劳斯函数 R 为

$$
R = p _ {\varphi} \dot {\varphi} + p _ {\psi} \dot {\psi} - L
$$

$$
p _ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = I _ {1} \dot {\varphi} \sin^ {2} \theta + I _ {3} \cos \theta (\dot {\varphi} \cos \theta + \dot {\psi})\tag{1}
$$

$$
p _ {\psi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\psi}} = I _ {3} (\dot {\varphi} \cos \theta + \dot {\psi})\tag{2}
$$

由上述两式 $\dot{\varphi},\dot{\psi}$ ，得

$$
\dot {\varphi} = \frac {1}{I _ {1} \sin^ {2} \theta} (p _ {\varphi} - p _ {\psi} \cos \theta)
$$

$$
\dot {\psi} = \frac {p _ {\psi}}{I _ {3}} - \frac {\cos \theta}{I _ {1} \sin^ {2} \theta} (p _ {\varphi} - p _ {\psi} \cos \theta)
$$

$$
R = - \frac {1}{2} I _ {1} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 I _ {1} \sin^ {2} \theta} (p _ {\varphi} - p _ {\psi} \cos \theta) ^ {2} + \frac {p _ {\psi} ^ {2}}{2 I _ {3}} + m g h \cos \theta
$$

劳斯方程为

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial R}{\partial \dot {\theta}}\right) - \frac {\partial R}{\partial \theta} = 0\tag{3}
$$

$$
\dot {\varphi} = \frac {\partial R}{\partial p _ {\varphi}}, \quad \dot {\psi} = \frac {\partial R}{\partial p _ {\psi}}\tag{4}
$$

$$
\dot {p} _ {\varphi} = - \frac {\partial R}{\partial \varphi}, \quad \dot {p} _ {\psi} = - \frac {\partial R}{\partial \psi}\tag{5}
$$

代入劳斯函数,式(3)可表示为

$$
- I _ {1} \ddot {\theta} + \frac {(p _ {\varphi} - p _ {\psi} \cos \theta) ^ {2} \cos \theta}{I _ {1} \sin^ {3} \theta} - \frac {p _ {\psi} (p _ {\varphi} - p _ {\psi} \cos \theta)}{I _ {1} \sin \theta} + m g h \sin \theta = 0
$$

由式(1)、(2)、(5)得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} p _ {\varphi} & = I _ {1} \dot {\varphi} \sin^ {2} \theta + I _ {3} \cos \theta (\dot {\varphi} \cos \theta + \dot {\psi}) = C _ {1} \\ & p _ {\psi} = I _ {3} (\dot {\varphi} \cos \theta + \dot {\psi}) = C _ {2} \end{array}
$$

用上述两个运动积分,非循环坐标 $\theta$ 满足的运动微分方程可改写为

$$
\ddot {\theta} = \frac {m g h \sin \theta}{I _ {1}} + \frac {(\alpha - \beta \cos \theta) ^ {2} \cos \theta}{\sin^ {3} \theta} - \frac {\beta (\alpha - \beta \cos \theta)}{\sin \theta}
$$

其中 $\alpha = \frac{C_1}{I_1},\beta = \frac{C_2}{I_1},C_1,C_2$ 由初始条件确定.

11.1.22 一个质量为 $m$ 的质点在重力作用下，在 $z = x^{2} + y^{2}$ 面内滑动， $z$ 轴竖直向上，采用柱坐标，用劳斯方程推导出非循环坐标满足的运动微分方程。

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {\rho} ^ {2} + \rho^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) - m g z \\ & \quad z = \rho^ {2}, \qquad \dot {z} = 2 \rho \dot {\rho} \\ & L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {\rho} ^ {2} + \rho^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + 4 \rho^ {2} \dot {\rho} ^ {2}) - m g \rho^ {2} \end{array}
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0, \varphi$ 为循环坐标，

$$
p _ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = m \rho^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = m C (C \text {为常量})
$$

$$
R = p _ {\varphi} \dot {\varphi} - L = \frac {1}{2 m \rho^ {2}} p _ {\varphi} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m (1 + 4 \rho^ {2}) \dot {\rho} ^ {2} + m g \rho^ {2}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial R}{\partial \dot {\rho}} = - m (1 + 4 \rho^ {2}) \dot {\rho}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial R}{\partial \dot {\rho}}\right) = - m (1 + 4 \rho^ {2}) \ddot {\rho} - 8 m \rho \dot {\rho} ^ {2}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial R}{\partial \rho} = - \frac {1}{m \rho^ {3}} p _ {\varphi} ^ {2} - 4 m \rho \dot {\rho} ^ {2} + 2 m g \rho
$$

代入 $p_{\varphi} = mC$ 

$$
\frac {\partial R}{\partial \rho} = - m C ^ {2} \rho^ {- 3} - 4 m \rho \dot {\rho} ^ {2} + 2 m g \rho
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial R}{\partial\dot{\rho}}\right) - \frac{\partial R}{\partial\rho} = 0$ ，可得

$$
(1 + 4 \rho^ {2}) \ddot {\rho} + 4 \rho \dot {\rho} ^ {2} - C ^ {2} \rho^ {- 3} + 2 g \rho = 0
$$

其中 $C=\rho^{2}\dot{\varphi}$ 为常量，由初始条件确定.

注意:不能直接用循环积分 $m\rho^{2}\dot{\varphi}=mC$ 消去拉格朗日函数中的循环坐标,再用拉格朗日方程得到非循环坐标满足的运动微分方程.原因是循环积分是一个非完整约束,不减少系统的自由度数.在这里,用 $m\rho^{2}\dot{\varphi}=mC$ 消去拉格朗日函数中的 $\dot{\varphi}$ ,将使 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial \rho}$ 的计算出现错误.

11.1.23 一个力学系统具有动能 $T = \frac{1}{2}\left(\dot{q}_1^2 +\frac{\dot{q}_2^2}{a + bq_1^2}\right)$ ，势能 $V = \frac{1}{2} (k_1q_1^2 +k_2)$ ，式中 $a,b,k_{1}$ 和 $k_{2}$ 均为常量，写出劳斯函数和劳斯方程。

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} \left(\dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {\dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2}}{a + b q _ {1} ^ {2}}\right) \\ & V = \frac {1}{2} (k _ {1} q _ {1} ^ {2} + k _ {2}) \\ & L = \frac {1}{2} \left(\dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {\dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2}}{a + b q _ {1} ^ {2}}\right) - \frac {1}{2} (k _ {1} q _ {1} ^ {2} + k _ {2}) \\ & \dot {p} _ {2} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {2}} = \frac {\dot {q} _ {2}}{a + b q _ {1} ^ {2}} \\ & \dot {q} _ {2} = (a + b q _ {1} ^ {2}) p _ {2} \\ & R = p _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} - L = \frac {1}{2} (a + b q _ {1} ^ {2}) p _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (k _ {1} q _ {1} ^ {2} + k _ {2}) \end{array}
$$

劳斯方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \dot {q} _ {2} & = \frac {\partial R}{\partial p _ {2}} = (a + b q _ {1} ^ {2}) p _ {2} \\ \dot {p} _ {2} & = - \frac {\partial R}{\partial q _ {2}} = 0 \\ \ddot {q} _ {1} + (p _ {2} ^ {2} b + k _ {1}) q _ {1} & = 0 \end{array}
$$

其中 $p_2 = \frac{\dot{q}_2}{a + bq_1^2} = C$ 为常量，由初始条件确定.

11.1.24 一个质量为 $m$ 的质点在势能 $V(r)$ 表示的有心力 $f(r)$ 作用下做圆周运动。采用极坐标，用劳斯方程讨论质点做圆周运动的稳定性条件。

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & L = \frac {1}{3} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) - V (r) \\ & p _ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \\ & R = p _ {\varphi} \dot {\varphi} - L = \frac {1}{2 m r ^ {2}} p _ {\varphi} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m \dot {r} ^ {2} + V (r) \end{array}
$$

因为

$$
\frac {\partial R}{\partial \varphi} = 0, \quad p _ {\varphi} = C (\text {常量})
$$

$$
R = \frac {C ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}} - \frac {1}{2} m \dot {r} ^ {2} + V (r)
$$

稳态运动条件为

$$
\left(\frac {\partial R}{\partial r}\right) \Bigg | _ {r = r _ {0}} = 0
$$

即

$$
\left(\frac {\mathrm{d} V}{\mathrm{d} r}\right) \Bigg | _ {r = r _ {0}} - \frac {C ^ {2}}{m r _ {0} ^ {2}} = 0
$$

将劳斯函数在稳态运动，在 $r=r_{0}$ 处作泰勒展开，令 $\rho=r-r_{0}$ ，略去对微分方程毫无影响的常数项，只保留二级小量项，得

$$
R = - \frac {1}{2} m \dot {\rho} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {3 C ^ {2}}{m r _ {0} ^ {2}} + \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} V}{\mathrm{d} r ^ {2}} \Big | _ {r = r _ {0}}\right) \rho^ {2} = 0
$$

得到上式的过程中用了稳态运动条件.

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial R}{\partial\rho}\right) - \frac{\partial R}{\partial\rho} = 0$ ，得

$$
\ddot {\rho} + \frac {1}{m} \left(\frac {3 C ^ {2}}{m r _ {0} ^ {4}} + \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} V}{\mathrm{d} r ^ {2}} \Big | _ {r = r _ {0}}\right) \rho = 0
$$

稳态运动稳定性条件为

$$
\left. \frac {3 C ^ {2}}{m r _ {0} ^ {4}} + \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} V}{\mathrm{d} r ^ {2}} \right| _ {r = r _ {0}} > 0
$$

如把 C 改写为 mh，稳定性条件也可改写为

$$
\left. \frac {3 m h ^ {2}}{r _ {0} ^ {4}} - \frac {\mathrm{d} f (r)}{\mathrm{d} r} \right| _ {r = r _ {0}} > 0
$$

## 11.2 泊松括号和泊松定理

11.2.1 (1) 若哈密顿函数 $H$ 和某正则变量和时间的函数 $F$ 都是运动常数, 证明 $\frac{\partial F}{\partial t}$ 也是一个运动常数;

(2) 一个自由粒子的一维运动, 哈密顿函数 $H$ 是运动常数, 直接计算说明 $F = x - \frac{p}{m} t$ 是运动常数, $\frac{\partial F}{\partial t}$ 也是运动常数.

证明 (1) $H$ 是运动常数，

$$
H = C
$$

$F$ 是运动常数，则

$$
\frac {\partial F}{\partial t} + [ F, H ] = 0
$$

上式两边对 $t$ 求偏导，

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} F}{\partial t ^ {2}} + \frac {\partial}{\partial t} [ F, H ] = \frac {\partial^ {2} F}{\partial t ^ {2}} + \left[ \frac {\partial F}{\partial t}, H \right] + \left[ F, \frac {\partial H}{\partial t} \right] = 0
$$

因为

$$
\frac {\partial H}{\partial t} = 0
$$

故

$$
\left[ F, \frac {\partial H}{\partial t} \right] = 0
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial t} \left(\frac {\partial F}{\partial t}\right) + \left[ \frac {\partial F}{\partial t}, H \right] = 0
$$

所以

$$
\frac {\partial F}{\partial t} = \text {常量}
$$

(2) 自由粒子做一维运动, 粒子质量为 m,

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2}
$$

$$
F = x - \frac {p}{m} t
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\partial F}{\partial t} + [ F, H ] & = - \frac {p}{m} + \left[ x - \frac {p}{m} t, \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} \right] \\ & = - \frac {p}{m} + \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \left(x - \frac {p}{m} t\right) \frac {\partial}{\partial p} \left(\frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2}\right) - \frac {\partial}{\partial p} \left(x - \frac {p}{m} t\right) \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \left(\frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2}\right) \\ & = - \frac {p}{m} + 1 \cdot \frac {p}{m} - \left(- \frac {1}{m} t\right) \cdot 0 = 0 \end{array}
$$

所以 $F = x - \frac{p}{m} t$ 为运动常数，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial F}{\partial t} = - \frac {p}{m} \\ \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \left(\frac {\partial F}{\partial t}\right) + \left[ \frac {\partial F}{\partial t}, H \right] \\ = \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \left(- \frac {p}{m}\right) + \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \left(- \frac {p}{m}\right) \frac {\partial}{\partial p} \left(\frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2}\right) - \frac {\partial}{\partial p} \left(- \frac {p}{m}\right) \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \left(\frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2}\right) \\ = 0 \end{array}
$$

所以 $\frac{\partial F}{\partial t}\left(= -\frac{p}{m}\right)$ 是运动常数.

11.2.2 如正则方程的解已经获得, $p_{i},q_{i}(i=1,2,\cdots,s)$ 均为 t 和 2s 个积分常数 $C_{1}$ 、 $C_{2}$ 、 $\cdots$ 、 $C_{2s}$ 的函数,则拉格朗日括号

$$
(C _ {\alpha}, C _ {\beta}) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} \left(\frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} \frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}} - \frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}} \frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}}\right)
$$

也是运动常数.

证明 已获得正则方程的解，

$$
\begin{array}{l} q _ {\alpha} = q _ {\alpha} (t, C _ {1}, C _ {2}, \dots , C _ {2 s}) \\ p _ {\alpha} = p _ {\alpha} (t, C _ {1}, C _ {2}, \dots , C _ {2 s}) \end{array}
$$

$$
\dot {q} _ {\alpha} = \frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {\alpha}}, \quad \dot {p} _ {\alpha} = - \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {\alpha}}
$$

则 $\dot{p}_{\alpha},\dot{q}_{\alpha}$ 以及 $H(p_{\alpha},q_{\alpha},t)$ 都是 $t,C_{1},C_{2},\cdots,C_{2s}$ 的已知函数，且 $q_{\alpha},p_{\alpha}$ 满足正则方程

$$
\begin{array}{r l}\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (C _ {\alpha}, C _ {\beta})&= \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} \left[ \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}}\right) \frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}} + \frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}}\right) - \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}}\right) \frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} - \frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}} \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}}\right) \right]\\&= \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} \left[ \frac {\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} \frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}} + \frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} \frac {\partial \dot {p} _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}} - \frac {\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}} \frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} - \frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}} \frac {\partial \dot {p} _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} \right]\\&= \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} \left[ \frac {\partial}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} \left(\frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {i}}\right) \frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}} + \frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} \frac {\partial}{\partial C _ {\beta}} \left(- \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial}{\partial C _ {\beta}} \left(\frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {i}}\right) \frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} - \frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}} \cdot \frac {\partial}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} \left(- \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {i}}\right) \right]\\&= \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} \left[ \right. \frac {\partial}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} \left(\frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {i}} \frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}}\right) - \frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {i}} \frac {\partial^ {2} p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha} \partial C _ {\beta}} - \frac {\partial}{\partial C _ {\beta}} \left(\frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {i}} \frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}}\right) + \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {i}} \frac {\partial^ {2} q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta} \partial C _ {\alpha}} - \frac {\partial}{\partial C _ {\beta}} \left(\frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {i}} \frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}}\right) + \frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {i}} \frac {\partial^ {2} p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta} \partial C _ {\alpha}} + \frac {\partial}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} \left(\frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {i}} \frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}}\right) - \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {i}} \frac {\partial^ {2} q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha} \partial C _ {\beta}}\\&= \frac {\partial}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} \left[ \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} \left(\frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {i}} \frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}} + \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {i}} \frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\beta}}\right) \right] - \frac {\partial}{\partial C _ {\beta}} \left[ \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} \left(\frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {i}} \frac {\partial q _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} + \frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {i}} \frac {\partial p _ {i}}{\partial C _ {\alpha}}\right) \right]\\&= \frac {\partial}{\partial C _ {\alpha}} \left(\frac {\partial H}{\partial C _ {\beta}}\right) - \frac {\partial}{\partial C _ {\beta}} \left(\frac {\partial H}{\partial C _ {\alpha}}\right)\\&= \frac {\partial^ {2} H}{\partial C _ {\alpha} \partial C _ {\beta}} - \frac {\partial^ {2} H}{\partial C _ {\beta} \partial C _ {\alpha}} = 0.\end{array}
$$

所以 $(C_{\alpha}, C_{\beta}) =$ 常量.

11.2.3 若 $\varphi$ 是坐标和动量的任意标量函数, 试证 $[\varphi, J_{z}] = 0$ .

证明 $\varphi$ 是坐标和动量的任意标量函数，意味着 $\varphi$ 是宗量 $r^2, p^2, r \cdot p$ 的任意函数，

$$
\begin{array}{c} \varphi = \varphi (r ^ {2}, p ^ {2}, r \cdot p) \\ J _ {x} = x p _ {y} - y p _ {x} \\ [ \varphi , J _ {z} ] = \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial x} \frac {\partial J _ {z}}{\partial p _ {x}} - \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial p _ {x}} \frac {\partial J _ {z}}{\partial x} + \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial y} \frac {\partial J _ {z}}{\partial p _ {y}} - \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial p _ {y}} \frac {\partial J _ {z}}{\partial y} + \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial z} \frac {\partial J _ {z}}{\partial p _ {z}} - \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial p _ {z}} \frac {\partial J _ {z}}{\partial z} \\ \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial x} = \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial r ^ {2}} \frac {\partial r ^ {2}}{\partial x} + \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {p})} \cdot \frac {\partial (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {p})}{\partial x} = 2 x \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial r ^ {2}} + p _ {x} \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {p})} \\ \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial y} = 2 y \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial r ^ {2}} + p _ {y} \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {p})} \\ \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial p _ {x}} = \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial p ^ {2}} \frac {\partial p ^ {2}}{\partial p _ {x}} + \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {p})} \frac {\partial (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {p})}{\partial p _ {x}} = 2 p _ {x} \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial p ^ {2}} + x \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {p})} \\ \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial p _ {y}} = 2 p _ {y} \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial p ^ {2}} + y \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {p})} \\ \frac {\partial J _ {z}}{\partial p _ {x}} = - y, \quad \frac {\partial J _ {z}}{\partial p _ {y}} = x, \quad \frac {\partial J _ {z}}{\partial p _ {z}} = 0 \\ \frac {\partial J _ {z}}{\partial x} = p _ {y}, \quad \frac {\partial J _ {z}}{\partial y} = - p _ {x}, \quad \frac {\partial J _ {z}}{\partial z} = 0 \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \lambda [ \varphi , J _ {z} ] & = \left(2 x \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial r ^ {2}} + p _ {x} \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {p})}\right) (- y) \\ & - \left(2 p _ {x} \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial p ^ {2}} + x \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {p})}\right) p _ {y} + \left(2 y \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial r ^ {2}} + p _ {y} \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {p})}\right) x \\ & - \left(2 p _ {y} \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial p ^ {2}} + y \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {p})}\right) (- p _ {x}) = 0 \end{array}
$$

11.2.4 试用泊松定理证明系统的角动量和动量的笛卡儿坐标分量 $J_{x}, J_{y}, J_{z}, p_{x}, p_{y}, p_{z}$ 中角动量的任何两个分量和动量的第三个分量（例如 $J_{x}, J_{z}$ 和 $p_{y}$ 或 $J_{x}, J_{y}$ 和 $p_{z}$ ）为运动常数，则 $J$ 和 $p$ 都是运动常数。

证明 $J_{x} = C_{1},\quad J_{z} = C_{2},\quad p_{y} = C_{3}$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} [ J _ {z}, J _ {x} ] & = \left[ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}), \sum_ {i} (y _ {i} p _ {z _ {i}} - z _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) \right] \\ & = \sum_ {j} \{\frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {j}} [ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) ] \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {x _ {j}}} [ \sum_ {i} (y _ {i} p _ {z _ {i}} - z _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) ] \\ & \quad - \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {x _ {j}}} [ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) ] \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {j}} [ \sum_ {i} (y _ {i} p _ {z _ {i}} - z _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) ] \\ & \quad + \frac {\partial}{\partial y _ {j}} [ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) ] \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {y _ {j}}} [ \sum_ {i} (y _ {i} p _ {z _ {i}} - z _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) ] \\ & \quad - \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {y _ {j}}} [ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) ] \frac {\partial}{\partial y _ {j}} [ \sum_ {i} (y _ {i} p _ {z _ {i}} - z _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) ] \\ & \quad + \frac {\partial}{\partial z _ {j}} [ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) ] \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {z _ {j}}} [ \sum_ {i} (y _ {i} p _ {z _ {i}} - z _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) ] \\ & \quad - \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {z _ {j}}} [ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) ] \frac {\partial}{\partial z _ {j}} [ \sum_ {i} (y _ {i} p _ {z _ {i}} - z _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) ] \} \\ & = \sum_ {j} [ (- p _ {x _ {j}}) (- z _ {j}) - x _ {j} p _ {z _ {j}} ] \\ & = \sum_ {i} (z _ {j} p _ {x _ {j}} - x _ {j} p _ {z _ {j}}) = J _ {\mathrm{y}} \end{array}
$$

由泊松定理, 因为 $J_{x}$ 、 $J_{x}$ 都是运动常数, 它们构成的泊松括号

$$
J _ {y} = \left[ J _ {z}, J _ {x} \right]
$$

也是运动常数.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} [ p _ {y}, J _ {z} ] & = \left[ \sum_ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}, \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) \right] \\ & = \sum_ {j} \left\{\frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {j}} (\sum_ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {x _ {j}}} [ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) ] \right. \\ & \quad - \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {x _ {j}}} (\sum_ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {j}} [ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) ] \\ & \quad + \frac {\partial}{\partial y _ {j}} (\sum_ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {y _ {j}}} [ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) ] \\ & \quad - \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {y _ {j}}} (\sum_ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) \frac {\partial}{\partial y _ {j}} [ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) ] \\ & \quad + \frac {\partial}{\partial z _ {j}} (\sum_ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {z _ {j}}} [ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) ] \\ & \quad - \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {z _ {j}}} (\sum_ {i} p _ {y _ {i}}) \frac {\partial}{\partial z _ {j}} [ \sum_ {i} (x _ {i} p _ {y _ {i}} - y _ {i} p _ {x _ {i}}) ] \\ & = \sum_ {j} [ - 1 \cdot (- p _ {x _ {j}}) ] = \sum_ {j} p _ {x _ {j}} = p _ {x} \end{array}
$$

由泊松定理, 因为 $p_{y}, J_{z}$ 都是运动常数, 则 $p_{x} = [p_{y}, J_{z}]$ 也是运动常数.

同样可得, 因为 $p_x, J_y$ 是运动常数, $p_z = [p_x, J_y]$ 也是运动常数, $J$ 和 $\pmb{p}$ 的所有分量都是运动常数, 所以 $J$ 和 $\pmb{p}$ 都是运动常数.

11.2.5 设 A 是平方反比律力作用下的有心运动中的拉普拉斯-龙格-楞次矢量，

$$
\boldsymbol {A} = \boldsymbol {p} \times \boldsymbol {J} - m ^ {2} \alpha \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{r}
$$

其中 J 是关于力心的角动量， $\alpha$ 是有心力中的系数， $F = -\frac{m\alpha}{r^{2}}e_{r}$ . 利用泊松括号的性质，求以下各泊松括号： $[A_{\alpha}, J_{\beta}]$ ， $[A_{\alpha}, H]$ （脚标 $\alpha, \beta$ 表示笛卡儿坐标的分量），并证明它们都是守恒量.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\text {解}} \\ & {\qquad A = p \times J - m ^ {2} \alpha   \frac {r}{r}} \\ & {\qquad p = p _ {x} i + p _ {y} j, \quad r = x i + y j} \\ & {\qquad J = r \times p = (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}) k} \\ & {A = (p _ {x} i + p _ {y} j) \times (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}) k - m ^ {2} \alpha   \frac {x i + y j}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}} \\ & {\qquad = \Big [ x p _ {y} ^ {2} - y p _ {x} p _ {y} - m ^ {2} \alpha   \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \Big ] i + \Big [ y p _ {x} ^ {2} - x p _ {x} p _ {y} - m ^ {2} \alpha   \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \Big ] j} \\ & {\qquad [ A _ {x}, J _ {x} ] = [ A _ {x}, J _ {y} ] = [ A _ {y}, J _ {x} ] = [ A _ {y}, J _ {y} ] = 0} \\ & {\qquad [ A _ {z}, J _ {x} ] = [ A _ {z}, J _ {y} ] = [ A _ {z}, J _ {z} ] = 0} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {[ A _ {x}, J _ {z} ] = \Big [ x p _ {y} ^ {2} - y p _ {x} p _ {y} - m ^ {2} \alpha \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, x p _ {y} - y p _ {x} \Big ]} \\ & {\quad = p _ {y} [ x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}, x p _ {y} - y p _ {x} ] + [ p _ {y}, x p _ {y} - y p _ {x} ] (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x})} \\ & {\quad - m ^ {2} \alpha \Big [ \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, x p _ {y} \Big ] + m ^ {2} \alpha \Big [ \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, y p _ {x} \Big ]} \\ & {\quad = [ p _ {y}, - y ] p _ {x} (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}) - m ^ {2} \alpha x \Big [ \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, p _ {y} \Big ]} \\ & {\quad + m ^ {2} \alpha y \Big [ \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, p _ {x} \Big ]} \\ & {\quad = p _ {x} (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}) + \frac {m ^ {2} \alpha x ^ {2} y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} + m ^ {2} \alpha y \Big [ \frac {1}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} - \frac {x ^ {2}}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \Big ]} \\ & {\quad = x p _ {x} p _ {y} - y p _ {x} ^ {2} + m ^ {2} \alpha \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} = - A _ {y}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {[ A _ {y}, J _ {z} ] = \Big [ p _ {x} (y p _ {x} - x p _ {y}) - m ^ {2} \alpha \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, x p _ {y} - y p _ {x} \Big ]} \\ & {\qquad = p _ {x} [ y p _ {x} - x p _ {y}, x p _ {y} - y p _ {x} ] + [ p _ {x}, x p _ {y} - y p _ {x} ] (y p _ {x} - x p _ {y})} \\ & {\qquad - m ^ {2} \alpha \Big [ \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, x p _ {y} \Big ] + m ^ {2} \alpha \Big [ \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, y p _ {x} \Big ]} \\ & {\qquad = [ p _ {x}, x ] p _ {y} (y p _ {x} - x p _ {y}) - m ^ {2} \alpha x \Big [ \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, p _ {y} \Big ]} \\ & {\qquad + m ^ {2} \alpha y ^ {2} \Big [ \frac {1}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, p _ {x} \Big ]} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & = (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}) p _ {y} - m ^ {2} \alpha x \left[ \frac {1}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} - \frac {y ^ {2}}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \right] \\ & \quad + m ^ {2} \alpha y ^ {2} \frac {(- x)}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \\ & = (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}) p _ {y} - m ^ {2} \alpha \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} = A _ {x} \end{array}
$$

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {x} ^ {2} + p _ {y} ^ {2}) - \frac {m \alpha}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} [ A _ {x}, H ] & = \left[ p _ {y} (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}) - m ^ {2} \alpha \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {x} ^ {2} + p _ {y} ^ {2}) - \frac {m \alpha}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \right] \\ & = \left[ x p _ {y} ^ {2}, \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} \right] - \left[ y p _ {x} p _ {y}, \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} ^ {2} \right] \\ & \quad + \left[ x p _ {y} ^ {2}, - \frac {m \alpha}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \right] + \left[ y p _ {x} p _ {y}, \frac {m \alpha}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \right] \\ & \quad - m ^ {2} \alpha \left[ \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} \right] - m ^ {2} \alpha \left[ \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} ^ {2} \right] \\ & = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} ^ {2} [ x, p _ {x} ^ {2} ] - \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} p _ {y} [ y, p _ {y} ^ {2} ] \\ & \quad + m a x \left[ \frac {1}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, p _ {y} ^ {2} \right] + y p _ {y} \left[ p _ {x}, \frac {m \alpha}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \right] \\ & \quad + y p _ {x} \left[ p _ {y}, \frac {m \alpha}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \right] - \frac {1}{2} m \alpha \left[ \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, p _ {x} ^ {2} \right] \\ & \quad - \frac {1}{2} m \alpha \left[ \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, p _ {y} ^ {2} \right] \\ & = \frac {1}{m} p _ {y} ^ {2} p _ {x} - \frac {1}{m} p _ {x} p _ {y} ^ {2} - m a x \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \cdot 2 p _ {y} \\ & \quad + m a y p _ {y} \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} + y p _ {x} \cdot m a \cdot \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \\ & \quad - \frac {1}{2} m a \left[ \frac {1}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} - \frac {x ^ {2}}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \right] \cdot 2 p _ {x} \\ & \quad - \frac {1}{2} m a x \left[ - \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \right] 2 p _ {y} = 0 \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} [ A _ {y}, H ] & = \left[ y p _ {x} ^ {2} - x p _ {x} p _ {y} - m ^ {2} \alpha \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {x} ^ {2} + p _ {y} ^ {2}) - \frac {m \alpha}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \right] \\ & = \left[ y p _ {x} ^ {2}, \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} ^ {2} \right] - \left[ x p _ {x} p _ {y}, \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} \right] + \left[ y p _ {x} ^ {2}, - \frac {m \alpha}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \right] \\ & \quad + \left[ x p _ {x} p _ {y}, \frac {m \alpha}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \right] - m ^ {2} \alpha \left[ \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {x} ^ {2} + p _ {y} ^ {2}) \right] \\ & = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} [ y, p _ {y} ^ {2} ] - \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} p _ {y} [ x, p _ {x} ^ {2} ] - m \alpha y \left[ p _ {x} ^ {2}, \frac {1}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \right] \\ & \quad + x p _ {x} \left[ p _ {y}, \frac {m \alpha}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \right] + x p _ {y} \left[ p _ {x}, \frac {m \alpha}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \right] \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \frac {1}{2} m \alpha \left[ \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, p _ {x} ^ {2} \right] - \frac {1}{2} m \alpha \left[ \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}}, p _ {y} ^ {2} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{m} p _ {x} ^ {2} p _ {y} - \frac {1}{m} p _ {x} ^ {2} p _ {y} - m \alpha y \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \cdot 2 p _ {x} \\ + x p _ {x} \cdot m \alpha \frac {y}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} + x p _ {y} \cdot m \alpha \cdot \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \\ - \frac {1}{2} m \alpha \left[ - \frac {y x}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \right] \cdot 2 p _ {x} - \frac {1}{2} m \alpha \left[ \frac {1}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} - \frac {y ^ {2}}{(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \right] 2 p _ {y} = 0 \end{array}
$$

$$
[ A _ {z}, H ] = 0 \quad (\text { 因为 } A _ {z} = 0)
$$

$H$ 是运动常数. 是守恒量, $[A_{\alpha}, H] = 0, A_{\alpha}$ 也是守恒量.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left[ \left[ A _ {x}, J _ {z} \right], H \right] = \left[ - A _ {y}, H \right] = 0} \\ & {\left[ \left[ A _ {y}, J _ {z} \right], H \right] = \left[ A _ {x}, H \right] = 0} \end{array}
$$

其余 $[A_{\alpha}, J_{\beta}] = 0$ ( $\alpha$ 不等于 $x, y, \beta$ 不等于 $z$ ).

所以

$$
[ [ A _ {\alpha}, J _ {\beta} ], H ] = 0
$$

这就证明了 $[A_{\alpha}, J_{\beta}]$ 、 $[A_{\alpha}, H]$ 都是守恒量。但不少都是零，不为零的 $[A_x, J_z]$ 、 $[A_y, J_z]$ 也没有给出新的守恒量。

注意: A 和 J 都是守恒量, 但 $A_{r}, A_{\varphi}$ 不是守恒量, 因此, $[A_{r}, J_{z}]$ 、 $[A_{\varphi}, J_{z}]$ 都不是守恒量, $[A_{r}, H]$ 、 $[A_{\varphi}, H]$ 也不等于零.

11.2.6 已知某系统的哈密顿函数

$$
H = q _ {1} p _ {1} - q _ {2} p _ {2} - a q _ {1} ^ {2} + b q _ {2} ^ {2}
$$

式中 a、b 均为常量（这个哈密顿函数并不来自拉格朗日函数，是假想的），证明 $F_{1}=q_{1}q_{2}$ ， $F_{2}=q_{1}e^{-t}, F_{3}=\frac{p_{2}-bq_{2}}{q_{1}}$ 都是运动常量，讨论它们的独立性，能否由它们找出新的独立的运动常量。

证明

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & H = q _ {1} p _ {1} - q _ {2} p _ {2} - a q _ {1} ^ {2} + b q _ {2} ^ {2} \\ & [ F _ {1}, H ] = [ q _ {1} q _ {2}, q _ {1} p _ {1} - q _ {2} p _ {2} - a q _ {1} ^ {2} + b q _ {2} ^ {2} ] \\ & \quad = q _ {2} [ q _ {1}, q _ {1} p _ {1} ] - q _ {1} [ q _ {2}, q _ {2} p _ {2} ] = q _ {2} q _ {1} - q _ {1} q _ {2} = 0 \\ & [ F _ {2}, H ] = [ q _ {1} e ^ {- t}, q _ {1} p _ {1} - q _ {2} p _ {2} - a q _ {1} ^ {2} + b q _ {2} ^ {2} ] \\ & \quad = q _ {1} e ^ {- t} [ q _ {1}, p _ {1} ] = q _ {1} e ^ {- t} \end{array}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial F _ {2}}{\partial t} + [ F _ {2}, H ] = \frac {\partial (q _ {1} e ^ {- t})}{\partial t} + q _ {1} e ^ {- t} = - q _ {1} e ^ {- t} + q _ {1} e ^ {- t} = 0
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} [ F _ {3}, H ] & = \left[ \frac {p _ {2} - b q _ {2}}{q _ {1}}, q _ {1} p _ {1} - q _ {2} p _ {2} - a q _ {1} ^ {2} + b q _ {2} ^ {2} \right] \\ & = q _ {1} (p _ {2} - b q _ {2}) \left[ \frac {1}{q _ {1}}, p _ {1} \right] - \frac {1}{q _ {1}} [ p _ {2} - b q _ {2}, q _ {2} p _ {2} ] + \frac {1}{q _ {1}} [ p _ {2} - b q _ {2}, b q _ {2} ^ {2} ] \\ & = - \frac {p _ {2} - b q _ {2}}{q _ {1}} + \frac {b q _ {2} + p _ {2}}{q _ {1}} - \frac {2 b q _ {2}}{q _ {1}} = 0 \end{array}
$$

$F_{i}$ 均满足 $\frac{\partial F_i}{\partial t} + [F_i, H] = 0 (i = 1, 2, 3)$ . 所以它们都是运动常量.

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c c c} \frac {\partial F _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} & \frac {\partial F _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} & \frac {\partial F _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} & \frac {\partial F _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} \\ \frac {\partial F _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} & \frac {\partial F _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} & \frac {\partial F _ {2}}{\partial p _ {1}} & \frac {\partial F _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} \\ \frac {\partial F _ {3}}{\partial q _ {1}} & \frac {\partial F _ {3}}{\partial q _ {2}} & \frac {\partial F _ {3}}{\partial p _ {1}} & \frac {\partial F _ {3}}{\partial p _ {2}} \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c c c} q _ {2} & q _ {1} & 0 & 0 \\ e ^ {- t} & 0 & 0 & 0 \\ - \frac {p _ {2} - b q _ {2}}{q _ {1} ^ {2}} & - \frac {b}{q _ {1}} & 0 & \frac {1}{q _ {1}} \end{array} \right)
$$

有

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c c} q _ {2} & q _ {1} & 0 \\ \mathrm{e} ^ {- t} & 0 & 0 \\ - \frac {p _ {2} - b q _ {2}}{q _ {1} ^ {2}} & - \frac {b}{q _ {1}} & \frac {1}{q _ {1}} \end{array} \right| = \frac {1}{q _ {1}} (0 - q _ {1} \mathrm{e} ^ {- t}) = - \mathrm{e} ^ {- t} \neq 0
$$

可见三个运动积分是相互独立的，

$$
[ F _ {1}, F _ {2} ] = [ q _ {1} q _ {2}, q _ {1} \mathrm{e} ^ {- t} ] = 0
$$

$$
\left[ F _ {1}, F _ {3} \right] = \left[ q _ {1} q _ {2}, \frac {p _ {2} - b q _ {2}}{q _ {1}} \right] = q _ {1} \cdot \frac {1}{q _ {1}} \left[ q _ {2}, p _ {2} \right] = 1
$$

$$
[ F _ {2}, F _ {3} ] = \left[ q _ {1} \mathrm{e} ^ {- t}, \frac {p _ {2} - b q _ {2}}{q _ {1}} \right] = 0
$$

由 $F_{1}, F_{2}, F_{3}$ 不能用泊松定理找出新的运动积分.

11.2.7 找出各向同性的三维谐振子尽可能多的运动积分,其拉格朗日函数为

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m \left(\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}\right) - \frac {1}{2} k \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}\right)
$$

解 $L = \frac{1}{2} m(\dot{x}^2 +\dot{y}^2 +\dot{z}^2) - \frac{1}{2} k(x^2 +y^2 +z^2)$ 

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {x} ^ {2} + p _ {y} ^ {2} + p _ {z} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} k (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2})
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial H}{\partial t} = 0, H$ 是守恒量.

$$
F _ {1} = H = \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {x} ^ {2} + p _ {y} ^ {2} + p _ {z} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} k (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}) = C _ {1}
$$

为运动积分.

改用柱坐标

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) - \frac {1}{2} k (r ^ {2} + z ^ {2}) \\ & H = \frac {1}{2 m} \left(p _ {r} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} p _ {\varphi} ^ {2} + p _ {z} ^ {2}\right) + \frac {1}{2} k (r ^ {2} + z ^ {2}) \\ & \frac {\partial H}{\partial \varphi} = 0, \quad p _ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = C _ {2} \end{array}
$$

为运动积分,改用直角坐标.可写为

$$
F _ {2} = J _ {z} = m (x \dot {y} - y \dot {x}) = x p _ {y} - y p _ {x} = C _ {2}
$$

根据对称性,可写出 $F_{3}$ 、 $F_{4}$ 两个运动积分:

$$
\begin{array}{l} F _ {3} = y p _ {z} - z p _ {y} = C _ {3} \\ F _ {4} = z p _ {x} - x p _ {z} = C _ {4} \end{array}
$$

$F_{1} = H$ ，显然有 $[F_1,F_i] = 0(i = 2,3,4)$ 不能得到新的运动积分，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} {[ F _ {2}, F _ {3} ] = [ x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}, y p _ {z} - z p _ {y} ]} \\ & {= [ x p _ {y}, y p _ {z} ] + [ y p _ {x} - z p _ {y} ]} \\ & {= - x p _ {z} + z p _ {x} = F _ {4}} \end{array}
$$

同样， $[F_2,F_4],[F_3,F_4]$ 均不能得到新的运动积分，

$$
H = H _ {1} + H _ {2} + H _ {3}
$$

其中

$$
H _ {1} = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2}, \quad H _ {2} = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k y ^ {2}
$$

$$
H _ {3} = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {z} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k z ^ {2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} {[ H _ {1}, H ]} & {= \left[ \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2}, H \right]} \\ & {= \left[ \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2}, \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} \right] + \left[ \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2}, \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} \right] = 0} \\ & {\frac {\partial H _ {1}}{\partial t} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial H _ {1}}{\partial t} + [ H _ {1}, H ] = 0} \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
F _ {5} = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} = C _ {5}
$$

为运动积分.

同样可得

$$
F _ {6} = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k y ^ {2} = C _ {6}
$$

$$
F _ {7} = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {z} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k z ^ {2} = C _ {7}
$$

均为运动积分, 显然, $F_{1}, F_{5}, F_{6}, F_{7}$ 中只有三个是独立的.

由泊松定理,可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} F _ {8} & = [ F _ {2}, F _ {5} ] = \left[ x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}, \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} \right] = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} [ x, p _ {x} ^ {2} ] - \frac {1}{2} k y [ p _ {x}, x ^ {2} ] \\ & = \frac {1}{m} p _ {y} p _ {x} - \frac {1}{2} k y (- 2 x) = \frac {1}{m} p _ {x} p _ {y} + k x y = C _ {8} \end{array}
$$

由对称性或同样用泊松定理可得

$$
F _ {9} = \frac {1}{m} p _ {y} p _ {z} + k y z = C _ {9}
$$

$$
F _ {1 0} = \frac {1}{m} p _ {x} p _ {z} + k x z = C _ {1 0}
$$

用泊松定理,再不能用这些运动积分得到新的运动积分.

11.2.8 (1) 对于任何正则变量和时间的函数 $a(q_{1}, q_{2}, \cdots, q_{s}, p_{1}, p_{2}, \cdots, p_{s}, t)$ , 证明

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} a}{\mathrm{d} t} = \frac {\partial a}{\partial t} + [ a, H ]
$$

(2) 某个两维振子, 动能、势能分别为

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2})
$$

$$
V = \frac {1}{2} k (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) + c x y
$$

其中 c 和 k 是常量.

(a) 用坐标变换证明该振子是一个各向异性的谐振子；

(b) 找出两个运动常数, 并用(1)的结论证明;

(c) 若 $c = 0$ ，找出第三个运动常数；

(d) 对于各向同性的振子, 证明对称矩阵

$$
A _ {i j} = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {i} p _ {j} + \frac {1}{2} k x _ {i} x _ {j}
$$

是运动常数,证明方法是设法将此矩阵的每一矩阵元用已知的运动常数表示出来.

解 (1)

$$
\dot {q} _ {i} = \frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {i}}, \quad \dot {p} _ {i} = - \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {i}}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\mathrm{d} a}{\mathrm{d} t} & = \frac {\partial a}{\partial t} + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} \left(\frac {\partial a}{\partial q _ {i}} \dot {q} _ {i} + \frac {\partial a}{\partial p _ {i}} \dot {p} _ {i}\right) \\ & = \frac {\partial a}{\partial t} + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} \left(\frac {\partial a}{\partial q _ {i}} \frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {i}} - \frac {\partial a}{\partial p _ {i}} \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {i}}\right) = \frac {\partial a}{\partial t} + [ a, H ] \\ & L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) - \frac {1}{2} k (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) - c x y \end{array}
$$

(2) 

(a) 作坐标变换, 使 $x, y$ 变为简正坐标 $\xi, \eta$ .

$$
\boldsymbol {M} = \left( \begin{array}{l l} m & 0 \\ 0 & m \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {K} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} k & c \\ c & k \end{array} \right)
$$

特征矩阵为

$$
K - \omega^ {2} M = \left( \begin{array}{c c} k - m \omega^ {2} & c \\ c & \dot {\lambda} - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\left| \boldsymbol {K} - \omega^ {2} \boldsymbol {M} \right| = 0
$$

$$
m ^ {2} \omega^ {4} - 2 m ^ {2} k \omega^ {2} + (k ^ {2} - c ^ {2}) = 0
$$

$$
[ m \omega^ {2} - (k + c) ] [ m \omega^ {2} - (k - c) ] = 0
$$

$$
\omega_ {1} ^ {2} = \frac {k + c}{m}, \quad \omega_ {2} ^ {2} = \frac {k - c}{m}
$$

$$
\operatorname{adj} (\boldsymbol {K} - \omega_ {1} ^ {2} \boldsymbol {M}) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} - c & c \\ c & - c \end{array} \right)
$$

取归一化了的列矢量 $\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}, -\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)$ 

$$
\operatorname{adj} (\boldsymbol {K} - \omega_ {2} ^ {2} \boldsymbol {M}) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} c & c \\ c & c \end{array} \right)
$$

取归一化了的列矢量 $\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}, \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ,

$$
\boldsymbol {G} = \left( \begin{array}{l l} u _ {1} ^ {(1)} & u _ {1} ^ {(2)} \\ u _ {2} ^ {(1)} & u _ {2} ^ {(2)} \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{l l} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} & \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \\ - \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} & \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {G} ^ {- 1} = \boldsymbol {G} ^ {T} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} & - \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \\ \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} & \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\left( \begin{array}{l} x \\ y \end{array} \right) = \boldsymbol {G} \left( \begin{array}{l} \xi \\ \eta \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} & \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \\ - \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} & \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l} \xi \\ \eta \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\left( \begin{array}{l} \xi \\ \eta \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} & - \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \\ \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} & \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l} x \\ y \end{array} \right)
$$

即作坐标变换 $x = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} (\xi +\eta),y = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}} (\eta -\xi),$ 

$$
\begin{array}{c} T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {\xi} ^ {2} + \dot {\eta} ^ {2}) \\ V = \frac {1}{2} (k - c) \xi^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (k + c) \eta^ {2} \end{array}
$$

这是一个各向异性的谐振子，在 $\xi, \eta$ 两方向的谐振动的角频率分别为 $\sqrt{\frac{k - c}{m}}$ 和 $\sqrt{\frac{k + c}{m}}$ .

(b) 

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {\xi} ^ {2} + \dot {\eta} ^ {2}) - \frac {1}{2} (k - c) \xi^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} (k + c) \eta^ {2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & H = \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {\xi} ^ {2} + p _ {\eta} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} (k - c) \xi^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (k + c) \eta^ {2} \\ & = H _ {1} + H _ {2} \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
H _ {1} = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {\xi} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (k - c) \xi^ {2}
$$

$$
H _ {2} = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {\eta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (k + c) \eta^ {2}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} H _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} t} = \frac {\partial H _ {1}}{\partial t} + [ H _ {1}, H ] = [ H _ {1}, H _ {1} + H _ {2} ] = [ H _ {1}, H _ {2} ] = 0
$$

同样

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} H _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} t} = [ H _ {2}, H _ {1} + H _ {2} ] = [ H _ {2}, H _ {1} ] = 0
$$

所以

$$
H _ {1} = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {\xi} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (k - c) \xi^ {2} = C _ {1}
$$

$$
H _ {2} = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {\eta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (k + c) \eta^ {2} = C _ {2}
$$

为运动常数，

$$
p _ {\xi} = m \dot {\xi} = m \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} (\dot {x} - \dot {y}) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} (p _ {x} - p _ {y})
$$

$$
p _ {\eta} = m \dot {\eta} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} (p _ {x} + p _ {y})
$$

上述两个运动常数也可写成

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{4 m} (p _ {x} - p _ {y}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} (k - c) (x - y) ^ {2} = C _ {1} \\ \frac {1}{4 m} (p _ {x} + p _ {y}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} (k + c) (x + y) ^ {2} = C _ {2} \end{array}
$$

(c) 若 $c = 0$ ，二维振子是各向同性的，

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {x} ^ {2} + p _ {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} k (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2})
$$

可找到

$$
J = m (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}) = C _ {3}
$$

为第三个运动积分. 证明如下:

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\mathrm{d} J}{\mathrm{d} t} & = \frac {\partial J}{\partial t} + [ J, H ] = [ m (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}), H ] \\ & = [ m x p _ {y}, \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} ] + [ m x p _ {y}, \frac {1}{2} k y ^ {2} ] \\ & - [ m y p _ {x}, \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} ^ {2} ] - [ m y p _ {x}, \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} ] \\ & = m p _ {y} \cdot \frac {1}{2 m} \cdot 2 p _ {x} + m x \cdot \frac {1}{2} k (- 2 y) \\ & - m p _ {x} \cdot \frac {1}{2 m} \cdot 2 p _ {y} - m y \cdot \frac {1}{2} k (- 2 x) \\ & = 0 \end{array}
$$

(d) 

$$
\boldsymbol {A} = \left( \begin{array}{l l} \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} & \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} p _ {y} + \frac {1}{2} k x y \\ \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} p _ {y} + \frac {1}{2} k x y & \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k y ^ {2} \end{array} \right)
$$

对于各向同性的振子，已证明了 $A_{11}, A_{22}$ 都是运动常数，

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} = E _ {1}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k y ^ {2} = E _ {2}
$$

由 $[A_{12}, H] = 0$ 很容易证明 $A_{12}$ 也是运动常数，但题目要求将 $A_{12}$ 表示成 $E_1, E_2$ 和

$$
m (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}) = J
$$

的函数. 不难看出:

$$
\begin{array}{r l} E _ {1} E _ {2} - \frac {k J ^ {2}}{4 m} & = \left(\frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2}\right) \left(\frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k y ^ {2}\right) - \frac {1}{4 m} [ m (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}) ] ^ {2} \\ & = \frac {1}{4 m ^ {2}} p _ {x} ^ {2} p _ {y} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} k ^ {2} x ^ {2} y ^ {2} + \frac {k}{4 m} (y ^ {2} p _ {x} ^ {2} + x ^ {2} p _ {y} ^ {2}) \\ & - \frac {k}{4 m} m ^ {2} (x ^ {2} p _ {y} ^ {2} + y ^ {2} p _ {x} ^ {2} - 2 x y p _ {x} p _ {y}) \\ & = \frac {1}{4 m ^ {2}} p _ {x} ^ {2} p _ {y} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} k ^ {2} x ^ {2} y ^ {2} + \frac {k}{2 m} x y p _ {x} p _ {y} \end{array}
$$

$$
= \left(\frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} p _ {y} + \frac {1}{2} k x y\right) ^ {2} = (A _ {1 2}) ^ {2}
$$

所以

$$
A _ {1 2} = A _ {2 1} = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} p _ {y} + \frac {1}{2} k x y = \pm \sqrt {E _ {1} E _ {2} - \frac {k}{4 m} J ^ {2}}
$$

为运动常数. 矩阵的所有元素均为运动常数, 该矩阵当然是运动常数.

11.2.9 已知

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \varphi = f _ {1} (q _ {1}, p _ {1}) f _ {2} (q _ {2}, p _ {2}) \\ & \psi = f _ {3} (q _ {1}, p _ {1}) f _ {4} (q _ {2}, p _ {2}) \end{array}
$$

计算泊松括号 $[\varphi,\psi]$ .

解

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varphi = f _ {1} (q _ {1}, p _ {1}) f _ {2} (q _ {2}, p _ {2}) \\ \psi = f _ {3} (q _ {1}, p _ {1}) f _ {4} (q _ {2}, p _ {2}) \end{array}
$$

$$
[ \varphi , \psi ] = \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial q _ {1}} \frac {\partial \psi}{\partial p _ {1}} - \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial p _ {1}} \frac {\partial \psi}{\partial q _ {1}} + \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial q _ {2}} \frac {\partial \psi}{\partial p _ {2}} - \frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial p _ {2}} \frac {\partial \psi}{\partial q _ {2}}
$$

这是用泊松括号的定义进行直接计算,更方便的办法是利用泊松括号运算的性质,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} {[ \varphi , \psi ] = [ f _ {1} (q _ {1}, p _ {1}) f _ {2} (q _ {2}, p _ {2}), f _ {3} (q _ {1}, p _ {1}) f _ {4} (q _ {2}, p _ {2}) ]} \\ & {= f _ {2} (q _ {2}, p _ {2}) [ f _ {1} (q _ {1}, p _ {1}), f _ {3} (q _ {1}, p _ {1}) ] f _ {4} (q _ {2}, p _ {2})} \\ & {\quad + f _ {1} (q _ {1}, p _ {1}) [ f _ {2} (q _ {2}, p _ {2}), f _ {4} (q _ {2}, p _ {2}) ] f _ {3} (q _ {1}, p _ {1})} \\ & {= f _ {2} f _ {4} [ f _ {1}, f _ {3} ] + f _ {1} f _ {3} [ f _ {2}, f _ {4} ]} \end{array}
$$

11.2.10 若系统的哈密顿函数可写成下列形式：

$$
H = H \left[ f \left(q _ {1}, q _ {2}, \dots , q _ {m}, p _ {2}, p _ {2}, \dots , p _ {m}\right), q _ {m + 1}, \dots , q _ {s}, p _ {m + 1}, \dots , p _ {s}, t \right]
$$

则 $f(q_{1},q_{2},\cdots,q_{m},p_{1},p_{2},\cdots,p_{n})$ 是守恒量.

证明

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\mathrm{d} f}{\mathrm{d} t} & = \frac {\partial f}{\partial t} + [ f, H ] \\ & = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {m} \left(\frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {i}} \cdot \frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {i}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial p _ {i}} \cdot \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {i}}\right) + \sum_ {i = m + 1} ^ {s} \left(\frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {i}} \cdot \frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {i}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial p _ {i}} \cdot \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {i}}\right) \\ & = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {m} \left(\frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {i}} \cdot \frac {\partial H}{\partial f} \cdot \frac {\partial f}{\partial p _ {i}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial p _ {i}} \cdot \frac {\partial H}{\partial f} \cdot \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {i}}\right) = 0 \end{array}
$$

所以 $f(q_{1},q_{2},\cdots,q_{m},p_{1},p_{2},\cdots,p_{m})$ 是守恒量.

11.2.11 若两个已知函数 $\varphi(q, p, t)$ 、 $\psi(q, p, t)$ 有下列恒等式：

$$
\varphi \Bigl \{\frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial t} + [ \varphi , H ] \Bigr \} \equiv \frac {\partial \psi}{\partial t} + [ \psi , H ]
$$

其中 $H$ 是哈密顿函数， $[\varphi, H]$ 、 $[\psi, H]$ 均为泊松括号，找出此正则方程的一个运动积分.

解

$$
\varphi \Bigl \{\frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial t} + [ \varphi , H ] \Bigr \} \equiv \frac {\partial \psi}{\partial t} + [ \psi , H ]
$$

$$
\varphi \left\{\frac {\partial \varphi}{\partial t} + [ \varphi , H ] \right\} = \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \left(\frac {1}{2} \varphi^ {2}\right) + \left[ \frac {1}{2} \varphi^ {2}, H \right] \equiv \frac {\partial \psi}{\partial t} + [ \psi , H ]
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial t} \left(\frac {1}{2} \varphi^ {2} - \psi\right) + \left[ \frac {1}{2} \varphi^ {2} - \psi , H \right] = 0
$$

所以 $\frac{1}{2}\varphi^2 -\psi$ 为运动常数.

## 11.3 哈密顿原理

11.3.1 求在 $xy$ 平面内经过两个固定点的一条曲线；绕 $z$ 轴的转动惯量为极小值，用平面极坐标写出该曲线必须满足的微分方程.

解 曲线的微元

其中

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \mathrm{d} s & = \sqrt {(\mathrm{d} r) ^ {2} + (r \mathrm{d} \varphi) ^ {2}} = \sqrt {1 + r ^ {2} \varphi^ {\prime 2}} \mathrm{d} r \\ & \varphi^ {\prime} = \frac {\mathrm{d} \varphi}{\mathrm{d} r} \end{array}
$$

设线密度为 $\eta$ ，微元绕 z 轴的转动惯量为

$$
\mathrm{d} I = \eta r ^ {2} \mathrm{d} s = \eta r ^ {2} \sqrt {1 + r ^ {2} \varphi^ {\prime 2}} \mathrm{d} r
$$

曲线绕 $z$ 轴的转动惯量为

$$
I = \int_ {r _ {0}} ^ {r _ {1}} \eta r ^ {2} \sqrt {1 + r ^ {2} \varphi^ {\prime 2}} \mathrm{d} r
$$

$I$ 为极小值要求

$$
\delta I = \delta \int_ {r _ {0}} ^ {r _ {1}} \eta r ^ {2} \sqrt {1 + r ^ {2} \varphi^ {\prime 2}} \mathrm{d} r = 0
$$

设

$$
F = r ^ {2} \sqrt {1 + r ^ {2} \varphi^ {\prime 2}}
$$

由欧拉方程

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial F}{\partial \varphi}\right) - \frac {\partial F}{\partial \varphi} = 0
$$

今 $\frac{\partial F}{\partial \varphi} = 0$ ，有 $\frac{\partial F}{\partial \varphi} = C(C$ 为常量）

即

$$
\begin{array}{r l} r ^ {2} \frac {2 r ^ {2} \phi}{2 \sqrt {1 + r ^ {2} \phi^ {2}}} & = C \\ \frac {r ^ {4}}{\sqrt {1 + r ^ {2} \phi^ {2}}} \phi & = C \end{array}
$$

这就是所要求的微分方程. 解此方程又有一个积分常数, 两个积分常数由曲线经过的两个固定点确定.

11.3.2 试用哈密顿原理求复摆做微振动的周期.

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {L = \frac {1}{2} I \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - m g l (1 - \cos \theta) \approx \frac {1}{2} I \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}} \\ & {S [ \theta (t) ] = \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} L \mathrm{d} t = \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} \left(\frac {1}{2} I \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}\right) \mathrm{d} t} \\ & {\qquad \delta S = \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} (I \dot {\theta} \delta \dot {\theta} - m g l \theta \delta \theta) \mathrm{d} t} \\ & {\qquad = \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} (I \dot {\theta} \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \delta \theta - m g l \theta \delta \theta) \mathrm{d} t} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} \left\{\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (I \dot {\theta} \delta \theta) - \left[ \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (I \dot {\theta}) + m g l \theta \right] \delta \theta \right\} \mathrm{d} t \\ = I \dot {\theta} \delta \theta \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} - \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} (I \ddot {\theta} + m g l \theta) \delta \theta \mathrm{d} t \\ = - \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} (I \ddot {\theta} + m g l \theta) \delta \theta \mathrm{d} t = 0 \end{array}
$$

这里用了在 $t = t_0$ 和 $t = t_{1},\delta \theta = 0$ 

$$
\begin{array}{l} I \ddot {\theta} + m g l \theta = 0 \\ \omega = \sqrt {\frac {m g l}{I}}, T = \frac {2 \pi}{\omega} = 2 \pi \sqrt {\frac {I}{m g l}} \end{array}
$$

11.3.3 试用哈密顿原理求解 7.4.30 题.

解 取 $O_{1}A, O_{2}B$ 偏离平衡位置的转角 $\varphi$ 为广义坐标，用7.4.30题已得出的动能和势能，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \left[ \frac {1}{2} m (a - b) ^ {2} + 2 M a ^ {2} \right] \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \\ & V = \frac {1}{2} (m g b + k a ^ {2}) \varphi^ {2} \\ & L = T - V = \left[ \frac {1}{2} m (a - b) ^ {2} + 2 M a ^ {2} \right] \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} (m g b + k a ^ {2}) \varphi^ {2} \\ & S = \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} \left\{\left[ \frac {1}{2} m (a - b) ^ {2} + 2 M a ^ {2} \right] \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} (m g b + k a ^ {2}) \varphi^ {2} \right\} d t \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/f2f3d4392cdb3f3ba138cff0f6c765da6570b229cd9b4f474025008682dcfb62.jpg)



图11.4


$\delta S = 0$ 要求被积函数 $F(\varphi, \dot{\varphi})$ 满足

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial F}{\partial \dot {\varphi}}\right) - \frac {\partial F}{\partial \varphi} = 0
$$

得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left[ (4 M + m) a ^ {2} + m b (b - 2 a) \right] \ddot {\varphi} + (m g b + k a ^ {2}) \varphi = 0} \\ & {\omega = \left[ \frac {m g b + k a ^ {2}}{(4 M + m) a ^ {2} + m b (b - 2 a)} \right] ^ {1 / 2}} \\ & {\frac {2 \pi}{\omega} = 2 \pi \left[ \frac {(4 M + m) a ^ {2} + m b (b - 2 a)}{m g b + k a ^ {2}} \right] ^ {1 / 2}} \end{array}
$$

周期为

11.3.4 在 $xy$ 平面上的一条通过 $P_{1}, P_{2}$ 的坐标分别为 $(x_{1}, y_{1}), (x_{2}, y_{2})$ 的一条曲线

$y = y(x)$ 绕 $x$ 轴转动时扫出的一个曲面，问 $y(x)$ 为何函数时该曲面面积最小？

解 在曲线上取线元 $\mathrm{d}S$ ，它绕 $x$ 轴一周扫出的面积为

$$
\mathrm{d} A = 2 \pi y \mathrm{d} S
$$

通过 $P_{1}, P_{2}$ 两点的曲线绕 $x$ 轴一周扫出的面积

$$
A = \int_ {P _ {1}} ^ {P _ {2}} 2 \pi y \mathrm{d} S = 2 \pi \int_ {x _ {1}} ^ {x _ {2}} y \sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\mathrm{d} x}\right) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} x
$$

$A$ 最小，要求 $\delta A = 0$ ，即

$$
\int_ {x _ {1}} ^ {x _ {2}} \delta \left[ y \sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\mathrm{d} x}\right) ^ {2}} \right] \mathrm{d} x = 0
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\delta \Big [ y \sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\mathrm{d} x}\right) ^ {2}} \Big ] \mathrm{d} x = \sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\mathrm{d} x}\right) ^ {2}} \delta y \mathrm{d} x + y \Big [ 1 + \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\mathrm{d} x}\right) ^ {2} \Big ] ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\mathrm{d} x} \delta (\mathrm{d} y)} \\ & {\qquad = \sqrt {1 + y ^ {\prime 2}} \delta y \mathrm{d} x + y y ^ {\prime} (1 + y ^ {\prime 2}) ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} \mathrm{d} (\delta y)} \\ & {\qquad = \sqrt {1 + y ^ {\prime 2}} \delta y \mathrm{d} x + \mathrm{d} [ y y ^ {\prime} (1 + y ^ {\prime 2}) ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} \delta y ]} \\ & {\qquad - \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} x} [ y y ^ {\prime} (1 + y ^ {\prime 2}) ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} ] \delta y \mathrm{d} x} \end{array}
$$

其中 $y'=\frac{dy}{dx}$ .

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {x _ {1}} ^ {x _ {2}} \delta [ y \sqrt {1 + y ^ {\prime 2}} ] \mathrm{d} x \\ = [ y y ^ {\prime} (1 + y ^ {\prime 2}) ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} \delta y ] \Bigg | _ {x _ {1}} ^ {x _ {2}} + \int_ {x _ {1}} ^ {x _ {2}} \left\{\sqrt {1 + y ^ {\prime 2}} - \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} x} [ y y ^ {\prime} (1 + y ^ {\prime 2}) ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} ] \right\} \delta y \mathrm{d} x = 0 \end{array}
$$

在 $x = x_{1}, x_{2}, \delta y = 0$ ，在别处， $\delta y$ 任意.

$$
\sqrt {1 + y ^ {\prime 2}} - \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} x} [ y y ^ {\prime} (1 + y ^ {\prime 2}) ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} ] = 0
$$

考虑到 $1 + y'^2 \neq 0$ ，上式经计算可得

$$
1 + y ^ {\prime 2} - y y ^ {\prime \prime} = 0
$$

因为

$$
y ^ {\prime \prime} = \frac {\mathrm{d} y ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} y} y ^ {\prime} = \frac {1}{2} \frac {\mathrm{d} y ^ {\prime 2}}{\mathrm{d} y}
$$

代入上式，

$$
1 + y ^ {\prime 2} - \frac {1}{2} y \frac {\mathrm{d} y ^ {\prime 2}}{\mathrm{d} y} = 0
$$

$$
2 \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{y} = \frac {\mathrm{d} y ^ {\prime 2}}{1 + y ^ {\prime 2}}
$$

$$
1 + y ^ {\prime 2} = C _ {1} ^ {2} y ^ {2}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\sqrt {C _ {1} ^ {2} y ^ {2} - 1}} = \mathrm{d} x
$$

$$
\frac {1}{C _ {1}} \operatorname{arccosh} C _ {1} y = x + C _ {2} ^ {\prime}
$$

$$
y = \frac {1}{C _ {1}} \cosh (C _ {1} x + C _ {2})
$$

其中 $C_1, C_2$ 均为积分常数，由 $y_1 = y(x_1), y_2 = y(x_2)$ 确定.

11.3.5 一个系统的拉格朗日函数为

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} \dot {x} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} x ^ {2}
$$

(1) 直接验证对路径 $x = A \sin t$ 有

$$
\delta \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{8}} L \mathrm{d} t = 0
$$

(2) 比较在 $t = 0$ 和 $t = \frac{\pi}{8}$ 均有同样的 $x$ 值的路径族 $x = A(\sin t + c\sin 8t)$ ( $c$ 可取各种值), 证明当 $c = 0$ 时, $\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{8}} L \mathrm{d}t$ 有最小值.

解 (1)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & L = \frac {1}{2} \dot {x} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} x ^ {2} \\ & \delta \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{8}} \left(\frac {1}{2} \dot {x} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} x ^ {2}\right) \mathrm{d} t = \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{8}} \delta \left(\frac {1}{2} \dot {x} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} x ^ {2}\right) \mathrm{d} t \\ & = \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{8}} (\dot {x} \delta \dot {x} - x \delta x) \mathrm{d} t = \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{8}} \left(\dot {x} \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \delta x - x \delta x\right) \mathrm{d} t \\ & = \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{8}} \left[ \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (\dot {x} \delta x) - \frac {\mathrm{d} \dot {x}}{\mathrm{d} t} \delta x - x \delta x \right] \mathrm{d} t \\ & = \dot {x} \delta x \Bigg | _ {t = 0} ^ {t = \frac {\pi}{8}} - \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{8}} (\ddot {x} + x) \delta x \mathrm{d} t = - \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{8}} (\ddot {x} + x) \delta x \mathrm{d} t \end{array}
$$

代入 $x = A\sin t$ 

因为

$$
\ddot {x} + x = - A \sin t + A \sin t = 0
$$

所以

$$
\delta \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{8}} L \mathrm{d} t = 0
$$

(2) 

$$
\int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{8}} L \mathrm{d} t = \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{8}} \left(\frac {1}{2} \dot {x} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} x ^ {2}\right) \mathrm{d} t
$$

将 $x = A(\sin t + c\sin 8t)$ 代入

$$
J = \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {\pi}{8}} \left[ \frac {1}{2} A ^ {2} (\cos t + 8 c \cos 8 t) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} A ^ {2} (\sin t + c \sin 8 t) ^ {2} \right] d t
$$

经计算可得

$$
J = \frac {1}{4} A ^ {2} \left(\sin \frac {\pi}{4} + \frac {6 3}{8} \pi c ^ {2}\right)
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}J}{\mathrm{d}c} = 0$ 得 $c = 0$ ，所以 $c = 0$ 时 $J$ 最小.

11.3.6 一个质点在竖直平面内从一点无初速地沿某一条光滑曲线滑到给定的另一点,要所需时间最短,求此光滑曲线满足的微分方程.

解

$$
J = \int \frac {\mathrm{d} s}{v} = \int_ {x _ {0}} ^ {x _ {1}} \frac {\sqrt {1 + y ^ {\prime 2}}}{\sqrt {2 g y}} \mathrm{d} x
$$

$$
\delta \int_ {x _ {0}} ^ {x _ {1}} \frac {\sqrt {1 + y ^ {\prime 2}}}{\sqrt {y}} \mathrm{d} x = 0
$$

$$
F = \frac {\sqrt {1 + y ^ {\prime 2}}}{\sqrt {y}}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial F}{\partial y ^ {\prime}} = \frac {y ^ {\prime}}{\sqrt {y} \cdot \sqrt {1 + y ^ {\prime 2}}}, \quad \frac {\partial F}{\partial y} = - \sqrt {1 + y ^ {\prime 2}} \frac {1}{2 y ^ {3 / 2}}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} x} \left(\frac {\partial F}{\partial y ^ {\prime}}\right) & = \frac {y ^ {\prime \prime}}{\sqrt {y} \cdot \sqrt {1 + y ^ {\prime 2}}} - \frac {y ^ {\prime}}{\sqrt {y}} \frac {y ^ {\prime} \cdot y ^ {\prime \prime}}{(1 + y ^ {\prime 2}) ^ {3 / 2}} - \frac {y ^ {\prime}}{\sqrt {1 + y ^ {\prime 2}}} \cdot \frac {y ^ {\prime}}{2 y ^ {3 / 2}} \\ & = \frac {y ^ {\prime \prime}}{y ^ {1 / 2} (1 + y ^ {\prime 2}) ^ {3 / 2}} - \frac {y ^ {\prime 2}}{2 y ^ {3 / 2} (1 + y ^ {\prime 2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \end{array}
$$

由欧拉方程 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial F}{\partial y'}\right) - \frac{\partial F}{\partial y} = 0$ 可得

$$
\frac {y ^ {\prime \prime}}{y ^ {1 / 2} (1 + y ^ {\prime 2}) ^ {3 / 2}} + \frac {1}{2 y ^ {3 / 2} (1 + y ^ {\prime 2}) ^ {1 / 2}} = 0
$$

两边乘一不为零的因子 $2y^{3/2}(1 + y'^2)^{3/2}$ , 微分方程可写为

$$
2 y y ^ {\prime \prime} + y ^ {2} + 1 = 0
$$

11.3.7 证明在圆柱面上任何两点间最短曲线是螺旋线

$$
z = A r \varphi + B
$$

$r, \varphi, z$ 是柱坐标， $z$ 轴沿圆柱的对称轴， $r$ 为圆柱的半径， $A, B$ 由所给两点的 $z, \varphi$ 值确定.

解 $\mathrm{ds} = \sqrt{(r\mathrm{d}\varphi)^2 + (\mathrm{dz})^2} = \sqrt{r^2 + z'^2}\mathrm{d}\varphi$ 

其中 $z'=\frac{dz}{d\varphi}$ ，r 为常量。

$$
\begin{array}{r l} S [ z (\varphi) ] & = \int \sqrt {r ^ {2} + z ^ {\prime 2}} \mathrm{d} \varphi \\ F & = \sqrt {r ^ {2} + z ^ {\prime 2}} \end{array}
$$

最短曲线满足

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial F}{\partial z ^ {\prime}}\right) - \frac {\partial F}{\partial z} = 0
$$

因为

$$
\frac {\partial F}{\partial z} = 0
$$

所以

$$
\frac {z ^ {\prime}}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + z ^ {\prime 2}}} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {c}} \text {为常量}
$$

$$
c z ^ {\prime 2} = r ^ {2} + z ^ {\prime 2}
$$

令 $A^2 = \frac{1}{c - 1}$ ，上式可改写为

$$
z ^ {\prime 2} = \frac {1}{c - 1} r ^ {2} = A ^ {2} r ^ {2}, \quad z ^ {\prime} = A r
$$

$$
z = A r \varphi + B
$$

11.3.8 分别用哈密顿原理和变形的哈密顿原理得到 9.3.27 题得到的运动微分方程.

解 用 9.3.27 题已得到的拉格朗日函数，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L & = \frac {1}{2} M \left[ \dot {s} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{3} l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - l \dot {s} \dot {\theta} \cos (\theta - \alpha) \right] \\ & + M g \left(s \sin \alpha + \frac {l}{2} \cos \theta\right) \end{array}
$$

用哈密顿原理，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\delta \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} L \mathrm{d} t = 0} \\ & {\delta \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} \left\{\frac {1}{2} M \left[ \dot {s} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{3} l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - l \dot {s} \dot {\theta} \cos (\theta - \alpha) \right] \right.} \\ & {\quad \left. + M g \left(s \sin \alpha + \frac {l}{2} \cos \theta\right) \right\} \mathrm{d} t = 0} \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/5eea9a8c91b52dafa39be45e0a5311e68b8a93cee73f384284ddc85ddafffee6.jpg)



图11.5


直接作变分运算或用欧拉方程也就是拉格朗日方程，

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {s}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial s} = 0
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \bigg (\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} \bigg) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta} = 0
$$

即得 9.3.27 题得到的运动微分方程,以下略.

下面用变形的哈密顿原理：

$$
\begin{array}{l} p _ {s} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {s}} = M \dot {s} - \frac {1}{2} M l \dot {\theta} \cos (\theta - \alpha) \\ p _ {\theta} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = \frac {1}{3} M l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} - \frac {1}{2} M l \dot {s} \cos (\theta - \alpha) \end{array}
$$

从上述两式解出 $\dot{s},\dot{\theta}$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\dot {s} = \frac {1}{M l \left[ 2 - \frac {3}{2} \cos^ {2} (\theta - \alpha) \right]} [ 2 l p _ {s} + 3 p _ {\theta} \cos (\theta - \alpha) ]} \\ & {\dot {\theta} = \frac {1}{M l ^ {2} \left[ 2 - \frac {3}{2} \cos^ {2} (\theta - \alpha) \right]} [ 3 l p _ {s} \cos (\theta - \alpha) + 6 p _ {\theta} ]} \\ & {H = p _ {s} \dot {s} + p _ {\theta} \dot {\theta} - L} \\ & {= \frac {1}{M l ^ {2} \left[ 2 - \frac {3}{2} \cos^ {2} (\theta - \alpha) \right]} \left[ 2 l ^ {2} p _ {s} ^ {2} - \frac {3}{2} l ^ {2} p _ {s} ^ {2} \cos^ {2} (\theta - \alpha) + 6 l p _ {s} p _ {\theta} \cos (\theta - \alpha) - \frac {9}{2} l p _ {s} p _ {\theta} \cos^ {3} (\theta - \alpha) + 6 p _ {\theta} ^ {2} - \frac {9}{2} p _ {\theta} ^ {2} \cos^ {2} (\theta - \alpha) \right] - M g (s \sin \alpha + \frac {l}{2} \cos \theta)} \end{array}
$$

由变形的哈密顿原理

$$
\delta \int [ p _ {s} \dot {s} + p _ {\theta} \dot {\theta} - H ] \mathrm{d} t = 0
$$

需满足欧拉方程

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial F}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial F}{\partial q _ {i}} = 0 \\ \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial F}{\partial \dot {p} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial F}{\partial p _ {i}} = 0 \end{array} \quad (i = 1, 2, \dots , s) \right.
$$

其中 $F = \sum_{i=1}^{s} p_i \dot{q}_i - H$ . 在这里 $F = p_s \dot{s} + p_\theta \dot{\theta} - H$ 

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left\{\frac {\partial}{\partial \dot {s}} [ p _ {s} \dot {s} + p _ {\theta} \dot {\theta} - H ] \right\} - \frac {\partial}{\partial s} [ p _ {s} \dot {s} + p _ {\theta} \dot {\theta} - H ] = 0
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left\{\frac {\partial}{\partial \dot {\theta}} [ p _ {s} \dot {s} + p _ {\theta} \dot {\theta} - H ] \right\} - \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta} [ p _ {s} \dot {s} + p _ {\theta} \dot {\theta} - H ] = 0
$$

实际上得到的运动微分方程就是哈密顿方程

$$
\dot {p} _ {s} = - \frac {\partial H}{\partial s}
$$

$$
\dot {p} _ {\theta} = - \frac {\partial H}{\partial \theta}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left\{\frac {\partial}{\partial \dot {p} _ {s}} [ p _ {s} \dot {s} + p _ {\theta} \dot {\theta} - H ] \right\} - \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {s}} [ p _ {s} \dot {s} + p _ {\theta} \dot {\theta} - H ] = 0
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left\{\frac {\partial}{\partial \dot {p} _ {\theta}} [ p _ {s} \dot {s} + p _ {\theta} \dot {\theta} - H ] \right\} - \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {\theta}} [ p _ {s} \dot {s} + p _ {\theta} \dot {\theta} - H ] = 0
$$

得到的运动微分方程就是哈密顿方程.

$$
\dot {s} = \frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {s}}
$$

$$
\dot {\theta} = \frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {\theta}}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/662f1b95e0a816bbabdc13900f5b1ee4177d9c8cf126556964f561c3a49dd398.jpg)


由11.1节的各题可知，由哈密顿正则方程得到的运动微分方程，和用拉格朗日方程得到的运动微分方程是完全相同的（用同样的广义坐标）以下略.

11.3.9 用哈密顿原理求 9.3.13 题所述系统的运动微分方程.

解 9.3.13 题已得到拉格朗日函数为


图11.6

\int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} (\delta L + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} Q _ {i} \delta q _ {i}) \mathrm{d} t = 0
$$

\begin{array}{r l} & {\int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} \Bigl \{\delta \Bigl [ \frac {1}{2} m (b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} - 2 b \dot {y} \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) - \frac {1}{2} k y ^ {2} - m g b (1 - \cos \theta) \Bigr ]} \\ & {\qquad + (- \gamma b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} + \gamma b \dot {y} \sin \theta) \delta \theta + (- \gamma \dot {y} + \gamma b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) \delta y \Bigr \} d t = 0} \\ & {\int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} \bigl \{m b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} \delta \dot {\theta} + m \dot {y} \delta \dot {y} - m b \dot {y} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta \delta \theta - m b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta \delta \dot {y}} \\ & {\qquad - m b \dot {y} \sin \theta \delta \dot {\theta} - k y \delta y - m g b \sin \theta \delta \theta + (- \gamma b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} + \gamma b \dot \sin \theta \delta \theta + (- \gamma b ^ {2} \dot {\theta}} \\ & {\qquad + \gamma b \dot {y} \sin \theta) \delta \theta + (- \gamma \dot {y} + \gamma b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) \delta y   d t = 0} $$

在 $t = t_0$ 和 $t = t_1, \delta \theta = 0, \delta y = 0$ ，积分号中被积函数中 $\delta \theta, \delta y$ 的系数分别为零，

$$
\begin{array}{l} - m b ^ {2} \ddot {\theta} - m b \dot {y} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (m b \dot {y} \sin \theta) - m g b \sin \theta - \gamma b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} + \gamma b \dot {y} \sin \theta = 0 \\ - m \ddot {y} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (m b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) - k y - \gamma \dot {y} + \gamma b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta = 0 \end{array}
$$

$$

与 9.3.13 题所得结果完全一样.

11.3.10 图 11.7 中质量为 m 的质点通过一个劲度系数为 k 的弹簧连在动坐标系 x=0 处，动系沿 x 正方向以 $s=vt+\frac{1}{3}at^{3}$ (其中 v、a 均为常量) 做直线运动，减震器对质点的作用力与相对速率的立方成正比，比例系数为 b，由哈密顿原理导出质点的运动微分方程.

解 设弹簧原长为 $l_{0}$ ，取静参考系，x 为广义坐标.

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {s} + \dot {x}) ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} m (v + a t ^ {2} + \dot {x}) ^ {2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{c} {V = \frac {1}{2} k (x - l _ {0}) ^ {2}} \\ {f = - b   \dot {x} ^ {3}, \qquad \delta W = f \mathrm{d} x} \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/1124d32e05f40f1a8903b55f4069473a4f29f98fe75be03f57995567fea2a78f.jpg)



图11.7


用非有势系统的哈密顿原理，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} (\delta L + \delta W) \mathrm{d} t = 0 \\ \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} \left\{\delta \left[ \frac {1}{2} m (v + a t ^ {2} + \dot {x}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k (x - l _ {0}) ^ {2} \right] - b \dot {x} ^ {3} \delta x \right\} \mathrm{d} t = 0 \\ \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} [ m (v + a t ^ {2} + \dot {x}) \delta \dot {x} - k (x - l _ {0}) \delta x - b \dot {x} ^ {3} \delta x ] \mathrm{d} t \\ = \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} \left\{\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} [ m (v + a t ^ {2} + \dot {x}) \delta x ] - \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} [ m (v + a t ^ {2} + \dot {x}) ] \delta x - k (x - l _ {0}) \delta x - b \dot {x} ^ {3} \delta x \right\} \mathrm{d} t \\ = m (v + a t ^ {2} + \dot {x}) \delta x \Bigg | _ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} - \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} [ m (2 a t + \ddot {x}) + k (x - l _ {0}) + b \dot {x} ^ {3} ] \delta x \mathrm{d} t = 0 \end{array}
$$

在 $t = t_0$ 和 $t = t_1, \delta x = 0$ ，积分中被积函数 $\delta x$ 的系数为零，即得运动微分方程

$$
m (2 a t + \ddot {x}) + k (x - l _ {0}) + b \dot {x} ^ {3} = 0
$$

11.3.11 一个具有单位质量的质点在外力 $F = -(x + t)$ 作用下沿 $x$ 轴运动，在 $t = 0$ 及 $t = 1$ 时均位于 $x = 0$ 处，用里茨法求出此问题的近似解，并与准确解作比较，比较 $t = 0.5$ 时的结果.
