提示: 近似解可用 $x(t)=t(1-t)(\alpha_{0}+\alpha_{1}t+\cdots+\alpha_{n}t^{n})$ 的形式, 仅要求对 n=0 作计算, 求精确解可作变换 $y=x+t$ .

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} T = & \frac {1}{2} \dot {x} ^ {2}, \quad V = - \frac {1}{2} (x + t) ^ {2} \\ & L = \frac {1}{2} \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (x + t) ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

由哈密顿原理或用拉格朗日方程可得运动微分方程为

$$
\ddot {x} = - (x + t)
$$

先求精确解，令 $y = x + t$ ，则 $x = y - t$ 

$$
\dot {x} = \dot {y} - 1, \quad \ddot {x} = \ddot {y}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \ddot {y} = - y \\ & y = c _ {1} \sin (t + c _ {2}) \\ & x = c _ {1} \sin (t + c _ {2}) - t \end{array}
$$

用 $t = 0$ 及 $t = 1$ 时 $x = 0$ 定出 $c_{1}, c_{2}$ , 可得

$$
c _ {1} = \frac {1}{\sin 1}, \quad c _ {2} = 0
$$

所以精确解为

$$
x = \frac {\sin t}{\sin 1} - t
$$

下面用里茨法求近似解：

$$
\int L \mathrm{d} t = \int \left[ \frac {1}{2} \dot {x} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} (x + t) ^ {2} \right] \mathrm{d} t
$$

设

$$
x (t) = \alpha_ {0} t (1 - t)
$$

$$
\dot {x} = \alpha_ {0} - 2 \alpha_ {0} t
$$

$$
\phi (\alpha_ {0}) = \int_ {0} ^ {1} \left[ \frac {1}{2} (\alpha_ {0} - 2 \alpha_ {0} t) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} (\alpha_ {0} t - \alpha_ {0} t ^ {2} + t) ^ {2} \right] d t
$$

经计算得

$$
\phi (\alpha_ {0}) = \frac {3}{2 0} \alpha_ {0} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{1 2} \alpha_ {0} - \frac {1}{6}
$$

由求 $\phi(\alpha_{0})$ 的极值确定 $\alpha_{0}$ 值，

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} \phi}{\mathrm{d} x _ {0}} = \frac {3}{1 0} \alpha_ {0} - \frac {1}{1 2} = 0
$$

$$
\alpha_ {0} = \frac {5}{1 8}
$$

近似解为

$$
x = \frac {5}{1 8} t (1 - t)
$$

t=0.5 时，精确解为 x=0.0697，近似解为 x=0.0694。

11.3.12 质点运动的拉格朗日函数为

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2})
$$

已知 t=0 时, r=2, $\varphi=0$ ; t=2 时, r=3, $\varphi=1$ . 试用里茨法求 t=1 时的质点位置的近似值, 并与精确值作比较.

提示：可采用图11.8(a)、(b)的折线所示的 $r(t)$ 和 $\varphi(t)$ 计算 $s = \int_0^2 L\mathrm{d}t$ ，由 $\frac{\partial s}{\partial\alpha} = 0$ $\frac{\partial s}{\partial\beta} = 0$ 决定待定的 $\alpha, \beta$ 值. 这样确定的 $r(t), \varphi(t)$ ，在 $t \neq 0, 1, 2$ 时，不能得到好的近似

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/216b488ea16dd1fb4b6611937ac77e460a6d73d60a611a8bc8d6075e18686068.jpg)



(a)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/ddd2d0e772f3c75250fe5393f5614a3fbf26ce6ceb93008953309295a7849f4d.jpg)



(b)



图11.8


值. 多考虑几点, 可得更好一点的近似.

解 采用提示的近似解，

$$
r (t) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} (\alpha - 2) t + 2 & 0 \leqslant t \leqslant 1 \\ (3 - \alpha) (t - 1) + \alpha & 1 \leqslant t \leqslant 2 \end{array} \right.
$$

$$
\varphi (t) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \beta t & 0 \leqslant t \leqslant 1 \\ (1 - \beta) (t - 1) + \beta & 1 \leqslant t \leqslant 2 \end{array} \right.
$$

$$
\dot {r} (t) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \alpha - 2 & 0 \leqslant t <   1 \\ 3 - \alpha & 1 <   t <   2 \end{array} \right.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \dot {\varphi} (t) & = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \beta & 0 <   t <   1 \\ 1 - \beta & 1 <   t <   2 \end{array} \right. \\ S & = \int_ {0} ^ {2} L \mathrm{d} t = \int_ {0} ^ {1} L \mathrm{d} t + \int_ {1} ^ {2} L \mathrm{d} t \\ & = \int_ {0} ^ {1} \frac {1}{2} \left\{(\alpha - 2) ^ {2} + [ (\alpha - 2) t + 2 ] ^ {2} \beta^ {2} \right\} \mathrm{d} t \\ & + \int_ {1} ^ {2} \frac {1}{2} \left\{(3 - \alpha) ^ {2} + [ (3 - \alpha) (t - 1) + \alpha ] ^ {2} (1 - \beta) ^ {2} \right\} \mathrm{d} t \end{array}
$$

经计算可得

$$
S = \frac {1}{2} \left\{1 6 - 9 \alpha + \frac {7}{3} \alpha^ {2} - 2 \left(3 + \alpha + \frac {1}{3} \alpha^ {2}\right) \beta + \left(\frac {1 3}{3} + \frac {5}{3} \alpha + \frac {2}{3} \alpha^ {2}\right) \beta^ {2} \right\}
$$

由 $\frac{\partial S}{\partial \alpha} = 0, \frac{\partial S}{\partial \beta} = 0$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & - 2 7 + 1 4 \alpha - 6 \beta - 4 \alpha \beta + 5 \beta^ {2} + 4 \alpha \beta^ {2} = 0 \\ & - 9 - 3 \alpha - \alpha^ {2} + 1 3 \beta + 5 \alpha \beta + 2 \alpha^ {2} \beta = 0 \end{array}
$$

由叠代法解得

$$
\alpha = 2. 2 0 7 6, \quad \beta = 0. 6 0 6 7
$$

即在 t=1 时, r=2.2076, $\varphi=0.6067$ .

下面求精确解. 从所给的拉格朗日函数可以看出, 质点未受外力, 做匀速直线运动,

$t = 0$ 时，

$$
x = r (0) \cos \varphi (0) = 2
$$

$$
y = r (0) \sin \varphi (0) = 0
$$

$t = 2$ 时， $x = r(2)\cos \varphi (2) = 3\cos 1 = 1.6209$ 

$$
y = r (2) \sin \varphi (2) = 3 \sin 1 = 2. 5 2 4 4
$$

$t = 1$ 时， $x = \frac{1}{2} [x(2) + x(0)] = 1.8105$ 

$$
y = \frac {1}{2} [ y (2) + y (0) ] = 1. 2 6 2 2
$$

$$
r = \sqrt {x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} = 2. 2 0 7 0
$$

$$
\varphi = \arctan \frac {y}{x} = 0. 6 0 8 8
$$

11.3.13 用莫培督原理重解 11.3.2 题.

解 用莫培督原理的雅可比形式

$$
\Delta W = \delta W = \delta \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} \sqrt {2 (E - V)} \sqrt {\sum_ {i , j = 1} ^ {s} a _ {i j} q _ {i} ^ {\prime} q _ {j} ^ {\prime}} d \tau = 0
$$

其中 $a_{ij}$ 是采用广义坐标 $q_{i}$ 时的惯性矩阵的元素， $\tau$ 为参量，可取时间、弧长等.

应满足欧拉方程或称雅可比方程

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial F}{\partial q _ {i} ^ {\prime}}\right) - \frac {\partial F}{\partial q _ {i}} = 0 (i = 1, 2, \dots , s)
$$

其中

$$
F = \sqrt {2 (E - V)} \sqrt {\sum_ {i , j = 1} ^ {s} a _ {i j} q _ {i} ^ {'} q _ {j} ^ {'}}
$$

今

$$
V = m g l (1 - \cos \theta) \approx \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}
$$

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} I \dot {\theta} ^ {2}
$$

$$
F = \sqrt {2 \left(E - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}\right)} \cdot \sqrt {I \dot {\theta} ^ {2}} = \sqrt {2 I} \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}} \dot {\theta}
$$

今取 $\tau = t$ 

$$
\frac {\partial F}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = \sqrt {2 I} \cdot \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}} = \sqrt {2 I} \sqrt {T} = I \dot {\theta}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial F}{\partial \theta} = \sqrt {2 I} \dot {\theta} \frac {\left(- \frac {1}{2} m g l\right) \cdot 2 \theta}{2 \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}}} = - \sqrt {2 I} \dot {\theta} \frac {m g l \theta}{2 \sqrt {\frac {1}{2} \mp \dot {\theta} ^ {2}}} = - m g l \theta
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial F}{\partial\dot{\theta}}\right) - \frac{\partial F}{\partial\theta} = 0$ ，得

$$
I \ddot {\theta} + m g l \theta = 0
$$

$$
\omega = \sqrt {\frac {m g l}{I}}
$$

周期为

$$
\frac {2 \pi}{\omega} = 2 \pi \sqrt {\frac {I}{m g l}}
$$

11.3.14 用莫培督原理重解 7.4.30 题.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/368da11ac7cd2464c5926fb0f08a724cf0227b981f8d1de4eb7e26f99f86dfcb.jpg)



图11.9


解 用 7.4.30 题已得到的动能和势能，

$$
\begin{array}{l} T = \left[ \frac {1}{2} m (a - b) ^ {2} + 2 M a ^ {2} \right] \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \\ V = \frac {1}{2} (m g b + k a ^ {2}) \varphi^ {2} \end{array}
$$

用上题做法,用莫培督原理的雅可比形式和雅可比方程,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Delta W & = \delta W = \delta \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t _ {1}} \sqrt {2 (E - V)} \cdot \sqrt {\sum_ {i , j = 1} ^ {s} a _ {i j} q _ {i} ^ {\prime} q _ {j} ^ {\prime}} \mathrm{d} \tau = 0 \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial F}{\partial q _ {i} ^ {\prime}}\right) - \frac {\partial F}{\partial q _ {i}} = 0 \quad (i = 1, 2, \dots , s) \end{array}
$$

今

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {F = \sqrt {2 \left(E - \frac {1}{2} (m g b + k a ^ {2}) \varphi^ {2}\right)} \cdot \sqrt {\left[ m (a - b) ^ {2} + 4 M a ^ {2} \right] \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}}} \\ & {\frac {\partial F}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = \sqrt {2 E - (m g b + k a ^ {2}) \varphi^ {2}} \cdot \sqrt {m (a - b) ^ {2} + 4 M a ^ {2}}} \\ & {\qquad = \sqrt {\left[ m (a - b) ^ {2} + 4 M a ^ {2} \right] \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}} \cdot \sqrt {m (a - b) ^ {2} + 4 M a ^ {2}}} \\ & {\qquad = \left[ m (a - b) ^ {2} + 4 M a ^ {2} \right] \dot {\varphi}} \end{array}
$$

其中用了 $T + V = E$ 

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {\partial F}{\partial \varphi} = \sqrt {m (a - b) ^ {2} + 4 M a ^ {2}} \dot {\varphi} \frac {- (m g b + k a ^ {2}) \cdot 2 \varphi}{2 \sqrt {2 E - (m g b + k a ^ {2}) \varphi^ {2}}}} \\ & {\quad = - \sqrt {m (a - b) ^ {2} + 4 M a ^ {2}} \dot {\varphi} \frac {m g b + k a ^ {2}}{\sqrt {m (a - b) ^ {2} + 4 M a ^ {2}} \dot {\varphi}} \varphi} \\ & {\quad = - (m g b + k a ^ {2}) \varphi} \\ & {\qquad [ m (a - b) ^ {2} + 4 M a ^ {2} ] \ddot {\varphi} + (m g b + k a ^ {2}) \varphi = 0} \\ & {\qquad \omega = \sqrt {\frac {m g b + k a ^ {2}}{m (a - b) ^ {2} + 4 M a ^ {2}}}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {2 \pi}{\omega} = 2 \pi \sqrt {\frac {m (a - b) ^ {2} + 4 M a ^ {2}}{m g b + k a ^ {2}}}} \\ & {\qquad \text {或} 2 \pi \sqrt {\frac {(4 M + m) a ^ {2} + m b (b - 2 a)}{m g b + k a ^ {2}}}} \end{array}
$$

周期为

11.3.15 用莫培督原理的雅可比形式推导有心运动的运动微分方程.

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) \\ & V = V (r) \\ & F = \sqrt {2 (E - V)} \cdot \sqrt {m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2})} \\ & \frac {\partial F}{\partial \dot {r}} = \sqrt {2 (E - V)} \cdot \frac {m \dot {r}}{\sqrt {m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2})}} = m \dot {r} \\ & E - V = T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) \end{array}
$$

这里用了

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {\partial F}{\partial r} = \sqrt {m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2})} \frac {2}{2 \sqrt {2 (E - V)}} \Big (- \frac {\partial V}{\partial r} \Big)} \\ & {\qquad + \sqrt {2 (E - V)} \cdot \frac {2 m r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}}{2 \sqrt {m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2})}}} \\ & {\qquad = - \frac {\partial V}{\partial r} + m r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\partial F}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = \sqrt {2 (E - V)} \frac {2 m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi}}{2 \sqrt {m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2})}} = m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \Big (\frac {\partial F}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} \Big) = m r ^ {2} \ddot {\varphi} + 2 m \dot {r} \dot {\varphi}} \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial F}{\partial\dot{r}}\right) - \frac{\partial F}{\partial r} = 0$ 及 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial F}{\partial\dot{\varphi}}\right) - \frac{\partial F}{\partial\varphi} = 0$ ，可得运动微分方程为

$$
m (\ddot {r} - r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) = - \frac {\partial V}{\partial r} = f (r)
$$

$$
m (r \ddot {\varphi} + 2 \dot {r} \dot {\varphi}) = 0
$$

后式也可写成

$$
m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = \text {常量}.
$$

## 11.4 正则变换

11.4.1 试证

$$
\begin{array}{l} {Q = \ln \left(\frac {1}{q} \sin p\right)} \\ {P = - q \omega t p} \end{array}
$$

为一正则变换.

证明 用 $p, Q$ 为自变量的正则变换条件

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {- \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} q _ {i} \mathrm{d} p _ {i} - c \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} P _ {i} \mathrm{d} Q _ {i} = \mathrm{d} S (\boldsymbol {p}, \boldsymbol {Q}, t _ {0})} \\ & {\qquad Q = \ln \left(\frac {1}{q} \sin p\right)} \\ & {\qquad \frac {1}{q} \sin p = \mathrm{e} ^ {Q}} \\ & {\qquad q = \sin p \mathrm{e} ^ {- Q}} \\ & {\qquad P = - q \cot p = - \sin p \mathrm{e} ^ {- Q} \cot p = - \cos p \mathrm{e} ^ {- Q}} \\ & {- q \mathrm{d} p - c P \mathrm{d} Q = - \sin p \mathrm{e} ^ {- Q} \mathrm{d} p + c \cos p \mathrm{e} ^ {- Q} \mathrm{d} Q} \end{array}
$$

是恰当微分,因为

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial Q} (- \sin p e ^ {- Q}) = \sin p e ^ {- Q}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial p} (c \cos p e ^ {- Q}) = - c \sin p e ^ {- Q}
$$

选 $c = -1$ ，有

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial Q} (- \sin p e ^ {- Q}) = \frac {\partial}{\partial p} (- \cos p e ^ {- Q})
$$

正则变换条件成立,所给变换是正则变换.

11.4.2 证明下列变换都是正则变换：

(1) $Q=q^{2}+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}},P=\frac{n}{2}\arctan\left(\frac{p}{nq}\right)$ ，式中n是一个常数；

(2) 

$$
Q _ {1} = q _ {1} ^ {2} + p _ {1} ^ {2}, \quad Q _ {2} = \frac {1}{2} (q _ {1} ^ {2} + q _ {2} ^ {2} + p _ {1} ^ {2} + p _ {2} ^ {2})
$$

$$
P _ {1} = \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {q _ {2}}{p _ {2}}\right) - \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {q _ {1}}{p _ {1}}\right)
$$

$$
P _ {2} = - \arctan \left(\frac {q _ {2}}{p _ {2}}\right)
$$

证明 (1)

$$
Q = q ^ {2} + \frac {p ^ {2}}{n ^ {2}}, P = \frac {n}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {p}{n q}\right)
$$

用正则变换条件，

$$
p \mathrm{d} q - c P \mathrm{d} Q = \mathrm{d} S
$$

$$
p = n \sqrt {Q - q ^ {2}}, \quad P = \frac {n}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {Q - q ^ {2}}}{q}\right)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial p}{\partial Q} = \frac {n}{2 \sqrt {Q - q ^ {2}}}
$$

$$
{\frac {\partial P}{\partial q}} = {\frac {n}{2}} {\frac {1}{1 + \left({\frac {{\sqrt {Q - q ^ {2}}}}{q}}\right) ^ {2}}} \left({\frac {1}{q}} {\frac {- 2 q}{2 {\sqrt {Q - q ^ {2}}}}} - {\frac {{\sqrt {Q - q ^ {2}}}}{q ^ {2}}}\right) = - {\frac {n}{2 {\sqrt {Q - q ^ {2}}}}}
$$

选 $c = 1$ ，满足 $p\mathrm{d}q - P\mathrm{d}Q = \mathrm{d}S$ ，所给变换是正则变换.

(2) 

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {1} = q _ {1} ^ {2} + p _ {1} ^ {2}, \quad Q _ {2} = \frac {1}{2} (q _ {1} ^ {2} + q _ {2} ^ {2} + p _ {1} ^ {2} + p _ {2} ^ {2}) \\ P _ {1} = \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {q _ {2}}{p _ {2}}\right) - \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {q _ {1}}{p _ {1}}\right) \\ P _ {2} = - \arctan \left(\frac {q _ {2}}{p _ {2}}\right) \end{array}
$$

用正则变换条件：

$$
- \sum_ {i} q _ {i} \mathrm{d} p _ {i} - c \sum_ {i} P _ {i} \mathrm{d} Q _ {i} = \mathrm{d} S
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {q _ {1} = \sqrt {Q _ {1} - p _ {1} ^ {2}}} \\ & {q _ {2} = \sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - q _ {1} ^ {2} - p _ {1} ^ {2} - p _ {2} ^ {2}} = \sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - (Q - p _ {1} ^ {2}) - p _ {1} ^ {2} - p _ {2} ^ {2}} = \sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}} \\ & {P _ {1} = \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{p _ {2}}\right) - \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {Q _ {1} - p _ {1} ^ {2}}}{p _ {1}}\right)} \\ & {P _ {2} = - \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{p _ {2}}\right)} \\ & {q _ {1} \mathrm{d} p _ {1} + q _ {2} \mathrm{d} p _ {2} + c P _ {1} \mathrm{d} Q _ {1} + c P _ {2} \mathrm{d} Q _ {2}} \\ & {\quad = \sqrt {Q _ {1} - p _ {1} ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} p _ {1} + \sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} p _ {2}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & + c \left[ \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{p _ {2}}\right) - \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {Q _ {1} - p _ {1} ^ {2}}}{p _ {1}}\right) \right] \mathrm{d} Q _ {1} \\ & - c \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{p _ {2}}\right) \mathrm{d} Q _ {2} \\ & \quad \frac {\partial \sqrt {Q _ {1} - p _ {1} ^ {2}}}{\partial p _ {2}} = \frac {\partial \sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{\partial p _ {1}} = 0 \end{array}
$$

可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\partial \sqrt {Q _ {1} - p _ {1} ^ {2}}}{\partial Q _ {1}} & = \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {1}} \left[ \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{p _ {2}}\right) - \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {Q _ {1} - p _ {1} ^ {2}}}{p _ {1}}\right) \right. \\ & = \frac {1}{2 \sqrt {Q _ {1} - p _ {1} ^ {2}}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \sqrt {Q _ {1} - p _ {1} ^ {2}}}{\partial Q _ {2}} = \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {1}} \left[ - \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{p _ {2}}\right) \right] = 0
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\partial \sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{\partial Q _ {1}} & = \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {2}} \left[ \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{p _ {2}}\right) - \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {Q _ {1} - p _ {1} ^ {2}}}{p _ {1}}\right) \right. \\ & = - \frac {1}{2 \sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\partial \sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{\partial Q _ {2}} & = \frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {2}} \left[ - \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{p _ {2}}\right) \right] = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {\partial}{\partial Q _ {2}} \left[ \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{p _ {2}}\right) - \frac {1}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {Q _ {1} - p _ {1} ^ {2}}}{p _ {1}}\right) \right] \\ & = \frac {\partial}{\partial Q _ {1}} \left[ - \arctan \left(\frac {\sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}}{p _ {2}}\right) \right] = \frac {p _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 (2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1}) \sqrt {2 Q _ {2} - Q _ {1} - p _ {2} ^ {2}}} \end{array}
$$

可见， $c = 1$ 

$$
- \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} q _ {i} \mathrm{d} p _ {i} - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} P _ {i} \mathrm{d} Q _ {i} = \mathrm{d} S
$$

成立,所给变换为正则变换.

11.4.3 证明 $Q = q + te^{p}, P = p$ 是一正则变换，找出 $S(q, Q, t)$ .

解 方法一：

$$
Q = q + t \mathrm{e} ^ {p}, P = p
$$

用

$$
p \mathrm{d} q - c P \mathrm{d} Q = \mathrm{d} S (q, Q, t)
$$

证明

$$
p = \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right), \quad P = p = \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right)
$$

$$
p \mathrm{d} q - c P \mathrm{d} Q = \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \mathrm{d} q - c \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \mathrm{d} Q
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial Q} \left[ \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \right] = \frac {t}{Q - q} \cdot \frac {1}{t} = \frac {1}{Q - q}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial q} \left[ - c \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \right] = - c \frac {t}{Q - q} \cdot \left(- \frac {1}{t}\right) = \frac {c}{Q - q}
$$

$c = 1$ ，两偏导数相等，

$$
\ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \mathrm{d} q - \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \mathrm{d} Q = \mathrm{d} S (q, Q, t)
$$

成立,变换为正则变换得到证明.

$$
\frac {\partial S}{\partial q} = \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right), \quad \frac {\partial S}{\partial Q} = - \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right)
$$

对前式积分，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {S = \int \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \mathrm{d} q + f (Q)} \\ & {\quad = q \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) - \int q \mathrm{d} \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) + f (Q)} \\ & {\quad = q \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) - \int q \cdot \frac {1}{Q - q} (- \mathrm{d} q) + f (Q)} \\ & {\quad = q \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) + \int \frac {- Q + q}{Q - q} \mathrm{d} q + Q \int \frac {1}{Q - q} \mathrm{d} q + f (Q)} \\ & {\quad = q \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) - q - Q \ln (Q - q) + f (Q)} \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/c028c88f471bb0e2213482c848697d0b1f21a7bc9b3ca86c4ffb47382844c6b6.jpg)


上式对 $Q$ 求偏导，与后式的 $\frac{\partial S}{\partial Q}$ 相等，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {q}{Q - q} - \ln (Q - q) - \frac {Q}{Q - q} + \frac {\mathrm{d} f (Q)}{\mathrm{d} Q} = - \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right)} \\ & {\quad \frac {\mathrm{d} f (Q)}{\mathrm{d} Q} = 1 + \ln t} \\ & {\quad f (Q) = \int (1 + \ln t) \mathrm{d} Q = Q (1 + \ln t)} \end{array}
$$


图11.10


所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} S & = q \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) - q - Q \ln (Q - q) + Q (1 + \ln t) \\ & = (Q - q) \left[ 1 - \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \right] \end{array}
$$

方法二:对于恰当微分,可在由自变量张成的空间内取便于积分的路径积出全微分的函数.取图11.10所示的积分路线,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & S = \int \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \mathrm{d} q - \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \mathrm{d} Q \\ & = \int_ {q _ {0}} ^ {q} \ln \left(\frac {Q _ {0} - q}{t}\right) \mathrm{d} q - \int_ {Q _ {0}} ^ {Q} \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \mathrm{d} Q \\ & = q \ln \left(\frac {Q _ {0} - q}{t}\right) \bigg | _ {q _ {0}} ^ {q} - \int_ {q _ {0}} ^ {q} \frac {q}{Q _ {0} - q} (- \mathrm{d} q) - Q \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \bigg | _ {q _ {0}} ^ {q} + \int_ {q _ {0}} ^ {q} \frac {Q}{Q - q} \mathrm{d} Q \\ & = q \ln \left(\frac {Q _ {0} - q}{t}\right) - q _ {0} \ln \left(\frac {Q _ {0} - q _ {0}}{t}\right) - (q - q _ {0}) - Q _ {0} \ln \left(\frac {Q _ {0} - q}{Q _ {0} - q _ {0}}\right) \\ & \quad - Q \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) + Q _ {0} \ln \left(\frac {Q _ {0} - q}{t}\right) + (Q - Q _ {0}) + q \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{Q _ {0} - q}\right) \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & = q \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) - Q \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) + (Q - q) - q _ {0} \ln \left(\frac {Q _ {0} - q _ {0}}{t}\right) \\ & \quad + Q _ {0} \ln \left(\frac {Q _ {0} - q _ {0}}{t}\right) - (Q _ {0} - q _ {0}) \\ & = (Q - q) \left[ 1 - \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \right] + F (q _ {0}, Q _ {0}, t) \end{array}
$$

在作上述积分时， $t$ 是视为常量的，因此上式中的 $F(q_0, Q_0, t)$ 是常量，可以去掉，但与 $Q$ 或 $q$ 不能分离的 $t$ 为参量，不能去掉，所以

$$
S = (Q - q) \left[ 1 - \ln \left(\frac {Q - q}{t}\right) \right]
$$

11.4.4 证明

$$
\begin{array}{c} Q = \ln (1 + q ^ {1 / 2} \cos p) \\ P = 2 (1 + q ^ {1 / 2} \cos p) q ^ {1 / 2} \sin p \end{array}
$$

是正则变换,证明产生这正则变换的母函数为

$$
S = - \left(\mathrm{e} ^ {Q} - 1\right) ^ {2} \tan p
$$

证明 我们用满足

$$
[ Q _ {i}, Q _ {j} ] = 0, \quad [ p _ {i}, p _ {j} ] = 0 \quad [ Q _ {i}, P _ {j} ] = c \delta_ {i j}
$$

(c 可为任一非零常数)的为正则变换来证明.

$$
[ Q, Q ] = 0, \quad [ P, P ] = 0
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} {[ Q, P ]} & {= \frac {\partial Q}{\partial q} \frac {\partial P}{\partial p} - \frac {\partial Q}{\partial p} \frac {\partial P}{\partial q} = \frac {\frac {1}{2} q ^ {- 1 / 2} \cos p}{1 + q ^ {1 / 2} \cos p} \cdot (- 2 q \sin^ {2} p + 2 q \cos^ {2} p + 2 q ^ {1 / 2} \cos p)} \\ & {\quad + \frac {q ^ {1 / 2} \sin p}{1 + q ^ {1 / 2} \cos p} (2 \sin p \cos p + q ^ {- 1 / 2} \sin p) = 1} \end{array}
$$

变换是正则变换已得到证明.

因为母函数是以 $Q, p$ 为独立变量，相应的正则变换条件为

$$
- q \mathrm{d} p - P \mathrm{d} Q = \mathrm{d} S (p, Q)
$$

$$
q = - \frac {\partial S}{\partial p} = (\mathrm{e} ^ {Q} - 1) ^ {2} \sec^ {2} p\tag{1}
$$

$$
P = - \frac {\partial S}{\partial Q} = 2 e ^ {Q} (e ^ {Q} - 1) \tan p\tag{2}
$$

母函数产生的上述正则变换,正是题目要证明的那个正则变换,从式(1)解出Q,得

$$
Q = \ln (1 + q ^ {1 / 2} \cos p)
$$

将它代入式(2)得

$$
P = 2 (1 + q ^ {1 / 2} \cos p) q ^ {1 / 2} \sin p
$$

就是题目所给的变换.

11.4.5 用拉格朗日括号检验 11.4.2 题的变换是正则变换.

解 用拉格朗日括号表述的正则变换条件为

$$
\begin{array}{r l r} {( q _ {i}, q _ {j}) = 0,} & {} & {(p _ {i}, p _ {j}) = 0} \\ {(q _ {i}, p _ {j}) = c \delta_ {i j}} & {} & {c \text {为非零常数}.} \end{array}
$$

其中 $(r_i, r_j) = \sum_k \left[ \frac{\partial Q_k(q, p, t)}{\partial r_i} \cdot \frac{\partial P_k(q, p, t)}{\partial r_j} - \frac{\partial Q_k(q, p, t)}{\partial r_j} \cdot \frac{\partial P_k(q, p, t)}{\partial r_i} \right]$ 

今

$$
Q = q ^ {2} + \frac {p ^ {2}}{n ^ {2}}, P = \frac {n}{2} \arctan \left(\frac {p}{n q}\right)
$$

$$
(q, q) = \frac {\partial Q}{\partial q} \frac {\partial P}{\partial q} - \frac {\partial Q}{\partial q} \frac {\partial P}{\partial q} = 0
$$

同样 $(p, p) = 0$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (q, p) & = \frac {\partial Q}{\partial q} \frac {\partial P}{\partial p} - \frac {\partial Q}{\partial p} \frac {\partial P}{\partial q} \\ & = 2 q \cdot \frac {n}{2} \frac {\frac {1}{n q}}{1 + \left(\frac {p}{n q}\right) ^ {2}} - \frac {2 p}{n ^ {2}} \cdot \frac {n}{2} \frac {\left(- \frac {p}{n q ^ {2}}\right)}{1 + \left(\frac {p}{n q}\right) ^ {2}} = 1 \end{array}
$$

证毕.

11.4.6 $\alpha, \beta$ 为何值时，变换

$$
Q = q ^ {a} \cos \beta p, \quad P = q ^ {a} \sin \beta p
$$

为一正则变换.

解 方法一:用正则变换条件:

从

$$
p \mathrm{d} q + c Q \mathrm{d} P = \mathrm{d} S
$$

得

$$
Q = q ^ {\alpha} \cos \beta p, \quad P = q ^ {\alpha} \sin \beta p
$$

$$
P = \frac {1}{\beta} \arcsin (P q ^ {- a})
$$

$$
Q = q ^ {\alpha} \sqrt {1 - P ^ {2} q ^ {- 2 \alpha}}
$$

$$
p \mathrm{d} q + c Q \mathrm{d} P = \frac {1}{\beta} \arcsin (P q ^ {- \alpha}) \mathrm{d} q + c q ^ {\alpha} \sqrt {1 - P ^ {2} q ^ {- 2 \alpha}} \mathrm{d} P
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial P} \left[ \frac {1}{\beta} \arcsin (P q ^ {- \alpha}) \right] = \frac {1}{\beta} \frac {1}{\sqrt {q ^ {2 \alpha} - P ^ {2}}}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial q} \left[ c q ^ {\alpha} \sqrt {1 - P ^ {2} q ^ {- 2 \alpha}} \right] = \frac {c \alpha q ^ {2 \alpha - 1}}{\sqrt {q ^ {2 \alpha} - P ^ {2}}}
$$

变换为正则变换,必须两偏导数相等,

$$
\frac {1}{\beta} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {q ^ {2 \alpha} - P ^ {2}}} = \frac {c \alpha q ^ {2 \alpha - 1}}{\sqrt {q ^ {2 \alpha} - P ^ {2}}}
$$

即

$$
\frac {1}{\beta} = c \alpha q ^ {2 \alpha - 1}
$$

$\alpha=\frac{1}{2}$ ， $\beta=\frac{1}{c\alpha}=\frac{2}{c}=c'$ （ $c'$ 为任何非零常数均可）.

方法二:用 11.4.4 题采用的方法.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} {[ Q, P ]} & {= \frac {\partial Q}{\partial q} \frac {\partial P}{\partial p} - \frac {\partial Q}{\partial p} \frac {\partial P}{\partial q}} \\ & {= \alpha q ^ {\alpha - 1} \cos \beta p \cdot \beta q ^ {\alpha} \cos \beta p - q ^ {\alpha} (- \beta \sin \beta p) \cdot \alpha q ^ {\alpha - 1} \sin \beta p} \\ & {= \alpha \beta q ^ {2 \alpha - 1}} \end{array}
$$

正则变换要求 $[Q, P] = C (C$ 为非零常数)也得到 $\alpha = \frac{1}{2}, \beta$ 为任意非零常数的结论.

11.4.7 证明变换

$$
\begin{array}{l l} Q _ {1} = a _ {1 1} q _ {1} + a _ {1 2} q _ {2}, & Q _ {2} = a _ {2 1} q _ {1} + a _ {2 2} q _ {2} \\ P _ {1} = b _ {1 1} p _ {1} + b _ {1 2} p _ {2}, & P _ {2} = b _ {2 1} p _ {1} + b _ {2 2} p _ {2} \end{array}
$$

在 $b_{ij} = |A_{ij}| / |A|$ 对所有 $i, j$ 均成立，其中 $|A|$ 是矩阵 $(a_{ij})$ 的行列式， $|A_{ij}|$ 是矩阵元 $a_{ij}$ 的代数余子式，且所有 $a_{ij}$ 和 $b_{ij}$ 都是常数这些条件下是正则变换。

证明

$$
b _ {1 1} = \frac {a _ {2 2}}{| A |}, \quad b _ {1 2} = - \frac {a _ {2 1}}{| A |}
$$

$$
b _ {2 1} = - \frac {a _ {1 2}}{| A |}, \quad b _ {2 2} = \frac {a _ {1 1}}{| A |}
$$

$$
P _ {1} = \frac {a _ {2 2}}{| A |} p _ {1} - \frac {a _ {2 1}}{| A |} p _ {2}, \quad P _ {2} = - \frac {a _ {1 2}}{| A |} p _ {1} + \frac {a _ {1 1}}{| A |} p _ {2}
$$

显然有

$$
[ Q _ {i}, Q _ {j} ] = 0, \qquad [ P _ {i}, P _ {j} ] = 0
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} [ Q _ {1}, P _ {1} ] & = \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} + \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} \\ & = a _ {1 1} \frac {a _ {2 2}}{| A |} + a _ {1 2} \left(- \frac {a _ {2 1}}{| A |}\right) = 1 \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} [ Q _ {1}, P _ {2} ] & = \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {3}}{\partial p _ {1}} + \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} \\ & = a _ {1 1} \left(- \frac {a _ {1 2}}{| A |}\right) + a _ {1 2} \frac {a _ {1 1}}{| A |} = 0 \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \left[ Q _ {2} ^ {*}, P _ {1} \right] & = \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} + \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial p _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} \\ & = a _ {2 1} \frac {a _ {2 2}}{| A |} + a _ {2 2} \left(- \frac {a _ {2 1}}{| A |}\right) = 0 \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} [ Q _ {2}, P _ {2} ] & = \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {1}} + \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial p _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} \\ & = a _ {2 1} \left(- \frac {a _ {1 2}}{| A |}\right) + a _ {2 2} \frac {a _ {1 1}}{| A |} = 1 \end{array}
$$

证毕.

11.4.8 (1) 要使

$$
Q _ {1} = q _ {1} ^ {2}, Q _ {2} = q _ {1} + q _ {2}
$$

$$
P _ {1} = P _ {1} \left(q _ {1}, q _ {2}, p _ {1}, p _ {2}\right), \quad P _ {2} = P _ {2} \left(q _ {1}, q _ {2}, p _ {1}, p _ {2}\right)
$$

为一正则变换, 找出 $P_{1}, P_{2}$ 最一般的函数形式.

提示: 可用拉格朗日括号表述的正则变换条件给出的偏微分方程组求解.

(2) 对上述正则变换,作何种特别的选择,可将

$$
H = \left(\frac {p _ {1} - p _ {2}}{2 q _ {1}}\right) ^ {2} + p _ {2} + (q _ {1} + q _ {2}) ^ {2}
$$

变换为

$$
H ^ {*} = P _ {1} ^ {2} + P _ {2}
$$

并用此题验证

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L ^ {*}}{\partial \dot {Q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L ^ {*}}{\partial Q _ {i}} = 0
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L ^ {*}}{\partial \dot {P} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L ^ {*}}{\partial P _ {i}} = 0
$$

成立.

解 （1）用拉格朗日括号表述的正则变换条件： $(q_{i}, q_{j}) = 0, (p_{i}, p_{j}) = 0, (q_{i}, p_{j}) = c\delta_{ij}$ .

$$
Q _ {1} = q _ {1} ^ {2}, \quad Q _ {2} = q _ {1} + q _ {2},
$$

$$
P _ {1} = P _ {1} (q _ {1}, q _ {2}, p _ {1}, p _ {2}), \quad P _ {2} = P _ {2} (q _ {1}, q _ {2}, p _ {1}, p _ {2})
$$

由

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (q _ {1}, q _ {2}) & = \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} + \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} \\ & = 2 q _ {1} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} + \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} - \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} = 0 \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

$$
(p _ {1}, p _ {2}) = \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} + \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial p _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {1}} = 0
$$

因 $\frac{\partial Q_1}{\partial p_1} = \frac{\partial Q_2}{\partial p_1} = \frac{\partial Q_1}{\partial p_2} = \frac{\partial Q_2}{\partial p_2} = 0$ ，此式没对 $\frac{\partial P_1}{\partial p_1}, \frac{\partial P_1}{\partial p_2}, \frac{\partial P_2}{\partial p_1}, \frac{\partial P_2}{\partial p_2}$ 间给出任何关系.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (q _ {1}, p _ {1}) & = \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} + \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {1}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial p _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} \\ & = 2 q _ {1} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} + \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {1}} = C \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (q _ {1}, p _ {2}) & = \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} + \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} \\ & = 2 q _ {1} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} + \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} = 0 \end{array}\tag{3}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (q _ {2}, p _ {1}) & = \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} + \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {1}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial p _ {1}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} \\ & = \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {1}} = 0 \end{array}\tag{4}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (q _ {2}, p _ {2}) & = \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} + \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} - \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} \\ & = \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} = C \end{array}\tag{5}
$$

由(4)、(5)两式,得

$$
P _ {2} = C p _ {2} + f (q _ {1}, q _ {2})\tag{6}
$$

将式(6)代入式(1)，

$$
2 q _ {1} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} + \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {2}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {1}} = 0\tag{7}
$$

将式(4)代入式(2)，

$$
2 q _ {1} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} = C\tag{8}
$$

将式(5)代入式(3)，

$$
2 q _ {1} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} = - C\tag{9}
$$

由式(7)、(8)、(9)， $dP_{1}$ 可写成

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \mathrm{d} P _ {1} & = \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} \mathrm{d} q _ {1} + \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} \mathrm{d} q _ {2} + \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} \mathrm{d} p _ {1} + \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial p _ {2}} \mathrm{d} p _ {2} \\ & = \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} \mathrm{d} q _ {1} + \frac {1}{2 q _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {2}}\right) \mathrm{d} q _ {2} + \frac {C}{2 q _ {1}} \mathrm{d} p _ {1} - \frac {C}{2 q _ {2}} \mathrm{d} p _ {2} \end{array}\tag{10}
$$

根据式(10)，设想 $P_{1}$ 为

$$
P _ {1} = \frac {C (p _ {1} - p _ {2})}{2 q _ {1}} + \frac {1}{2 q _ {1}} \int \left(\frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {2}}\right) d q _ {2} + h (q _ {1})\tag{11}
$$

它显然满足式(10). 如能满足恰当微分条件, 又满足式(1), 则设想的 $P_{1}$ 是对的.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} = & - \frac {C (p _ {1} - p _ {2})}{2 q _ {1} ^ {2}} - \frac {1}{2 q _ {1} ^ {2}} \int \left(\frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {2}}\right) \mathrm{d} q _ {2} \\ & + \frac {1}{2 q _ {1}} \int \left(\frac {\partial^ {2} f}{\partial q _ {1} ^ {2}} - \frac {\partial^ {2} f}{\partial q _ {1} \partial q _ {2}}\right) \mathrm{d} q _ {2} + \frac {\mathrm{d} h}{\mathrm{d} q _ {1}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial q _ {2}} \left(\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}}\right) = - \frac {1}{2 q _ {1} ^ {2}} \left(\frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {2}}\right) + \frac {1}{2 q _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial^ {2} f}{\partial q _ {1} ^ {2}} - \frac {\partial^ {2} f}{\partial q _ {1} \partial q _ {2}}\right)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} = \frac {1}{2 q _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {2}}\right)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial q _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}}\right) = - \frac {1}{2 q _ {1} ^ {2}} \left(\frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {2}}\right) + \frac {1}{2 q _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial^ {2} f}{\partial q _ {1} ^ {2}} - \frac {\partial^ {2} f}{\partial q _ {1} \partial q _ {2}}\right)
$$

有

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial q _ {2}} \left(\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}}\right) = \frac {\partial}{\partial q _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}}\right)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}}\right) = - \frac {C}{2 q _ {1} ^ {2}}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}} = \frac {C}{2 q _ {1}}, \quad \frac {\partial}{\partial q _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}}\right) = - \frac {C}{2 q _ {1} ^ {2}}
$$

有

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}}\right) = \frac {\partial}{\partial q _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {1}}\right)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {2}} \left(\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}}\right) = \frac {C}{2 q _ {1} ^ {2}}, \quad \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}} = - \frac {C}{2 q _ {1}}, \quad \frac {\partial}{\partial q _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}}\right) = \frac {C}{2 q _ {1} ^ {2}}
$$

有

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial p _ {2}} \left(\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}}\right) = \frac {\partial}{\partial q _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial p _ {2}}\right)
$$

其余应相等的偏导数都是 0=0 的恒等式.

式(11)和式(6)也满足式(1)的要求，

$$
2 q _ {1} \frac {\partial P _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} + \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {2}} - \frac {\partial P _ {2}}{\partial q _ {1}} = 2 q _ {1} \cdot \frac {1}{2 q _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {2}}\right) + \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {2}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {1}} = 0
$$

所以 $P_{1}, P_{2}$ 的最一般表达式分别为式(11)和式(6). 其中 $C$ 是任意的非零常数, $f$ 是 $q_{1}, q_{2}$ 的任意函数, $h$ 是 $q_{1}$ 的任意函数.

(2) 

$$
p _ {1} \mathrm{d} q _ {1} + p _ {2} \mathrm{d} q _ {2} - P _ {1} \mathrm{d} Q _ {1} - P _ {2} \mathrm{d} Q _ {2} = \mathrm{d} S (\boldsymbol {q}, \boldsymbol {Q})
$$

因为正则变换中不显含 $t$ ，故 $S$ 中也不显含 $t$ . 又取 $C = 1$ ，正则变换后的哈密顿函数 $H^{*}$ 与原哈密顿函数 $H$ 相等，

$$
H ^ {*} = H
$$

变换后要求

$$
H ^ {*} = P _ {1} ^ {2} + P _ {2}
$$

$$
P _ {1} ^ {2} + P _ {2} = \left(\frac {p _ {1} - p _ {2}}{2 q _ {1}}\right) ^ {2} + p _ {2} + (q _ {1} + q _ {2}) ^ {2}
$$

将式(11)、式(6)的 $P_{1}, P_{2}$ 代入上式，

$$
C = 1, h (q _ {1}) = 0, \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {1}} - \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {2}} = 0, f = (q _ {1} + q _ {2}) ^ {2}
$$

这个 $f = (q_{1} + q_{2})^{2}$ ，确有 $\frac{\partial f}{\partial q_1} -\frac{\partial f}{\partial q_2} = 0.$ 

因此要使变换后 $H^{*} = P_{1}^{2} + P_{2}$ ，用的正则变换为

$$
P _ {1} = \frac {p _ {1} - p _ {2}}{2 q _ {1}}, \quad P _ {2} = p _ {2} + (q _ {1} + q _ {2}) ^ {2}
$$

$$
H ^ {*} = P _ {1} \dot {Q} _ {1} + P _ {2} \dot {Q} _ {2} - L ^ {*}
$$

$$
L ^ {*} = P _ {1} \dot {Q} _ {1} + P _ {2} \dot {Q} _ {2} - H ^ {*} = P _ {1} \dot {Q} _ {1} + P _ {2} \dot {Q} _ {2} - P _ {1} ^ {2} - P _ {2}
$$

由正则方程得

$$
\dot {Q} _ {1} = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial P _ {1}} = 2 P _ {1}, \quad \dot {Q} _ {2} = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial P _ {2}} = 1
$$

$$
\dot {P} _ {1} = - \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial Q _ {1}} = 0, \quad \dot {P} _ {2} = - \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial Q _ {2}} = 0
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L ^ {*}}{\partial \dot {Q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L ^ {*}}{\partial Q _ {i}} = 0, \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L ^ {*}}{\partial \dot {P} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L ^ {*}}{\partial P _ {i}} = 0 (i = 1, 2)
$$

得

$$
\begin{array}{r l r} \dot {P} _ {1} = 0, & \quad \dot {P} _ {2} = 0 \\ \dot {Q} _ {1} - 2 P _ {1} = 0, & \quad \dot {Q} _ {2} - 1 = 0 \end{array}
$$

两组方程结果相同，正则方程成立，可见要验证的 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L^*}{\partial\dot{Q}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L^*}{\partial Q_i} = 0, \frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L^*}{\partial\dot{P}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L^*}{\partial P_i} = 0$ 成立.

11.4.9 求出下列母函数产生的正则变换：

(1) $S_{1}(\pmb {q},\pmb {Q},t) = \sum_{i = 1}^{s}q_{i}Q_{i};$ 

(2) $S_{2}(p, Q, t) = -\tan p(\mathrm{e}^{Q} - 1)$ ; 

(3) $S_{3}(\pmb {q},\pmb {P},t) = \sum_{i = 1}^{s}q_{i}P_{i};$ 

(4) $S_{4}(\pmb {p},\pmb {P},t) = \sum_{i = 1}^{s}p_{i}P_{i}.$ 

解 (1)

$$
S _ {1} (\boldsymbol {q}, \boldsymbol {Q}, t) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} q _ {i} Q _ {i}
$$

由 $\sum_{i=1}^{s} p_i \mathrm{d}q_i - \sum_{i=1}^{s} P_i \mathrm{d}Q_i = \mathrm{d}S_1(\boldsymbol{q}, \boldsymbol{Q})$ 得

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} p _ {i} = \frac {\partial S _ {1}}{\partial q _ {i}} = Q _ {i} & (i = 1, 2, \dots , s) \\ P _ {i} = - \frac {\partial S _ {1}}{\partial Q _ {i}} = - q _ {i} \end{array} \right.
$$

(2) $S_{2}(p,Q,t) = -\tan p(\mathrm{e}^{Q} - 1)$ 

$$
q = - \frac {\partial S _ {2}}{\partial p} = \sec^ {2} p \left(\mathrm{e} ^ {Q} - 1\right)
$$

$$
P = - \frac {\partial S _ {2}}{\partial Q} = \mathrm{e} ^ {Q} \tan p
$$

也可把变换写成

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & Q = \ln (1 + q \cos^ {2} p) \\ & P = (1 + q \cos^ {2} p) \tan p \end{array}
$$

(3) $S_{3}(\pmb {q},\pmb {P},t) = \sum_{i = 1}^{s}q_{i}P_{i}$ 

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} p _ {i} = \frac {\partial S _ {3}}{\partial q _ {i}} = P _ {i} & (i = 1, 2, \dots , s) \\ Q _ {i} = \frac {\partial S _ {3}}{\partial P _ {i}} = q _ {i} \end{array} \right.
$$

为恒等变换.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (4) S _ {4} (\boldsymbol {p}, \boldsymbol {P}, t) & = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} p _ {i} P _ {i} \\ & \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} q _ {i} = - \frac {\partial S _ {4}}{\partial p _ {i}} = - P _ {i} \\ Q _ {i} = \frac {\partial S _ {4}}{\partial P _ {i}} = p _ {i} \end{array} \right. \quad (i = 1, 2, \dots , s) \end{array}
$$

11.4.10 证明 $Q_{1} = p_{1}^{2}, Q_{2} = p_{2}^{2} + q_{2}, P_{1} = -\frac{q_{1}}{2p_{1}} + t, P_{2} = p_{2} + t$ 是正则变换，并求出产生该正则变换的 $S_{1}(q, Q, t), S_{3}(q, P, t)$ 两种形式的母函数.

解 只要能找到母函数,也就证明了变换是正则变换.

先找 $S_{1}(q, Q, t)$ 形式的母函数：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & p _ {1} \mathrm{d} q _ {1} + p _ {2} \mathrm{d} q _ {2} - P _ {1} \mathrm{d} Q _ {1} - P _ {2} \mathrm{d} Q _ {2} \\ & = p _ {1} \mathrm{d} q _ {1} + p _ {2} \mathrm{d} q _ {2} - \left(- \frac {q _ {1}}{2 p _ {1}} + t\right) 2 p _ {1} \mathrm{d} p _ {1} - (p _ {2} + t) (2 p _ {2} \mathrm{d} p _ {2} + \mathrm{d} q _ {2}) \\ & = p _ {1} \mathrm{d} q _ {1} - t \mathrm{d} q _ {2} + (q _ {1} - 2 p _ {1} t) \mathrm{d} p _ {1} - 2 p _ {2} (p _ {2} + t) \mathrm{d} p _ {2} \\ & = \mathrm{d} (p _ {1} q _ {1} - p _ {1} ^ {2} t) + \mathrm{d} (- q _ {2} t) + \mathrm{d} \left(- \frac {2}{3} p _ {2} ^ {3} - p _ {2} ^ {2} t\right) \end{array}
$$

在上述演算中，t 视为常量.

再用变换关系，将 $p_1, p_2$ 表示成 $q_1, q_2, Q_1, Q_2$ 和 $t$ 的函数，将

$$
p _ {1} = Q _ {1} ^ {1 / 2}, \quad p _ {2} = (Q _ {2} - q _ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}
$$

代入法甫式,即得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} S _ {1} (\boldsymbol {q}, \boldsymbol {Q}, t) & = Q _ {1} ^ {1 / 2} q _ {1} - Q _ {1} t - q _ {2} t - \frac {2}{3} (Q _ {2} - q _ {2}) ^ {3 / 2} - (Q _ {2} - q _ {2}) t \\ & = Q _ {1} ^ {1 / 2} q _ {1} - \frac {2}{3} (Q _ {2} - q _ {2}) ^ {3 / 2} - (Q _ {1} + Q _ {2}) t \end{array}
$$

现找 $S_{3}(\pmb {q},\pmb {P},t)$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & p _ {1} \mathrm{d} q _ {1} + p _ {2} \mathrm{d} q _ {2} + Q _ {1} \mathrm{d} P _ {1} + Q _ {2} \mathrm{d} P _ {2} \\ & = p _ {1} \mathrm{d} q _ {1} + p _ {2} \mathrm{d} q _ {2} + p _ {1} ^ {2} \left(\frac {q _ {1}}{2 p _ {1} ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} p _ {1} - \frac {1}{2 p _ {1}} \mathrm{d} q _ {1}\right) + (p _ {2} ^ {2} + q _ {2}) \mathrm{d} p _ {2} \\ & = \frac {1}{2} p _ {1} \mathrm{d} q _ {1} + p _ {2} \mathrm{d} q _ {2} + \frac {1}{2} q _ {1} \mathrm{d} p _ {1} + (p _ {2} ^ {2} + q _ {2}) \mathrm{d} p _ {2} \\ & = \mathrm{d} \left(\frac {1}{2} p _ {1} q _ {1} + p _ {2} q _ {2} + \frac {1}{3} p _ {2} ^ {3}\right) \end{array}
$$

也用变换关系将上式中的 $p_1, p_2$ 表成 $q_1, q_2, P_1, P_2$ 和 $t$ 的函数，代入

$$
\begin{array}{r l} p _ {1} & = - \frac {q _ {1}}{2 (P _ {1} - t)}, \quad p _ {2} = P _ {2} - t \\ S _ {3} (\boldsymbol {q}, \boldsymbol {P}, t) & = \frac {1}{2} q _ {1} \left[ - \frac {q _ {1}}{2 (P _ {1} - t)} \right] + (P _ {2} - t) q _ {2} + \frac {1}{3} (P _ {2} - t) ^ {3} \\ & = - \frac {q _ {1} ^ {2}}{4 (P _ {1} - t)} + (P _ {2} - t) q _ {2} + \frac {1}{3} (P _ {2} - t) ^ {3} \end{array}
$$

注意:获得 $S_{1}(q,Q,t)$ 后,不能用它将其中 $Q_{1},Q_{2}$ 用变换关系换成 $Q_{1}(q,P,t)$ 、 $Q_{2}(q,P,t)$ 得到 $S_{3}(q,P,t)$ ,原因是 $S_{3}(q,P,t)$ 与 $S_{1}(q,Q,t)$ 间并不存在下列偏导数关系:

$$
\frac {\partial S _ {3}}{\partial P _ {1}} = \frac {\partial S _ {1}}{\partial Q _ {1}} \frac {\partial Q _ {1}}{\partial P _ {1}} + \frac {\partial S _ {1}}{\partial Q _ {2}} \frac {\partial Q _ {2}}{\partial P _ {1}}
$$

11.4.11 证明

$$
\begin{array}{c} Q = \ln \left(1 + q ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \cos p\right) \\ P = \left(1 + q ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \cos p\right) q ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \sin p \end{array}
$$

为正则变换,并求出产生它的母函数.

提示: 用 $\mathrm{d}S = -q\mathrm{d}p - cP\mathrm{d}Q$ 时需令 $c = 2$ .

解

$$
\begin{array}{c} {Q = \ln \left(1 + q ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \cos p\right)} \\ {P = \left(1 + q ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \cos p\right) q ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \sin p} \\ {\mathrm{d} S = - q \mathrm{d} p - 2 P \mathrm{d} Q} \end{array}
$$

需将式中的 q、P 均表成 p、Q 的函数(这里变换关系中不显含 t)，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {q = (\mathrm{e} ^ {Q} - 1) ^ {2} \sec^ {2} p} \\ & {P = \mathrm{e} ^ {Q} (\mathrm{e} ^ {Q} - 1) \tan p} \\ & {\mathrm{d} S = - (\mathrm{e} ^ {Q} - 1) ^ {2} \sec^ {2} p \mathrm{d} p - 2 \mathrm{e} ^ {Q} (\mathrm{e} ^ {Q} - 1) \tan p \mathrm{d} Q} \\ & {\quad = \mathrm{d} [ - (\mathrm{e} ^ {Q} - 1) ^ {2} \tan p ]} \\ & {\quad S (p, Q) = - (\mathrm{e} ^ {Q} - 1) ^ {2} \tan p} \end{array}
$$

所以

注意: 此题与 11.4.4 题就差一个 2 的因子, 母函数也与 11.4.4 题相同, 说明一般讲

采用正则变换条件时应考虑不为零的常数 $C$ 不一定总等于1.

11.4.12 用母函数 $S_{1} = Q_{1}^{1 / 2}q_{1} - \frac{2}{3} (Q_{2} - q_{2})^{3 / 2} - (Q_{1} + Q_{2})t$ 产生的正则变换来求解哈密顿函数为 $H = p_1^2 +p_2^2 +q_2$ 的系统的运动.

解

$$
S _ {1} = Q _ {1} ^ {1 / 2} q _ {1} - \frac {2}{3} (Q _ {2} - q _ {2}) ^ {3 / 2} - (Q _ {1} + Q _ {2}) t
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} p _ {i} \mathrm{d} q _ {i} - c \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {s} P _ {i} \mathrm{d} Q _ {i} + (c H ^ {*} - H) \mathrm{d} t = \mathrm{d} S _ {1} (\pmb {q}, \pmb {Q}, t) \text {取} c = 1,} \\ & {\quad p _ {1} = \frac {\partial S _ {1}}{\partial q _ {1}} = Q _ {1} ^ {1 / 2}} \\ & {\quad p _ {2} = \frac {\partial S _ {1}}{\partial q _ {2}} = (Q _ {2} - q _ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \\ & {\quad P _ {1} = - \frac {\partial S _ {1}}{\partial Q _ {1}} = \frac {1}{2} Q _ {1} ^ {- 1 / 2} q _ {1} + t} \\ & {\quad P _ {2} = - \frac {\partial S _ {1}}{\partial Q _ {2}} = (Q _ {2} - q _ {2}) ^ {1 / 2} + t} \\ & {\quad H = p _ {1} ^ {2} + p _ {2} ^ {2} + q _ {2}} \\ & {\quad q _ {2} = Q _ {2} - (P _ {2} - t) ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad H ^ {*} = H + \frac {\partial S _ {1}}{\partial t} = p _ {1} ^ {2} + p _ {2} ^ {2} + q _ {2} - (Q _ {1} + Q _ {2})} \\ & {\quad = Q _ {1} + (Q _ {2} - q _ {2}) + q _ {2} - (Q _ {1} + Q _ {2}) = 0} \\ & {\quad \dot {Q} _ {i} = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial P _ {i}}, \dot {P} _ {i} = - \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial Q _ {i}} (i = 1, 2)} \\ & {\quad Q _ {1} = \text {常量}, \qquad Q _ {2} = \text {常量}} \\ & {\quad P _ {1} = \text {常量}, \qquad P _ {2} = \text {常量}} \\ & {\quad q _ {1} = 2 Q ^ {1 / 2} (P _ {1} - t) = A t + B} \\ & {\quad q _ {2} = Q _ {2} - (P _ {2} - t) ^ {2} = - t ^ {2} + C t + D} \end{array}
$$

由

得

其中 A、B、C、D 均为常量，由初条件确定.

11.4.13 一质量为 m 的质点在势场 $V(q)$ 中做一维运动, 质点受到正比于其速度的阻力 $-2m\gamma\dot{q}$ .

(1) 证明可由拉格朗日函数 $L = \mathrm{e}^{2\gamma t}\left[\frac{1}{2} m\dot{q}^2 - V(q)\right]$ 得到运动微分方程；

(2) 用母函数 $S_{3}(q, P, t) = \mathrm{e}^{\gamma t} q P$ ，求正则变换后的哈密顿函数 $H^{*}(Q, P, t)$ ，对于 $V(q) = \frac{1}{2} m \omega^{2} q^{2}$ ，证明变换后的哈密顿函数

$$
H ^ {*} = \frac {1}{2 m} P ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} Q ^ {2} + \gamma Q P
$$

为运动常数；

(3) 对于弱阻尼情况 $\gamma < \omega$ ，由(2)问中的运动常数求阻尼振子的解 $q(t)$ .

解 （1）系统的运动微分方程为

$$
m \ddot {q} = - \frac {\partial V}{\partial q} - 2 m \gamma \dot {q}
$$

若令

$$
L = \mathrm{e} ^ {2 \gamma t} \left[ \frac {1}{2} m \dot {q} ^ {2} - V (q) \right]
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{q}}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q} = 0$ 可得

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\mathrm{e} ^ {2 \gamma_ {t}} m \dot {q}\right) + \mathrm{e} ^ {2 \gamma_ {t}} \frac {\partial V}{\partial q} = 0
$$

$$
\mathrm{e} ^ {2 \gamma_ {t}} m \ddot {q} + 2 \gamma m \mathrm{e} ^ {2 \gamma_ {t}} \dot {q} + \mathrm{e} ^ {2 \gamma_ {t}} \frac {\partial V}{\partial q} = 0
$$

$$
m \ddot {q} = - \frac {\partial V}{\partial q} - 2 m \gamma \dot {q}
$$

证明了由所给的拉格朗日函数可导出运动微分方程.

(2) 先由拉格朗日函数求出正则变换前的哈密顿函数 $H(q, p, t)$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & p = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q}} = m e ^ {2 \gamma_ {t}} \dot {q}, \quad \dot {q} = \frac {1}{m} e ^ {- 2 \gamma_ {t}} p \\ & H = p \dot {q} - L = \frac {1}{m} e ^ {- 2 \gamma_ {t}} p ^ {2} - e ^ {2 \gamma_ {t}} \left[ \frac {1}{2} m \left(\frac {1}{m} e ^ {- 2 \gamma_ {t}} p\right) ^ {2} - V (q) \right] \\ & = \frac {1}{2 m} e ^ {- 2 \gamma_ {t}} p ^ {2} + e ^ {2 \gamma_ {t}} V (q) \\ & \quad p d q + Q d P + (H ^ {*} - H) d t = d S _ {3} (q, P, t) \\ & \quad p = \frac {\partial S _ {3}}{\partial q} = \frac {\partial}{\partial q} (e ^ {\gamma_ {t}} q P) = e ^ {\gamma_ {t}} P \\ & \quad Q = \frac {\partial S _ {3}}{\partial P} = \frac {\partial}{\partial P} (e ^ {\gamma_ {t}} q P) = e ^ {\gamma_ {t}} q \\ & \quad q = e ^ {- \gamma_ {t}} Q \\ & H ^ {*} = H + \frac {\partial S _ {3}}{\partial t} = \frac {1}{2 m} e ^ {- 2 \gamma_ {t}} p ^ {2} + e ^ {2 \gamma_ {t}} V (q) + \gamma e ^ {\gamma_ {t}} q P \\ & = \frac {1}{2 m} F ^ {2} + e ^ {2 \gamma_ {t}} V (e ^ {- \gamma_ {t}} Q) + \gamma Q P \end{array}
$$

对于 $V(q) = \frac{1}{2} m\omega^2 q^2$ 

$$
H ^ {*} = \frac {1}{2 m} P ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} q ^ {2} \cdot e ^ {2 \gamma t} + \gamma Q P = \frac {1}{2 m} P ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} Q ^ {2} + \gamma Q P
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial H^*}{\partial t} = 0$ ，所以 $H^{*}$ 为运动常数.

(3) 对于(2)问的 $V(q)=\frac{1}{2}m\omega^{2}q^{2}$ ,

$$
H ^ {*} = \frac {1}{2 m} P ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} Q ^ {2} + \gamma Q P
$$

$$
\dot {P} = - \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial Q} = - m \omega^ {2} Q - \gamma P\tag{1}
$$

$$
\dot {Q} = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial P} = \frac {1}{m} P + \gamma Q\tag{2}
$$

$$
P = m (\dot {Q} - \gamma Q)
$$

将式(2)两边对 $t$ 求导，用式(1)消去 $\dot{P}$ ，用式(2)消去 $P$ ，可得

$$
\ddot {Q} + (\omega^ {2} - \gamma^ {2}) Q = 0
$$

对于弱阻尼情况, $\gamma<\omega$ ,

$$
Q = A \sin (\omega_ {\gamma} t + \alpha)
$$

其中 $\omega_{\gamma} = \sqrt{\omega^2 - \gamma^2}, A, \alpha$ 为积分常数，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} P & = m (\dot {Q} - \gamma Q) \\ & = m [ A \omega_ {\gamma} \cos (\omega_ {\gamma} t + \alpha) - \gamma A \sin (\omega_ {\gamma} t + \alpha) ] \end{array}
$$

可得 $H^{*} = \frac{1}{2m} P^{2} + \frac{1}{2} m\omega^{2}Q^{2} + \gamma QP = \frac{1}{2} m\omega_{\gamma}^{2}A^{2}$ 

$$
A = \frac {1}{\omega_ {\gamma}} \sqrt {\frac {2 H ^ {*}}{m}}
$$

$$
q = \mathrm{e} ^ {- \gamma_ {t}} Q = \frac {1}{\omega_ {\gamma}} \sqrt {\frac {2 H ^ {*}}{m}} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \gamma_ {t}} \sin (\omega_ {\gamma} t + \alpha)
$$

其中 $\omega_{\gamma} = \sqrt{\omega^2 - \gamma^2}$ , $H^{*}, \alpha$ 为常数由初条件确定.

## 11.5 哈密顿-雅可比方程

11.5.1 一个质量为 m 的质点以初速 v、对水平线仰角为 $\alpha$ 的方向，在不计阻力的空气中抛射，用哈密顿-雅可比方程求其运动规律.

解 取抛射点为坐标原点，x 轴沿水平方向，y 轴竖直向上，质点在 xy 平面内运动，

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {x} ^ {2} + p _ {y} ^ {2}) + m g y
$$

$H$ 中不显含 $x$ , 它是循环坐标, $p_x = \alpha'$ , $H$ 不显含 $t$ , 有能量积分, 积分常数为 $h$ ,

$$
S = - h t + W
$$

特性函数 W 满足的哈密顿-雅可比方程为

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left[ \alpha^ {\prime 2} + \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial y}\right) ^ {2} \right] + m g y = h
$$

可令 $W = W'(y) + \alpha'x, W'(y)$ 满足的方程为

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left[ \alpha^ {\prime 2} + \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} y}\right) ^ {2} \right] + m g y = h
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} y} = \sqrt {2 m} \sqrt {h - \frac {\alpha^ {\prime 2}}{2 m} - m g y}
$$

$$
W ^ {\prime} (y) = \sqrt {2 m} \int \sqrt {h - \frac {\alpha^ {\prime 2}}{2 m} - m g y} d y = - \frac {2 \sqrt {2 m}}{3 m g} \left(h - \frac {\alpha^ {\prime 2}}{2 m} - m g y\right) ^ {3 / 2}
$$

$$
W = - \frac {2 \sqrt {2 m}}{3 m g} \left(h - \frac {\alpha^ {\prime 2}}{2 m} - m g y\right) ^ {3 / 2} + \alpha^ {\prime} x
$$

$$
\beta = t - \frac {\partial W}{\partial h} = t + \frac {\sqrt {2 m}}{m g} \left(h - \frac {\alpha^ {\prime 2}}{2 m} - m g y\right) ^ {1 / 2}
$$

$$
h - \frac {\alpha^ {2}}{2 m} - m g y = \left[ \frac {m g}{\sqrt {2 m}} (\beta - t) \right] ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} m g ^ {2} (\beta - t) ^ {2}\tag{1}
$$

代入初条件定常量 h 和 $\alpha'$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & h = \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {x 0} ^ {2} + p _ {y 0} ^ {2}) = \frac {1}{2 m} (m v) ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} \\ & \alpha^ {\prime} = p _ {x 0} = m v \cos \alpha \end{array}
$$

代入式(1)，

$$
\frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2 m} (m v \cos \alpha) ^ {2} - m g y = \frac {1}{2} m g ^ {2} (\beta - t) ^ {2}\tag{2}
$$

由 $t = 0$ 时 $y = 0$ ，可定出 $\beta = \frac{1}{g} v\sin \alpha$ ，代入式(2)，解出

$$
\begin{array}{l} y = v t \sin \alpha - \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2} \\ p _ {x} = \alpha^ {\prime} = m v \cos \alpha \\ \dot {x} = v \sin \alpha \\ x = \int_ {0} ^ {t} v \sin \alpha d t = v t \sin \alpha \end{array}
$$

11.5.2 求对称陀螺在重力作用下绕固定点转动时的哈密顿主函数.

解 取固定点为坐标原点,取固连于刚体的惯量主轴为 x、y、z 轴,z 轴为旋转对称轴, $I_{1}$ 、 $I_{2}=I_{1}$ 、 $I_{3}$ 为相应的主转动惯量,以静坐标系为参考系,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} T & = \frac {1}{2} (I _ {1} \omega_ {x} ^ {2} + I _ {1} \omega_ {y} ^ {2} + I _ {3} \omega_ {z} ^ {2}) \\ & V = m g l \cos \theta \end{array}
$$

用欧拉角 $\psi$ 、 $\varphi$ 、 $\theta$ 为广义坐标，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\omega_ {x} = \dot {\varphi} \sin \theta \sin \psi + \dot {\theta} \cos \psi} \\ & {\omega_ {y} = \dot {\varphi} \sin \theta \cos \psi - \dot {\theta} \sin \psi} \\ & {\omega_ {z} = \dot {\varphi} \cos \theta + \dot {\psi}} \\ & {L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} [ I _ {1} (\dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + \dot {\theta} ^ {2}) + I _ {3} (\dot {\varphi} \cos \theta + \dot {\psi}) ^ {2} ] - m g l \cos \theta} \\ & {p _ {\psi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\psi}} = I _ {3} (\dot {\varphi} \cos \theta + \dot {\psi})} \\ & {p _ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = I _ {1} \dot {\varphi} \sin^ {2} \theta + I _ {3} \dot {\varphi} \cos^ {2} \theta + I _ {3} \dot {\psi} \cos \theta} \\ & {p _ {\theta} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = I _ {1} \dot {\theta}} \\ & {\dot {\theta} = \frac {p _ {\theta}}{I _ {1}}} \\ & {\dot {\varphi} = \frac {1}{I _ {1} \sin^ {2} \theta} (p _ {\varphi} - p _ {\psi} \cos \theta)} \end{array}
$$

$$
\dot {\psi} = \frac {p _ {\psi}}{I _ {3}} - \frac {p _ {\varphi} - p _ {\psi} \cos \theta}{I _ {1} \sin^ {2} \theta} \cos \theta
$$

动能是广义速度的齐二次函数，

$$
H = T + V = \frac {1}{2} \left[ \frac {\left(p _ {\varphi} - p _ {\psi} \cos \theta\right) ^ {2}}{I _ {1} \sin^ {2} \theta} + \frac {p _ {\theta} ^ {2}}{I _ {1}} + \frac {p _ {\psi} ^ {2}}{I _ {3}} \right] + m g l \cos \theta
$$

用机械能守恒的哈密顿-雅可比方程

$$
H \Big (\psi , \varphi , \theta , \frac {\partial W}{\partial \psi}, \frac {\partial W}{\partial \varphi}, \frac {\partial W}{\partial \theta} \Big) = h
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 I _ {1} \sin^ {2} \theta} \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial \varphi} - \frac {\partial W}{\partial \psi} \cos \theta\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 I _ {1}} \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial \theta}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 I _ {3}} \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial \psi}\right) ^ {2} + m g l \cos \theta = h
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial H}{\partial \varphi} = 0, \frac{\partial H}{\partial \psi} = 0, \varphi, \psi$ 均是循环坐标，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\partial W}{\partial \varphi} & = p _ {\varphi} = \alpha_ {2}, \quad \frac {\partial W}{\partial \psi} = p _ {\psi} = \alpha_ {3} \\ W & = \alpha_ {2} \varphi + \alpha_ {3} \psi + W ^ {\prime} (\theta) \end{array}
$$

$W^{\prime}(\theta)$ 满足的微分方程为

$$
\frac {1}{2 I _ {1} \sin^ {2} \theta} \left(\alpha_ {2} - \alpha_ {3} \cos \theta\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 I _ {1}} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} \theta}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 I _ {3}} \alpha_ {3} ^ {2} + m g l \cos \theta = h
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} \theta} = \left[ 2 I _ {1} h - \frac {1}{\sin^ {2} \theta} \left(\alpha_ {2} - \alpha_ {3} \cos \theta\right) ^ {2} - \frac {I _ {1}}{I _ {3}} \alpha_ {3} ^ {2} - 2 I _ {1} m g l \cos \theta \right] ^ {1 / 2}
$$

$$
W ^ {\prime} = \int \left[ 2 I _ {1} h - \frac {1}{\sin^ {2} \theta} \left(\alpha_ {2} - \alpha_ {3} \cos \theta\right) ^ {2} - \frac {I _ {1}}{I _ {3}} \alpha_ {3} ^ {2} - 2 I _ {1} m g l \cos \theta \right] ^ {1 / 2} d \theta
$$

哈密顿主函数为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} S & = - h t + W = - h t + \alpha_ {2} \varphi + \alpha_ {3} \psi + W ^ {\prime} \\ & = - h t + \alpha_ {2} \varphi + \alpha_ {3} \psi + \int \left[ 2 I _ {1} h - \frac {1}{\sin^ {2} \theta} (\alpha_ {2} - \alpha_ {3} \cos \theta) ^ {2} \right. \\ & \left. - \frac {I _ {1}}{I _ {3}} \alpha_ {3} ^ {2} - 2 I _ {1} m g l \cos \theta \right] ^ {1 / 2} d \theta \end{array}
$$

其中 h 是总机械能, $\alpha_{2}$ 为进动角动量, $\alpha_{3}$ 为自转角动量, l 是质心在固定点上方离固定点的距离, 也是质心的 z 坐标.

11.5.3 用哈密顿-雅可比方程求二维势场 $V = \frac{k}{r}$ ( $k$ 为常量)中运动的质点的轨道方程, 可采用 $u = r + x$ 和 $v = r - x$ 为广义坐标.

解

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) - \frac {k}{r}
$$

改用 $u, v$ 为广义坐标，

$$
\begin{array}{l} {u = r + x = r (1 + \cos \varphi)} \\ {v = r - x = r (1 - \cos \varphi)} \\ {r = \frac {1}{2} (u + v), \quad \dot {r} = \frac {1}{2} (\dot {u} + \dot {v})} \\ {\cos \varphi = \frac {x}{r} = \frac {u}{r} - 1 = \frac {2 u}{u + v} - 1 = \frac {u - v}{u + v}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \sin \varphi & = \sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {u - v}{u + v}\right) ^ {2}} = \frac {2}{u + v} \sqrt {u v} \\ & - \sin \varphi \cdot \dot {\varphi} = \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {u - v}{u + v}\right) = \frac {2}{(u + v) ^ {2}} (\dot {u} v - u \dot {v}) \end{array}
$$

$$
\dot {\varphi} = \frac {u \dot {v} - \dot {u} v}{(u + v) \sqrt {u v}}
$$

$$
L = \frac {1}{8} m \left[ \dot {u} ^ {2} + 2 \dot {u} \dot {v} + \dot {v} ^ {2} + \frac {(u \dot {v} - \dot {u} v) ^ {2}}{u v} \right] - \frac {2 k}{u + v}
$$

$$
p _ {u} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {u}} = \frac {m (u + v)}{4 u} \dot {u}, \quad \dot {u} = \frac {4 u}{m (u + v)} p _ {u}
$$

$$
p _ {v} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {v}} = \frac {m (u + v)}{4 v} \dot {v}, \quad \dot {v} = \frac {4 v}{m (u + v)} p _ {v}
$$

$$
H = \frac {2}{m (u + v)} (u p _ {u} ^ {2} + v p _ {v} ^ {2}) + \frac {2 k}{u + v}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial S}{\partial t} + \frac {2}{m (u + v)} \left[ u \left(\frac {\partial S}{\partial u}\right) ^ {2} + v \left(\frac {\partial S}{\partial v}\right) ^ {2} \right] + \frac {2 k}{u + v} = 0
$$

可令

$$
S = - h t + W
$$

$W$ 满足的偏微分方程为

$$
\frac {2}{m (u + v)} \left[ u \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial u}\right) ^ {2} + v \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial v}\right) ^ {2} \right] + \frac {2 k}{u + v} = h
$$

$$
\frac {2}{m} \left[ u \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial u}\right) ^ {2} + v \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial v}\right) ^ {2} \right] - h (u + v) + 2 k = 0
$$

可令

$$
W = W _ {1} (u) + W _ {2} (v)
$$

$$
\frac {2}{m} \left[ u \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} u}\right) ^ {2} \right] - h u + k + \frac {2}{m} \left[ v \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} v}\right) ^ {2} \right] - h v + k = 0
$$

令

$$
\frac {2}{m} \left[ u \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} u}\right) ^ {2} \right] - h u + k = \alpha
$$

则

$$
\frac {2}{m} \left[ v \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} v}\right) ^ {2} \right] - h v + k = - \alpha
$$

$$
\left[ \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} u}\right) ^ {2} - \frac {m h}{2} \right] u = \frac {m (\alpha - k)}{2}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} u} = \sqrt {\frac {m}{2 u} (\alpha - k) + \frac {m h}{2}}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} v} = \sqrt {\frac {m h}{2} - \frac {m}{2 v} (\alpha + k)}
$$

$$
W _ {1} = \int \sqrt {\frac {1}{2} m h + \frac {m (\alpha - k)}{2 u}} d u
$$

$$
W _ {2} = \int \sqrt {\frac {1}{2} m h - \frac {m (\alpha + k)}{2 v}} d v
$$

$$
S = - h t + \int \left[ \frac {1}{2} m h + \frac {m (\alpha - k)}{2 u} \right] ^ {1 / 2} d u + \int \left[ \frac {1}{2} m h - \frac {m (\alpha + k)}{2 v} \right] ^ {1 / 2} d v
$$

由 $\frac{\partial S}{\partial \alpha} = \beta$ 即得轨道方程.

11.5.4 某系统的哈密顿函数为

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {2} - k q _ {1}) ^ {2}
$$

其中 m、k 均为常量。用哈密顿正则方程和哈密顿-雅可比方程解此系统的运动轨道。

解

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {2} - k q _ {1}) ^ {2}
$$

用哈密顿正则方程求解，

$$
\dot {q} _ {1} = \frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {1}} = \frac {p _ {1}}{m}
$$

$$
\dot {q} _ {2} = \frac {\partial H}{\partial p _ {2}} = \frac {1}{m} (p _ {2} - k q _ {1})
$$

$$
\dot {p} _ {1} = - \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {1}} = \frac {k}{m} (p _ {2} - k q _ {1})
$$

$$
\dot {p} _ {2} = - \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {2}} = 0
$$

$$
p _ {2} = \alpha
$$

$$
\dot {p} _ {1} = \frac {k}{m} (\alpha - k q _ {1})
$$

$$
\ddot {q} _ {1} = \frac {1}{m} \dot {p} _ {1} = \frac {k}{m ^ {2}} (\alpha - k q _ {1}) = \frac {k ^ {2}}{m ^ {2}} \left(\frac {\alpha}{k} - q _ {1}\right)
$$

令

$$
q _ {1} ^ {\prime} = q _ {1} - \frac {\alpha}{k}
$$

$$
\ddot {q} _ {1} ^ {\prime} + \frac {k ^ {2}}{m ^ {2}} q _ {1} ^ {\prime} = 0
$$

$$
q _ {1} ^ {\prime} = A \cos \left(\frac {k}{m} t + \varphi\right)
$$

$$
q _ {1} = \frac {\alpha}{k} + A \cos \left(\frac {k}{m} t + \varphi\right)
$$

$$
\dot {q} _ {2} = \frac {1}{m} (\alpha - k q _ {1}) = - \frac {k}{m} A \cos \left(\frac {k}{m} t + \varphi\right)
$$

$$
q _ {2} = - A \sin \left(\frac {k}{m} t + \varphi\right) + \beta
$$

消去 $t$ 得轨道方程

$$
\left(q _ {1} - \frac {\alpha}{k}\right) ^ {2} + (q _ {2} - \beta) ^ {2} = A ^ {2}
$$

若用系统的能量 h 来表示振幅 A, 则

$$
h = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2}) = \frac {A ^ {2} k ^ {2}}{2 m}
$$

$$
A ^ {2} = \frac {2 m h ^ {2}}{k ^ {2}}
$$

轨道方程可表为

$$
\left(q _ {1} - \frac {\alpha}{k}\right) ^ {2} + (q _ {2} - \beta) ^ {2} = \frac {2 m h}{k ^ {2}}
$$

用哈密顿-雅可比方程求解，

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {2} - k q _ {1}) ^ {2}
$$

因为

$$
\frac {\partial H}{\partial t} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {2}} = 0
$$

可令

$$
S = - h t + \alpha q _ {2} + W (q _ {1})
$$

W 满足约化的哈密顿-雅可比方程，

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left[ \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} q _ {1}}\right) ^ {2} + (\alpha - k q _ {1}) ^ {2} \right] = h
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} q _ {1}} = \sqrt {2 m h - (\alpha - k q _ {1}) ^ {2}}
$$

$$
W = \int \sqrt {2 m h - (\alpha - k q _ {1}) ^ {2}} d q _ {1}
$$

$$
S = - h t + \alpha q _ {2} + \int \sqrt {2 m h - (\alpha - k q _ {1}) ^ {2}} d q _ {1}
$$

轨道方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \beta & = \frac {\partial S}{\partial \alpha} = q _ {2} - \frac {1}{2 k} \int \frac {- 2 (\alpha - k q _ {1})}{\sqrt {2 m h - (\alpha - k q _ {1}) ^ {2}}} d (\alpha - k q _ {1}) \\ & = q _ {2} - \frac {1}{k} \sqrt {2 m h - (\alpha - k q _ {1}) ^ {2}} \\ & \left(q _ {1} - \frac {\alpha}{k}\right) ^ {2} + (q _ {2} - \beta) ^ {2} = \frac {2 m h}{k ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

两种方法得到同样的结果.

11.5.5 用哈密顿-雅可比方程求哈密顿函数为

$$
H = \frac {1}{2} (p _ {1} ^ {2} + p _ {2} ^ {2}) (q _ {1} ^ {2} + q _ {2} ^ {2}) ^ {- 1} + (q _ {1} ^ {2} + q _ {2} ^ {2}) ^ {- 1}
$$

的质点的轨道.

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} H = & \frac {1}{2} (p _ {1} ^ {2} + p _ {2} ^ {2}) (q _ {1} ^ {2} + q _ {2} ^ {2}) ^ {- 1} + (q _ {1} ^ {2} + q _ {2} ^ {2}) ^ {- 1} \\ & S = - h t + W \end{array}
$$

W 满足约化了的哈密顿-雅可比方程，

$$
\frac {1}{2 \left(q _ {1} ^ {2} + q _ {2} ^ {2}\right)} \left[ \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial q _ {1}}\right) ^ {2} + \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial q _ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \right] + \frac {1}{q _ {1} ^ {2} + q _ {2} ^ {2}} = h
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} \left[ \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial q _ {1}}\right) ^ {2} + \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial q _ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \right] - h (q _ {1} ^ {2} + q _ {2} ^ {2}) + 1 = 0
$$

令

$$
W = W _ {1} (q _ {1}) + W _ {2} (q _ {2})
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} q _ {1}}\right) ^ {2} - h q _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} = \alpha
$$

则

$$
\frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} q _ {2}}\right) ^ {2} - h q _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} = - \alpha
$$

$$
\left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} q _ {1}}\right) ^ {2} = 2 \left(h q _ {1} ^ {2} + \alpha - \frac {1}{2}\right)
$$

$$
\left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} q _ {2}}\right) ^ {2} = 2 \left(h q _ {2} ^ {2} - \alpha - \frac {1}{2}\right)
$$

$$
W _ {1} = \int \sqrt {2 \left(h q _ {1} ^ {2} + \alpha - \frac {1}{2}\right)} d q _ {1}
$$

$$
W _ {2} = \int \sqrt {2 \left(h q _ {2} ^ {2} - \alpha - \frac {1}{2}\right)} d q _ {2}
$$

$$
S = - h t + \int \sqrt {2 \left(h q _ {1} ^ {2} + \alpha - \frac {1}{2}\right)} d q _ {1} + \int \sqrt {2 \left(h q _ {2} ^ {2} - \alpha - \frac {1}{2}\right)} d q _ {2}
$$

$$
\beta^ {\prime} = \frac {\partial S}{\partial \alpha} = \sqrt {2} \int \frac {\mathrm{d} q _ {1}}{\sqrt {h q _ {1} ^ {2} - \left(\frac {1}{2} - \alpha\right)}} - \sqrt {2} \int \frac {\mathrm{d} q _ {2}}{\sqrt {h q _ {2} ^ {2} - \left(\alpha + \frac {1}{2}\right)}}
$$

用积分公式：

$$
\int \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + a ^ {2}}} = \ln (x + \sqrt {x ^ {2} + a ^ {2}})
$$

$$
\beta^ {\prime} = \sqrt {\frac {2}{h}} \left[ \ln \left(\sqrt {h} q _ {1} + \sqrt {h q _ {1} ^ {2} + \alpha - \frac {1}{2}}\right) - \ln \left(\sqrt {h} q _ {2} + \sqrt {h q _ {2} ^ {2} - \alpha - \frac {1}{2}}\right) \right.
$$

引入另一个常数 $\beta = \exp \left(\sqrt{\frac{h}{2}}\beta'\right)$ ，轨道方程可写为

$$
\sqrt {h} q _ {1} + \sqrt {h q _ {1} ^ {2} + \alpha - \frac {1}{2}} = \beta \left(\sqrt {h} q _ {2} + \sqrt {h q _ {2} ^ {2} - \alpha - \frac {1}{2}}\right)
$$

11.5.6 用哈密顿-雅可比方程求哈密顿函数为 $H = p^2 - q$ 的系统的运动.

解 方法一:用

$$
H \Big (\boldsymbol {x}, \pm \frac {\partial W}{\partial \boldsymbol {x}} \Big) = h
$$

现取 $x = p$ ，则 $y = -q$ ，上式中 $\frac{\partial W}{\partial x}$ 前取负号.

$$
p ^ {2} + \frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} p} = h
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} p} = h - p ^ {2}, \quad W = h p - \frac {1}{3} p ^ {3}
$$

$$
- q = \frac {\partial S}{\partial p} = h - p ^ {2}
$$

$$
\beta = \frac {\partial S}{\partial h} = - t + p
$$

$$
\begin{array}{c} {p = t + \beta} \\ {q = p ^ {2} - h = (t + \beta) ^ {2} - h} \end{array}
$$

方法二：用 $H\left(x, \pm \frac{\partial W}{\partial x}\right) = h$ 时，取 $x = q$ ，则 $y = p, \frac{\partial W}{\partial x}$ 前取正号，

$$
\left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} q}\right) ^ {2} - q = h
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} q} = \sqrt {q + h}
$$

$$
W = \int \sqrt {q + h} \mathrm{d} q = \frac {2}{3} (q + h) ^ {3 / 2}
$$

$$
S = - h t + W = - h t + \frac {2}{3} (q + h) ^ {3 / 2}
$$

$$
\beta = \frac {\partial S}{\partial h} = - t + (q + h) ^ {1 / 2}
$$

$$
q + h = (t + \beta) ^ {2}, \quad q = (t + \beta) ^ {2} - h
$$

11.5.7 (1) 对于一个哈密顿函数为 $H = \frac{1}{2} p^2$ 的粒子的运动, 求解母函数 $S(q, \alpha, t)$ 的哈密顿-雅可比方程, 求出正则变换 $q = q(\beta, \alpha), p = p(\beta, \alpha)$ , 其中 $\beta$ 和 $\alpha$ 分别是变换后的坐标和动量. 说明所得的结果;

(2) 若存在一扰动, 哈密顿函数变为 $H = \frac{1}{2} q^2 + \frac{1}{2} p^2$ , 仍用 (1) 所得的正则变换, 用 $\alpha$ 、 $\beta$ 、 $t$ 表示变换后的哈密顿函数 $H^*$ , 求解 $\beta(t)$ 和 $\alpha(t)$ , 并证明扰动后的解是简谐的.

解 (1)

$$
\frac {\partial S}{\partial t} + H (q, p, t) = 0
$$

今要求 $S(q, \alpha, t)$ , 其中 $\alpha$ 是变换后的动量 $P$ , 则

$$
p = \frac {\partial S}{\partial q}
$$

$$
Q = \beta = \frac {\partial S}{\partial P} = \frac {\partial S}{\partial \alpha}
$$

今 $H = \frac{1}{2} p^2$ ，哈密顿-雅可比方程为

$$
\frac {\partial S}{\partial t} + \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {\partial S}{\partial q}\right) ^ {2} = 0
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial H}{\partial t} = 0$ ，可令 $S = -ht + W$ 

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} q} = \sqrt {2 h}, \quad W = \sqrt {2 h} q
$$

$$
S = - h t + \sqrt {2 h} q
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial H}{\partial x} = 0$ ，可令

$$
S = - h t + \alpha q
$$

比较两个 $S$ 的式子，可得 $\alpha = \sqrt{2h}, h = \frac{1}{2}\alpha^2,$ 

所以

$$
S = - \frac {1}{2} \alpha^ {2} t + \alpha q
$$

$$
p = \frac {\partial S}{\partial q} = \alpha = P
$$

$$
\beta = Q = \frac {\partial S}{\partial P} = \frac {\partial S}{\partial \alpha} = - \alpha t + q
$$

所以

$$
q = \beta + \alpha t
$$

$\beta,\alpha$ 均为常量,在原参考系中以恒定速度 $\alpha$ 做匀速运动.

正则变换关系为

$$
q = \beta + \alpha t, p = \alpha
$$

(2) 

$$
H = \frac {1}{2} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} q ^ {2}
$$

用(1)问中解得的母函数产生的正则变换，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} H ^ {*} & = H + \frac {\partial S}{\partial t} = \frac {1}{2} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} q ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} \alpha^ {2} \\ & = \frac {1}{2} \alpha^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (\beta + \alpha t) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} \alpha^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} (\beta + \alpha t) ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

由哈密顿正则方程. 注意到 $Q = \beta, P = \alpha$ , 则

$$
\dot {\beta} = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial \alpha} = (\beta + \alpha t) t
$$

$$
\dot {\alpha} = - \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial \beta} = - (\beta + \alpha t)
$$

注意:这里用的正则变换不是解哈密顿-雅可比方程得到的母函数产生的,因此变换后的哈密顿函数 $H^{*} \neq 0$ , 自然, 新正则变量均不再是常数.

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \ddot {\alpha} & = - (\dot {\beta} + \alpha + \dot {\alpha} t) \\ & = - [ (\beta + \alpha t) t + \alpha - (\beta + \alpha t) t ] = - \alpha \\ & \alpha = \alpha_ {0} \sin (t + \varphi) \end{array}
$$

其中 $\alpha_0, \varphi$ 为常量，

$$
\beta = - \dot {\alpha} - \alpha t = - \alpha_ {0} [ \cos (t + \varphi) + t \sin (t + \varphi) ]
$$

由坐标变换关系，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} p & = \alpha = \alpha_ {0} \sin (t + \varphi) \\ q & = \beta + \alpha t = - \dot {\alpha} = - \alpha_ {0} \cos (t + \varphi) \end{array}
$$

这就证明了扰动后的解是简谐振动.

11.5.8 一个质量为 $m$ 的质点在 $xy$ 平面内运动，受到力心位于 $(1,0)$ 和 $(-1,0)$ ，势能为 $V = A_{1}r_{1}^{-1} + A_{2}r_{2}^{-1}$ （其中 $r_1, r_2$ 分别是到两个力心的距离， $A_{1}$ 和 $A_{2}$ 是常数）的力场作用，用椭圆坐标 $\xi, \eta$ ，它与 $x, y$ 的变换关系为

$$
x = \operatorname{ch} \xi \cos \eta , \quad y = \operatorname{sh} \xi \sin \eta
$$

用哈密顿-雅可比方程求质点的运动轨道(用 $\xi,\eta$ 表达即可).

解

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2})
$$

$$
V = \frac {A _ {1}}{r _ {1}} + \frac {A _ {2}}{r _ {2}}
$$

$$
r _ {1} = [ (x - 1) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} ] ^ {1 / 2}, \quad r _ {2} = [ (x + 1) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} ] ^ {1 / 2}
$$

作坐标变换，

$$
x = \operatorname{ch} \xi \cos \eta , \quad y = \operatorname{sh} \xi \sin \eta
$$

$$
\dot {x} = \dot {\xi} \operatorname{sh} \xi \cos \eta - \dot {\eta} \operatorname{ch} \xi \sin \eta
$$

$$
\dot {y} = \dot {\xi} \operatorname{ch} \xi \sin \eta + \dot {\eta} \operatorname{sh} \xi \cos \eta
$$

可得

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m \left(\dot {\xi} ^ {2} + \dot {\eta} ^ {2}\right) \left(\mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi - \cos^ {2} \eta\right)
$$

$$
r _ {1} = \left[ (\operatorname{ch} \xi \cos \eta - 1) ^ {2} + (\operatorname{sh} \xi \sin \eta) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} = \operatorname{ch} \xi - \cos \eta
$$

$$
r _ {2} = \left[ (\operatorname{ch} \xi \cos \eta + 1) ^ {2} + (\operatorname{sh} \xi \sin \eta) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} = \operatorname{ch} \xi + \cos \eta
$$

$$
V = \frac {A _ {1}}{\operatorname{ch} \xi - \cos \eta} + \frac {A _ {2}}{\operatorname{ch} \xi + \cos \eta}
$$

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {\xi} ^ {2} + \dot {\eta} ^ {2}) (\mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi - \cos^ {2} \eta) - \frac {A _ {1}}{\mathrm{ch} \xi - \cos \eta} - \frac {A _ {2}}{\mathrm{ch} \xi + \cos \eta}
$$

$$
p _ {\xi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\xi}} = m \dot {\xi} (\mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi - \cos^ {2} \eta)
$$

$$
p _ {\eta} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\eta}} = m \dot {\eta} (\mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi - \cos^ {2} \eta)
$$

$$
\dot {\xi} = \frac {p _ {\xi}}{m (\mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi - \cos^ {2} \eta)}, \quad \dot {\eta} = \frac {p _ {\eta}}{m (\mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi - \cos^ {2} \eta)}
$$

$$
H = T + V = \frac {p _ {\xi} ^ {2} + p _ {\eta} ^ {2}}{2 m (\operatorname{ch} ^ {2} \xi - \cos^ {2} \eta)} + \frac {A _ {1}}{\operatorname{ch} \xi - \cos \eta} + \frac {A _ {2}}{\operatorname{ch} \xi + \cos \eta}
$$

哈密顿-雅可比方程为

$$
\frac {\partial S}{\partial t} + \frac {1}{2 m (\mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi - \cos^ {2} \gamma)} \left[ \left(\frac {\partial S}{\partial \xi}\right) ^ {2} + \left(\frac {\partial S}{\partial \eta}\right) ^ {2} \right] + \frac {A _ {1}}{\mathrm{ch} \xi - \cos \eta} + \frac {A _ {2}}{\mathrm{ch} \xi + \cos \eta} = 0
$$

$$
S = - \lambda + W _ {1} (\xi) + W _ {2} (\eta)
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m \left(\mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi - \cos^ {2} \eta\right)} \left[ \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \xi}\right) ^ {2} + \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} \eta}\right) ^ {2} \right] + \frac {A _ {1}}{\mathrm{ch} \xi - \cos \eta} + \frac {A _ {2}}{\mathrm{ch} \xi + \cos \eta} = h
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left[ \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \xi}\right) ^ {2} + \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} \eta}\right) ^ {2} \right] + A _ {1} (\operatorname{ch} \xi + \cos \eta) + A _ {2} (\operatorname{ch} \xi - \cos \eta) = h (\operatorname{ch} ^ {2} \xi - \cos^ {2} \eta)
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \xi}\right) ^ {2} + (A _ {1} + A _ {2}) \mathrm{ch} \xi - h \mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi + \frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} \eta}\right) ^ {2} + (A _ {1} - A _ {2}) \cos \eta + h \cos^ {2} \eta = 0
$$

令

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \xi}\right) ^ {2} + (A _ {1} + A _ {2}) \mathrm{ch} \xi - h \mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi = \frac {1}{2 m} \alpha
$$

则

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} \eta}\right) ^ {2} + (A _ {1} - A _ {2}) \cos \eta + h \cos^ {2} \eta = - \frac {1}{2 m} \alpha
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \xi} = [ \alpha + 2 m h \mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi - 2 m (A _ {1} + A _ {2}) \mathrm{ch} \xi ] ^ {1 / 2}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} \eta} = [ - \alpha - 2 m h \cos^ {2} \eta - 2 m (A _ {1} - A _ {2}) \cos \eta ] ^ {1 / 2}
$$

$$
W _ {1} = \int [ \alpha + 2 m h \mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi - 2 m (A _ {1} + A _ {2}) \mathrm{ch} \xi ] ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} \xi
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} W _ {2} & = \int [ - \alpha - 2 m h \cos^ {2} \eta - 2 m (A _ {1} - A _ {2}) \cos \eta ] ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} \eta \\ S & = - h t + \int [ \alpha + 2 m h \mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi - 2 m (A _ {1} + A _ {2}) \mathrm{ch} \xi ] ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} \xi \\ & + \int [ - \alpha - 2 m h \cos^ {2} \eta - 2 m (A _ {1} - A _ {2}) \cos \eta ] ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} \eta \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\partial S}{\partial \alpha} = \beta$ 可得轨道方程.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \beta & = \int \frac {1}{2} [ \alpha + 2 m h \mathrm{ch} ^ {2} \xi - 2 m (A _ {1} + A _ {2}) \mathrm{ch} \xi ] ^ {- 1 / 2} \mathrm{d} \xi \\ & - \int \frac {1}{2} [ - \alpha - 2 m h \cos^ {2} \eta - 2 m (A _ {1} - A _ {2}) \cos \eta ] ^ {- 1 / 2} \mathrm{d} \eta \end{array}
$$

11.5.9 一个质量为 $m$ 的质点在球坐标表达的势场中运动, $V = f(r) + \frac{1}{r^2} g(\theta)$ , 其中 $f(r), g(\theta)$ 是已知的函数, 求轨道的一般解.

解

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m \left(\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}\right) - f (r) - \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} g (\theta)
$$

$$
p _ {r} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {r}} = m \dot {r}, \quad p _ {\theta} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = m r ^ {2} \dot {\theta}
$$

$$
p _ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = m r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi}
$$

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} \left(p _ {r} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} p _ {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta} p _ {\varphi} ^ {2}\right) + f (r) + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} g (\theta)
$$

$$
S = - h t + \alpha_ {\varphi} \varphi + W
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \Big [ \Big (\frac {\partial W}{\partial r} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} \Big (\frac {\partial W}{\partial \theta} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta} \alpha_ {\varphi} ^ {2} \Big ] + f (r) + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} g (\theta) = h
$$

$$
r ^ {2} \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial r}\right) ^ {2} + 2 m r ^ {2} f (r) - 2 m h r ^ {2} + \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial \theta}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {\alpha_ {\varphi} ^ {2}}{\sin^ {2} \theta} + 2 m g (\theta) = 0
$$

$$
W (r, \theta) = W _ {1} (\theta) + W _ {2} (r)
$$

令

则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \theta}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {\alpha_ {\varphi} ^ {2}}{\sin^ {2} \theta} + 2 m g (\theta) = \alpha_ {\theta}} \\ & {r ^ {2} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} r}\right) ^ {2} + 2 m r ^ {2} f (r) - 2 m h r ^ {2} = - \alpha_ {\theta}} \\ & {\left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} r}\right) ^ {2} + 2 m f (r) - 2 m h = - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta}}{r ^ {2}}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \theta} = \sqrt {\alpha_ {\theta} - 2 m g (\theta) - \frac {\alpha_ {\varphi} ^ {2}}{\sin^ {2} \theta}}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} r} = \sqrt {2 m [ h - f (r) ] - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta}}{r ^ {2}}}
$$

$$
W _ {1} = \int \sqrt {\alpha_ {\theta} - 2 m g (\theta) - \frac {\alpha_ {\varphi} ^ {2}}{\sin^ {2} \theta}} \mathrm{d} \theta
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & W _ {2} = \int \sqrt {2 m [ h - f (r) ] - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta}}{r ^ {2}}} \mathrm{d} r \\ & S = - h t + \alpha_ {\varphi} \varphi + \int \sqrt {\alpha_ {\theta} - 2 m g (\theta) - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta}}{\sin^ {2} \theta}} \mathrm{d} \theta + \int \sqrt {2 m [ h - f (r) ] - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta}}{r ^ {2}}} \mathrm{d} r \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\partial S}{\partial \alpha_{\varphi}} = \beta_{\varphi}, \frac{\partial S}{\partial \alpha_{\theta}} = \beta_{\theta}$ 给出轨道方程.

11.5.10 一个质量为 $m$ 的粒子在一个恒力及一个与力心的距离平方成反比的力场中运动, 其势能为 $V = \frac{A}{r} - Bz (A, B$ 均为常量, $r$ 为球坐标), 求其运动学方程.

提示: 可采用抛物线坐标 $\xi, \eta, \theta$ , 它与柱坐标 $\rho, \varphi, z$ 的关系为

$$
\xi = r + z, \quad \eta = r - z, \quad \theta = \varphi , \quad r ^ {2} = \rho^ {2} + z ^ {2}
$$

解 将 $r, \rho, \varphi, z$ 表成 $\xi, \eta, \theta$ 的函数，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & r = \frac {1}{2} (\xi + \eta), \qquad z = \frac {1}{2} (\xi - \eta) \\ & \rho^ {2} = r ^ {2} - z ^ {2} = \xi \eta , \qquad \varphi = \theta \\ & L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {\rho} ^ {2} + \rho^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) - \frac {A}{r} + B z \\ & \qquad = \frac {1}{2} m \Big [ \frac {(\xi \dot {\eta} + \dot {\xi} \eta) ^ {2}}{4 \xi \eta} + \xi \eta \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} (\dot {\xi} - \dot {\eta}) ^ {2} \Big ] - \frac {2 A}{\xi + \eta} + \frac {1}{2} B (\xi - \eta) \\ & p _ {\xi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\xi}} = \frac {m (\xi + \eta)}{4 \xi} \dot {\xi}, \qquad p _ {\eta} = \frac {m (\xi + \eta)}{4 \eta} \dot {\eta}, \qquad p _ {\theta} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = m \xi \eta \dot {\theta} \\ & H = \frac {2}{m (\xi + \eta)} (\xi p _ {\xi} ^ {2} + \eta p _ {\eta} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2 m \xi \eta} - p _ {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {2 A}{\xi + \eta} - \frac {1}{2} B (\xi - \eta) \end{array}
$$

哈密顿-雅可比方程为

因为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {\partial S}{\partial t} + \frac {2}{m (\xi + \eta)} \Big [ \xi \Big (\frac {\partial S}{\partial \xi} \Big) ^ {2} + \eta \Big (\frac {\partial S}{\partial \eta} \Big) ^ {2} \Big ] + \frac {1}{2 m \xi \eta} \Big (\frac {\partial S}{\partial \theta} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {2 A}{\xi + \eta} - \frac {1}{2} B (\xi - \eta) = 0} \\ & {\text {为}} \\ & {\text {令}} \\ & {\qquad \qquad \qquad \qquad \qquad \qquad \qquad \qquad \qquad \qquad \qquad \qquad \frac {\partial H}{\partial t} = 0, \qquad \frac {\partial H}{\partial \theta} = 0} \\ & {\qquad \qquad \qquad S = - h t + \alpha_ {\theta} \theta + W (\xi , \eta)} \end{array}
$$

可令

$W$ 满足的偏微分方程为

可将 $W$ 分离为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {2}{m (\xi + \eta)} \Big [ \xi \Big (\frac {\partial W}{\partial \xi} \Big) ^ {2} + \eta \Big (\frac {\partial W}{\partial \eta} \Big) ^ {2} \Big ] + \frac {1}{2 m \xi \eta} \alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {2 A}{\xi + \eta} - \frac {1}{2} B (\xi - \eta) = h} \\ & {\xi \Big (\frac {\partial W}{\partial \xi} \Big) ^ {2} + \eta \Big (\frac {\partial W}{\partial \eta} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {\xi + \eta}{4 \xi \eta} \alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2} + m A - \frac {1}{4} m (\xi^ {2} - \eta^ {2}) = \frac {1}{2} m (\xi + \eta) h} \\ & {\xi \Big (\frac {\partial W}{\partial \xi} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4 \xi} \alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{4} m B \xi^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m h \xi + \frac {1}{2} m A} \\ & {\qquad + \eta \Big (\frac {\partial W}{\partial \eta} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4 \eta} \alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} m B \eta^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m h \eta + \frac {1}{2} m A = 0} \\ & {\text {分离为}} \\ & {\qquad W (\xi , \eta) = W _ {1} (\xi) + W _ {2} (\eta)} \\ & {\qquad \xi \Big (\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \xi} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4 \xi} \alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{4} m B \xi^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m h \xi + \frac {1}{2} m A = \frac {1}{2} m \alpha} \end{array}
$$

令

则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\eta \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} \eta}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4 \eta} \alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} m B \eta^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m h \eta + \frac {1}{2} m A = - \frac {1}{2} m \alpha} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \xi} = \left[ \frac {1}{2} m h + \frac {1}{4} m B \xi + \frac {m (\alpha - A)}{2 \xi} - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{4 \xi^ {2}} \right] ^ {1 / 2}} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} \eta} = \left[ \frac {1}{2} m h - \frac {1}{4} m B \eta - \frac {m (\alpha + A)}{2 \eta} - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{4 \eta^ {2}} \right] ^ {1 / 2}} \\ & {W _ {1} (\xi) = \int \left[ \frac {1}{2} m h + \frac {1}{4} m B \xi + \frac {m (\alpha - A)}{2 \xi} - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{4 \xi^ {2}} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} \xi} \\ & {W _ {2} (\eta) = \int \left[ \frac {1}{2} m h - \frac {1}{4} m B \eta - \frac {m (\alpha + A)}{2 \eta} - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{4 \eta^ {2}} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} \eta} \\ & {\quad S = - h t + \alpha_ {\theta} \theta + \int \left[ \frac {1}{2} m h + \frac {1}{4} m B \xi + \frac {m (\alpha - A)}{2 \xi} - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{4 \xi^ {2}} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} \xi} \\ & {\quad + \int \left[ \frac {1}{2} m h - \frac {1}{4} m B \eta - \frac {m (\alpha + A)}{2 \eta} - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{4 \eta^ {2}} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} \eta} \end{array}
$$

运动学方程由

$$
\frac {\partial S}{\partial h} = - t _ {0}, \quad \frac {\partial S}{\partial \alpha_ {\theta}} = \beta_ {\theta}, \quad \frac {\partial S}{\partial \alpha} = \beta
$$

获得， $h, \alpha_{\theta}, \alpha, t_{0}, \beta_{\theta}, \beta$ 由初始条件确定.

11.5.11 一个质量为 $m$ 的粒子在下列势场中运动

$$
V = \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{r _ {1} r _ {2}} \left[ f \left(\frac {r _ {2} + r _ {1}}{2 \sigma}\right) + g \left(\frac {r _ {2} - r _ {1}}{2 \sigma}\right) \right],
$$
