其中 $r_1, r_2$ 分别为粒子距两个固定点 $P_1, P_2$ 的距离， $\sigma$ 是一个常数， $f(x), g(x)$ 是给定的任意函数。求用椭圆坐标 $\xi, \eta, \theta$ 表达的哈密顿主函数。 $\xi, \eta, \theta$ 和柱坐标的关系为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \rho = \sigma [ (\xi^ {2} - 1) (1 - \eta^ {2}) ] ^ {1 / 2} \\ z = \sigma \xi \eta , \quad \varphi = \theta \end{array}
$$

选坐标轴 z 和 $\sigma$ 时使 $P_{1}, P_{2}$ 在 z 轴上且分别处在 $z = \sigma$ 和 $z = -\sigma$ 两点.

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {\rho} ^ {2} + \rho^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) - V \\ & \rho = \sigma \sqrt {(\xi^ {2} - 1) (1 - \eta^ {2})}, \quad z = \sigma \xi \eta , \quad \varphi = \theta \\ & \dot {\rho} = \frac {\sigma}{\sqrt {(\xi^ {2} - 1) (1 - \eta^ {2})}} [ (1 - \eta^ {2}) \xi \dot {\xi} - (\xi^ {2} - 1) \eta \dot {\eta} ] \\ & \dot {z} = \sigma (\dot {\xi} \eta + \dot {\xi} \dot {\eta}), \qquad \dot {\varphi} = \dot {\theta} \\ & r _ {1} = \sqrt {\rho^ {2} + (z - \sigma) ^ {2}} = \sigma (\xi - \eta) \\ & r _ {2} = \sigma (\xi + \eta) \\ & \frac {r _ {1} + r _ {2}}{2 \sigma} = \xi , \qquad \frac {r _ {2} - r _ {1}}{2 \sigma} = \eta \\ & T = \frac {1}{2} m \sigma^ {2} (\xi^ {2} - \eta^ {2}) \left(\frac {\dot {\xi} ^ {2}}{\xi^ {2} - 1} + \frac {\dot {\eta} ^ {2}}{1 - \eta^ {2}}\right) + \frac {1}{2} m \sigma^ {2} (\xi^ {2} - 1) (1 - \eta^ {2}) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \\ & V = \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{r _ {1} r _ {2}} \left[ f \left(\frac {r _ {2} + r _ {1}}{2 \sigma}\right) + g \left(\frac {r _ {2} - r _ {1}}{2 \sigma}\right) \right] = \frac {1}{\xi^ {2} - \eta^ {2}} [ f (\xi) + g (\eta) ] \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & L = \frac {1}{2} m \sigma^ {2} (\xi^ {2} - \eta^ {2}) \left(\frac {\dot {\xi} ^ {2}}{\xi^ {2} - 1} + \frac {\dot {\eta} ^ {2}}{1 - \eta^ {2}}\right) + \frac {1}{2} m \sigma^ {2} (\xi^ {2} - 1) (1 - \eta^ {2}) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \\ & \quad - \frac {1}{\xi^ {2} - \eta^ {2}} [ f (\xi) + g (\eta) ] \\ & \quad p _ {\xi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\xi}} = m \sigma^ {2} \frac {\xi^ {2} - \eta^ {2}}{\xi^ {2} - 1} \dot {\xi} \\ & \quad p _ {\eta} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\eta}} = m \sigma^ {2} \frac {\xi^ {2} - \eta^ {2}}{1 - \eta^ {2}} \dot {\eta} \\ & \quad p _ {\theta} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = m \sigma^ {2} (\xi^ {2} - 1) (1 - \eta^ {2}) \dot {\theta} \\ & H = \frac {1}{2 m \sigma^ {2} (\xi^ {2} - \eta^ {2})} \left[ (\xi^ {2} - 1) p _ {\xi} ^ {2} + (1 - \eta^ {2}) p _ {\eta} ^ {2} + \left(\frac {1}{\xi^ {2} - 1} + \frac {1}{1 - \eta^ {2}}\right) p _ {\theta} ^ {2} \right] \\ & \quad + \frac {1}{\xi^ {2} - \eta^ {2}} [ f (\xi) + g (\eta) ] \end{array}
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial H}{\partial t} = 0, \frac{\partial H}{\partial \theta} = 0$ ，哈密顿主函数可写成

W 满足的偏微分方程为

$$
S = - h t + \alpha_ {\theta} \theta + W (\xi , \eta)
$$

令

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {1}{2 m \sigma^ {2} (\xi^ {2} - \eta^ {2})} \Big [ (\xi^ {2} - 1) \Big (\frac {\partial W}{\partial \xi} \Big) ^ {2} + (1 - \eta^ {2}) \Big (\frac {\partial W}{\partial \eta} \Big) ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad + \Big (\frac {1}{\xi^ {2} - 1} + \frac {1}{1 - \eta^ {2}} \Big) \alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2} \Big ] + \frac {1}{\xi^ {2} - \eta^ {2}} [ f (\xi) + g (\eta) ] = h} \\ & {\qquad W (\xi , \eta) = W _ {1} (\xi) + W _ {2} (\eta)} \\ & {(\xi^ {2} - 1) \Big (\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \xi} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{\xi^ {2} - 1} + 2 m \sigma^ {2} f (\xi) - 2 m \sigma^ {2} h \xi^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad + (1 - \eta^ {2}) \Big (\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} \eta} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{1 - \eta^ {2}} + 2 m \sigma^ {2} g (\eta) + 2 m \sigma^ {2} h \eta^ {2} = 0} \\ & {\qquad (\xi^ {2} - 1) \Big (\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \xi} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{\xi^ {2} - 1} + 2 m \sigma^ {2} f (\xi) - 2 m \sigma^ {2} h \xi^ {2} = \alpha^ {\prime}} \\ & {\qquad (1 - \eta^ {2}) \Big (\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} \eta} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{1 - \eta^ {2}} + 2 m \sigma^ {2} g (\eta) + 2 m \sigma^ {2} h \eta^ {2} = - \alpha^ {\prime}} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \xi} = \Big [ \frac {2 m \sigma^ {2} h \xi^ {2}}{\xi^ {2} - 1} + \frac {\alpha^ {\prime} - 2 m \sigma^ {2} f (\xi)}{\xi^ {2} - 1} - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{(\xi^ {2} - 1) ^ {2}} \Big ] ^ {1 / 2}} \\ & {\qquad = \Big [ 2 m \sigma^ {2} h + \frac {\alpha - 2 m \sigma^ {2} f (\xi)}{\xi^ {2} - 1} - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{(\xi^ {2} - 1) ^ {2}} \Big ] ^ {1 / 2}} \end{array}
$$

则

其中 $\alpha=\alpha'+2m\sigma^{2}h$ ，也是常数，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} \eta} & = \left[ - \frac {2 m \sigma^ {2} h \eta^ {2}}{1 - \eta^ {2}} - \frac {\alpha^ {\prime} + 2 m \sigma^ {2} g (\eta)}{1 - \eta^ {2}} - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{(1 - \eta^ {2}) ^ {2}} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ & = \left[ 2 m \sigma^ {2} h - \frac {\alpha + 2 m \sigma^ {2} g (\eta)}{1 - \eta^ {2}} - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{(1 - \eta^ {2}) ^ {2}} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ S & = - h t + \alpha_ {\theta} \theta + \int \left[ 2 m \sigma^ {2} h + \frac {\alpha - 2 m \sigma^ {2} f (\xi)}{\xi^ {2} - 1} - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{(\xi^ {2} - 1) ^ {2}} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} \xi \end{array}
$$

$$
+ \int \left[ 2 m \sigma^ {2} h - \frac {\alpha + 2 m \sigma^ {2} g (\eta)}{1 - \eta^ {2}} - \frac {\alpha_ {\theta} ^ {2}}{(1 - \eta^ {2}) ^ {2}} \right] ^ {1 / 2} d \eta
$$

11.5.12 用拉格朗日-沙比方法重解 11.5.4 题.

解 方法一：

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {2} - k q _ {1}) ^ {2}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial H}{\partial t} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial H}{\partial q _ {2}} = 0
$$

可令

W 满足的(偏)微分方程为

$$
S = - h t + \alpha q _ {2} + W (q _ {1})
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} q _ {1}}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m} (\alpha - k q _ {1}) ^ {2} = h
$$

令 $q_{1} = x$ ，则 $\frac{\mathrm{d}W}{\mathrm{d}q_1} = p$ 

$$
F = \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m} (\alpha - k x) ^ {2} - h
$$

$$
P = \frac {\partial F}{\partial p} = \frac {1}{m} p
$$

$$
X = \frac {\partial F}{\partial x} = - \frac {k}{m} (\alpha - k x)
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} x}{P} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} p}{x}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\frac {1}{m} p} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} p}{- \frac {k}{m} (\alpha - k x)}
$$

$$
p \mathrm{d} p = k (\alpha - k x) \mathrm{d} x
$$

由 $\frac{\partial H}{\partial t} = 0, H = h$ 

$$
p ^ {2} = - (\alpha - k x) ^ {2} + \alpha^ {\prime}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m} (\alpha - k x) ^ {2} = h
$$

可见

$$
\alpha^ {\prime} = 2 m h
$$

又

$$
p = \frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} q _ {1}}
$$

所以

$$
\left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} q _ {1}}\right) ^ {2} = 2 m h - (\alpha - k q _ {1}) ^ {2}
$$

$$
W = \int \sqrt {2 m h - (\alpha - k q _ {1}) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} q _ {1}
$$

$$
S = - h t + a q _ {2} + \int \sqrt {2 m h - (\alpha - k q _ {1}) ^ {2}} d q _ {1}
$$

与 11.5.4 题求得的哈密顿主函数完全相同,以下同 11.5.4 题.

方法二:仍用拉格朗日-沙比方法,不考虑 $q_{2}$ 是循环坐标,考虑到 $\frac{\partial H}{\partial t}=0,H=h$ ,

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial q _ {1}}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial q _ {2}} - k q _ {1}\right) ^ {2} = h
$$

令 $q_{1} = x, q_{2} = y$ ，则 $\frac{\partial W}{\partial q_1} = p, \frac{\partial W}{\partial q_2} = q$ 

$$
F = \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m} (q - k x) ^ {2} - h
$$

$$
X = \frac {\partial F}{\partial x} = - \frac {k}{m} (q - k x)
$$

$$
Y = \frac {\partial F}{\partial y} = 0
$$

$$
P = \frac {\partial F}{\partial p} = \frac {1}{m} p
$$

$$
Q = \frac {\partial F}{\partial q} = \frac {1}{m} (q - k x)
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} x}{P} = \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{Q} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} P}{X} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{Y} \text {得}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\frac {1}{m} p} = \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\frac {1}{m} (q - k x)} = \frac {\mathrm{d} p}{\frac {k}{m} (q - k x)} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{0}
$$

由最后一个等号可见， $\mathrm{d}q = 0,q = \alpha$ 

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\frac {1}{m} p} = \frac {\mathrm{d} p}{\frac {k}{m} (\alpha - k x)}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & p \mathrm{d} p = k (\alpha - k x) \mathrm{d} x \\ & p ^ {2} = - (\alpha - k x) ^ {2} + \alpha^ {\prime} \end{array}
$$

由 $H = \frac{1}{2m} p_1^2 +\frac{1}{2m} (p_2 - kq_1)^2 = h$ 以及 $p = p_{1},q = p_{2} = \alpha$ 可见， $\alpha^{\prime} = 2mh$ 

所以

$$
p ^ {2} = 2 m h - (a - k x) ^ {2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & p = \sqrt {2 m h - (\alpha - k x) ^ {2}} \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\frac {1}{m} \sqrt {2 m h - (\alpha - k x) ^ {2}}} = \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\frac {1}{m} (\alpha - k x)} \\ & y = \int \frac {\alpha - k x}{\sqrt {2 m h - (\alpha - k x) ^ {2}}} \mathrm{d} x + \beta \\ & = \frac {1}{k} \sqrt {2 m h - (\alpha - k x) ^ {2}} + \beta \\ & (y - \beta) ^ {2} = \frac {1}{k ^ {2}} [ 2 m h - (\alpha - k x) ^ {2} ] \\ & \left(q _ {1} - \frac {\alpha}{k}\right) ^ {2} + (q _ {2} - \beta) ^ {2} = \frac {2 m h}{k ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

即

11.5.13 质量为 $m$ 的粒子在势场 $V(r) = \frac{k}{r^2} (k > 0)$ 且为常量)中运动, 用拉格朗日-沙比方法求轨道方程.

解

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} \left(p _ {r} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} p _ {\varphi} ^ {2}\right) + \frac {k}{r ^ {2}}
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial H}{\partial t} = 0$ ，可令

$$
S = - h t + W (r, \varphi)
$$

$W$ 满足的偏微分方程为

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left[ \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial r}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial \varphi}\right) \right] + \frac {k}{r ^ {2}} = h
$$

用拉格朗日-沙比方法，取 $r = x, \varphi = y$ 

则

$$
\frac {\partial W}{\partial r} = p, \quad \frac {\partial W}{\partial \varphi} = q
$$

$$
F = \frac {1}{2 m} \left(p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{x ^ {2}} q ^ {2}\right) + \frac {k}{x ^ {2}} - h
$$

$$
X = \frac {\partial F}{\partial x} = - \frac {q ^ {2}}{m x ^ {3}} - \frac {2 k}{x ^ {3}}
$$

$$
Y = \frac {\partial F}{\partial y} = 0
$$

$$
P = \frac {\partial F}{\partial p} = \frac {1}{m} p
$$

$$
Q = \frac {\partial F}{\partial q} = \frac {1}{m x ^ {2}} q
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} x}{P} = \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{Q} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} p}{X} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{Y}
$$

得

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\frac {1}{m} p} = \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\frac {1}{m x ^ {2}} q} = \frac {\mathrm{d} p}{\frac {q ^ {2}}{m x ^ {3}} + \frac {2 k}{x ^ {3}}} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{0}
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} q = 0
$$

所以

$$
q = \alpha
$$

$$
\frac {1}{m} p \mathrm{d} p = \left(\frac {\alpha^ {2}}{m x ^ {3}} + \frac {2 k}{x ^ {3}}\right) \mathrm{d} x = \left(\frac {\alpha^ {2}}{m} + 2 k\right) \frac {1}{x ^ {3}} \mathrm{d} x
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} = - \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {\alpha^ {2}}{m} + 2 k\right) \frac {1}{x ^ {2}} + h
$$

$$
p = \frac {1}{x} \sqrt {2 m h x ^ {2} - (\alpha^ {2} + 2 m k)}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\frac {1}{m} \cdot \frac {1}{x} \sqrt {2 m h x ^ {2} - (\alpha^ {2} + 2 m k)}} = \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\frac {1}{m x ^ {2}} \alpha}
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} y = \frac {\alpha}{x \sqrt {2 m h x ^ {2} - (\alpha^ {2} + 2 m k)}} \mathrm{d} x
$$

令 $x = \frac{1}{u},\mathrm{d}x = -\frac{1}{u^2}\mathrm{d}u,$ 

$$
\mathrm{d} y = - \frac {\alpha}{\sqrt {2 m h}} \frac {\mathrm{d} u}{\sqrt {1 - \frac {\alpha^ {2} + 2 m k}{2 m h} u ^ {2}}}
$$

$$
\varphi = y = \frac {\alpha}{\sqrt {\alpha^ {2} + 2 m k}} \left[ \arccos \left(\sqrt {\frac {\alpha^ {2} + 2 m k}{2 m h}} u\right) + \frac {\pi}{2} \right]
$$

选择适当的极轴,可得上述的积分常数,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\sqrt {\frac {\alpha^ {2} + 2 m k}{2 m h}} u = \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {\alpha^ {2} + 2 m k}{\alpha}} \varphi - \frac {\pi}{2}\right) = \sin \left(\sqrt {\frac {\alpha^ {2} + 2 m k}{\alpha}} \varphi\right)} \\ & {\qquad r = \frac {1}{u} = \sqrt {\frac {\alpha^ {2} + 2 m k}{2 m h}} \csc \left(\sqrt {1 + \frac {2 m k}{\alpha^ {2}}} \varphi\right)} \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/f021f2f3d5f8d9bb8221d9e194fed927407231570bf8116f15ebbef8548f669c.jpg)


11.5.14 一个电机使一根竖直轴随之旋转, 轴的下端挂一个长度为 l、质量为 m 的单摆, 单摆被限制在一个平面内运动, 这个平面由电动机带动以恒定的角速度 $\omega$ 旋转, 如图 11.11 所示.


图11.11


(1) 用拉格朗日-沙比方法得到质点的运动微分方程；

(2) 求出在转动参考系中的平衡位置及绕平衡位置做小振动的频率,说明平衡的稳定条件.

解 (1)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} T & = \frac {1}{2} m (l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + l ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \omega^ {2}) \\ V & = m g l (1 - \cos \theta) \end{array}
$$

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m (l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) - m g l (1 - \cos \theta)
$$

$$
p _ {\theta} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta}
$$

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m l ^ {2}} p _ {\theta} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + m g l (1 - \cos \theta)
$$

哈密顿-雅可比方程为

$$
\frac {\partial S}{\partial t} + \frac {1}{2 m l ^ {2}} \left(\frac {\partial S}{\partial \theta}\right) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + m g l (1 - \cos \theta) = 0
$$

用拉格朗日-沙比方法，令 $\theta = x, t = y$ ，则

$$
\frac {\partial S}{\partial \theta} = p, \quad \frac {\partial S}{\partial t} = q
$$

$$
F = q + \frac {1}{2 m l ^ {2}} p ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \sin^ {2} x + m g l (1 - \cos x)
$$

$$
X = \frac {\partial F}{\partial x} = - m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \sin x \cos x + m g l \sin x
$$

$$
Y = \frac {\partial F}{\partial y} = 0
$$

$$
P = \frac {\partial F}{\partial p} = \frac {1}{m l ^ {2}} p
$$

$$
Q = \frac {\partial F}{\partial q} = 1
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{P} = \frac{\mathrm{d}y}{Q} = -\frac{\mathrm{d}p}{X} = -\frac{\mathrm{d}q}{Y}$ 得

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\frac {1}{m l ^ {2}} p} = \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{1} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{0} = \frac {\mathrm{d} p}{m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \sin x \cos x - m g l \sin x}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} p}{\mathrm{d} y} = m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \sin x \cos x - m g l \sin x
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\mathrm{d} y} = \frac {1}{m l ^ {2}} p
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} x}{\mathrm{d} y ^ {2}} = \frac {1}{m l ^ {2}} \frac {\mathrm{d} p}{\mathrm{d} y} = \frac {1}{m l ^ {2}} (m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \sin x \cos x - m g l \sin x) = \left(\omega^ {2} \cos x - \frac {g}{l}\right) \sin x
$$

将 $x, y$ 分别换回 $\theta, t$ , 上式为

$$
\ddot {\theta} - \left(\omega^ {2} \cos \theta - \frac {g}{l}\right) \sin \theta = 0
$$

(2) 平衡位置由

$$
\left(\omega^ {2} \cos \theta - \frac {g}{l}\right) \sin \theta = 0
$$

确定, 可能有两个平衡位置, 它们是 $\theta = \theta_{10} = 0, \theta = \theta_{20} = \arccos\left(\frac{g}{l\omega^2}\right)$ (当 $\frac{g}{l\omega^2} < 1$ 时).

在 $\theta=\theta_{10}=0$ 附近，运动微分方程可近似为

$$
\dot {\theta} - \left(\omega^ {2} - \frac {g}{l}\right) \theta = 0
$$

当 $\omega^2 < \frac{g}{l}$ 时，可在 $\theta = 0$ 附近做小振动，小振动的角频率为 $\Omega_1 = \sqrt{\frac{g}{l} - \omega^2}, \theta = 0$ 是稳定平衡位置；当 $\omega^2 \geqslant \frac{g}{l}$ 时，运动不可能限在 $\theta = 0$ 附近，不能围绕 $\theta = 0$ 做小振动， $\theta = 0$ 是不稳定平衡位置。

在 $\theta = \theta_{20} = \arccos \left(\frac{g}{l\omega^2}\right)$ 附近（只有在 $\frac{g}{l\omega^2} < 1$ 时才存在这个平衡位置），将 $\cos \theta, \sin \theta$ 在 $\theta_{20}$ 附近作泰勒展开，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \cos \theta & = \cos \theta_ {2 0} - \sin \theta_ {2 0} (\theta - \theta_ {2 0}) + \dots \\ & \approx \frac {g}{l \omega^ {2}} - \sqrt {1 - \frac {g ^ {2}}{l ^ {2} \omega^ {4}}} (\theta - \theta_ {2 0}) \\ \sin \theta & = \sin \theta_ {2 0} + \cos \theta_ {2 0} (\theta - \theta_ {2 0}) + \dots \\ & \approx \sqrt {1 - \frac {g ^ {2}}{l ^ {2} \omega^ {4}}} + \frac {g}{l \omega^ {2}} (\theta - \theta_ {2 0}) \end{array}
$$

令 $\theta' = \theta - \theta_{20}$ , 在 $\theta = \theta_{20}$ 附近的运动微分方程为

$$
\ddot {\theta} ^ {\prime} - \left[ \omega^ {2} \left(\frac {g}{l \omega^ {2}} - \sqrt {1 - \frac {g ^ {2}}{l ^ {2} \omega^ {4}}} \theta^ {\prime}\right) - \frac {g}{l} \right] \left[ \sqrt {1 - \frac {g ^ {2}}{l ^ {2} \omega^ {4}}} + \frac {g}{l \omega^ {2}} \theta^ {\prime} \right] = 0
$$

只保留一级小量，

$$
\ddot {\theta} ^ {\prime} + \omega^ {2} \left(1 - \frac {g ^ {2}}{l ^ {2} \omega^ {4}}\right) \theta^ {\prime} = 0
$$

当 $1 - \frac{g^2}{l^2\omega^4} > 0$ ，即当 $\omega^2 > \frac{g}{l}$ 时，存在着第二个平衡位置，而且是稳定平衡位置，可围绕它做

小振动,小振动的角频率为

$$
\Omega_ {2} = \omega \sqrt {1 - \frac {g ^ {2}}{l ^ {2} \omega^ {4}}}
$$

11.5.15 一个质量为 $m$ 的质点在随时间变化的势场中沿直线运动, $V = mAxt^2$ , $A$ 为常数. 用拉格朗日-沙比方法求此质点运动的一般解.

解

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2} - m A x t ^ {2}
$$

$$
p = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = m \dot {x}
$$

$$
H = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} + m A x t ^ {2}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial S}{\partial t} + \frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\partial S}{\partial x}\right) ^ {2} + m A x t ^ {2} = 0
$$

用拉格朗日-沙比方法. 令 $\alpha = x, t = y$ , 则 $\frac{\partial S}{\partial x} = p, \frac{\partial S}{\partial t} = q$ ,

$$
F = q + \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} + m A x y ^ {2}
$$

$$
X = \frac {\partial F}{\partial x} = m A y ^ {2}
$$

$$
Y = \frac {\partial F}{\partial y} = 2 m A x y
$$

$$
P = \frac {\partial F}{\partial p} = \frac {p}{m}
$$

$$
Q = \frac {\partial F}{\partial q} = 1
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} x}{P} - \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{Q} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} p}{X} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{Y}
$$

得

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\underline {{p}}} = \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{1} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} p}{m A y ^ {2}} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{2 m A x y}
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} p = - m A y ^ {2} \mathrm{d} y
$$

$$
p = - \frac {1}{3} m A y ^ {3} + a m
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} x = \frac {p}{m} \mathrm{d} y = \frac {1}{m} \left(- \frac {1}{3} m A y ^ {3} + a m\right) \mathrm{d} y
$$

$$
x = - \frac {1}{1 2} A y ^ {4} + \alpha y + \beta
$$

也即

$$
x = - \frac {1}{1 2} A y ^ {4} + \alpha t + \beta
$$

## 11.6 作用变量、角变量及其应用

11.6.1 用作用变量和角变量分析一个粒在下列有心力作用下的运动,这个有心力

的势能为

$$
V (r) = - \frac {k}{r} - \frac {\beta}{r ^ {2}}
$$

其中 $r$ 是粒子离力心的距离， $k$ 和 $\beta$ 是正的常量。设 $-\frac{mk^2}{2h^2 - 4m\beta} < E < 0$ ，其中 $E$ 是粒子的机械能， $h$ 是粒子对力心的角动量。在粒子运动所跨越的 $r$ 的范围内有 $\beta \ll kr$ 。

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} H & = \frac {p _ {r} ^ {2}}{2 m} + \frac {p _ {\varphi} ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}} - \frac {k}{r} - \frac {\beta}{r ^ {2}} \\ & \frac {\partial H}{\partial t} = 0 \end{array}
$$

特性函数 $W$ 满足的哈密顿-雅可比方程为

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial r}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m r ^ {2}} \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial \varphi}\right) ^ {2} - \frac {k}{r} - \frac {\beta}{r ^ {2}} = E
$$

可作变量分离，

$$
W = W _ {r} (r, E, h) + W _ {\varphi} (\varphi , E, h)
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {r}}{\mathrm{d} r}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m r ^ {2}} \left(\frac {\partial W _ {\varphi}}{\mathrm{d} \varphi}\right) ^ {2} - \frac {k}{r} - \frac {\beta}{r ^ {2}} = E
$$

因为

$$
\frac {\partial H}{\partial \varphi} = 0, \quad p _ {\varphi} = h (h \text {为常量})
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {\varphi}}{\mathrm{d} \varphi} = h
$$

$$
W _ {\varphi} = h \varphi
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {r}}{\mathrm{d} r} = \sqrt {2 m} \left(E + \frac {k}{r} + \frac {\beta}{r ^ {2}} - \frac {h ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}}\right) ^ {1 / 2}
$$

$$
W _ {r} = \pm \sqrt {2 m} \int \left(E + \frac {k}{r} + \frac {2 m \beta - h ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} r
$$

在 $r - p_r$ 子相空间中的轨道方程为

$$
p _ {r} = \frac {\partial W _ {r}}{\partial r} = \sqrt {2 m} \left(E + \frac {k}{r} + \frac {2 m \beta - h ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}}\right) ^ {1 / 2}
$$

在 $\varphi - p_{\varphi}$ 子相空间中的轨道方程为

$$
p _ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial W _ {\varphi}}{\partial \varphi} = h
$$

在子相空间中如要做周期运动，在 $r - p_r$ 子相空间中必须在 $r_1 \leqslant r \leqslant r_2$ 范围内运动， $r_1, r_2$ 是

或

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & E + \frac {k}{r} + \frac {2 m \beta - h ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}} = 0 \\ & 2 m r ^ {2} E + 2 k m r + 2 m \beta - h ^ {2} = 0 \end{array}
$$

的两个根. $r_{1}\neq r_{2}$ ，且均为正实数，要求

$$
\begin{array}{r l} r & = \frac {- 2 k m \pm \sqrt {4 k ^ {2} m ^ {2} - 4 \cdot 2 m E (2 m \beta - h ^ {2})}}{4 m E} \\ & = \frac {- k m \pm \sqrt {k ^ {2} m ^ {2} - 2 m E (2 m \beta - h ^ {2})}}{2 m E} \end{array}
$$

中

(1) $-\frac{km}{2mE}>0$ ,要求E<0;

(2) $k^2 m^2 - 2mE(2m\beta - h^2) > 0$ ，由此要求 $k^2 m > 2E(2m\beta - h^2)$ ，如 $E < 0, 2m\beta - h^2 > 0, r_1, r_2$ 均为实根，但有一个为负值，如 $E < 0, 2m\beta - h^2 < 0, |E| < \frac{k^2m}{2h^2 - 4m\beta}$ ，则 $r_1, r_2$ 均为正实根，题设条件正是这种情况。

在 $\varphi-p_{\varphi}$ 子相空间中轨道也是闭合的， $\varphi$ 运动在 $0\sim2\pi(p_{\varphi}$ 不变）间.

系统是一个可分离的周期系统,可定义一组作用变量和角变量,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} J _ {r} (E, h) & = \oint \frac {\partial W _ {r} (r , E , h)}{\partial r} \mathrm{d} r \\ & = \sqrt {2 m} \oint \left(E + \frac {k}{r} + \frac {2 m \beta - h ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} r \\ & = 2 \sqrt {2 m} \int_ {r _ {1}} ^ {r _ {2}} \left(E + \frac {k}{r} + \frac {2 m \beta - h ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} r \end{array}
$$

这里用了 $p_r(r, E, h)$ 对 $r$ 轴是对称的. 作变换, 令

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & r = \frac {1}{u}, \quad \mathrm{d} r = - \frac {1}{u ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} u \\ & J _ {r} (E, h) = - 2 \sqrt {2 m} \int_ {u _ {1}} ^ {u _ {2}} \frac {1}{u ^ {2}} \left(E + k u + \frac {2 m \beta - h ^ {2}}{2 m} u ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm{d} u \end{array}
$$

用积分公式

$$
\int \sqrt {\frac {X}{x ^ {2}}} \mathrm{d} x = - \sqrt {\frac {X}{x}} + \frac {b}{2} \int \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{x \sqrt {X}} + c \int \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\sqrt {X}}
$$

其中

$$
X = a + b x + c x ^ {2}
$$

$$
\int \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{x \sqrt {X}} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {- a}} \arcsin \left(\frac {b x + 2 a}{x \sqrt {- q}}\right) \quad (\text {当} a <   0, q <   0 \text {时})
$$

其中

$$
q = 4 a c - b ^ {2}
$$

$$
\int \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\sqrt {X}} = - \frac {1}{\sqrt {- c}} \arcsin \left(\frac {2 c x + b}{\sqrt {- q}}\right) \quad (\text {当} c <   0, q <   0 \text {时})
$$

今

$$
a = E <   0, b = k, \quad c = \frac {2 m \beta - h ^ {2}}{2 m} <   0
$$

$$
q = 4 E \frac {2 m \beta - h ^ {2}}{2 m} - k ^ {2} = \frac {2 E (2 m \beta - h ^ {2}) - m k ^ {2}}{m} <   0
$$

可用上述三个积分公式,经计算可得

$$
J _ {r} (E, h) = 2 \pi \left(h ^ {2} - 2 m \beta\right) ^ {1 / 2} - \pi k \left(- \frac {2 m}{E}\right) ^ {1 / 2}
$$

$$
J _ {\varphi} (E, h) = \oint \frac {\partial W _ {\varphi} (\varphi , E , h)}{\partial \varphi} \mathrm{d} \varphi = \oint h \mathrm{d} \varphi = \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} h \mathrm{d} \varphi = 2 \pi h
$$

从上述两式解出 E, h,

$$
E = - \frac {2 m \pi^ {2} k ^ {2}}{\left[ (J _ {\varphi} ^ {2} - 8 \pi^ {2} m \beta) ^ {1 / 2} - J _ {r} \right] ^ {2}}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} {h = \frac {J _ {\varphi}}{2 \pi}} \\ {H ^ {*} (J _ {r}, J _ {\varphi}) = E = - \frac {2 m \pi^ {2} k ^ {2}}{\left[ (J _ {\varphi} ^ {2} - 8 \pi^ {2} m \beta) ^ {1 / 2} - J _ {r} \right] ^ {2}}} \end{array}
$$

由哈密顿正则方程

$$
\begin{array}{l} \dot {J} _ {r} = - \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial w _ {r}} = 0 \\ \dot {J} _ {\varphi} = - \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial w _ {\varphi}} = 0 \\ \dot {w} _ {r} = \nu_ {r} = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J _ {r}} = \frac {4 m \pi^ {2} k ^ {2}}{\left[ (J _ {\varphi} ^ {2} - 8 \pi^ {2} m \beta) ^ {1 / 2} - J _ {r} \right] ^ {3}} = \frac {(- 2 m E) ^ {3 / 2}}{2 m ^ {2} \pi k} \end{array}
$$

这里用了 $(J_{\varphi}^{2} - 8\pi^{2}m\beta)^{1 / 2} - J_{r} = \left(\frac{2m\pi^{2}k^{2}}{-E}\right)^{1 / 2} = \frac{2m\pi k}{(-2mE)^{1 / 2}}$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \dot {w} _ {\varphi} & = \nu_ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J _ {\varphi}} \\ & = \frac {4 m \pi^ {2} k ^ {2}}{\left[ (J _ {\varphi} ^ {2} - 8 \pi^ {2} m \beta) ^ {1 / 2} - J _ {r} \right] ^ {3}} \cdot \frac {2 J _ {\varphi}}{2 (J _ {\varphi} ^ {2} - 8 \pi^ {2} m \beta) ^ {1 / 2}} \\ & = \frac {J _ {\varphi}}{(J _ {\varphi} ^ {2} - 8 \pi^ {2} m \beta) ^ {1 / 2}} \nu_ {r} \end{array}
$$

由

$$
w _ {i} = \sum_ {k = 1} ^ {s} \frac {\partial W _ {k} (q _ {k} , J)}{\partial J _ {i}}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} w _ {r} & = \frac {\partial W _ {r}}{\partial J _ {r}} + \frac {\partial W _ {r}}{\partial J _ {r}} \\ & = \frac {\partial W _ {r}}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J _ {r}} + \frac {\partial W _ {r}}{\partial h} \frac {\partial h}{\partial J _ {r}} + \frac {\partial W _ {\varphi}}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J _ {r}} + \frac {\partial W _ {\varphi}}{\partial h} \frac {\partial h}{\partial J _ {r}} \\ & = \frac {\partial W _ {r}}{\partial E} \nu_ {r} = \nu_ {r} F (r) \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{r l} F (r) & = \frac {\partial W _ {r} (r , E , h)}{\partial E} = \sqrt {\frac {m}{2}} \int \left(E + \frac {k}{r} + \frac {2 m \beta - h ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}}\right) ^ {- 1 / 2} \mathrm{d} r \\ w _ {\varphi} & = \frac {\partial W _ {r}}{\partial J _ {\varphi}} + \frac {\partial W _ {\varphi}}{\partial J _ {\varphi}} \\ & = \frac {\partial W _ {r}}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J _ {\varphi}} + \frac {\partial W _ {r}}{\partial h} \frac {\partial h}{\partial J _ {\varphi}} + \frac {\partial W _ {\varphi}}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J _ {\varphi}} + \frac {\partial W _ {\varphi}}{\partial h} \frac {\partial h}{\partial J _ {\varphi}} \\ & = \nu_ {\varphi} F (r) - G (r) + \frac {\varphi}{2 \pi} \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

其中

$$
G (r) = - \frac {\partial W _ {r}}{\partial h} \frac {\partial h}{\partial J _ {\varphi}} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {m}{2}} \int \left(E + \frac {k}{r} + \frac {2 m \beta - h ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}}\right) ^ {- 1 / 2} \left(\frac {h}{m r ^ {2}}\right) d r
$$

从(1)式原则上可以得到

$$
r = r (w _ {r})\tag{3}
$$

r 是 $w_{r}$ 的周期函数, 周期为 1,

$$
w _ {r} = \nu_ {r} t + \lambda_ {r}\tag{4}
$$

$$
w _ {\varphi} = \nu_ {\varphi} t + \lambda_ {\varphi}\tag{5}
$$

r 是 t 的周期函数, 周期为 $\frac{1}{\nu_{r}}$ .

将式(3)代入式(2)，解出 $\varphi$ ，可得

$$
\varphi = \varphi (w _ {r}, w _ {\varphi})\tag{6}
$$

$\varphi$ 是 $(w_{r}, w_{\varphi})$ 的双重周期函数，周期单元为 $(1,0), (0,1)$ ，时间上改变 $\frac{1}{\nu_r}$ ， $w_{r}$ 改变 1，时间上改变 $\frac{1}{\nu_{\varphi}}$ ， $w_{\varphi}$ 改变 1。一般讲， $\varphi$ 不是时间的周期函数，只有存在一对整数 $K_{r}, K_{\varphi}$ ，有 $\frac{K_{r}}{\nu_{r}} = \frac{K_{\varphi}}{\nu_{\varphi}}$ 时， $\varphi$ 才是时间的周期函数。

如能获得 $r = r(w_{r})$ 和 $\varphi = \varphi (w_r,w_\varphi)$ ，就能由式(4)、(5)得到 $r(t)$ 和 $\varphi (t)$ 

如势能函数中的 $\beta=0$ ，质点在平方反比律有心力场中运动，

$$
\nu_ {r} = \nu_ {\varphi} = \nu = \frac {(- 2 m E) ^ {3 / 2}}{2 m ^ {2} \pi k}
$$

$r(t)$ 和 $\varphi(t)$ 都是时间的周期函数, 周期为 $\frac{1}{\nu}$ . 在这种情况下, 在空间的轨道是闭合的, 是一个椭圆.

如 $\beta$ 是非零小量，轨道虽不闭合，但近似一个椭圆，近心点围绕力心缓慢进动，不需求出 $r(t)$ 和 $\varphi(t)$ ，就能知道进动的速率。比较式(2)和式(5)，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\nu_ {\varphi} F (r) - G (r) + \frac {\varphi}{2 \pi} = \nu_ {\varphi} t + \lambda_ {\varphi}} \\ & {\varphi = 2 \pi \nu_ {\varphi} t - 2 \pi \nu_ {\varphi} F (r) + 2 \pi G (r) + 2 \pi \lambda_ {\varphi}} \end{array}
$$

因 $r(t)$ 是 $t$ 的周期函数，周期为 $\frac{1}{\nu_r}$ ，在此期间， $\varphi$ 的改变量

$$
\Delta \varphi = 2 \pi \nu_ {\varphi} \frac {1}{\nu_ {r}} = 2 \pi \left(\frac {\nu_ {\varphi}}{\nu_ {r}}\right)
$$

这里用了 $r\left(t + \frac{1}{\nu_r}\right) = r(t)$ ，

故

$$
F \left[ r \left(t + \frac {1}{\nu_ {r}}\right) \right] = F [ r (t) ]
$$

$$
G \left[ r \left(t + \frac {1}{\nu_ {r}}\right) \right] = G [ r (t) ]
$$

如 $\nu_{\varphi} = \nu_r, \Delta \varphi = 2\pi$ ，没有进动；如 $\nu_{\varphi} \neq \nu_r, \Delta \varphi \neq 2\pi$ ，进动角为

$$
2 \pi (\nu_ {\varphi} / \nu_ {r}) - 2 \pi
$$

进动速率为

$$
\frac {2 \pi (\nu_ {\varphi} / \nu_ {r}) - 2 \pi}{1 / \nu_ {r}} = 2 \pi (\nu_ {\varphi} - \nu_ {r})
$$

如对 $\beta$ 这个小量作一级近似，

$$
\nu_ {\varphi} = \frac {J _ {\varphi}}{\left(J _ {\varphi} ^ {2} - 8 \pi^ {2} m \beta\right) ^ {1 / 2}} \nu_ {r} \approx \left(1 + \frac {4 \pi^ {2} m \beta}{J _ {\varphi} ^ {2}}\right) \nu_ {r}
$$

$J_{\varphi}$ 用 $\beta = 0$ 时的值作近似，

$$
J _ {\varphi} = 2 \pi h \approx 2 \pi m \cdot \frac {\pi a b}{T}
$$

其中 T 是椭圆运动的周期, a、b 分别是椭圆轨道的长半轴和短半轴,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {T ^ {2}}{a ^ {3}} = \frac {4 \pi^ {2} m}{k}} \\ & {J _ {\varphi} ^ {2} \approx \pi^ {2} m b ^ {2} k / a} \\ & {\nu_ {\varphi} \approx \left(1 + \frac {4 \beta a}{k b ^ {2}}\right) \nu_ {r}} \end{array}
$$

进动速率近似为 $\frac{8\pi\beta a}{kb^{2}}\nu_{r}$ ，其中a、b是 $\beta=0$ 时椭圆轨道的长半轴和短半轴.

11.6.2 一质量为 $m$ 的质点在势能 $V(x) = F|x|(F$ 是一正值常量)作用下做一维运动, 利用作用变量、角变量把运动的周期表达成能量的函数.

解

即

$$
H = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} + F x & 0 \leqslant x \leqslant \frac {E}{F} \\ \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} - F x & - \frac {E}{F} \leqslant x \leqslant 0 \end{array} \right.
$$

关于质点的运动范围为 $\left[-\frac{E}{F}, \frac{E}{F}\right]$ ( $E$ 为总能量)作如下说明: 因为质点的机械能守恒,

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} + F | x | = E
$$

在 $x$ 的最大值， $p = 0, x_{\max} = \frac{E}{F}$ . 同样，在 $x$ 的最小值， $p = 0, F(-x_{\min}) = E, x_{\min} = -\frac{E}{F}$ ，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & J = \oint p \mathrm{d} x = 2 \int_ {- \frac {E}{F}} ^ {\frac {E}{F}} \sqrt {2 m (E - V)} \mathrm{d} x \\ & = 2 \left[ \int_ {- \frac {E}{F}} ^ {0} \sqrt {2 m (E + F x)} \mathrm{d} x + \int_ {0} ^ {\frac {E}{F}} \sqrt {2 m (E - F x)} \mathrm{d} x \right] \\ & = \frac {8 \sqrt {2 m}}{3 F} E ^ {3 / 2} \\ & E = \left(\frac {3 F J}{8 \sqrt {2 m}}\right) ^ {2 / 3} \\ & H ^ {*} (J) = E = \left(\frac {3 F J}{8 \sqrt {2 m}}\right) ^ {2 / 3} \\ & \nu = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J} = \left(\frac {3 F}{8 \sqrt {2 m}}\right) ^ {2 / 3} \cdot \frac {2}{3} J ^ {- 1 / 3} \\ & = \left(\frac {3 F}{8 \sqrt {2 m}}\right) ^ {2 / 3} \cdot \frac {2}{3} \left(\frac {8 \sqrt {2 m}}{3 F} E ^ {3 / 2}\right) ^ {- 1 / 3} = \frac {F}{4 \sqrt {2 m E}} \\ & T = \frac {1}{\nu} = \frac {4 \sqrt {2 m E}}{F} \end{array}
$$

11.6.3 一质量为 $m$ 的质点在势能 $V = -\frac{k}{|x|}$ ( $k$ 为正值常量)作用下做一维运动，能量为负值时,运动是有界的和振荡的.用作用变量、角变量把运动的周期表达成能量的函数.

解

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} - \frac {k}{| x |}
$$

即

$$
H = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} - \frac {k}{x} & 0 \leqslant x \leqslant \frac {k}{E} \\ \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} + \frac {k}{x} & \frac {k}{E} \leqslant x \leqslant 0 \end{array} \right.
$$

注意 $E < 0$ 

$$
p = \sqrt {2 m \left(E + \frac {k}{| x |}\right)}
$$

$$
J = \oint p \mathrm{d} x = 2 \left[ \int_ {\frac {k}{E}} ^ {0} \sqrt {2 m \left(E - \frac {k}{x}\right)} \mathrm{d} x + \int_ {0} ^ {- \frac {k}{E}} \sqrt {2 m \left(E + \frac {k}{x}\right)} \mathrm{d} x \right]
$$

对于第一个积分，令 $x' = -x, \mathrm{d}x = -\mathrm{d}x'$ ，

$$
\int_ {\frac {k}{E}} ^ {0} \sqrt {2 m \left(E - \frac {k}{x}\right)} d x = - \int_ {- \frac {k}{E}} ^ {0} \sqrt {2 m \left(E + \frac {k}{x ^ {\prime}}\right)} d x ^ {\prime} = \int_ {0} ^ {- \frac {k}{E}} \sqrt {2 m \left(E + \frac {k}{x}\right)} d x
$$

所以

$$
J = 4 \int_ {0} ^ {- \frac {k}{E}} \sqrt {2 m \left(E + \frac {k}{x}\right)} d x
$$

作变换，令 $x = u^2, \mathrm{d}x = 2u\mathrm{d}u$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} J & = 4 \int_ {0} ^ {\sqrt {- \frac {k}{E}}} \sqrt {2 m \left(E + \frac {k}{u ^ {2}}\right)} \cdot 2 u \mathrm{d} u \\ & = 8 \sqrt {2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {\sqrt {- \frac {k}{E}}} \sqrt {(k - \sqrt {- E} u) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} u \end{array}
$$

用积分公式：

$$
\int \sqrt {a ^ {2} - x ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} x = \frac {1}{2} \left[ x \sqrt {a ^ {2} - x ^ {2}} + a ^ {2} \arcsin \left(\frac {x}{a}\right) \right]
$$

$$
J = \frac {2 \pi \sqrt {2 m}}{\sqrt {- E}} k
$$

$$
E = - \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m k ^ {2}}{J ^ {2}}
$$

$$
H ^ {*} (J) = E = - \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m k ^ {2}}{J ^ {2}}
$$

$$
\nu = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J} = 1 6 \pi^ {2} m k ^ {2} J ^ {- 3} = \frac {(- E) ^ {3 / 2}}{\pi k \sqrt {2 m}}
$$

$$
T = \frac {1}{\nu} = \pi k \sqrt {2 m} (- E) ^ {- 3 / 2}
$$

11.6.4 利用作用变量、角变量求力常数各不相同的三维谐振子的频率，并求出笛卡儿坐标以及与之共轭的动量表成作用变量、角变量的函数.

解

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} (p _ {x} ^ {2} + p _ {y} ^ {2} + p _ {z} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} x ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} y ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {3} z ^ {2}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} x ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} y ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 m} p _ {z} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {3} z ^ {2} = E
$$

设

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} p _ {y} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} y ^ {2} = \alpha_ {2} \quad (\alpha_ {2} \text {为常量})
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} p _ {z} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {3} z ^ {2} = \alpha_ {3} \quad (\alpha_ {3} \text {为常量})
$$

则

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} p _ {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} x ^ {2} = E - \alpha_ {2} - \alpha_ {3}
$$

[NO TEXT] 

$$
\frac {p _ {y} ^ {2}}{2 m \alpha_ {2}} + \frac {y ^ {2}}{\frac {2 \alpha_ {2}}{k _ {2}}} = 1
$$

在 $y - p_y$ 子相空间， $p_y = p_y(y)$ 是一个椭圆方程，椭圆的长、短半轴分别为 $\sqrt{2m\alpha_2}$ 和 $\sqrt{\frac{2\alpha_2}{k_2}}$ 

$$
J _ {2} = \oint p _ {y} \mathrm{d} y = \pi \sqrt {2 m \alpha_ {2}} \cdot \sqrt {\frac {2 \alpha_ {2}}{k _ {2}}} = 2 \pi \alpha_ {2} \sqrt {\frac {m}{k _ {2}}}
$$

同理，

$$
J _ {3} = 2 \pi \alpha_ {3} \sqrt {\frac {m}{k _ {3}}}
$$

$$
J _ {1} = 2 \pi (E - \alpha_ {2} - \alpha_ {3}) \sqrt {\frac {m}{k _ {1}}}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} H ^ {*} (J _ {1}, J _ {2}, J _ {3}) & = E = \frac {J _ {1}}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {1}}{m}} + \alpha_ {2} + \alpha_ {3} \\ & = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \left(J _ {1} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {1}}{m}} + J _ {2} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {2}}{m}} + J _ {3} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {3}}{m}}\right) \end{array}
$$

$$
\nu_ {1} = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J _ {1}} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {1}}{m}}
$$

同理

$$
\nu_ {2} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {2}}{m}}, \quad \nu_ {3} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {3}}{m}}.
$$

要求新旧正则变量间的变换关系,需找出母函数.

$$
W (\pmb {q}, \pmb {J}) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {3} W _ {i} (q _ {i}, \pmb {\alpha} (\pmb {J})) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {3} W _ {i} (q _ {i}, \pmb {J})
$$

$W_{i}$ 满足的微分方程为

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} x}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} x ^ {2} + \alpha_ {2} + \alpha_ {3} = E
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} y}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} y ^ {2} = \alpha_ {2}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W _ {3}}{\mathrm{d} z}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {3} z ^ {2} = \alpha_ {3}
$$

$$
W _ {1} = \int \sqrt {2 m \left[ (E - \alpha_ {2} - \alpha_ {3}) - \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} x ^ {2} \right]} d x = \int \sqrt {2 m \left[ \left(\frac {J _ {1}}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {1}}{m}}\right) - \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} x ^ {2} \right]} d x
$$

$$
W _ {2} = \int \sqrt {2 m \left[ \left(\frac {J _ {2}}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {2}}{m}}\right) - \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} y ^ {2} \right]} d y
$$

$$
W _ {3} = \int \sqrt {2 m \left[ \left(\frac {J _ {3}}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {3}}{m}}\right) - \frac {1}{2} k _ {3} z ^ {2} \right]} d z
$$

由

$$
w _ {i} = \frac {\partial W (\boldsymbol {q} , \boldsymbol {J})}{\partial J _ {i}} = \sum_ {k = 1} ^ {s} \frac {\partial W _ {k} (q _ {k} , \boldsymbol {J})}{\partial J _ {i}}
$$

1 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & w _ {1} = \frac {\partial W _ {1}}{\partial J _ {1}} + \frac {\partial W _ {2}}{\partial J _ {1}} + \frac {\partial W _ {3}}{\partial J _ {1}} = \frac {\partial W _ {1}}{\partial J _ {1}} \\ & \qquad = \sqrt {2 m} \int \frac {\frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {1}}{m}}}{2 \left(\sqrt {\frac {J _ {1}}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {1}}{m}} - \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2}}\right)} \mathrm{d} x = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \arcsin \left(\sqrt {\frac {\pi}{J _ {1}} \sqrt {k _ {1} m}} x\right) \\ & x = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\frac {\pi}{J _ {1}} \sqrt {m k _ {1}}}} \sin (2 \pi w _ {1}) \end{array}
$$

同样可得

$$
p _ {x} = \frac {\partial W _ {1}}{\partial x} = \sqrt {2 m \left(\frac {J _ {1}}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k _ {1}}{m}} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} x ^ {2}\right)} = \sqrt {\frac {J _ {1}}{\pi} \sqrt {m k _ {1}}} \cos (2 \pi w _ {1})
$$

同样可得

$$
p _ {y} = \sqrt {\frac {J _ {2}}{\pi} \sqrt {m k _ {2}}} \cos (2 \pi w _ {2})
$$

$$
p _ {z} = \sqrt {\frac {J _ {3}}{\pi} \sqrt {m k _ {3}}} \cos (2 \pi w _ {3})
$$

如将 $w_{1} = \nu_{1}t + \varphi_{1}, w_{2} = \nu_{2}t + \varphi_{2}, w_{3} = \nu_{3}t + \varphi_{3}$ 代入，可得 $x, y, z, p_{x}, p_{y}, p_{z}$ 与 $t$ 的函数关系，其中 $\varphi_{1}, \varphi_{2}, \varphi_{3}, E, \alpha_{2}, \alpha_{3}$ 六个积分常数由初始条件确定.

11.6.5 一个力常数为 $k$ 的线性谐振子, 它的质量突然增加一个小量 $\varepsilon$ , 用正则微扰论求振子的频率增量 $\nu - \nu^0$ , 算到 $\varepsilon$ 的二次方为止. 证明在 $\varepsilon$ 的同级近似下所得结果与增量的严格预期值是一致的.

解

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 (m + \varepsilon)} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2 m \left(1 + \frac {\varepsilon}{m}\right)} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2}
$$

$$
= \frac {1}{2 m} \left(1 - \frac {\varepsilon}{m} + \frac {\varepsilon^ {2}}{m ^ {2}}\right) p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2} = H _ {0} + \varepsilon H _ {1} + \varepsilon^ {2} H _ {2}\tag{1}
$$

$$
H _ {0} = \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2}, \quad H _ {1} = - \frac {1}{2 m ^ {2}} p ^ {2}, \quad H _ {2} = \frac {1}{2 m ^ {3}} p ^ {2}
$$

方法一:用作用量、角变量求精确解.

$$
\frac {1}{2 (m + \varepsilon)} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2} = E
$$

$$
\frac {p ^ {2}}{2 (m + \varepsilon) E} + \frac {q ^ {2}}{2 E / k} = 1
$$

$$
J = \oint p d q = \pi \sqrt {2 (m + \varepsilon) E} \cdot \sqrt {\frac {2 E}{k}} = 2 \pi E \sqrt {\frac {m + \varepsilon}{k}}
$$

$$
H ^ {*} = E = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m + \varepsilon}} J
$$

$$
\nu = \dot {w} = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m + \varepsilon}}
$$

$$
\dot {J} = - \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial w} = 0
$$

$$
w = \nu t + \beta
$$

$$
\nu = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m + \varepsilon}}
$$

展开到 $\varepsilon$ 的二次方项

$$
\nu = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} \Big (1 - \frac {\varepsilon}{2 m} + \frac {3 \varepsilon^ {2}}{8 m ^ {2}} \Big)
$$

零级近似

$$
\nu^ {0} = \nu | _ {\varepsilon = 0} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \nu - \nu^ {0} & = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} \left(- \frac {\varepsilon}{2 m} + \frac {3 \varepsilon^ {2}}{8 m ^ {2}}\right) \\ & = - \frac {1}{4 \pi} \varepsilon k ^ {1 / 2} m ^ {- 3 / 2} + \frac {3}{1 6 \pi} \varepsilon^ {2} k ^ {1 / 2} m ^ {- 5 / 2} \end{array}
$$

方法二:用正则微扰论解.

先求 $\varepsilon = 0$ 的情况，

$$
H (q, p, 0) = H _ {0} (q, p)
$$

特征函数 $W^0$ 满足的方程为

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W ^ {0}}{\mathrm{d} q}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2} = E
$$

$$
W ^ {0} = \int \sqrt {2 m \left(E - \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2}\right)} \mathrm{d} q
$$

用方法一得到的 E 和 J 的函数关系, 令 $\varepsilon=0$ , 即得这里 E 和 $J^{0}$ 的函数关系,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & J ^ {0} = 2 \pi E \sqrt {\frac {m}{k}} \\ & E = \frac {J ^ {0}}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} \\ w ^ {0} = \frac {\partial W ^ {0}}{\partial J ^ {0}} = \frac {\partial W ^ {0}}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J ^ {0}} \\ & = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} \int \frac {\sqrt {2 E}}{2 \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2}}} d q = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \arcsin \left(\sqrt {\frac {\pi \sqrt {k m}}{J ^ {0}}} q\right) \\ & q = \sqrt {\frac {J ^ {0}}{\pi \sqrt {k m}}} \sin (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ & H _ {0} ^ {*} = E = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} J ^ {0} \\ & \dot {w} ^ {0} = v ^ {0} = \frac {\partial H _ {0} ^ {*}}{\partial J ^ {0}} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} \\ & \sqrt {\frac {J ^ {0}}{\pi \sqrt {k m}}} = \sqrt {\frac {2 J ^ {0}}{k} \cdot \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}}} = \sqrt {\frac {2 J ^ {0} v ^ {0}}{k}} \\ & q = \sqrt {\frac {2 J ^ {0} v ^ {0}}{k}} \sin (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ & p = m \dot {q} = m \sqrt {\frac {2 J ^ {0} v ^ {0}}{k}} 2 \pi \dot {w} ^ {0} \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ & = 2 \pi m v ^ {0} \sqrt {\frac {2 J ^ {0} v ^ {0}}{k}} \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \end{array}
$$

所以

将上述 q、p 代入式(1)，即得 $H^{*}(w^{0}, J^{0}, \varepsilon)$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} H ^ {*} (w ^ {0}, J ^ {0}, \varepsilon) & = \frac {1}{2 m} \left(1 - \frac {\varepsilon}{m} + \frac {\varepsilon^ {2}}{m ^ {2}}\right) 4 \pi^ {2} m ^ {2} (\nu^ {0}) ^ {2} \frac {2 J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}}{k} \cos^ {2} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ & \quad + \frac {1}{2} k \frac {2 J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}}{k} \sin^ {2} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ & = J ^ {0} \nu^ {0} + J ^ {0} \nu^ {0} \left(- \frac {\varepsilon}{m} + \frac {\varepsilon^ {2}}{m ^ {2}}\right) \cos^ {2} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \end{array}
$$

可见

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} H _ {0} ^ {*} (J ^ {0}) = \nu^ {0} J ^ {0} \\ H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0}, J ^ {0}) = - \frac {J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}}{m} \cos^ {2} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) = - \frac {J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}}{2 m} [ 1 - \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) ] \\ H _ {2} ^ {*} (w ^ {0}, J ^ {0}) = \frac {J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}}{2 m ^ {2}} [ 1 - \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) ] \end{array} \right.\tag{2}
$$

由(2)式,可直接写出

$$
H _ {0} ^ {*} (J) = \nu^ {0} J\tag{3}
$$

$$
H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0}, J) = - \frac {J \nu^ {0}}{2 m} [ 1 - \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) ]
$$

$$
H _ {2} ^ {*} (w ^ {0}, J) = \frac {J \nu^ {0}}{2 m ^ {2}} [ 1 - \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) ]
$$

$$
H _ {1} ^ {*} (J) = \overline {{{{H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0} , J)}}}} = \int_ {0} ^ {1} \left(- \frac {J \nu^ {0}}{2 m}\right) [ 1 - \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) ] d w ^ {0} = - \frac {1}{2 m} \nu^ {0} J\tag{4}
$$

$$
\overline {{{H _ {2} ^ {*}}}} = \overline {{{H _ {2} ^ {*} (w ^ {0} , J)}}} = \int_ {0} ^ {1} H _ {2} ^ {*} (w ^ {0}, J) \mathrm{d} w ^ {0} = \frac {J \nu^ {0}}{2 m ^ {2}}
$$

$$
\frac {\overline {{\partial H _ {1} ^ {*}}}}{\partial J} = \overline {{\frac {\partial H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0} , J)}{\partial J}}} = \int_ {0} ^ {1} \left(- \frac {1}{2 m} \nu^ {0}\right) [ 1 - \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) ] d w ^ {0} = - \frac {\nu^ {0}}{2 m}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\overline {{\left(\frac {\partial H _ {1} ^ {*}}{\partial J} H _ {1} ^ {*}\right)}} = \overline {{\left[ \frac {\partial H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0} , J)}{\partial J} H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0} , J) \right]}}} \\ & {\qquad = \int_ {0} ^ {1} \left(- \frac {\nu^ {0}}{2 m}\right) [ 1 - \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) ] \left(- \frac {J \nu^ {0}}{2 m}\right) [ 1 - \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) ] d w ^ {0}} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {3 J (\nu^ {0}) ^ {2}}{8 m ^ {2}}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \nu^ {0}}{\partial J} = 0
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} H _ {2} ^ {*} (J) & = \overline {{H _ {2} ^ {*}}} + \frac {1}{\nu^ {0} (J)} \left[ \overline {{\frac {\partial H _ {1} ^ {*}}{\partial J}}} \cdot \overline {{H _ {1} ^ {*}}} - \overline {{\left(\frac {\partial H _ {1} ^ {*}}{\partial J} H _ {1} ^ {*}\right)}} \right] \\ & \quad + \frac {1}{2 \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}} \frac {\partial \nu^ {0}}{\partial J} \left[ \overline {{(H _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {2}}} - (\overline {{H _ {1} ^ {*}}}) ^ {2} \right] \\ & = \frac {J \nu^ {0}}{2 m ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{\nu^ {0}} \left[ \left(- \frac {\nu^ {0}}{2 m}\right) \left(- \frac {1}{2 m} \nu^ {0} J\right) - \frac {3 J \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}}{8 m ^ {2}} \right] \\ & = \frac {3 J (\nu^ {0}) ^ {2}}{8 m ^ {2}} \end{array}\tag{5}
$$

由(1)、(3)、(4)、(5)式，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} H ^ {*} (J) & = H _ {0} ^ {*} (J) + \varepsilon H _ {1} ^ {*} (J) + \varepsilon^ {2} H _ {2} ^ {*} (J) \\ & = \nu^ {0} J - \frac {\varepsilon}{2 m} \nu^ {0} J + \varepsilon^ {2} \frac {3 \nu^ {0} J}{8 m ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

由哈密顿正则方程，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \nu & = \dot {w} = \frac {\partial H ^ {*} (J , \varepsilon)}{\partial J} = \nu^ {0} - \frac {\varepsilon}{2 m} \nu^ {0} + \frac {3 \varepsilon^ {2} \nu^ {0}}{8 m ^ {2}} \\ \nu - \nu^ {0} & = - \frac {\varepsilon}{2 m} \nu^ {0} + \frac {3 \varepsilon^ {2}}{8 m ^ {2}} \nu^ {0} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} \left(- \frac {\varepsilon}{2 m} + \frac {3 \varepsilon^ {2}}{8 m ^ {2}}\right) \end{array}
$$

在同级近似下,两种方法所得结果完全相同.

11.6.6 用正则微扰论求有限振幅振动的单摆运动, 设总能量 $E \ll mgl$ , 求振子较之小振幅振动的频率增量, 只考虑一级近似.

提示：

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m l ^ {2}} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} - \frac {1}{2 4} m g l \theta^ {4}
$$

前两项为 $H_{0}$ ，最后一项为 $H_{1}$ .

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \\ & V = m g l (1 - \cos \theta) = m g l \left[ 1 - \left(1 - \frac {1}{2} \theta^ {2} + \frac {1}{4 !} \theta^ {4}\right) \right] \\ & = - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} - \frac {1}{2 4} m g l \theta^ {4} \\ & H = \frac {1}{2 m l ^ {2}} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} - \frac {\lambda}{2 4} m g l \theta^ {4} \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

(以后令 $\lambda=1$ )

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & H _ {0} = \frac {1}{2 m l ^ {2}} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} \\ & H _ {1} = - \frac {1}{2 4} m g l \theta^ {4} \\ & \frac {1}{2 m l ^ {2}} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} = E \\ & \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m l ^ {2} E} + \frac {\theta^ {2}}{(2 E / m g l)} = 1 \\ & J ^ {0} = \oint p d \theta = \pi \sqrt {2 m l ^ {2} E} \cdot \sqrt {\frac {2 E}{m g l}} = 2 \pi E \sqrt {\frac {l}{g}} \\ & H _ {0} ^ {*} = E = \frac {J ^ {0}}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\nu^ {0} = \dot {w} ^ {0} = \frac {\partial H _ {0} ^ {*}}{\partial J ^ {0}} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W ^ {0}}{\mathrm{d} \theta}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} = E
$$

$$
W ^ {0} = \int \sqrt {2 m l ^ {2}} \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}} \mathrm{d} \theta
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} w ^ {0} & = \frac {\partial W ^ {0}}{\partial J ^ {0}} = \frac {\partial W ^ {0}}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J ^ {0}} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} \int \sqrt {2 m l ^ {2}} \cdot \frac {\mathrm{d} \theta}{2 \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \arcsin \left(\sqrt {\frac {m g l}{2 E}} \theta\right) = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \arcsin \left(\sqrt {\frac {\pi m l \sqrt {g l}}{J ^ {0}}} \theta\right) \end{array}
$$

得最后一个等式时用了 $E = \frac{J^0}{2\pi}\sqrt{\frac{g}{l}}$ 

$$
\theta = \sqrt {\frac {J ^ {0}}{\pi m l \sqrt {g l}}} \sin (2 \pi w ^ {0})
$$

$$
\dot {\theta} = \sqrt {\frac {J ^ {0}}{\pi m l \sqrt {g l}}} 2 \pi \nu^ {0} \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) = \sqrt {\frac {J ^ {0}}{\pi m l ^ {2}} \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}}} \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0})
$$

或

$$
\begin{array}{c} \dot {\theta} = \sqrt {\frac {2 J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}}{m l ^ {2}}} \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ p = m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} = \sqrt {2 m l ^ {2} J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}} \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \end{array}
$$

将 $\theta, p$ 代入式(1)，得

$$
H ^ {*} \left(w ^ {0}, J ^ {0}, \lambda\right) = J ^ {0} \nu^ {0} - \frac {\lambda J ^ {0 ^ {2}} \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}}{6 m g l} \sin^ {4} \left(2 \pi w ^ {0}\right)
$$

在上述计算中用了 $\nu^0 = \frac{1}{2\pi}\sqrt{\frac{q}{l}}$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & H _ {0} ^ {*} (J ^ {0}) = J ^ {0} \nu^ {0} \\ & H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0}, J ^ {0}) = - \frac {J ^ {0 ^ {2}} \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}}{6 m g l} \sin^ {4} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ & = - \frac {J ^ {0 ^ {2}} \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}}{6 m g l} \left[ \frac {1 - \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0})}{2} \right] ^ {2} \\ & = - \frac {J ^ {0 ^ {2}} \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}}{2 4 m g l} \left[ \frac {3}{2} - 2 \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) + \frac {1}{2} \cos (8 \pi w ^ {0}) \right] \end{array}
$$

$$
H _ {1} ^ {*} (J) = \overline {{{{H _ {1} ^ {*} (\omega^ {0} , J)}}}} = \overline {{{{H _ {1} ^ {*} (\omega^ {0} , J ^ {0}) |}}}} _ {J ^ {2} \rightarrow J} = - \frac {1}{1 6 m g l} J ^ {2} \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}
$$

$$
H ^ {*} (J, \lambda) = H _ {0} ^ {*} (J) + \lambda H _ {1} ^ {*} (J) = J \nu^ {0} - \frac {\lambda}{1 6 m g l} J ^ {2} \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}
$$

$$
\nu = \left. \frac {\partial H ^ {*} (J , \lambda)}{\partial J} \right| _ {1 = 1} = \nu^ {0} - \frac {J \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}}{8 m g l}
$$

$$
\nu - \nu^ {0} = - \frac {J \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}}{8 m g l}
$$

其中 $\nu^0 = \frac{1}{2\pi}\sqrt{\frac{g}{l}}.$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} S _ {1} (w ^ {0}, J) & = \int \frac {\overline {{H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0} , J)}} - H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0} , J)}{\nu^ {0} (J)} \mathrm{d} w ^ {0} \\ & = \int \left\{- \frac {J ^ {2} \nu^ {0}}{1 6 m g l} + \frac {J ^ {2} \nu^ {0}}{2 4 m g l} \left[ \frac {3}{2} - 2 \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) + \frac {1}{2} \cos (8 \pi w ^ {0}) \right] \right\} \mathrm{d} w ^ {0} \\ & = \frac {J ^ {2} \nu^ {0}}{2 4 m g l} \left[ - \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sin (4 \pi w ^ {0}) + \frac {1}{1 6 \pi} \sin (8 \pi w ^ {0}) \right] \\ & S _ {0} (w ^ {0}, J) = w ^ {0} J \\ S (w ^ {0}, J, \lambda) & = S _ {0} (w ^ {0}, J) + \lambda S _ {1} (w ^ {0}, J) \\ & = w ^ {0} J + \lambda \frac {J ^ {2} \nu^ {0}}{2 4 m g l} \left[ - \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sin (4 \pi w ^ {0}) + \frac {1}{1 6 \pi} \sin (8 \pi w ^ {0}) \right] \end{array}
$$

由正则变换条件

$$
\begin{array}{l} p _ {i} = \frac {\partial S (\boldsymbol {q} , \boldsymbol {P} , t)}{\partial q _ {i}}, \quad Q _ {i} = \frac {\partial S (\boldsymbol {q} , \boldsymbol {P} , t)}{\partial P _ {i}} \\ J ^ {0} = \left. \frac {\partial S}{\partial w ^ {0}} \right| _ {\lambda = 1} = J - \frac {J ^ {2} \nu^ {0}}{1 2 m g l} \left[ \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) - \frac {1}{4} \cos (8 \pi w ^ {0}) \right] \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & w = \frac {\partial S}{\partial J} \Big | _ {\lambda = 1} = w ^ {0} + \frac {J \nu^ {0}}{1 2 m g l} \left[ - \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sin (4 \pi w ^ {0}) + \frac {1}{1 6 \pi} \sin (8 \pi w ^ {0}) \right] \\ & = w ^ {0} - \frac {J \nu^ {0}}{1 9 2 \pi m g l} \left[ 8 \sin (4 \pi w ^ {0}) - \sin (8 \pi w ^ {0}) \right] \end{array}
$$

由 $H^{*}(J,\lambda)$ 用哈密顿正则方程，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \dot {J} = - \left. \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial w} \right| _ {\lambda = 1} = 0, J = C _ {1} \\ \dot {w} = \nu = \left. \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J} \right| _ {\lambda = 1} = \nu^ {0} - \frac {J \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}}{8 m g l} \\ w = v t + C _ {2} \end{array}
$$

$\theta, p$ 与 $w^0, J^0$ 的变换关系前已求得，

或

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \theta & = \sqrt {\frac {J ^ {0}}{\pi m l \sqrt {g l}}} \sin (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ & \theta = \sqrt {\frac {2 J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}}{m g l}} \sin (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ & p = \sqrt {2 m l ^ {2} J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}} \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ & p = 2 \pi m l ^ {2} \nu^ {0} \sqrt {\frac {2 J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}}{m g l}} \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \end{array}
$$

或

11.6.7 如图 11.12 所示, 一质量为 $m$ 的质点被约束在一直线上运动, 并被系于劲度系数均为 $k$ 、原长为 $b$ 的两弹簧的端点, 另一端固定. 质点运动轨道离弹簧的固定端点的最短距离为 $a, a > b$ . 由正则微扰论, 考虑一级微扰, 求有限振幅的振荡频率的最低级修正.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/f6316713c7c8a378f9cc8904b0ac4c82447f5a5b89bfdfb0271e4895af663ec9.jpg)



图11.12


解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2} \\ & V = 2 \times \frac {1}{2} k (\sqrt {a ^ {2} + x ^ {2}} - b) ^ {2} - 2 \times \frac {1}{2} k (a - b) ^ {2} \\ & = k x ^ {2} + 2 k a b \left(1 - \sqrt {1 + \frac {x ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}}\right) \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & = k x ^ {2} + 2 k a b \left[ 1 - \left(1 + \frac {x ^ {2}}{2 a ^ {2}} - \frac {x ^ {4}}{8 a ^ {4}}\right) \right] \\ & = k \left(1 - \frac {b}{a}\right) x ^ {2} + \frac {k b}{4 a ^ {3}} x ^ {4} \\ & H = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} + k \left(1 - \frac {b}{a}\right) x ^ {2} + \lambda \frac {k b}{4 a ^ {3}} x ^ {4} \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

以后令 $\lambda = 1$ 

$$
H _ {0} = \frac {\dot {p} ^ {2}}{2 m} + k \left(1 - \frac {b}{a}\right) x ^ {2}
$$

$$
H _ {1} = \frac {k b}{4 a ^ {3}} x ^ {4}
$$

$$
\frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} + k \left(1 - \frac {b}{a}\right) x ^ {2} = E
$$

$$
\frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m E} + \frac {x ^ {2}}{\frac {E}{k \left(1 - \frac {b}{a}\right)}} = 1
$$

$$
J ^ {0} = \oint p \mathrm{d} x = \pi \sqrt {2 m E} \cdot \sqrt {\frac {E}{k \left(1 - \frac {b}{a}\right)}} = \pi E \sqrt {\frac {2 m a}{k (a - b)}}
$$

$$
H _ {0} ^ {*} = E = \frac {J ^ {0}}{\pi} \sqrt {\frac {k (a - b)}{2 m a}}
$$

特征函数 $W^0$ 满足的方程为

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W ^ {0}}{\mathrm{d} x}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {k (a - b)}{a} x ^ {2} = E
$$

$$
W ^ {0} = \int \sqrt {2 m \left[ E - \frac {k (a - b)}{a} x ^ {2} \right]} \mathrm{d} x
$$

$$
\nu^ {0} = \dot {w} ^ {0} = \frac {\partial H _ {0} ^ {*}}{\partial J ^ {0}} = \frac {1}{\pi} \sqrt {\frac {k (a - b)}{2 m a}}\tag{2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} w ^ {0} & = \frac {\partial W ^ {0}}{\partial J ^ {0}} = \frac {\partial W ^ {0}}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J ^ {0}} \\ & = \frac {1}{\pi} \sqrt {\frac {k (a - b)}{2 m a}} \cdot \sqrt {2 m} \int \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{2 \sqrt {E - \frac {k (a - b)}{a} x ^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \arcsin \left(\sqrt {\frac {k (a - b)}{a J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}}} x\right) \end{array}
$$

得最后一步用了

$$
E = \frac {J ^ {0}}{\pi} \sqrt {\frac {k (a - b)}{2 m a}} = J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & x = \sqrt {\frac {J ^ {0} \nu^ {0} a}{k (a - b)}} \sin (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ & p = m \dot {x} = \sqrt {2 m J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}} \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \end{array}
$$

将 $x, p$ 代入式(1)，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} H ^ {*} (w ^ {0}, J ^ {0}, \lambda) & = J ^ {0} \nu^ {0} + \lambda \frac {k b}{4 a} \left[ \frac {J ^ {0} \nu^ {0} a}{k (a - b)} \right] ^ {2} \sin^ {4} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ H _ {0} ^ {*} (J ^ {0}) & = J ^ {0} \nu^ {0} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0}, J ^ {0}) & = \frac {k b}{4 a (a - b) ^ {2}} \left(\frac {J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}}{k}\right) ^ {2} \sin^ {4} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ & = \frac {k b}{4 a (a - b) ^ {2}} \left(\frac {J ^ {0} \nu^ {0}}{k}\right) ^ {2} \cdot \frac {1}{4} \left[ \frac {3}{2} - 2 \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) + \frac {1}{2} \cos (8 \pi w ^ {0}) \right] \end{array}
$$

$$
H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0}, J) = \frac {k b}{1 6 a (a - b) ^ {2}} \left(\frac {J \nu^ {0}}{k}\right) ^ {2} \left[ \frac {3}{2} - 2 \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) + \frac {1}{2} \cos (8 \pi w ^ {0}) \right]\tag{3}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {H _ {1} ^ {*} (J) = \overline {{{H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0} , J)}}} = \int_ {0} ^ {1} H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0}, J) \mathrm{d} w ^ {0} = \frac {3 k b}{3 2 a (a - b) ^ {2}} \Big (\frac {J \nu^ {0}}{k} \Big) ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad H ^ {*} (J) = H _ {0} ^ {*} (J) + H _ {1} ^ {*} (J) = J \nu^ {0} + \frac {3 k b}{3 2 a (a - b) ^ {2}} \Big (\frac {J \nu^ {0}}{k} \Big) ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad \nu = \frac {\partial H ^ {*} (J)}{\partial J} = \nu^ {0} + \frac {3 k b}{1 6 a (a - b) ^ {2}} \frac {J \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}}{k ^ {2}}} \\ & {\qquad \nu - \nu^ {0} = \frac {3 b J \nu^ {0 ^ {2}}}{1 6 a (a - b) ^ {2} k} = \frac {3 b J}{3 2 \pi^ {2} m a ^ {2} (a - b)}} \\ & {\qquad j = - \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial w} = 0} \\ & {\qquad J = C _ {1} \quad (\text {常量})} \end{array}\tag{4}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\dot {w} = \nu = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J}} \\ & {w = \nu t + C _ {2}, C _ {2} \text {为常量}} \end{array}
$$

为求 $J^0, w^0$ 与 $J, \omega$ 间的变换关系，需求出母函数 $S(w^0, J)$ ，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} S _ {0} (w ^ {0}, J) & = w ^ {0} J \\ S _ {1} (w ^ {0}, J) & = \int \frac {H _ {1} ^ {*} (J) - H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0} , J)}{\nu^ {0} (J)} \mathrm{d} w ^ {0} \end{array}
$$

代入式(2)、(3)、(4)，经计算可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} S _ {1} (w ^ {0}, J) & = \frac {b J ^ {2} \nu^ {0}}{2 5 6 \pi k a (a - b) ^ {2}} [ 8 \sin (4 \pi w ^ {0}) - \sin (8 \pi w ^ {0}) ] \\ S (w ^ {0}, J = S _ {0} (w ^ {0}, J) + S _ {1} (w ^ {0}, J) = w ^ {0} J + \frac {b J ^ {2} \nu^ {0}}{2 5 6 \pi k a (a - b) ^ {2}} [ 8 \sin (4 \pi w ^ {0}) - \sin (8 \pi w ^ {0}) ] \\ J ^ {0} & = \frac {\partial S}{\partial w ^ {0}} = J + \frac {b J ^ {2} \nu^ {0}}{3 2 k a (a - b) ^ {2}} [ 4 \cos (4 \pi w ^ {0}) - \cos (8 \pi w ^ {0}) ] \\ w & = \frac {\partial S}{\partial J} = w ^ {0} + \frac {b J \nu^ {0}}{1 2 8 \pi k a (a - b) ^ {2}} [ 8 \sin (4 \pi w ^ {0}) - \sin (8 \pi w ^ {0}) ] \end{array}
$$

x、p 与 $w^{0}$ 、 $J^{0}$ 的变换关系前面已经得到，不再重述.

11.6.8 一个非线性谐振子的势能为

$$
V (x) = \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} - \frac {1}{3} m \lambda x ^ {3}
$$

其中 $\lambda$ 是个小量, 求受扰的作用、角变量与未受扰的线性谐振子的作用、角变量间的变换关系.

解

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} - \frac {1}{3} m \lambda x ^ {3}\tag{1}
$$

$$
H _ {0} = \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2}
$$

$$
H _ {1} = - \frac {1}{3} m \lambda x ^ {3}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} = E
$$

$$
\frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m E} + \frac {x ^ {2}}{\frac {2 E}{k}} = 1
$$

$$
J ^ {0} = \oint p \mathrm{d} q = \pi \sqrt {2 m E} \cdot \sqrt {\frac {2 E}{k}} = 2 \pi E \sqrt {\frac {m}{k}}
$$

$$
H _ {0} ^ {*} (J ^ {0}) = E = \frac {J ^ {0}}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}}
$$

$$
\nu^ {0} = \dot {w} ^ {0} = \frac {\partial H _ {0} ^ {*}}{\partial J ^ {0}} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}}
$$

$$
H _ {0} ^ {*} (J ^ {0}) = \nu^ {0} J ^ {0}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W ^ {0}}{\mathrm{d} x}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} = E
$$

$$
W ^ {0} = \int \sqrt {2 m} \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} x
$$

$$
w ^ {0} = \frac {\partial W ^ {0}}{\partial J ^ {0}} = \frac {\partial W ^ {0}}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J ^ {0}} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} \sqrt {2 m} \int \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{2 \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2}}} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \arcsin \left(\sqrt {\frac {k}{2 E}} x\right)
$$

$$
x = \sqrt {\frac {2 E}{k}} \sin (2 \pi w ^ {0}) = \sqrt {\frac {2 \nu^ {0} J ^ {0}}{k}} \sin (2 \pi w ^ {0})
$$

$$
p = m \dot {x} = m \sqrt {\frac {2 \nu^ {0} J ^ {0}}{k}} \cdot 2 \pi \nu^ {0} \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) = \sqrt {2 m \nu^ {0} J ^ {0}} \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0})
$$

将 $x, p$ 代入(1)式，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} H ^ {*} (w ^ {0}, J ^ {0}, \lambda) & = J ^ {0} \nu^ {0} - \frac {1}{3} m \lambda \left(\frac {2 \nu^ {0} J ^ {0}}{k}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \sin^ {3} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ H _ {0} ^ {*} (J ^ {0}) & = J ^ {0} \nu^ {0} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} H _ {1} ^ {0} (w ^ {0}, J ^ {0}) & = - \frac {1}{3} m \lambda \left(\frac {2 \nu^ {0} J ^ {0}}{k}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \sin^ {3} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \\ H _ {1} ^ {*} (J) & = \overline {{H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0} , J)}} = \overline {{H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0} , J ^ {0}) | _ {J ^ {0} \to J}}} \\ & = - \frac {1}{3} m \lambda \left(\frac {2 \nu^ {0} J}{k}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \int_ {0} ^ {1} \sin^ {3} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) d w ^ {0} = 0 \\ H _ {0} ^ {*} (J) & = \nu^ {0} J \\ H ^ {*} (J, \lambda) & = H _ {0} ^ {*} (J) + H _ {1} ^ {*} (J) = \nu^ {0} J \\ \dot {w} & = \nu = \frac {\partial H ^ {*} (J , \lambda)}{\partial J} = \nu^ {0} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} S _ {1} (w ^ {0}, J) & = \int \frac {H _ {1} ^ {*} (J) - H _ {1} ^ {*} (w ^ {0} , J)}{\nu^ {0} (J)} \mathrm{d} w ^ {0} \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{3} m \nu^ {0 1 / 2} \left(\frac {2 J}{k}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \int \sin^ {3} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \mathrm{d} w ^ {0} \\ & = - \frac {\lambda}{1 8 \pi} m \nu^ {0 1 / 2} \left(\frac {2 J}{k}\right) ^ {3 / 2} [ 3 \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) - \cos^ {3} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) ] \\ & S _ {0} (w ^ {0}, J) = w ^ {0} J \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} S (w ^ {0}, J) & = S _ {0} (w ^ {0}, J) + S _ {1} (w ^ {0}, J) \\ & = w ^ {0} J - \frac {\lambda}{1 8 \pi} m \nu^ {0 1 / 2} \left(\frac {2 J}{k}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \left[ 3 \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) - \cos^ {3} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \right] \\ & w = \frac {\partial S}{\partial J} = w ^ {0} - \frac {\lambda}{1 8 \pi} m \nu^ {0 1 / 2} \cdot \frac {3}{2} \left(\frac {2 J}{k}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \cdot \frac {2}{k} \left[ 3 \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) - \cos^ {3} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \right] \\ & = w ^ {0} - \frac {\lambda m}{6 \pi k} \left(\frac {2 J \nu^ {0}}{k}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \left[ 3 \cos (2 \pi w ^ {0}) - \cos^ {3} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \right] \\ & J ^ {0} = \frac {\partial S}{\partial w ^ {0}} = J + \frac {\lambda}{3} m \nu^ {0 1 / 2} \left(\frac {2 J}{k}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \sin^ {3} (2 \pi w ^ {0}) \end{array}
$$

其中 $\nu^0 = \frac{1}{2\pi}\sqrt{\frac{k}{m}}.$ 

11.6.9 如图 11.13 所示, 一个质量为 $m$ 、绳长为 $l$ 的平面摆被约束在一斜面上做小振动, 当平面的倾角 $\alpha$ 缓慢变化时, 它的振幅和能量将如何变化? 如 $\alpha = \alpha_{1}$ 时, 振幅为 $A_{1}$ , 缓慢变到 $\alpha = \alpha_{2}$ 时, 振动的振幅 $A_{2}$ 多大? 在此过程中, 对系统做了多少功?

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/820c9e4a83a9a8a1cbd54bba468a73fe2bfc6be5c93f8fa9410108f59df30ea0.jpg)



图11.13


解

$$
\begin{array}{c} {{T = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2}}} \\ {{V = m g l (1 - \cos \theta) \sin \alpha \approx \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} \sin \alpha}} \end{array}
$$

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m l ^ {2}} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} \sin \alpha = E
$$

$$
\frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m l ^ {2} E} + \frac {\theta^ {2}}{\frac {2 E}{m g l \sin \alpha}} = 1
$$

$$
J = \oint p d \theta = \pi \sqrt {2 m l ^ {2} E} \cdot \sqrt {\frac {2 E}{m g l \sin \alpha}} = 2 \pi E \sqrt {\frac {l}{g \sin \alpha}}
$$

$$
E = \frac {J}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {g \sin \alpha}{l}}
$$

$$
H ^ {*} = E = \frac {J}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {g \sin \alpha}{l}}
$$

$$
\nu = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {g \sin \alpha}{l}}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m l ^ {2}} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} \theta}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} \sin \alpha = E
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} \theta} = \sqrt {2 m l ^ {2}} \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} \sin \alpha}
$$

$$
W = \sqrt {2 m l ^ {2}} \int \sqrt {E} - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} \mathrm{sin} \alpha \mathrm{d} \theta
$$

$$
w = \frac {\partial W}{\partial J} = \frac {\partial W}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \arcsin \left(\sqrt {\frac {m g l \sin \alpha}{2 E}} \theta\right)
$$

$$
\theta = \sqrt {\frac {2 E}{m g l \sin \alpha}} \sin (2 \pi w)
$$

$$
A = \sqrt {\frac {2 E}{m g l \sin \alpha}} = \sqrt {\frac {2}{m g l \sin \alpha} \frac {J}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {g \sin \alpha}{l}}}
$$

因为 $J$ 是不变量，

$$
A \propto (\sin \alpha) ^ {- 1 / 4}, \quad \frac {A _ {2}}{A _ {1}} = \left(\frac {\sin \alpha_ {1}}{\sin \alpha_ {2}}\right) ^ {1 / 4}
$$

所以

$$
A _ {2} = \left(\frac {\sin \alpha_ {1}}{\sin \alpha_ {2}}\right) ^ {1 / 4} A _ {1}
$$

前已求得

$$
E = \frac {J}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {g \sin \alpha}{l}}
$$

在倾角 $\alpha$ 从 $\alpha_{1}$ 缓慢变到 $\alpha_{2}$ 的过程中，需对系统做的功为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} W & = E _ {2} - E _ {1} = \frac {J}{2 \pi} \left(\sqrt {\frac {g \sin \alpha_ {2}}{l}} - \sqrt {\frac {g \sin \alpha_ {1}}{l}}\right) \\ & = E _ {1} \sqrt {\frac {l}{g \sin \alpha_ {1}}} \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} (\sqrt {\sin \alpha_ {2}} - \sqrt {\sin \alpha_ {1}}) \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & = \frac {E _ {1}}{\sqrt {\sin \alpha_ {1}}} (\sqrt {\sin \alpha_ {2}} - \sqrt {\sin \alpha_ {1}}) \\ & A _ {1} ^ {2} = \frac {2 E _ {1}}{m g l \sin \alpha_ {1}}, \quad E _ {1} = \frac {1}{2} m g l A _ {1} ^ {2} \sin \alpha_ {1} \\ & W = \frac {1}{2} m g l A _ {1} ^ {2} \sqrt {\sin \alpha_ {1}} (\sqrt {\sin \alpha_ {2}} - \sqrt {\sin \alpha_ {1}}) \end{array}
$$

11.6.10 考虑 11.6.2 题所述系统, 假定参量 F 从初始值缓慢变化, 质点的能量、振动周期和振动振幅将如何变化?

解 11.6.2 题所述系统是一质量为 $m$ 的质点，在势能 $V(x) = F|x|(F$ 是正值常量）的力作用下做一维运动.

方法一:用 11.6.2 题已得的一些式子,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} J & = \frac {8 \sqrt {2 m}}{3 F} E ^ {3 / 2} \\ H ^ {*} (J) & = E = \left(\frac {3 F J}{8 \sqrt {2 m}}\right) ^ {2 / 3} \\ \nu & = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J} = \left(\frac {3 F}{8 \sqrt {2 m}}\right) ^ {2 / 3} \cdot \frac {2}{3} J ^ {- 1 / 3} \\ T & = \frac {1}{\nu} = \left(\frac {3 F}{8 \sqrt {2 m}}\right) ^ {- 2 / 3} \cdot \frac {3}{2} J ^ {1 / 3} \end{array}
$$

周期

$J$ 是绝热不变量，从 $E(F)$ 的函数关系可见， $E \propto F^{2/3}$ 

从 $T(F)$ 的函数关系可见，

$$
T \propto F ^ {- 2 / 3}
$$

特征函数 W 满足的方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\partial W}{\partial x}\right) ^ {2} + F | x | = E} \\ & {W = \int \sqrt {2 m (E - F | x |)} \mathrm{d} x} \\ & {w = \frac {\partial W}{\partial J} = \frac {\partial W}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J} = \nu \frac {\partial W}{\partial E}} \\ & {= \nu \int \frac {\sqrt {2 m} \mathrm{d} x}{2 \sqrt {E - F | x |}}} \\ & {= \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} {\sqrt {2 m}   \frac {\nu}{F} (\sqrt {E} - \sqrt {E - F x})} & {x \geqslant 0} \\ {\sqrt {2 m}   \frac {\nu}{F} (\sqrt {E + F x} - \sqrt {E})} & {x \leqslant 0} \end{array} \right.} \end{array}
$$

注意:积分常数可以任取,这里取 $w(0)=0$ ,

$$
\dot {w} = \nu = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J}
$$

$$
w = \nu t + C
$$

从 $x \geqslant 0$ 的 $w(x) = \nu t + C$ 解出 $x$ , 得

$$
x = \frac {1}{F} \left[ E - \frac {F ^ {2}}{2 m \nu^ {2}} \left(\nu t + C - \frac {\nu}{F} \sqrt {2 m E}\right) ^ {2} \right]
$$

从 $x \leqslant 0$ 的 $w(x) = \nu t + C$ 解出 $x$ , 得

$$
x = \frac {1}{F} \left[ - E + \frac {F ^ {2}}{2 m \nu^ {2}} \left(\nu t + C + \frac {\nu}{F} \sqrt {2 m E}\right) ^ {2} \right]
$$

$$
x _ {\max} = \frac {E}{F}, \quad x _ {\min} = - \frac {E}{F}
$$

振幅

$$
A = \frac {1}{2} (x _ {\max} - x _ {\min}) = \frac {E}{F}
$$

因为

$$
E \propto F ^ {2 / 3}
$$

所以

$$
A \propto F ^ {2 / 3 - 1} \quad \text {即} A \propto F ^ {- 1 / 3}
$$

方法二:用矢量力学的方法讨论 $A \propto F^{-1/3}$ 的问题:

$$
V (x) = F | x | = \left\{ \begin{array}{c c} F x & x \geqslant 0 \\ - F x & x \leqslant 0 \end{array} \right.
$$

$$
f (x) = - \frac {\mathrm{d} V}{\mathrm{d} x} = \left\{ \begin{array}{c c} {- F} & {x > 0} \\ {F} & {x <   0} \end{array} \right.
$$

$$
m \ddot {x} = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} - F & x > 0 \\ F & x <   0 \end{array} \right.
$$

设质点运动的初始条件为

$t = 0$ 时， $x = x_0 > 0$ ， $\dot{x} = v_{0} > 0$ 

在 $x \geqslant 0$ 的区域内，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\dot {x} = v _ {0} - \frac {F}{m} t} \\ & {x = x _ {0} + v _ {0} t - \frac {F}{2 m} t ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad = x _ {0} + \frac {m v _ {0} ^ {2}}{2 F} - \left(\frac {m v _ {0} ^ {2}}{2 F} - v _ {0} t + \frac {F}{2 m} t ^ {2}\right)} \\ & {\quad = x _ {0} + \frac {m v _ {0} ^ {2}}{2 F} - \left(\frac {F}{2 m} t - \sqrt {\frac {m}{2 F}} v _ {0}\right) ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad x _ {\max} = x _ {0} + \frac {m v _ {0} ^ {2}}{2 F}} \end{array}
$$

为求 $x_{\min}$ , 先求出 $x = 0$ 时的 $\dot{x}, x = 0$ 时的 $t_1$ 满足

$$
x _ {0} + v _ {0} t _ {1} - \frac {F}{2 m} t _ {1} ^ {2} = 0
$$

$$
t _ {1} = \frac {m}{F} \left(v _ {0} + \sqrt {v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 F x _ {0}}{m}}\right)
$$

因为 $t_1 > 0$ ，另一个 $t_1 < 0$ 的根已舍去.

$$
\dot {x} = v _ {0} - \frac {F}{m} t _ {1} = - \sqrt {v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 F x _ {0}}{m}}
$$

$$
m \ddot {x} = F (x <   0)
$$

令 $t' = t - t_1$ ，则仍有

$$
m \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} x}{\mathrm{d} t ^ {\prime 2}} = F (x <   0)
$$

初始条件：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \text { ：} \quad t ^ {\prime} = 0, x = 0, \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\mathrm{d} t ^ {\prime}} = - \sqrt {v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 F x _ {0}}{m}} \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\mathrm{d} t ^ {\prime}} = - \sqrt {v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 F x _ {0}}{m}} + \frac {F}{m} t ^ {\prime} \\ & x = - \sqrt {v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 F x _ {0}}{m}} t ^ {\prime} + \frac {F}{2 m} t ^ {\prime 2} \\ & = - \frac {m}{2 F} \left(v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 F x _ {0}}{m}\right) + \frac {m}{2 F} \left(v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 F x _ {0}}{m}\right) - \sqrt {v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 F x _ {0}}{m}} t ^ {\prime} + \frac {F}{2 m} t ^ {\prime 2} \\ & = - \frac {m}{2 F} \left(v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 F x _ {0}}{m}\right) + \left[ \sqrt {\frac {F}{2 m}} t ^ {\prime} - \sqrt {\frac {m}{2 F} \left(v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 F x _ {0}}{m}\right)} \right] ^ {2} \\ & x _ {\min} = - \frac {m}{2 F} \left(v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 F x _ {0}}{m}\right) = - \left(x _ {0} + \frac {m v _ {0} ^ {2}}{2 F}\right) \\ & E = T + V = (T + V) | _ {t = 0} = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {0} ^ {2} + F x _ {0} \\ & x _ {\max} = \frac {E}{F}, \quad x _ {\min} = - \frac {E}{F} \\ & A = \frac {1}{2} (x _ {\max} - x _ {\min}) = \frac {E}{F} \end{array}
$$

所以

仔细比较两种方法,可以看出结果是相同的.

说明: 此系统并不做简谐振动, 但是周期运动, 最大值与平衡位置间的距离仍等于最小值与平衡位置间的距离, 从画势能 V-x 图可以看出这一点, 且 x=0 为平衡位置, 所以 $A=x_{max}$ .

11.6.11 考虑 11.6.3 题所述系统, 假定参量 k 从初始值开始缓慢变化, 质点的能量、振动周期和振动振幅将如何变化?

解 11.6.3 题所述系统是一个质量为 $m$ 的质点, 在势能 $V = -\frac{k}{|x|}$ ( $k$ 为正值常量) 的力作用下做一维运动, 能量为负值.

解 11.6.3 题已得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & J = \frac {2 \pi \sqrt {2 m k}}{\sqrt {- E}} \\ & - E = \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m k ^ {2}}{J ^ {2}} \\ & H ^ {*} = E = - \frac {8 \pi^ {2} m k ^ {2}}{J ^ {2}} \\ & \nu = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J} = 1 6 \pi^ {2} m k ^ {2} J ^ {- 3} \\ & T = \nu^ {- 1} = \frac {J ^ {3}}{1 6 \pi^ {2} m k ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

周期

因为 $J$ 是绝热不变量，由 $E(k)$ 及 $T(k)$ 的函数关系可知，

$$
- E \propto k ^ {2}, \quad T \propto k ^ {- 2}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} x}\right) ^ {2} - \frac {k}{| x |} = E
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} x} = \sqrt {2 m} \sqrt {E + \frac {k}{| x |}}
$$

$$
W = \int \sqrt {2 m} \sqrt {E + \frac {k}{| x |}} \mathrm{d} x
$$

$$
w = \frac {\partial W}{\partial J} = \frac {\partial W}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J} = \nu \cdot \sqrt {2 m} \int \frac {1}{2 \sqrt {E + \frac {k}{| x |}}} d x
$$

先考虑 $x \geqslant 0$ 部分的运动，

$$
w = \nu \sqrt {2 m} \int \frac {1}{2 \sqrt {E + \frac {k}{x}}} \mathrm{d} x
$$

令 $x = u^2, \mathrm{d}x = 2u\mathrm{d}u$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} w & = \frac {1}{2} \nu \sqrt {2 m} \int \frac {u}{\sqrt {k + E u ^ {2}}} \cdot 2 u d u \\ & = \frac {\nu \sqrt {2 m}}{E} \left[ \int \frac {k + E u ^ {2}}{\sqrt {k + E u ^ {2}}} d u - k \int \frac {d u}{\sqrt {k + E u ^ {2}}} \right] \\ & = \frac {\nu \sqrt {2 m}}{E \sqrt {- E}} \left[ \int \sqrt {k - (\sqrt {- E} u) ^ {2}} d (\sqrt {- E} u) - k \frac {d (\sqrt {- E} u)}{\sqrt {k - (\sqrt {- E} u) ^ {2}}} \right] \end{array}
$$

用积分公式

则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int \sqrt {a ^ {2} - x ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} x = \frac {1}{2} \left[ x \sqrt {a ^ {2} - x ^ {2}} + a ^ {2} \arcsin \left(\frac {x}{a}\right) \right] \\ w = - \frac {\nu \sqrt {2 m}}{2 (- E) ^ {3 / 2}} \left[ \sqrt {- E x} \sqrt {x + E x} - k \arcsin \sqrt {\frac {- E x}{k}} \right] \end{array}
$$

经计算可得

$$
\dot {w} = \frac {\nu \sqrt {2 m}}{2 \sqrt {k + E x}} \sqrt {x} \dot {x}
$$

又有

所以

$$
\dot {w} = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J} = \nu
$$

$$
\nu = \frac {\nu \sqrt {2 m}}{2 \sqrt {k + E x}} \sqrt {x} \dot {x}
$$

$$
\dot {x} = \frac {2 \sqrt {k + E x}}{\sqrt {2 m x}}
$$

$x = x_{\max}$ 时， $\dot{x} = 0,k + Ex_{\max} = 0$ 

$$
x _ {\max} = \frac {k}{- E}
$$

再考虑 x<0 部分的运动.

$$
w = \nu \sqrt {2 m} \int \frac {1}{2 \sqrt {E - \frac {k}{x}}} \mathrm{d} x
$$

令 $x = u^2$ ，和上面的计算相仿，可得

$$
w = \frac {\nu \sqrt {2 m}}{2 (- E) ^ {3 / 2}} \left[ \sqrt {E x} \sqrt {x - E x} - k \arcsin \sqrt {\frac {E x}{k}} \right]
$$

$$
\dot {w} = - \frac {\nu \sqrt {2 m}}{2 \sqrt {k - E x}} \sqrt {- x} \dot {x} = \nu
$$

$$
\dot {x} = - \frac {2 \sqrt {k - E x}}{\sqrt {2 m} \sqrt {- x}}
$$

$x = x_{\min}$ 时， $\dot{x} = 0$ ，由此得到

$$
x _ {\min} = \frac {k}{E}
$$

振幅

$$
A = \frac {1}{2} (x _ {\max} - x _ {\min}) = \frac {k}{- E}
$$

因为

$$
- E \propto k ^ {2}
$$

所以

$$
A \propto k ^ {- 1}
$$

说明:(1)上题所作的说明也适用于本题,因此, $x_{\min}$ 可以不作计算, $A=x_{\max}$ .

(2) $\frac{\mathrm{d}W}{\mathrm{d}x} = \pm \sqrt{2m}\sqrt{E + \frac{k}{|x|}}$ ，不是只能取正号，因此在 $x > 0$ 的区域， $\dot{x} > 0, \dot{x} < 0$ 均是可能的；在 $x < 0$ 的区域， $\dot{x} > 0, \dot{x} < 0$ 也均是可能的。

11.6.12 一个完全弹性的球在两个平行的墙之间做一维运动, 设两墙相距为 l, 问:

(1) 当一个墙缓慢地移动时；

(2) 当球的质量缓慢变化时, 球的能量、振动周期将如何变化?

解

$$
J = \oint p \mathrm{d} x = 2 p l
$$

$$
E = \frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2}, \quad p = \sqrt {2 m E}
$$

$$
J = 2 \sqrt {2 m E} l
$$

$$
H ^ {*} = E = \frac {1}{2 m} \left(\frac {J}{2 l}\right) ^ {2} = \frac {1}{8 m l ^ {2}} J ^ {2}
$$

$$
\nu = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J} = \frac {1}{4 m l ^ {2}} J
$$

$$
T = \nu^ {- 1} = \frac {4 m l ^ {2}}{J}
$$

由 $E = \frac{1}{2ml^2} J^2, T = \frac{4ml^2}{J}$ 可见：

(1) $l$ 缓慢变化时, $E \propto l^{-2}$ , $T \propto l^2$ ;

(2) $m$ 缓慢变化时, $E \propto m^{-1}$ , $T \propto m$ .

11.6.13 在小振动极限下,考虑一个质量为 m 的单摆,假定长为 l 的摆线非常缓慢地被缩短(通过在支承点处一个无摩擦小孔向上拉摆线,如图11.14所示),使l在一个周期内的相对变化很小.问振动幅度怎样随l改变?

解

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/e9808627711328e95cb12bc8cbd2e166bfa7ad3a56392f13687cb1a93e61d9e6.jpg)



图11.14


$$
\begin{array}{r l} & H = \frac {1}{2 m l ^ {2}} p ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} = E \\ & \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m l ^ {2}} + \frac {\theta^ {2}}{\frac {2 E}{m g l}} = 1 \\ J = & \oint p d \theta = \pi \sqrt {2 m l ^ {2} E} \cdot \sqrt {\frac {2 E}{m g l}} = 2 \pi E \sqrt {\frac {l}{g}} \\ & H ^ {*} = E = \frac {J}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} \\ & \nu = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} \\ & \frac {1}{2 m l ^ {2}} \left(\frac {d W}{d \theta}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2} = E \\ & W = \sqrt {2 m l ^ {2}} \int \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}} d \theta \\ & w = \frac {\partial W}{\partial J} = \frac {\partial W}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J} = \nu \cdot \sqrt {2 m l ^ {2}} \int \frac {d \theta}{2 \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} \cdot \sqrt {2 m l ^ {2}} \cdot \frac {1}{2 \sqrt {\frac {1}{2} m g l}} \int \frac {d (\sqrt {\frac {1}{2} m g l} \theta)}{\sqrt {E - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \arcsin (\sqrt {\frac {m g l}{2 E}} \theta) \\ & \theta = \sqrt {\frac {2 E}{m g l}} \sin (2 \pi w) \\ & A = \sqrt {\frac {2 E}{m g l}} = \sqrt {\frac {2}{m g l} \cdot \frac {J}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}}} = \sqrt {\frac {J}{\pi m \sqrt {g}}} l ^ {- 3 / 4} \\ & A \propto l ^ {- 3 / 4}. \end{array}
$$

振幅

所以

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/4586e82a327372f11267b6023136a77f27e79f655d34ccd317c7ff7183f8525b.jpg)



图11.15


11.6.14 一个质量为 $m$ 的粒子, 被限制在一个盒子里仅能沿 $x$ 轴运动, 盒子的两端的面以比粒子的速率小得多的速率 $v$ 向中心移动, 如图 11.15 所示.

(1) 若盒壁相距 $x_{0}$ 时粒子的动量为 $p_{0}$ ，求出以后任何时候粒子的动量（粒子和盒壁的碰撞是完全弹性的，并设在所考虑的时间内粒子的速率都远小

于光速);

(2) 当盒壁相距 $x$ 时, 为使两壁以恒定速率 $v$ 运动, 必须施加在每一盒壁平均外力多大?

解 因为两壁的速率 v 比粒子的速率小得多, 可认为两壁间的距离缓慢地变小, 对粒子而言, 是一个绝热不变量问题.

(1) 粒子的作用变量 J 为不变量，

$$
\begin{array}{l} J = \oint p \mathrm{d} x = p \cdot 2 x = p _ {0} \cdot 2 x _ {0} \\ p = \frac {p _ {0} x _ {0}}{x} \end{array}
$$

取两壁相距为 $x_0$ 时为 $t = 0$ ，则 $x = x_0 - 2vt$ 

$$
p = \frac {p _ {0} x _ {0}}{x _ {0} - 2 v t}
$$

(2) 粒子对两壁的作用只在发生碰撞时存在. 现考虑平均作用力, 可以视为连续作用. 以两盒壁与粒子为系统, 设作用于两壁的平均外力大小均为 $\overline{F}$ , 方向均与其速度相同.

方法一:对系统用动能定理,注意两壁动能不变,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {1}{2 m} p ^ {2}\right) = 2 \overline {{F}} v} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left[ \frac {1}{2 m} \frac {p _ {0} ^ {2} x _ {0} ^ {2}}{(x _ {0} - 2 v t) ^ {2}} \right] = 2 \overline {{F}} v} \\ & {\frac {1}{2 m} p _ {0} ^ {2} x _ {0} ^ {2} (- 2) \frac {(- 2 v)}{(x _ {0} - 2 v t) ^ {3}} = 2 \overline {{F}} v} \\ & {\overline {{F}} = \frac {p _ {0} ^ {2} x _ {0} ^ {2}}{m (x _ {0} - 2 v t) ^ {3}} = \frac {p _ {0} ^ {2} x _ {0} ^ {2}}{m x ^ {3}}} \end{array}
$$

所以

方法二:考虑粒子与盒壁发生碰撞时动量的增量.以盒壁为参考系,在两壁相距为 x 时,粒子对静参考系的速率为 $\frac{p}{m}$ , 对盒壁参考系,速率为 $\frac{p}{m} + v$ , 做弹性碰撞,碰撞使粒子动量的增量(绝对值)为

$$
\Delta p = m \left(\frac {p}{m} + v\right) - \left[ - m \left(\frac {p}{m} + v\right) \right] = 2 (p + m v)
$$

做这样一次碰撞经历的时间为

$$
\Delta t = \frac {2 x}{\underline {{p}}} = \frac {2 m x}{p}
$$

粒子所受的平均作用力的大小也就是一个盒壁所受的平均作用力的大小，

$$
\overline {{F}} = \frac {\Delta p}{\Delta t} = \frac {2 (p + m v)}{\frac {2 m x}{p}} \approx \frac {p ^ {2}}{m x}
$$

这里用了 $p \gg mv$ . 代入 $p = \frac{p_0 x_0}{x}$ , 即得

$$
\overline {{{F}}} = \frac {p _ {0} ^ {2} x _ {0} ^ {2}}{m x ^ {3}}
$$

11.6.15 一摆由一根长为 $L$ 、质量为 $M$ 均质刚性杆和一个质量为 $\frac{1}{3} M$ 沿杆爬动的昆虫组成, 如图 11.16 所示. 杆的一端安上轴, 在竖直平面内摆动. 当 $t = 0$ 时, 杆偏离铅垂线的角度为 $\theta_0$ , 处于静止, 昆虫爬在杆上有轴的那一端以固定速率 $v$ 慢慢爬向杆的底端, 假设 $\theta_0 \ll 1\mathrm{rad}$ .

(1) 求当昆虫沿杆爬了 l 时摆的摆动频率；

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/71275dc8914eeab64acd18ef2a2ee2d23f0c86325d60ce4ed9186f0feb589f6c.jpg)


(2) 求当昆虫爬到杆的底端时摆的振幅；

(3) 为了使(1)、(2)中的结论有效, 昆虫的爬动应该多慢?

图11.16

解 (1)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {1}{3} M L ^ {2} + \frac {1}{3} M l ^ {2}\right) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{3} M v ^ {2} \\ & \approx \frac {1}{6} M (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2}) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \\ & \nu = \left(\frac {1}{2} M g L + \frac {1}{3} M g l\right) (1 - \cos \theta) \approx \frac {1}{1 2} M g (3 L + 2 l) \theta^ {2} \\ & L = \frac {1}{6} M (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2}) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{1 2} M g (3 L + 2 l) \theta^ {2} \\ & p = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta} = \frac {1}{3} M (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2}) \dot {\theta} \\ & H = p \dot {\theta} - L = \frac {3 p ^ {2}}{2 M (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2})} + \frac {1}{1 2} M g (3 L + 2 l) \theta^ {2} = E \\ & \frac {p ^ {2}}{\frac {2}{3} M (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2}) E} + \frac {\theta^ {2}}{\frac {1 2 E}{M g (3 L + 2 l)}} = 1 \\ & J = \oint p d \theta = \pi \sqrt {\frac {2 M (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2}) E}{3}} \cdot \sqrt {\frac {1 2 E}{M g (3 L + 2 l)}} \\ & = 2 \pi E \sqrt {\frac {2 (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2})}{(3 L + 2 l) g}} \\ & H ^ {*} = E = \frac {J}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {(3 L + 2 l) g}{2 (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2})}} \\ & \therefore = \frac {\partial H ^ {*}}{\partial J} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {(3 L + 2 l) g}{2 (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2})}} \\ & \frac {3}{2 M (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2})} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} \theta}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{1 2} M g (3 L + 2 l) \theta^ {2} = E \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d} W}{\mathrm{d} \theta} = \sqrt {\frac {2 M (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2})}{3}} \left[ E - \frac {1}{1 2} M g (3 L + 2 l) \theta^ {2} \right] \\ & W = \sqrt {\frac {2 M (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2})}{3}} \int \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{1 2} M g (3 L + 2 l) \theta^ {2}} d \theta \\ & w = \frac {\partial W}{\partial J} = \frac {\partial W}{\partial E} \frac {\partial E}{\partial J} \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \sqrt {\frac {(3 L + 2 l) g}{2 (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2})}} \cdot \sqrt {\frac {2 M (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2})}{3}} \int \frac {\mathrm{d} \theta}{2 \sqrt {E - \frac {1}{1 2} M g (3 L + 2 l) \theta^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \int \frac {1}{\sqrt {E - \frac {1}{1 2} M g (3 L + 2 l) \theta^ {2}}} \mathrm{d} \left(\sqrt {\frac {1}{1 2} M g (3 L + 2 l) \theta}\right) \\ & = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \arcsin \left(\sqrt {\frac {M g (3 L + 2 l)}{1 2 E} \theta}\right) \\ & \theta = \sqrt {\frac {1 2 E}{M g (3 L + 2 l)}} \sin (2 \pi w) \\ & A = \sqrt {\frac {1 2 E}{M g (3 L + 2 l)}} = \sqrt {\frac {1 2}{M g (3 L + 2 l)} \sqrt {\frac {J}{2 \pi}} \sqrt {\frac {(3 L + 2 l) g}{2 (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2})}}} \end{array}
$$

振幅

对于一个缓变系统,作用变量 J 非常近似为常量,所以振幅 A 与参量 l 的关系为

$$
A \propto [ (3 L + 2 l) (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2}) ] ^ {- 1 / 4}
$$

在 $l = 0$ 时， $A = \theta_0$ . 设 $l = L$ 时， $A = \theta_1$ ，则

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\theta_ {1}}{\theta_ {0}} & = \left[ \frac {3 L \cdot L ^ {2}}{(3 L + 2 L) (L ^ {2} + L ^ {2})} \right] ^ {1 / 4} = \left(\frac {3}{1 0}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \\ & \theta_ {1} = \left(\frac {3}{1 0}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \theta_ {0}. \end{array}
$$

(3) 为使(1)、(2)的结果有效, 必须满足绝热不变量要求的条件, 要求昆虫的爬动速率 v = i 很慢, 要求.

$$
\frac {l}{l} \ll \nu \qquad \text { 即 } \frac {v}{l} \ll \nu
$$

$$
v \ll {\frac {1}{2 \pi}} l   \sqrt {\frac {(3 L + 2 l) g}{2 (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2})}} \quad {\text {或}} v \ll {\frac {l}{2 \pi}} \sqrt {\frac {3 g}{2 L}}
$$

l 可以很小,但不能严格等于零.上式才有可能满足.

## 第十二章 狭义相对论力学

## 12.1 洛伦兹变换

12.1.1 简单叙述一下需要狭义相对论来解决的难题;叙述一种可能不需要狭义相对论的早期理论,并举出一个证明这种理论是错误的实验;叙述一个证明狭义相对论可信的近代实验.

解 牛顿力学遇到的困难主要是:根据麦克斯韦的电磁场理论,电磁波在真空中的传播速度恒为 c,与辐射源的速度无关,这与惯性系间的伽利略变换相矛盾,真空中的光速不遵从经典力学的速度合成法则.

早期人们提出以太理论,认为以太既在介质中存在又在真空中存在,是电磁波的荷载者.麦克斯韦的电磁理论仅对以太或对以太为静止的参考系才成立.测量地球相对于以太的运动速度的迈克耳孙实验证明以太理论是错误的.实验结果是地球相对于以太是静止的,这显然与认为只有以太是绝对静止的参考系相抵触.既然不能说明以太的存在,以太理论必须放弃.

赫特的实验可以证明狭义相讨论可信. 高速飞行的正电子在湮没时发出两个 $\gamma$ 光子, 实验发现两个光子能同时到达在距湮没发生的地点等距离的探测器, 表明从高速飞行的辐射源向不同方向发射的光有恒定的速率.

12.1.2 菲佐实验所用的装置如图 12.1 所示. 光源发出的、在真空中波长为 $\lambda$ 的光, 经镀有半透膜的玻璃片 M 分成两束相干光, 一束透射, 经 $\mathbf{M}_1, \mathbf{M}_2, \mathbf{M}_3$ , 再在 M 处透射, 射入望远镜 T; 另一束经 M 反射, 经 $\mathbf{M}_3, \mathbf{M}_2, \mathbf{M}_1$ , 再在 M 处反射, 射入望远镜 T, 前者顺水速而行, 后者逆水速而行. 设水速为 $v$ , 水的折射率为 $n$ , 若光相对于静止水中以太的速率为 $\frac{c}{n}$ , 以 $v$ 的速率运动的水带动以太的速率为 $kv$ , 用钠黄光作光源, $\lambda = 5893 \times 10^{-10} \mathrm{~m}, l = 1.5 \mathrm{~m}, v = 7.0 \mathrm{~m/s}, n = 1.33$ , 观察到与 $v = 0$ 的情况相比, 测得移动的干涉条纹为 $\delta = 0.19$ , 根据以太存在的假设, $k$ 应取何值?

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/dd2895711b0e8a829030d1729fc16bed0301a0ba8c92569b695da1fb0a76461a.jpg)



图12.1


解 顺水而行的光束在水中相对于以太的速率为 $\frac{c}{n} - kv$ ，逆水而行的光束在水中相对于以太的速率为 $\frac{c}{n} + kv$ ，两束光从光源出发到达望远镜的光程差为

$$
\left(\frac {2 l}{\frac {c}{n} - k v} - \frac {2 l}{\frac {c}{n} + k v}\right) c
$$

两束光由于此光程差引起移动的干涉条纹数为

$$
\delta = \left[ \frac {2 l}{\frac {c}{n} - k v} - \frac {2 l}{\frac {c}{n} + k v} \right] \frac {c}{\lambda}
$$

解出

$$
k = \frac {\delta \lambda c}{A _ {m} ^ {2}} = \frac {0 . 1 9 \times 5 8 9 3 \times 1 0 ^ {- 1 0} \times 3 \times 1 0 ^ {8}}{4 \times 1 . 5 \times 7 . 0 \times (1 . 3 3) ^ {2}} = 0. 4 5
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/008a8d7487abc5122951177a0320b711ccf85a075760041d3a273a3f91568e86.jpg)



图12.2


12.1.3 改用本题图的实验装置做上述菲佐实验, 半径为 R、折射率为 n 的玻璃圆柱绕固定的 O 点以恒定角速度 $\omega$ 转动. 两束光通过玻璃的距离均为 2l, 求射入望远镜 T 的

两束光的光程差,设圆柱拖动以太的系数为 k.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/fd150f17-e73e-4d09-9a26-1b16115e20c5/1f5448ed46f6bfc0d6e1c8bd606b4e5149e1c06a86679f96901fbd8c14404fb7.jpg)


解 考虑图12.3中 $A$ 点处以太的速度在光的运行方向的分量为


图12.3


$$
\begin{array}{l} {\pm k \omega r \mathrm{cos} \alpha = \pm k \omega d = \pm k \omega \sqrt {R ^ {2} - \left(\frac {l}{2}\right) ^ {2}} = \pm \frac {1}{2} k \omega \sqrt {4 R ^ {2} - l ^ {2}}} \\ {\text {与} \alpha \text {无关}.} \end{array}
$$

两束光的光程差为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\frac {2 l}{\frac {c}{n} - \frac {1}{2} k \omega \sqrt {4 R ^ {2} - l ^ {2}}} - \frac {2 l}{\frac {c}{n} + \frac {1}{2} k \omega \sqrt {4 R ^ {2} - l ^ {2}}}\right) c \\ = \frac {8 l c k \omega n ^ {2} \sqrt {4 R ^ {2} - l ^ {2}}}{4 c ^ {2} - k ^ {2} \omega^ {2} n ^ {2} (4 R ^ {2} - l ^ {2})} \end{array}
$$

12.1.4 一个速度为 $v$ 的航天旅行者和他的地球上的朋友，在出发时对好了钟的时刻分别为 $t' = 0$ （旅行者的钟）和 $t = 0$ （地球上的钟）。地球上的朋友同时观察两个钟，直接观察 $t$ ，用望远镜观察 $t'$ ，试问，当 $t'$ 读数为1小时时， $t$ 的读数为多少？

解 用洛伦兹变换公式, 在动坐标系 $x' = 0, t' = 1$ 小时时相应于地球坐标系中的位置和时间分别为

$$
x = \frac {x ^ {\prime} + v t ^ {\prime}}{\sqrt {1 - \frac {v ^ {2}}{c ^ {2}}}} = \frac {v}{\sqrt {1 - \frac {v ^ {2}}{c ^ {2}}}}
$$

$$
t _ {1} = \frac {t ^ {\prime} + v \frac {x ^ {\prime}}{c ^ {2}}}{\sqrt {1 - \frac {v ^ {2}}{c ^ {2}}}} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \frac {v ^ {2}}{c ^ {2}}}} (\text {小时})
$$

注意:上述写法所用的单位为:长度以米计,时间以小时计.

动钟为 $t' = 1$ 小时的信号传到地球上的朋友处 (x = 0)，需时（地球上钟的示数）

$$
